Mawu Oyamba

'Bhavishya Malika' ndi gulu la malemba mazana ambiri olembedwa zaka pafupifupi 600 zapitazo ndi a Panchasakhas (abwenzi asanu/ogwirizana) a Ambuye Shri Chaitanya Mahaprabhu. Malemba amenewa adabisika kwa zaka mazana ambiri, ndipo panthawiyo, anthu ambiri sanawadziwe. Komabe, mphepo yamkuntho yayikulu yomwe idagunda Odisha mu 1999 idakopa chidwi cha anthu ambiri ku 'Bhavishya Malika' chifukwa zidanenedwa kuti maulosi okhudza mphepo yamkunthoyo anali ataperekedwa kale m'malemba amenewo.

Ngakhale zinali choncho, popeza zinalembedwa m'chilankhulo cha Odia, Bhavishya Malika idangokhala ku Odisha panthawiyo. Pasanathe aliyense kunja kwa Odisha yemwe anali ndi chidziwitso cha malemba amenewa. Koma pang'onopang'ono, nthawi zinasintha. M'chaka cha 2020, mliri wa COVID-19 unakopanso chidwi cha anthu ku Bhavishya Malika. Zinali zitangochitika mliriwu pomwe kulimbikitsa ndi kufalitsa malemba a Malika kunayamba kunja kwa Odisha, ndipo njirayi yapitirira mpaka pano.

Chifukwa cha izi, anthu osawerengeka ayamba kuphunzira za Bhavishya Malika m'zaka zingapo zapitazi. Komabe, monga zimachitikira nthawi zambiri, limodzi ndi kufalitsa kwabwino kumeneku, kutanthauzira molakwika ndi zabodza zokhudza mavesi ena a Malika nayenso anayamba kufalikira. Mwachitsanzo, anthu ena anayamba kufalitsa mphekesera kuti zaka za Kaliyuga ndi 10,000; kuti dzina la mfumu yomaliza ya Puri lidzakhala Mukunda Dev; kuti zochitika zazikulu za Malika ndi machitidwe opatulika owononga (Sanhaar-Leela) a Ambuye Kalki zidzachitika panthawi ya Meena-Shani Yoga yotsatira (nthawi yapakati pa 2054-57); kuti dera la Khandagiri silili ku Bhubaneswar koma lili kwina; ndipo kuti pali zaka zambiri zotsalira Satyayuga isanayambe, ndi zina zotero.

Zabodza zoterezi zinayamba kubweretsa kukayikira kwambiri m'maganizo mwa okhulupirira. Chifukwa chosowa yankho loyenera pa kukayikira kumeneku, chikhulupiriro chawo mu Malika chinayamba kuchepa. Cholinga chachikulu cha nkhaniyi sikungopatsa owerenga atsopano chidziwitso cholondola ndi chenicheni chokhudza Bhavishya Malika komanso kuthetsa kukayikira komwe kumabwera m'maganizo mwa okhulupirira. Tikukhulupirira kuti popeza chidziwitso cholondola chokhudza zinthu zofunika izi, okhulupirira adzatha kulimbikitsa chikhulupiriro chawo mu Malika.

1 Bhavishya Malika – Mau Oyamba Achidule

1.1 Mau Oyamba

Pakati pa zaka za m'ma 1500 ndi 1600, oyera mtima asanu akuluakulu obadwira ku Odisha (Shishu Ananta Das, Yashobanta Das, Achyutananda Das, Balarama Das, ndi Jagannatha Das) analemba mazana ambiri a mabuku otere momwe maulosi osawerengeka analembedwa. Mabuku amenewa anadziwika kuti 'Bhavishya Malika'. Sri Chaitanya Mahaprabhu, wobadwa mu 1486 ndipo amadziwika kuti ndi kubadwanso kawiri kwa Bhagawan Sri Krishna ndi Jaganmata Radharani, anali ndi ubale wakuya ndi oyera mtima asanuwa. Sri Chaitanya Mahaprabhu anawatcha asanuwa kuti 'Panchasakha' (Anzake Asanu). A Panchasakha amenewa analemba mabuku a Malika motsatira lamulo la Mahaprabhu mwiniwake. Kuwonjezera pa a Panchasakha, oyera mtima ena akuluakulu anapitirizanso mwambo wolemba Malika. Mwachitsanzo, wokhulupirira Salabega wobadwa m'zaka za m'ma 1600, Saint Arkhit Das ndi Saint Hadidas obadwa m'zaka za m'ma 1700, Saint Bhima Bhoi wa m'zaka za m'ma 1800, Thakur Shri Abhiram Paramahansa wa m'zaka za m'ma 1900, ndi zina zotero.

Maulosi atsatanetsatane analembedwa m'mabuku a Malika amenewa pa nkhani monga zaka za Kaliyuga, kubadwa kwa Ambuye Kalki, chiwonongeko cha oipa, chitetezo cha odzipereka, kukhazikitsidwa kwa Dharma, ndi kufika kwa Satyayuga.

1.2 Kodi a Panchasakha anali ndani?

Mmodzi mwa a Panchasakha, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, analemba m'buku lake limodzi la Malika kuti:

Odia Sloka:

ବଳବନ୍ତ ଯଶୋବନ୍ତ ଅନନ୍ତ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ,
ଜଗନ୍ନାଥ ଦାସ ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ନାମେ ଖ୍ୟାତ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Balabanta Yaśhobanta Ananta Achyuta,
Jagannātha Dāsa Pañchasakhā nāme khyāta.

Tanthauzo:
Balarama Das, Yashobanta Das, Shishu Ananta Das, Achyutananda Das, ndi Jagannatha Das anadziwika ngati a Panchasakha.

Ndikofunika kudziwa kuti mbiri ya a Panchasakha ndi yakale kwambiri. Pamapeto pa nthawi iliyonse (Yuga), a Panchasakha amabwera Padziko Lapansi, obadwa kuchokera ku gawo la Ambuye, kuti apereke chithandizo chawo chamtengo wapatali pa ntchito ya Ambuye yobwezeretsa Dharma.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das analemba m'buku lake la Malika ‘Udaya Kahani’ kuti:

Odia Sloka:

ଆମ୍ଭେ ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ପଞ୍ଚକୁଳରେ,
ଜନମ ହୋଇଲୁ କୃଷ୍ଣ ଆଜ୍ଞାରେ।
ଯୁଗ ଯୁଗରେ ଯେ ଜନମ ହେଉ,
କୃଷ୍ଣଲୀଳାମାନ ମୁଖରେ ଗାଉ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Ambhe Pañchasakhā pañchakulare,
janama hoilu Kṛṣhṇa ājñāre.
yuga yugare je janama heu,
Kṛṣhṇalīḷāmāna mukhare gau.

Tanthauzo:
M'ndime iyi, Saint Achyutananda Das akuti: "Ife, anzathu asanu, timabadwa m'mabanja asanu osiyanasiyana motsatira lamulo la Ambuye Krishna. Nthawi iliyonse, timabadwa ndipo timaimba za Leela zaumulungu za Krishna kuchokera m'kamwa mwathu."

1.3 Kuwonekera kwa a Panchasakha M'nthawi Zinayi

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, m'buku lake la Malika ‘Udaya Kahani’, akufotokoza maonekedwe omwe oyera mtima asanu akuluakulu anatenga mu Yuga iliyonse mwa zinayi:

Odia Sloka:

ଆଦିଅନନ୍ତ ଯୁଗଟି ଅନ୍ତରେ, ସତ୍ୟ ପ୍ରବେଶ ହୋଇଲା ମହୀରେ।
ଅସୁର ବିନାଶ କରିବା ପାଇଁ, ପ୍ରଭୁ ଜନମିଲେ ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣେ ଯାଇଁ।
ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ଋଷିତନୁ ବହିଲେ, କୃପାଜଳ ଋଷି ଗାଗର୍ବ ହେଲେ।
ମାର୍କଣ୍ଡ ଯେ ସ୍ୱୟଂଭୂ ନାରଦ, ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ଜନମ କହିଲି ଭେଦ।
ରାମ ଜନମ ତ୍ରେତୟା ଯୁଗରେ, ତ୍ରେତୟା ଗୁଣ କହିଲେ ନ ସରେ।
ତ୍ରେତୟାରେ ନଳ ନୀଳ ସୁଷେଣ, ଜାମ୍ବବ ହନୁ ପଞ୍ଚସଖାମାନ।
ତ୍ରେତୟା ଅନ୍ତେ ଦ୍ୱାପର ପ୍ରବେଶ, ଗୋପାଳ ମୂରତି ଶ୍ରୀକୃଷ୍ଣ ବେଶ।
ପଞ୍ଚସଖାମାନେ ହେଲେ ଜନମ, ସୁବଳ ସୁବାହୁ ସୁଦାମ ଦାମ।
ଶ୍ରୀବତ୍ସ ଘେନି ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ହେଲେ, ଦ୍ୱାପର ଲୀଳାମାନ ସାଙ୍ଗ କଲେ।
ଅବନୀ ମଧ୍ୟରେ ନାମ ସ୍ଥାପିଲେ, ହରିନାମରେ ଜଗତ ମୋହିଲେ।
ସ୍ତ୍ରୀ ଶୂଦ୍ର ଆଦି ପାଷାଣ୍ଡ ଯେତେ, ହରିନାମ ଶୁଣି ହେଲେ ମୁକତେ।
ଆମ୍ଭେ ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ଜନମ ହେଲୁ, ଅନନ୍ତ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ନାମ ବହିଲୁ।
ଜଗନ୍ନାଥ ଯଶୋବନ୍ତ ହୋଇଲେ, ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ମେଳ ବଳରାମରେ।
ଏମନ୍ତ ନାମରେ ଆମ୍ଭେ ଜନମ, କହିଦେଲୁ ବାବୁ ତୋର ଆଗେଣ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Adi Ananta Yugaṭi Antare, Satya Prabesha Hoilā Mahire.
Asura Bināsha Karibā Pāiñ, Prabhu Janamile Brāhmaṇe Jāiñ.
Pañchasakhā Ṛuṣi Tanu Bahile, Kṛupājala Ṛuṣi Gāgarba Hele.
Mārkaṇḍa Je Swayambhū Nārada, Pañchasakhā Janama Kahili Bheda.
Rāma Janama Tretayā Yugare, Tretayā Guṇa Kahile Na Sare.
Tretayāre Naḷa Nīḷa Suṣeṇa, Jāmbaba Hanu Pañchasakhāmāna.
Tretayā Ante Dwāpara Prabesha, Gopāḷa Mūrati Shrī Kṛuṣṇa Besha.
Pañchasakhāmāne Hele Janama, Subaḷa Subāhu Sudāma Dāma.
Shrībatsa Gheni Pañchasakhā Hele, Dwāpara Līḷāmāna Sāṅga Kale.
Abanī Madhyare Nāma Sthāpile, Harināmare Jagata Mohile.
Strī Shūdra Ādi Pāṣāṇḍa Jete, Harināma Shuṇi Hele Mukate.
Ambhe Pañchasakhā Janama Helu, Ananta Achyuta Nāma Bahilu.
Jagannātha Jashobanta Hoile, Pañchasakhā Meḷa Baḷarāmare.
Emanta Nāmare Ambhe Janama, Kahidelu bābu tora āgeṇa.

Tanthauzo:

Ndiko kuti, m'nthawi zakale, pamapeto pa Ananta Yuga pamene Satyayuga inayamba, Ambuye Wamkulu anabadwa m'maonekedwe a Brahmin kuti awononge ziwanda. Panthawiyo, oyera mtima asanuwo anawonekera kuchokera m'thupi la Ambuye monga Ansembe: Kripajala, Garga (Gagarba), Markandeya, Swayambhu, ndi Narada.

Kutsatira Satyayuga, mu Tretayuga, pamene Ambuye anachita masewera osawerengeka m'kubadwa kwake kwa Rama, anzake asanu omwewo anabwera Padziko Lapansi m'maonekedwe a Nala, Nila, Sushena, Jambavan (Jambeba), ndi Hanuman.

Pamapeto pa Tretayuga, Dwaparyuga inafika, ndipo Ambuye anabadwa m'maonekedwe a mnyamata woweta ng'ombe, Shri Krishna. Panthawiyi, Subala, Subahu, Sudama, Dama, ndi Shribatsa anakhala a Panchasakha ndipo anatenga nawo mbali m'masewera aumulungu ndi Shri Krishna.

Komanso, mu Kaliyuga, pamene Ambuye anawonekera m'kubadwa kwake kwa Chaitanya, anzake asanuwa anabadwa kuti afalitse dzina loyera (Hari-naam), kukhazikitsa njira yosavuta yopulumutsira aliyense. Mu Kaliyuga, anadziwika ngati Shishu Ananta Das, Achyutananda Das, Jagannatha Das, Yashobanta Das, ndi Balarama Das.

1.4 Mbiri Yatsatanetsatane ya a Panchasakha M'nthawi Zosiyanasiyana

Pamene wophunzira Ramadas anafunsa za mayina enieni a a Panchasakha m'nthawi zinayi, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das anapereka nkhani yatsatanetsatane iyi m'buku lake la Malika ‘Udaya Kahani’.

Odia Sloka:

ସତ୍ୟ ଯୁଗରେ ଋଷି କୃପାଜଳ, ତ୍ରେତୟା ସଖାଟି ଅଟଇ ନଳ।
ଦ୍ୱାପରେ ସୁଦାମ ସଖାଟି ହୋଇ, କଳି ଯୁଗରେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବୋଲାଇ।
ଗାର୍ଗବ ଋଷି ସତ୍ୟ ଯୁଗରେ ହେଲେ, ତ୍ରେତୟା ଜାମ୍ବବ ନାମ ବହିଲେ।
ଦ୍ୱାପରେ ସୁବାହୁ ବ୍ରଜ ଅଟନ୍ତି, ଯଶୋବନ୍ତ ନାମ କଳିରେ ପାନ୍ତି।
ସ୍ୱୟଂଭୂ ସତ୍ୟ ଯୁଗରେ ସେ ଜାଣେ, ତ୍ରେତୟାରେ ଅଟେ ସେହି ସୁଷେଣ।
ଗୋପରେ ଶ୍ରୀବତ୍ସ ଦ୍ୱାପରେ ଜାତ, କଳି ଯୁଗରେ ନାମ ଶିଶୁ ଅନନ୍ତ।
ସତ୍ୟ ଯୁଗରେ ସଖା ନାରଦ ଋଷି, ତ୍ରେତୟାରେ ନୀଳ ନାମ ପ୍ରକାଶି।
ଦାମ ଗୋପାଳ ଦ୍ୱାପର ଯୁଗରେ, ବଳରାମ ଦାସ ନାମ କଳିରେ।
ମାର୍କଣ୍ଡ ଋଷି ଅଟେ ହନୁମାନ, ସୁବଳ ସଖାଟି ଦ୍ୱାପରେ ପୁଣ।
ଜଗନ୍ନାଥ ନାମ କଳିରେ ହେଲେ, ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ନାମ ପ୍ରକାଶ କଲେ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Satya Yugare Ṛuṣi Kṛupājala, Tretayā Sakhāṭi Aṭai Naḷa.
Dwāpare Sudāma Sakhāṭi Hoi, Kaḷi Yugare Achyuta Bolāi.
Gārgaba Ṛuṣi Satya Yugare Hele, Tretayā Jāmbaba Nāma Bahile.
Dwāpare Subāhu Braja Aṭanti, Jashobanta Nāma Kaḷire Pānti.
Swayambhū Satya Yugare Se Jāṇe, Tretayāre Aṭe Sehi Suṣeṇa.
Gopare Shrībatsa Dwāpare Jāta, Kaḷi Yugare Nāma Shishu Ananta.
Satya Yugare Sakhā Nārada Ṛuṣi, Tretayāre Nīḷa Nāma Prakāshi.
Dāma Gopāḷa Dwāpara Yugare, Baḷarāma Dāsa Nāma Kaḷire.
Mārkaṇḍa Ṛuṣi Aṭe Hanumāna, Subaḷa Sakhāṭi Dwāpare Puṇa.
Jagannātha Nāma Kaḷire Hele, Pañchasakhā Nāma Prakāsha Kale.

Kumasulira kwa Mayina:
  • Achyutananda Das: Anali Sage Kripajala mu Satyayuga, Nala mu Tretayuga, ndi Sudama mu Dwaparayuga.
  • Yashobanta Das: Anali Sage Garga mu Satyayuga, Jambavan mu Tretayuga, ndi Subahu mu Dwaparayuga.
  • Shishu Ananta Das: Anali Sage Swayambhu mu Satyayuga, Sushena mu Tretayuga, ndi Shribatsa mu Dwaparayuga.
  • Balarama Das: Anali Sage Narada mu Satyayuga, Nila mu Tretayuga, ndi Dama mu Dwaparayuga.
  • Jagannatha Das: Anali Sage Markandeya mu Satyayuga, Hanuman mu Tretayuga, ndi Subala mu Dwaparayuga.

1.5 Mphamvu Zapadera ndi Luso la a Panchasakha

A Panchasakha anali oyera mtima odziwa zonse, odzizindikira okha, komanso odziwa Mulungu. Anali Yogis apamwamba, ofunafuna, ndi odzipereka, aliyense akuchita bwino m'munda wosiyana wa sayansi yauzimu. Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akufotokoza izi m'buku lake la Malika:

Odia Sloka:

ଆଗମ୍ୟ ଭାବ ଜାଣେ ଯଶୋବନ୍ତ,
ଗାର କଟା ଯନ୍ତ୍ର ଜାଣେ ଅନନ୍ତ।
ଆଗତ ଅନାଗତ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଭଣେ,
ବଳରାମ ଦାସ ତତ୍ତ୍ୱ ବଖାଣେ।
ଭକ୍ତିର ଭାବ ଜାଣେ ଜଗନ୍ନାଥ,
ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ଏ ଓଡ଼ିଶା ମହନ୍ତ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Āgamya Bhāba Jāṇe Jashobanta,
Gāra Kaṭā Jantra Jāṇe Ananta.
Āgata Anāgata Achyuta Bhaṇe,
Baḷarāma Dāsa Tattwa Bakhāṇe.
Bhaktira Bhāba Jāṇe Jagannātha,
Pañchasakhā E Oḍiśā Mahanta.

Tanthauzo:
Mwa a Panchasakha obadwira ku Odisha:
  • Yashobanta Das anali wodziwa zinsinsi zakuya za zakale ndi zam'tsogolo (Agam-Nigam).
  • Shishu Ananta Das anali katswiri pa sayansi ya zithunzi zamatsenga ndi makina (Yantra-Vigyan).
  • Achyutananda Das anali wopenya zakale, zamakono, ndi zam'tsogolo (Trikal-Darshi).
  • Balarama Das anali katswiri wozama wa zenizeni zenizeni ndi filosofi (Tattwa-Vigyan).
  • Jagannatha Das anali wosayerekezeka mu chikondi chodzipereka choyera (Bhakti-Bhaav).

1.6 Malo Opatulika (Ashrams) a a Panchasakha

A Panchasakha adakhazikitsa ma Peetha awo ndi malo awo opatulika m'malo asanu osiyanasiyana ku Odisha. Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akufotokoza izi m'buku lake la Malika 'Chakada Madana':

Odia Sloka:

ନେମାଳେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଅଢ଼ଙ୍ଗେ ଯଶୋବନ୍ତ, ବାଲିପାଟଣାରେ ରହିଲେ ଅନନ୍ତ।
ସାତ ଲହଡ଼ିରେ ଜଗନ୍ନାଥ ରହି, କ୍ଷେତ୍ରବରେ ବଳବନ୍ତ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Nemāḷe Achyuta Aḍhaṅge Jashobanta, Bālipāṭaṇāre rahiḷe Ananta.
Sāta Lahaḍire Jagannātha rahi, Khetrabare Baḷabanta.

Tanthauzo:
  • Achyutananda Das adakhazikitsa mpando wake ku Nemalo (chigawo cha Cuttack).
  • Yashobanta Das adakhazikitsa Peetha yake ku Adhanga (chigawo cha Jagatsinghpur).
  • Shishu Ananta Das (Ananta) adakhazikitsa nyumba yake ya amonke ku Balipatna (chigawo cha Khurda).
  • Jagannatha Das adakhazikitsa Ashram yake ku Sata Lahadi.
  • Balarama Das (Balabanta) adakhazikitsa Shrikhetra (Puri) kukhala malo ake okhala.

1.7 A Panchasakha – Kubadwa kwa Mulungu kwa Ambuye

A Panchasakha anena kangapo m'mabuku awo a Malika kuti iwo ndi kubadwa kwapang'ono (Anshavatar) kwa Ambuye Wamkulu Mwiniwake. Mahapurush Shishu Ananta Das akulemba m'buku lake 'Chumbak Malika':

Odia Sloka:

ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବୋଲିଣ କଳିଯୁଗେ ନାମ ସୁଦାମ ସଖା ଅଟନ୍ତି,
ଶ୍ରୀଅଙ୍ଗରୁ ଜାତ କଟିରୁ ଭକତ ଶୁଣ ହୋ ବାରଙ୍ଗ ଚେତି।
ମୁଖୁଁ ଯଶୋବନ୍ତ ହୃଦୁଁ ଆମ୍ଭେ ଜାତ ବାହୁ ମୂଁଳୁ ବଳରାମ,
ରାଧାଙ୍କ ହାସ୍ୟରୁ ଜଗନ୍ନାଥ ଦାସ ବାରଙ୍ଗ ହେତୁରେ ଘେନ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Achyuta boliṇa Kaḷiyuge nāma Sudāma sakhā aṭanti,
Shrī-aṅgaru jāta kaṭiru bhakata shuṇa ho Bāraṅga cheti.
Mukhuṃ Jashobanta hṛuduṃ ambhe jāta bāhu mūṃḷu Baḷarāma,
Rādhāṅka hāsyaru Jagannātha Dāsa Bāraṅga heture ghena.

Tanthauzo:
Mu Kaliyuga, Achyutananda Das ndi bwenzi Sudama wochokera ku Dwaparayuga ndipo adabadwa kuchokera m'chiuno (Kati) cha Ambuye. Yashobanta Das adabadwa kuchokera m'kamwa mopatulika (Shri-mukh) cha Mahaprabhu. Shishu Ananta Das adawonekera kuchokera mumtima (Hriday) wa Ambuye, ndipo Balarama Das adabadwa kuchokera m'manja (Bahu) a Ambuye. Kuwonjezera apo, Jagannatha Das adatsika kuchokera kuseka (Haasya) kwa Amayi Radharani.

Osati Shishu Ananta Das ji yekha, komanso Mahapurush Achyutananda Das walembanso za izi m'buku lake la Malika, 'Chakada Madana'. Mahapurush akulemba:

Odia Sloka:

ନିରାକାର ଦେହୁ ପାଞ୍ଚଟି ଜ୍ୟୋତି,
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଅନନ୍ତ ପଞ୍ଚକ ମୂରତି।
ଯଶୋବନ୍ତ ଜଗନ୍ନାଥ ବଳରାମ,
ଜନମ ହୋଇଲୁ ଏ ମର୍ତ୍ତ୍ୟ ଧାମ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Nirākāra dehu pāṃchaṭi jyoti,
Achyuta Anaṃta paṃchaka mūrati.
Jashobaṃta Jagannātha Balarāma,
janama hoilu e martya dhāma.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, kuchokera ku thupi lowala la Ambuye Wopanda Mawonekedwe, ife a Panchasakha asanu tidatsika, ndipo tidabadwa padziko lapansi pansi pa mayina a Achyutananda, Shishu Ananta, Jashobanta, Jagannatha, ndi Balarama.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akulemba m'buku la Malika, 'Barnatika':

Odia Sloka:

ଶ୍ରୀ କୃଷ୍ଣ ଶରୀର ପଞ୍ଚଭୂତରୁ,
ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ଜନ୍ମ କହିଛି ଗୁରୁ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Shrī Kṛiṣhṇa sharīra paṃchabhūtaru,
paṃchasakhā janma kahichi guru.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, kubadwa kwa a Panchasakha kunachokera ku zinthu zisanu za thupi la Ambuye Shri Krishna.

1.8 Maulosi Mwa Lamulo la Mulungu

Mavesi a Malika amaonedwa kuti ndi mawu a Mahaprabhu Mwiniwake. Mmodzi mwa a Panchasakha, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, akulemba m'buku lake la Malika 'Shiva-kalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta':

Odia Sloka:

ମୁଁ କହୁଛି ବୋଲି ମୋର କଥା ନୁହେଁ ଆଦ୍ୟୁଁ ଖଞ୍ଜିଛନ୍ତି ଧାତା,
କର୍ମବାମ ହେଲେ କଳ୍ପନା ଗ୍ରାସିବ ପ୍ରକୃତି କାଟିବେ ଚିତା।

Kumasulira mawu:

Muṃ kahuchi boli mora kathā nuheṃ ādyuṃ khañjichanti dhātā,
Karmabāma hele kalpanā grāsiba prakṛuti kāṭibe chitā.

Tanthauzo:
Mahapurush akuti: "Chilichonse chimene ndikulemba, awa si mawu anga. Zonsezi ndi zinthu zomwe Ambuye adazikonza kale kuyambira pachiyambi."

Mahapurush Shishu Ananta Das adauzanso wophunzira wake mu 'Patta Madana':

Odia Sloka:

ଆହୁରି କଥାଏ ଶୁଣରେ ବାରଙ୍ଗ ହେତୁରଖ ଯାକୁସାର,
ଭାବବିନୋଦିଆ ହରିଙ୍କ ଏ ବାଣୀ ଅଟେ ସିନା ଅଗୋଚର।

Kumasulira mawu:

Āhuri kathāe shuṇare Bāraṅga heturakha jākusāra,
Bhābabinodiā Hariṅka e bāṇī aṭe sinā agochara.

Tanthauzo:
"O wophunzira Baranga, mvera! Awa si mawu anga, koma ndi mawu a Ambuye Shri Hari."

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das adalemba m'buku lake la Malika, 'Anakara Samhita':

Odia Sloka:

ଛତିଶ ସଂହିତା ଅଠସ୍ତରୀ ଗୀତା ବଂଶାନୁ ସପ୍ତବିଂଶରେ,
ଉପବଂଶାନୁ ଦ୍ୱାଦଶ ଖଣ୍ଡ ବେନି ଭବିଷ୍ୟ ଶତ ଖଣ୍ଡ ରେ।
ପଦ ପଦ୍ୟାବଳି ଲକ୍ଷେକ ଯେ ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ ସବୁ ଶ୍ରୀକୃଷ୍ଣ ମହିମା,
ତୋ ଆଗେ କହିଲୁଁ ବ୍ରଜ କୁମର ବ୍ରହ୍ମ ସାରସ୍ୱତ ସୀମା।

Kumasulira mawu:

Chatisha saṃhitā aṭhastarī gītā baṃshānu saptabiṃshare,
upabaṃshānu dwādasha khaṃḍa beni bhabiṣhya shata khaṃḍa re.
Pada padyābali lakṣheka je graṃtha sabu Shrīkṛiṣhṇa mahimā,
to āge kahilu braja kumara brahma sāraswata sīmā.

Tanthauzo:
Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akuti mabuku omwe adalemba akuphatikizapo ma Samhita 36, ma Gita 78, ma Banshanucharitra 27, ma Upabanshanucharitra 24, mabuku a Malika 100, ndi mazana masauzande a mavesi (padas). Mahapurush adanenanso kuti mabuku onsewa adalembedwa ndi ulemerero kapena chisomo cha Ambuye Shri Krishna.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das adalemba m'buku lake la Malika, 'Shibakalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta':

Odia Sloka:

ଶ୍ରୀରଙ୍ଗା ଚରଣ ବିନୁ ନ ଜାଣଇ ନୀଳାଚଳେ ମନ ମୋର,
ତାଙ୍କ କଥାମାନ ଉଲ୍ଲେଖ କରଇ ନ କହେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଛାର।

Kumasulira mawu:

Shrī raṃgācharaṇa binu na jāṇai nīlāchale mana mora,
tāṃka kathāmāna ullekha karai na kahe Achyuta chāra.

Tanthauzo:
Mu sloka iyi, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akuti maganizo ake sagwirizana ndi china chilichonse kupatula mapazi a Ambuye Wamkulu. Maganizo ake amakhalabe otanganidwa mu Nilachala Dhama, Puri, ndiko kuti, mu chikondi chodzipereka kwa Ambuye. Akunenanso kuti chilichonse chimene akulemba m'mabuku a Malika, onse ndi mawu enieni olankhulidwa ndi Ambuye; sanalembepo chilichonse mwa kufuna kwake.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das adalembanso m'buku lake la Malika, 'Parardha Jataka':

Odia Sloka:

ପ୍ରଭୁଙ୍କର ଆଜ୍ଞା ହେଲା ଯେପରି,
ଆମ୍ଭେ ଲେଖିଲୁ ପୋଥି ସେହିପରି।

Kumasulira mawu:

Prabhunkara ājñā helā jepari,
āmbhe lekhilu pothi sehipari.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, monga momwe Ambuye Wamkulu adalamulira ndi kulamula, ndalemba buku la Malika m'njira yomweyo.

Achyutanandaji adalemba mu 'Agata Bhabishya Prabandha Malika' yake:

Odia Sloka:

ଶ୍ରୀ ମୁଖରୁ ଯାହା ମୋତେ ଆଜ୍ଞା ହେଉଅଛି,
ସେହି ପଦମାନ ସବୁ ପୁରାଣେ ଲେଖୁଛି।

Kumasulira mawu:

Shrī mukharu jāhā mote ājñā heuachi,
sehi padamāna sabu purāṇe lekhuchi.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, mwa lamulo la Ambuye Wamkulu, mwa malangizo Ake enieni, ndikulemba Malika. Chilichonse chimene chinachokera kwa Ambuye, ndikufotokoza ma sloka omwewo mu Malika Purana.

1.9 Malika Ndi Buku Lachinsinsi Ndipo Maulosi Ake Ndi Obisika

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akulemba mu 'Shiva-kalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta':

Odia Sloka:

ଯୋଗୀମାନେ ଯୋଗ ଅନ୍ତ ନ ପାଇବେ ଆଉ କେବା ସାମରଥା।

Kumasulira mawu:

Yogīmāne yoga anta na pāibe āu kebā sāmarathā.

Tanthauzo:
Ngakhale ma Yogi akuluakulu ndi anzeru sangathe kupeza chinsinsi cha zinthu zobisika izi kudzera mu Yoga, osatinso anthu ena.

Achyutananda Das akulembanso za chikhalidwe chobisika cha bukuli:

Odia Sloka:

ଓଲଟ ପାଲଟ କକ୍ଷା ଏହି ଅଙ୍କ, କର ହୃଦେ ତୁହି ଠାବ।
କଳିଯୁଗ ଲୀଳା ଶେଷ ଯେ ହୋଇବ, ମସିହାରେ ନାହିଁ ଠାବ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Olaṭa pālaṭa kakshā ehi aṅka, kara hṛude tuhi ṭhāba.
Kaḷiyuga līḷā shesha je hoiba, masihāre nāhiṃ ṭhāba.

Tanthauzo:
Maulosi alembedwa kutengera kuwerengera manambala, ndipo manambalawa abisika powasinthasintha. Zochitika zidzachitika koma osati malinga ndi zaka za kalendala wamba; zimatsatira kuwerengera kwachinsinsi kwachizindikiro.

Ponena za kubadwa kwa Kalki, akulemba:

Odia Sloka:

ଗୁପତରେ ଆମ୍ଭେ ଜନମ ହେବୁ,
ଗୁପତରେ ସନ୍ ସାଲ ଚାଲିବୁ।
ଗୁପତ ଭକତ ଜାଣନ୍ତି ଯାହା,
ଗୁପତ ଅଙ୍କ ଏ ଅଟେ ଏହା।
ଗୁପତରେ କେତେ ଖେଳ କରିବୁ,
ଗୁପତ ଅଙ୍କକୁ ଚଳାଇ ଥିବୁ।
ମୋର ଭକତଟି ଜାଣିବେ ସେହି,
ଗୁପତ ଅଙ୍କ ବୋଲିଟି ଯେ ଏହି।
ଗୁପତ ଗୁପତ ଅତି ଗୁପତ,
କହୁଅଛନ୍ତି ସେ ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Gupatare ambhe janama hebu,
gupatare san sāla chālibu.
Gupata bhakata jāṇanti jāhā,
gupata aṅka e aṭe ehā.
Gupatare kete kheḷa karibu,
gupata aṅkaku chaḷāi thibu.
Mora bhakataṭi jāṇibe sehi,
gupata aṅka boliṭi ye ehi.
Gupata gupata ati gupata,
kahuachanti se dīna Achyuta.

Tanthauzo:
Kubadwa kwa Ambuye kudzakhala mwachinsinsi. Nthawi ndi zaka zidzapita mobisika. Okhawo odzipereka achinsinsi ndi omwe adzadziwe izi chifukwa manambala abisika.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das analemba m'buku lake la Malika, 'Chakada Madana':

Odia Sloka:

ଗୁପତ ଗୁପତ ଏ ମହା ଗୁପତ ଆନେ ନ କହିବୁ ତୁହି,
ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଯେ ଗୀତରେ କହଇ ମୋ ପାଦକୁ ଥାଅ ଛୁଇଁ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Gupata gupata e mahā gupata āne na kahibu tuhi,
dīna Achyuta je gītare kahai mo pādaku thāa chuiṃ.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiye kuti, awa si mawu achinsinsi chabe, koma mawu achinsinsi kwambiri amene Achyutananda Das ji analemba pokumbukira Ambuye ndi kusinkhasinkha pa mapazi Ake ngati maluwa a lotus.

M'buku lina, Mahapurush anauza wophunzira wake Ramadasa:

Odia Sloka:

କଳ୍କୀ ପ୍ରକାଶ ହେଲେ ତୋହ ମୋହ ଦେଖା,
ତେରଜଣ ମଧ୍ୟେ ଥିବୁ ଆମ୍ଭେ ପଞ୍ଚସଖା।
ଗୁପତ ଅତି ଗୁପତ ଅଟଇ ହେ ରାମ,
କହୁଅଛି ବ୍ରଜ ବାଳ ହେତୁରେ ତୁ ଘେନ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Kalki prakāsha hele toha moha dekhā,
terajaṇa madhye thibu āmbhe pañchasakhā.
Gupata ati gupata aṭai he Rāma,
kahuachi braja bāla heture tu ghena.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiye kuti, Ambuye Kalki adzabwera, kuonekera kwawo kwaumulungu kudzachitika, ndipo ife a Panchasakhas tidzapeza mwayi wabwino wotumikira ndi kukhala ogonjera pa mapazi Ake. Koma masewera aumulungu awa sadzachitika mobisa chabe, koma mobisa kwambiri.

Mahapurush analembanso kuti:

Odia Sloka:

ଗୁପତ ଗୁପତ ଏ ମହା ଗୁପତ ଶୁଣ ତୁ ରେ ରାମଚନ୍ଦ୍ର,
ବ୍ରହ୍ମା ଶଙ୍କରକୁ ଭେଦ ତ ନଥିବ ନ ଜାଣିବେ ଇନ୍ଦ୍ର ଚନ୍ଦ୍ର।

Kumasulira mawu:

Gupata gupata e mahā gupata shuṇa tu re Rāmachandra,
Brahmā Shaṅkaraku bheda ta nathiba na jāṇibe Indra Chandra.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiye kuti, O wophunzira Ramachandra, mawu a Malika si achinsinsi chabe, koma achinsinsi kwambiri. Ngakhale Prajapati Brahma ndi Ambuye Shankar sadzatha kumvetsa zinsinsi zawo, osatinso Ambuye Indra ndi Ambuye Chandra.

Sant Bhima Bhoi analembanso za nkhaniyi m'buku lake la Malika, 'Stuti Chintamani':

Odia Sloka:

ସଂସାର ମଧ୍ୟରେ କେମନ୍ତେ ଜାଣିବେ ନର ଅଙ୍ଗେ ଦେହ ବହି,
ଗତ ଆଗତ ଯୁଗର ବ୍ୟବସ୍ଥା ସମସ୍ତଙ୍କୁ ଜଣାନାହିଁ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Saṃsāra madhyare kemaṃte jāṇibe nara aṅge deha bahi,
gata āgata yugara byabasthā samastaṃku jaṇānāhiṃ.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiye kuti, amuna ndi akazi osawerengeka padziko lapansi sadzatha kupeza zambiri za dongosolo la nthawi, kutha kwa Kali Yuga, Malika, ndi maulosi ake. Okhawo amene Ambuye adzawapatsa chisomo Chake, amene anali odzipereka kwa Ambuye m'nthawi zakale, ndiwo adzatha kudziwa za maulosi ozama kwambiriwa.

Tsopano funso likubuka, kodi ndi ndani odzipereka a Ambuye ochokera m'nthawi zakale amene Ambuye adzawapatsa chisomo Chake ndipo adzakhulupirira mawu a Malika, adzatha kudziwa za iwo, ndipo adzakhala ndi chidziwitso cholondola chokhudza kutha kwa Kali Yuga ndi Kalki avatar!

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das anapereka yankho la funsoli m'buku lake la Malika, 'Shibakalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta'. Mahapurush akulemba kuti:

Odia Sloka:

କୋଟିକେ ଗୋଟିଏ ଜାଣନ୍ତି ଏ ରସ ତିନି ସସ୍ରେ ଗଣାସେହି,
ମହିମା ପ୍ରକାଶ ନିଶ୍ଚେ ସେହି ଦାସ ଆନେ ମୁଁ କହନ୍ତି ନାହିଁ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Koṭike goṭie jāṇaṃti e rasa tini sasre gaṇāसेhi,
mahimā prakāsha nishche sehi dāsa āne muṃ kahaṃti nāhiṃ.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiye kuti, munthu mmodzi yekha mwa anthu mamiliyoni khumi (crore) ndiye adzatha kudziwa za maulosiwa, ndipo chiwerengero chonse cha anthu otere padziko lonse lapansi chidzakhala 3,000 okha.

1.10 Maulosi a Malika Sadzalephera

Bhavishya Malika ili ndi mawu a Ambuye Mwiniwake, choncho palibe funso loti adzalephera. M'buku la Malika ‘Chakada Madana’, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akulemba kuti:

Odia Sloka:

ପୃଥିବୀ ଦୁଇଫାଳ ହେଲେ ହୋଇବ, ପୂର୍ବ ସୂର୍ଯ୍ୟ ଅବା ପଶ୍ଚିମେ ଯିବ।
ପର୍ବତ ଶିଖରେ ଫୁଟିବ କଇଁ, ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବଚନ ମିଥ୍ୟା ନୁହଇଁ।
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତର ବାଣୀ ଅଟେ ପଥରର ଗାର, ଆନ କରିବାକୁ ନାହିଁ କ୍ଷମତା କାହାର।

Kumasulira mawu:

Pṛuthibī duiphāḷa hele hoiba, pūrba sūrya abā pashchime jiba.
Parbata shikhare phuṭiba kaiñ, Achyuta bachana mithyā nuhaiñ.
Achyutara bāṇī aṭe patharara gāra, āna karibāku nāhiñ khyamatā kāhāra.

Tanthauzo:
"Dziko lapansi likhoza kugawanika kawiri, dzuwa lotuluka kum'mawa likhoza kutuluka kumadzulo, duwa la lotus likhoza kuphuka pamwamba pa phiri—koma mawu a Achyutananda sangakhale abodza. Ali ngati mzere wojambulidwa pamwala, ndipo palibe amene ali ndi mphamvu yowatsutsa."

Mu ‘Shiva-kalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta’, akulemba kuti:

Odia Sloka:

ସୀତା ଠାକୁରାଣୀ ଅସତୀ ହୋଇବେ ପର୍ବତେ ଫୁଟିବ କଇଁ,
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବଚନ ତିଳେ ନ ଟଳିବ ପାଷାଣର ଗାର ଏହି।

Kumasulira mawu:

Sītā ṭhākurāṇī asatī hoibe parbate phuṭiba kaiñ,
Achyuta bachana tiḷe na taḷiba pāṣāṇara gāra ehi.

Tanthauzo:
Mu Kaliyuga, anthu akhoza kutchula Amayi Sita kuti ndi wosakhulupirika, ndipo duwa la lotus likhoza kuphuka pamwamba pa phiri, koma mawu a Achyutananda Das sadzalephera, chifukwa ali ngati mzere wojambulidwa pamwala.

1.11 Mu ‘Amara Jumara Samhita’, pogwira mawu a Ambuye wopanda mawonekedwe (Nirakar), akulemba kuti:

Odia Sloka:

ଏ କଥା ଯେବେ ନ ହୋଇବ, ଆମ୍ଭ ଶ୍ରୀମୁଖ ମିଥ୍ୟା ହେବ।

Kumasulira mawu:

E kathā jebe na hoiba, ambha Shrīmukha mithyā heba.

Tanthauzo:
M'vesi ili, Ambuye wopanda mawonekedwe Mwiniwake akuti: "Ngati mawu a Malika sakwaniritsidwa, ndiye kuti pakamwa panga poyera padzatsimikiziridwa kuti ndi bodza." Chifukwa awa ndi mawu ochokera pakamwa pa Ambuye, sangalepherenso.

Mahapurush analembanso mu 'Chakada Madana':

Odia Sloka:

ଶ୍ରୀ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ ବାଣୀ ପଥରର ଗାର ପର୍ବତେ ଫୁଟିବ କଇଁ,
ପୂର୍ବ ସୂର୍ଯ୍ୟ ଅବା ପଶ୍ଚିମକୁ ଯିବ ମୋ ବଚନ ସତ୍ୟ ଏହି।

Kumasulira mawu:

Shrī Achyuta bāṇī patharara gāra parbate phuṭiba kaiṃ,
pūrba sūrya abā pashchimaku jiba mo bachana satya ehi.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiye kuti, dzuwa likhoza kutuluka kumadzulo m'malo mwa kum'mawa, duwa la lotus likhoza kuphuka pamwamba pa phiri, koma mawu a Achyutananda sangasinthidwe. Mawu a Achyutananda ali ojambulidwa pamwala; ndiwo choonadi chotheratu.

Kubadwanso kwa Achyutananda Das ji, Sant Hadi Das, amene anabadwa m'zaka za zana la 18 pafupi ndi Chatia m'chigawo cha Jajpur ku Odisha, analembanso mabuku ambiri a Malika.

M'buku lake la Malika 'Kali Chautisha', analemba kuti:

Odia Sloka:

ଟଳିବ ନାହିଁଟି ଏ କଥା ନିଶ୍ଚେ ହୋଇବ ଦିନେ,
ଟାକି ରହିଛନ୍ତି ଭରସା କରି ଭକତମାନେ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Ṭaliba nāhiṃṭi e kathā nishche hoiba dine,
ṭāki rahichaṃti bharasā kari bhakatamāne.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiye kuti, zinthu zolembedwa m'mabuku a Malika sizidzasinthidwa konse – zidzachitikadi tsiku lina. Odzipereka onse akudikirira ndi chikhulupiriro zochitika izi.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das analembanso m'buku lake la Malika, 'Agata Bhabishyat Malika':

Odia Sloka:

ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଦାସ ବାରମ୍ବାର,
ବୁଝାଏ ଭକତଙ୍କୁ ଧୀର।
ନମଣ ଏହାକୁ ଯେ ଆନ,
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଦାସ କରେ ଧ୍ୟାନ।
କଳି କଳ୍ମଷ ନାଶଯିବ,
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବାକ୍ୟ ନ ଟଳିବ।
ଅନନ୍ତ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତଙ୍କ ଲୀଳା,
ସାରିବେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ଚକାଡୋଳା।

Kumasulira mawu:

Achyuta dāsa bārambāra,
bujhāe bhakataṃku dhīra.
Namaṇa ehāku je āna,
Achyuta dāsa kare dhyāna.
Kali kalmaṣha nāshajiba,
Achyuta bākya na ṭaliba.
Anaṃta Achyutanka līlā,
sāribe prabhu chakāḍolā.

Tanthauzo:
Akulemba kuti ndikufotokozera mobwerezabwereza kwa odzipereka moleza mtima. Odzipereka sayenera kuganiza kuti mawu awa ndi abodza, chifukwa Achyuta adalemba mabukuwa ali mkati mwa kusinkhasinkha. Machimo ndi zowawa za Kali Yuga zidzawonongedwa, ndipo masewera aumulungu a Ambuye Kalki ndi otsimikizika. Mahapurush akulembanso kuti mawu ake sadzasinthidwa konse.

Mahapurush analembanso m'buku lina la Malika:

Odia Sloka:

ଅମୃତ ଭକ୍ଷି ଯାଇପାରେ ପ୍ରାଣ,
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବଚନ ନୋହିବ ଆନ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Amṛita bhakṣhi jāipāre prāṇa,
Achyuta bachana nohiba āna.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiye kuti, ngakhale chinthu chosatheka monga kutaya moyo m'malo mopeza moyo wosatha pomwa madzi a moyo (Amrit) chikhoza kutheka, koma mawu a Achyutananda Das sangakhale olakwika.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akulemba mu 'Udaya Kahani' yake:

Odia Sloka:

ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବଚନ ଆନ ନୋହିବ,
କହିଲେ ଅପ୍ରାଧ ନିଶ୍ଚେ ପାଇବ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Achyuta bachana āna nohiba,
kahile aparādha nishche pāiba.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiye kuti, mawu a Achyutananda Das sangakhale olakwika. Aliyense amene angatchule mawu awa kuti ndi abodza adzakhala wogawana nawo mlanduwo.

Mahapurush akulemba mu 'Chakada Madana':

Odia Sloka:

ମୋ ଶ୍ରୀମୁଖ ବାଣୀ ଅନ୍ୟଥା ନୋହିବ ପର୍ବତେ ଫୁଟିବ କଇଁ,
ନେମାଳ ବଟକୁ ଲୟ ଲଗାଇଣ ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ କହଇ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Mo shrīmukha bāṇī anyathā nohiba parbate phuṭiba kaiṃ,
Nemāla baṭaku laya lagāiṇa dīna Achyutī kahai.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiye kuti, mawu anga sangakhale osiyana, ngakhale duwa la lotus litaphuka paphiri! Mawu awa adalembedwa ali mkati mwa kusinkhasinkha pansi pa mtengo wa Nemal banyan (Nemal Vata).

M'buku la Malika 'Chakada Madana' lomwelo, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das analembanso kuti:

Odia Sloka:

ମହୀଫାଟି ଜଳୁପଛେ ପ୍ରଳୟ ଅନଳ,
ଯାହା କହିଛନ୍ତି ତାହା ସର୍ବଦା ଅଚଳ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Mahīphāṭi jalupache pralaya anala,
jāhā kahichaṃti tāhā sarbadā achala.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiye kuti, moto umene ungayambe pambuyo poti dziko lapansi lagawanika ukhoza kubweretsa chiwonongeko (Pralaya) padziko lonse lapansi, koma mawu anga sangakhale abodza. Mawu anga ndi osatsutsika komanso osasinthika.

2 Cholinga cha Malika – Chipulumutso cha Okhulupirira

Nthawi zambiri, anthu ambiri amachita mantha akawerenga maulosi a Malika ndikuwatsutsa ngati "njira zochititsa mantha." Komabe, kuganiza kumeneku nkulakwika kwathunthu. Cholinga chachikulu ndi chitetezo chauzimu ndi chitsogozo cha odzipereka pakusintha kwa nthawi.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akulemba m'buku lake la Malika ‘Shiva-kalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta’:

Odia Sloka:

ହେତୁ ରସାଇବା ପାଇଁକି ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଶାହାସ୍ତ୍ର ପୁରାଣ କଲା,
କଳିକାଳଠାରୁ ବଳି କାଳ ଯହିଁ ହକ କଥାଟା ଲେଖିଲା।

Kumasulira mawu:

Hetu rasāibā pāiṃki Achyuta shāhāstra purāṇa kalā,
kalikālaṭhāru bali kāla jahiṃ haka kathāṭā lekhilā.

Tanthauzo:
Kumapeto kwa Kaliyuga, kuti adzutsire chidziwitso chogona cha odzipereka, akulemba mabuku a Malika awa. Cholinga chake ndi chakuti mu Kaliyuga yozama, pamene mdima wa umbuli ukufalikira kulikonse, odzipereka a Ambuye athe kufika kwa Mahaprabhu Kalki kudzera mu Malika, kulola kuti odzipereka ndi Mulungu agwirizane. Pachifukwa ichi, akulemba maulosi akuluakulu m'mabuku a Malika kuyambira Kaliyuga mpaka Sangam Yuga (Nthawi Yosintha) ndi kupitirira mu Ananta Yuga (Adi Satyayuga) yomwe ikubwera.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akulembanso kuti:

Odia Sloka:

ଅବନୀକୁ ଚେତେଇବା ପାଇଁ ଚେତାବନୀ,
ଅବନୀ ହିତେ ଲେଖା ଏ ଭବିଷ୍ୟ ବାଣୀ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Abanīku cheteibā pāiñ chetābanī,
Abanī hite lekhā e bhabishya bāṇī.

Tanthauzo:
Cholinga cha maulosi amene ndalembawa m'mabuku a Malika ndi kupereka chenjezo kudziko lapansi. Akufotokoza kuti maulosiwa adalembedwa kuti dziko lapansi likhale labwino.

Mahapurush akulembanso mu ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ପୂର୍ବର ବାସନା ମୂଳେ ଜ୍ଞାନ ଉଦୟ ହେଲା,
ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ ହିତରେ ମାଳିକା ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ ଲେଖି ରଖିଲା।

Kumasulira mawu:

Pūrbara bāsanā mūḷe jñāna udaya helā,
Bhaktanka hitare Māḷikā grantha lekhi rakhilā.

Tanthauzo:
Chifukwa cha 'vasana' yakale (zizindikiro), kutanthauza kudzipereka kwa Ambuye kuchokera m'moyo wakale, chidziwitso chinandidzera m'moyo uno, ndipo kenako ndidalemba mabuku a Malika kuti apindulitse ndi kukhala abwino kwa odzipereka.

Mahapurush akulembanso mu ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ସତର୍କ ଘଣ୍ଟି ଯେ ଚେତାବନୀ ଏହି ଜାଣ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Chakaḍā Maḍāṇa satarka ghaṇṭi je chetābanī ehi jāṇa.

Tanthauzo:
Mawu a m'mabuku a Malika ndi cholinga chochenjeza; dziwani kuti ali ngati machenjezo.

Mahapurush analembanso kuti:

Odia Sloka:

ସାରସ୍ୱତ ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ ଏହି ଚେତାବନୀ ପୁଣ,
ବାରଣ ବେଳେ ପ୍ରକାଶ ଭକ୍ତ ହିତେ ପୁଣ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Sāraswata grantha ehi chetābanī puṇa,
bāraṇa beḷe prakāsha bhakta hite puṇa.

Tanthauzo:
Mavesi a Malika ali ngati machenjezo kwa anthu. Analembedwa kuti apindulitse okhulupirira kumapeto kwa Kaliyuga. Choncho, ziyenera kukhala zomveka kwa ife kuti Malika safalitsa mantha, koma amakonza njira yopulumutsira. Cholinga chokha cha Bhavishya Malika ndi ubwino wa dziko lapansi.

2.1 Malika si za anthu onse

Tiyenera kudziwa bwino kuti Malika si za aliyense. Panchasakha Mahapurushas aletsa ophunzira awo m'mabuku a Malika kufalitsa mawu amenewa pamaso pa opusa. Mahapurush adati izi si za aliyense; si anthu onse omwe adzatha kuzimvetsa. Okhulupirira a Ambuye okha ndi omwe adzatha kuzikhulupirira.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akulemba m'buku lake la Malika lotchedwa ‘Chakada Madana’ kuti:

Odia Sloka:

ଲକ୍ଷେ ପଞ୍ଚାଅଶୀ ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ କଳିପୁଣି ଆଗତ ଭବିଷ୍ୟ ବାଣୀ,
ଭକତ ହିତରେ ପ୍ରକାଶ କରିଲି ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଯେ ଭଣି।

Kumasulira mawu:

Lakshe pañchāashī grantha kaḷi puṇi āgata bhabishyabāṇī,
Bhakata hitare prakāsha karili dīna Achyuta je bhaṇi.

Tanthauzo:
Ndalemba mabuku okwana 1,85,000 ndipo kuwonjezera pamenepo, ndinalemba maulosi ambiri omwe akubwera. Ndikufalitsa zonsezi kuti zipindulitse okhulupirira.

Achyutananda Das kenako akuti kwa wophunzira wake Ramadas m'buku lake la Malika lotchedwa ‘Chakada Madana’ kuti:

Odia Sloka:

ଶୁଣ ରାମଚନ୍ଦ୍ର ଏ ପରମତତ୍ତ୍ୱ ମୂର୍ଖକୁ ନ କର ପ୍ରକାଶ,
ତତେ ସପ୍ତବାର ନିୟମ ଦେଉଛି କହଇ ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ।
ଲକ୍ଷେ ପଞ୍ଚାଅଶୀ ତିନି ଅଠର ମାଳିକା କଲି ପ୍ରକାଶ,
ବାରଣ ବେଳକୁ ଏ ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ ରଖିଛି ଜ୍ଞାନୀ ଜନଙ୍କ ପୀୟୂଷ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Shuṇa Rāmachandra e paramatattwa mūrkhaku na kara prakāsha,
tate saptabāra niyama deuchi kahai dīna Achyuta.
Lakshe pañchāashī tini aṭhara mālikā kali prakāsha,
bāraṇa beḷaku e grantha rakhichi jñānī janaṅka pīyūṣa.

Tanthauzo:
Mvera, wophunzira Ramadas, awa ndi mawu achinsinsi. Usawauze pamaso pa opusa, apo ayi udzachita tchimo. Mabuku amenewa ali ngati machenjezo kwa anthu omwe adzakhala ngati madzi a moyo kwa okhulupirira kumapeto kwa Kaliyuga.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das analemba mu ‘Chakada Madana’ kuti:

Odia Sloka:

ହୋଇବ ଡାକିନୀ ଗୋଳ ପଡ଼ିବ ଚହଳ,
ଅବନୀ କମ୍ପିବ ସଦା ହୋଇ ଟଳଟଳ।
ନାନା ଉତ୍ପାତ ମାନ ଅନ୍ତରୀକ୍ଷେ ହେବ,
ଭକ୍ତ ବିନା ଅନ୍ୟ କେହି ଜାଣି ନ ପାରିବ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Hoiba ḍākinī goḷa paḍiba chahaḷa,
Abanī kampiba sadā hoi taḷataḷa.
Nānā utpāta māna antarikṣe heba,
Bhakta binā anya kehi jāṇi na pāriba.

Tanthauzo:
Kumapeto kwa Kaliyuga, pa nthawi yokhazikitsa Dharma, nkhondo yoopsa idzachitika, ndipo padzakhala chisokonezo padziko lapansi. Dziko lapansi lidzagwedezeka. Zisokonezo zambiri zidzachitika mumlengalenga. Koma kupatula okhulupirira, palibe wina aliyense amene adzatha kumvetsa kuti awa ndi masewera owononga a Ambuye.

Mahapurush akuti kwa wophunzira Ramadas m'buku lake la Malika lotchedwa ‘Chakada Madana’ kuti:

Odia Sloka:

ଶୁଣ ବାବୁରାମ ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ମୂର୍ଖଙ୍କୁ କହିବୁ ନାହିଁ,
ଘୋର କଳିକାଳ ଟାହି ଯେ ଟାପରା ବାନା ନିନ୍ଦା ହେବ ତହିଁ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Shuṇa Bāburāma Chakaḍā Maḍāṇa mūrkhanku kahibu nāhiñ,
Ghora Kaḷikāḷa ṭāhi je ṭāparā bānā nindā heba tahiñ.

Tanthauzo:
O wophunzira Ramadas, usauze mawu a buku la ‘Chakada Madana’ pamaso pa opusa. Mu Kaliyuga yakuya, opusa adzawanyoza, zomwe zidzachititsa kuti Ambuye anyozedwe.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das analembanso m'buku lake la Malika lotchedwa ‘Chakada Madana’ kuti:

Odia Sloka:

ମୂର୍ଖଙ୍କୁ ଏ ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ ଅଟଇଟି ବିଷ ଜ୍ଞାନୀଙ୍କୁ ଅଟେ ପିୟୂଷ,
ଅଜ୍ଞାନୀ ଆଗରେ ପ୍ରକାଶ କରିଲେ ମୋ ଅମୃତେ ହେବ ବିଷ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Mūrkhanku e grantha aṭaiṭi biṣa jñānīnku aṭe piyūṣa,
Ajñānī āgare prakāsha karile mo amṛute heba biṣa.

Tanthauzo:
Kwa opusa, mawu a Malika amenewa ali ngati poizoni, pamene kwa anzeru ali ngati 'Piyush' kapena madzi a moyo. Kuuza mawu amenewa kwa osadziwa kuli ngati kusakaniza poizoni mu madzi a moyo.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das analembanso kamodzi mu ‘Chakada Madana’ kuti:

Odia Sloka:

ଶୁଣ ରାମଚନ୍ଦ୍ର କହୁଛି ଏହି ନିଷ୍ଠା ବଚନ,
ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ଅଟଇ ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ ଗଣ୍ଠିଧନ।
ବାରଣ ବେଳକୁ ରଖିଛି ଏହା ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ ପାଇଁ,
ଗୁପତରେ ଖେଳ ହୋଇବ ତୋତେ ଦେଲି ଚେତେଇ।
ଗଣ୍ଠିଆ ଭକତ ଅଟଇ ଗଣ୍ଠି କଥାରେ ମନ,
ସାତଶ ବାସ୍ତରୀ ପରାର୍ଦ୍ଧ ଭବିଷ୍ୟତ ପୁରାଣ।
ଧର୍ମ ସଂସ୍ଥାପନ ହୋଇବ ଭକ୍ତ ହେବେ ମୁକତ,
ଶୁଣ ରାମଚନ୍ଦ୍ର ଦାସରେ ମୋର ବଚନ ସତ୍ୟ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Shuṇa Rāmachandra kahuchi ehi niṣṭhā bachana,
Chakaḍā Maḍāṇa aṭai bhaktaṅka gaṇṭhidhana.
Bāraṇa beḷaku rakhichi ehā bhaktaṅka pāiṃ,
Gupatare kheḷa hoiba tote deli chetei.
Gaṇṭhiā bhakata aṭai gaṇṭhi kathāre mana,
Sātasha bāstarī parārddha bhabishyata purāṇa.
Dharma saṃsthāpana hoiba bhakta hebe mukata,
Shuṇa Rāmachandra dāsare mora bachana satya.

Tanthauzo:
Mvera wophunzira Ramadas, mawu anga a Malika amenewa ali ngati 'Ganthidhana' kapena chuma chokondedwa kwambiri kwa okhulupirira. Ambuye adzakhazikitsa Dharma ndipo okhulupirira adzamasulidwa. Masewera amenewa adzachitika mobisa kwambiri. Ndikulemba mabuku a Malika amenewa chifukwa chokha cha chipulumutso cha okhulupirira. Adzawathandiza pa ‘Barana Bela’ kapena kumapeto kwa Kaliyuga.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das analembanso m'buku lake la Malika lotchedwa ‘Chakada Madana’ kuti:

Odia Sloka:

ନେମାଳ ବଟକୁ ଲୟ ଲଗେଇ,
ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ଖେଦା ଲେଖଇ।
ସାରସ୍ୱତ ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ ଅଟଇ ଏହି,
ବେଳକୁ ରଖିଛି ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ ପାଇଁ।
ଉତ୍ତମ ଶିଷ୍ୟରେ କର ପ୍ରକାଶ,
ବାନା ନିନ୍ଦା କହେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଦାସ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Nemāḷa baṭaku laya lagei,
Chakaḍā Maḍāṇa khedā lekhai.
Sāraswata grantha aṭai ehi,
Beḷaku rakhichi bhaktaṅka pāiñ.
Uttama shiṣyare kara prakāsha,
Bānā nindā kahe Achyuta Dāsa.

Tanthauzo:
Pansi pa mtengo wa Banyan ku Nemalo, ndikulemba buku la Malika la ‘Chakada Madana’ ili. Lidzakhala lothandiza kwa okhulupirira kumapeto kwa Kaliyuga. Liululireni pakati pa okhulupirira abwino okha; apo ayi, padzakhala kunyoza mawu amenewa ndi Ambuye.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akulembanso kamodzi mu ‘Chakada Madana’ kuti:

Odia Sloka:

ଲାଗି ଅଲଗାରେ ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ଭକ୍ତ ଗଣ୍ଠିଧନ ଜାଣ,
ଭକତ ଜାଣିବେ ଏହାର ଯେ ଭେଦ ଶୂନ୍ୟରେ ଅଛି ପ୍ରମାଣ।
ବାରଣ ବେଳରେ ଏ ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ ପ୍ରକାଶ ମୂର୍ଖେ ନ କର ପ୍ରକାଶ,
ନେମାଳ ବଟ ଯେ ସାଧୁ ପଦାଶ୍ରିତ ଭଣେ ପାମର ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Lāgi alagāre Chakaḍā Maḍāṇa bhakta gaṇṭhidhana jāṇa,
bhakata jāṇibe ehāra je bheda shūnyare achi pramāṇa.
Bāraṇa beḷare e grantha prakāsha mūrkhe na kara prakāsha,
Nemāḷa baṭa je sādhu padāshrita bhaṇe pāmara Achyuta.

Tanthauzo:
Chakada Madana ndi chuma cha okhulupirira. Kumapeto kwa Kaliyuga, okhulupirira okha ndi omwe adzatha kumvetsa zinthu zake ndi zinsinsi zake. Usachiuze pamaso pa opusa.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das analemba mu ‘Shiva-kalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta’ kuti:

Odia Sloka:

ଚେତୁଆ ଭକତମାନଙ୍କ ନିମନ୍ତେ ତିନି ଅଠର ଖଣ୍ଡ,
ଭବିଷ୍ୟ ଲେଖିଲା ପାମର ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ନ ଜାଣନ୍ତି ଦୁରୁଦଣ୍ଡ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Chetuā bhaktamānaṅka nimante tini aṭhara khaṇḍa,
Bhabishya lekhilā pāmara Achyuta na jāṇanti durudaṇḍa.

Tanthauzo:
Mahapurush walemba mabuku 318 a maulosi a Malika chifukwa chokha cha ubwino wa okhulupirira odzuka. Opusa ndi ochimwa sadzatha kudziwa chinsinsi cha mabuku amenewa.

Analembanso kuti:

Odia Sloka:

ମ୍ଳେଚ୍ଛ ବିନାଶି ପ୍ରଭୁ ମେଦିନୀ ରଖିବେ,
ସନାତନ ଧର୍ମ ନିଶ୍ଚେ ପ୍ରଚାର କରିବେ।
କଳିଯୁଗ ଶେଷରେ ପ୍ରକାଶ ନରହରି,
ନାମ ଭଜଏ ଘୋର କଳିରେ ଯିବ ତରି।
ବିପଦ କାଳରେ ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ ରକ୍ଷା ପାଇଁ,
ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ରଚଇ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Mlechha bināshi Prabhu medinī rakhibe,
Sanātana Dharma nishche prachāra karibe.
Kaḷiyuga sheshare prakāsha Narahari,
Nāma bhajae ghora kaḷire jiba tari.
Bipada kāḷare bhaktaṅka rakṣā pāiñ,
Chakaḍā Maḍāṇa grantha Achyuta rachai.

Tanthauzo:
Kumapeto kwa Kaliyuga, Ambuye adzabwera ngati munthu. Adzawononga oipa ndi ochimwa kuti ateteze Dziko lapansi ndipo adzakhazikitsanso Sanatana Dharma. Mahapurush analemba kuti pa nthawi imeneyo, kuti atsogolere okhulupirira ndi kuwateteza, akulemba mabuku a Malika. Mu vesi ili, Mahapurush analemba momveka bwino kuti cholinga cha kulemba Malika ndi kuteteza okhulupirira pa nthawi ya masoka ndi kukonza njira yopulumutsira iwo.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akulembanso m'buku lake la Malika, 'Chakada Madana':

Odia Sloka:

ଧର୍ମ ସଂସ୍ଥାପନ ପାପ ନାଶନ,
ଖେଦା ଚେତାବନୀ ଭବିଷ୍ୟ ଜ୍ଞାନ।
ସବୁ ଲେଖିଗଲି ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ ପାଇଁ,
ଭକ୍ତ ମୋହର ଯେ ସୋଦର ଭାଇ।
ଭକତ ମୋ ପଞ୍ଚ ପ୍ରାଣଟି ଜାଣ,
ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ ନିମନ୍ତେ ଖେଦା ଲିଖନ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Dharma saṃsthāpana pāpa nāshana,
khedā chetābanī bhabishya jñāna.
Sabu lekhigali bhaktanka pāiṃ,
bhakta mohara je sodara bhāī.
Bhakata mo pañcha prāṇaṭi jāṇa,
bhaktanka nimaṃte khedā likhana.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, maulosi omwe ndalemba m'mabuku anga a Malika okhudza kutha kwa machimo ndi kukhazikitsidwa kwa Dharma kumapeto kwa Kali Yuga, onse ndi a okhulupirira chifukwa okhulupirira ali ngati abale anga. Okhulupirira ndi mpweya wanga wamoyo wosanu (Pancha-prana), ndipo ndalemba mabuku onsewa a Malika chifukwa cha iwo okha.

Mahapurush analembanso kuti:

Odia Sloka:

ସାତଶ ବାସ୍ତରୀ ପରାର୍ଦ୍ଧ ଅଟଇ ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ଜାଣ,
ଘୋର କଳିଯୁଗ ଦୁଷ୍ଟଜନ ଛନ୍ତି ଡର ଲାଗୁଅଛି ପୁଣ।
ମୂର୍ଖଙ୍କ ଆଗରେ ପ୍ରକାଶ ନ କର ନିୟମ ଦେଉଛି ମୁହିଁ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Sātasha bāstarī parārddha aṭai Chakaḍā Maḍāṇa jāṇa,
ghora kaliyuga duṣhṭajana chaṃti ḍara lāguachi puṇa.
Mūrkhanka āgare prakāsha na kara niyama deuchi muṃhi.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, wophunzira Rama, muli 772 Parardhas kapena mitu m'buku la Malika lotchedwa 'Chakada Madana'. Usawauze pamaso pa opusa. Muli anthu oipa ambiri mu Kali Yuga yoopsa; lisungeni kutali ndi onsewo.

Mu 'Chakada Madana' momwemo, Mahapurush akulemba kuti:

Odia Sloka:

ଶୁଣ ରାମଦାସ ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ମୂର୍ଖେ ନ କର ପ୍ରକାଶ,
ନୋହିଲା ନୋହିଲା ପଡୁଥିବ ହୁରି ଭକ୍ତ ଜାଣିବ ଅବଶ୍ୟ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Shuṇa Rāmadāsa Chakaḍā Maḍāṇa mūrkhe na kara prakāsha,
nohilā nohilā paḍuthiba huri bhakta jāṇiba abashya.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, O wophunzira Ramadasa, usauze maulosi a Malika amenewa pamaso pa opusa. Adzanena kuti mawu amenewa ndi abodza, kuti zochitika izi sizidzachitika. Choncho, auzeni okhulupirira okha; iwo okha ndi omwe adzatha kukhulupirira izi.

3 Maulosi a Malika Omwe Akwaniritsidwa

Bhavishya Malika imakhala ndi mawu a Mahaprabhu Mwiniwake, choncho n'zosadabwitsa kuti maulosi osawerengeka a Malika akwaniritsidwa mpaka pano. Tidzakambirana nanu maulosi ena otere omwe atsimikizira kukhala owona 100%.

3.1 Ulosi Wokhudza Atal Bihari Vajpayee Ji

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das analemba m'mabuku ake a Malika 'Kalikalpa Gita' ndi 'Achyuta Thara' kuti:

Odia Sloka:

ତେର ଦିନ ପକ୍ଷ ହୋଇବ ଯେବେ, କଳ୍କୀଙ୍କ ଲୀଳା ଲାଗିବ ତେବେ।
କ୍ଷେତ୍ରରେ ହୋଇବ ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣ ରାଜା, ଫେରିବ କିଛିତାହାର ତରିଜା।
କିଛି ଦିନ ଭୋଗ କରିବ ମହୀ, ତେର ଦିନ ସେହି ଛତ୍ର ଉଡ଼ାଇ।
ସେ ଅଟନ୍ତି ଅଟଳ ବିହାରୀ ବାଜପେୟୀ, ଦିଲ୍ଲୀ ରାଜ ସିଂହାସନେ ବସିଲେକ ତହିଁ।
କ୍ଷେତ୍ରରେ ହୋଇଲେ ଯେବେ ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣ ଯେ ରାଜା, ତାଙ୍କ ସମୟରେ କିଛି ହୋଇଲା ତରିଜା।
ତେରଦିନ ଛତ୍ର ସେହି ଉଡ଼େଇଣ ଦେଲେ, ତାପରେ ପାଞ୍ଚ ବରଷ ମହୀ ଭୋଗ କଲେ।
ସେ ଅଟନ୍ତି ଅଟଳ ବିହାରୀ ବାଜପେୟୀ, ଦିଲ୍ଲୀ ରାଜ ସିଂହାସନେ ବସିଲେକ ତହିଁ।
ପ୍ରଥମେ ତେର ଦିନ ପ୍ରଧାନ ମନ୍ତ୍ରୀ ହେଲେ, ତାପରେ ପାଞ୍ଚ ବରଷ ଶାସନ କରିଲେ।
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବଚନ ଅଟେ ପଥରର ଗାର, ଆନ କରିବାକୁ ନାହିଁ ଶକତି କାହାର।

Kumasulira mawu:

Tera dina pakṣa hoiba jebe, Kaḷkīṅka līḷā lāgiba tebe.
Khetratre hoiba Brāhmaṇa rājā, pheriba kichhitāhāra tarijā.
Kichhi dina bhoga kariba mahī, tera dina sehi chhatra uḍāi.
Se aṭanti Aṭala Bihārī Bājapeyī, Dillī rāja siṃhāsane basileka tahiñ.
Khetratre hoile jebe Brāhmaṇa je rājā, tāṅka samayare kichhi hoilā tarijā.
Teradina chhatra sehi uḍeiṇa dele, tāpare pāñcha baraṣa mahī bhoga kale.
Se aṭanti Aṭala Bihārī Bājapeyī, Dillī rāja siṃhāsane basileka tahiñ.
Prathame tera dina pradhāna mantrī hele, tāpare pāñcha baraṣa shāsana karile.
Achyuta bachana aṭe patharara gāra, āna karibāku nāhiñ shakati kāhāra.

Tanthauzo:
Zaka mazana ambiri zapitazo, Mahapurush analemba za wandale wofunika wa dziko la India lodziimira. Adanena kuti pamene Brahmin adzalamulira India, kusintha kwakukulu kudzawonekera padziko lapansi. Choyamba, adzalamulira masiku 13, ndipo pambuyo pake adzakhala ndi mphamvu zaka zisanu. Mahapurush analemba kuti dzina la munthu uyu wolamulira kuchokera ku Delhi lidzakhala "Atal Bihari Vajpayee". Monga mbiri ikusonyezera, Shri Vajpayee adatumikira ngati PM masiku 13 mu 1996 ndipo kenako kwa zaka zisanu zonse kuyambira 1999 mpaka 2004.

3.2 Ulosi Wokhudza Mahatma Gandhi

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, zaka 600 zapitazo m'buku lake lodziwika bwino 'Shiva-kalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta', anapereka ulosi wambiri wokhudza Mahatma Gandhi.

Odia Sloka:

ଜନମ ହୋଇ ଗୁଜରାଟରେ, ମୋହନ ମୋହିବ ରାମ ନାମରେ।
ଅହିଂସାକୁ ଅସ୍ତ୍ର କରିବ ଜାଣ, ଫିରିଙ୍ଗୀ ହଟିବେ ସେ କାଳେ ଜାଣ।
ତା ଅନ୍ତେ ଧର୍ମ ହୋଇବ ହାଣି, ମୋହନ ରାଜୁତି ହୋଇବ ହାଣି।
ଗୁଳି ଚୋଟେ ତାର ପ୍ରାଣ ଯିବ, ଗଡସେ କଳିମଞ୍ଜି ପୋତିବ।
ଜାଣି ନ ପାରିବେ ସଂସାର ପ୍ରାଣୀ, ଟେକି କହୁଥିବେ କଥା ନ ଜାଣି।

Kumasulira mawu:

Janama hoi Gujarāṭare, Mohana mohiba Rāma nāmare.
Ahinsāku astra kariba jāṇa, phiriṅgī haṭibe se kāḷe jāṇa.
Tā ante dharma hoiba hāṇi, Mohana rājuti hoiba hāṇi.
Guḷi choṭe tāra prāṇa jiba, Gaḍse kaḷimañji potiba.
Jāṇi na pāribe saṃsāra prāṇī, ṭeki kahuthibe kathā na jāṇi.

Tanthauzo:
"Adzabadwira m'boma la Gujarat. Dzina lake lidzakhala 'Mohan' ndipo adzakhala akuyimba dzina la Rama nthawi zonse. 'Ahimsa' (Kusachita Chiwawa) adzakhala chida chake, ndipo chifukwa cha khama lake, a British (Firingis) adzakakamizika kuchoka ku India."

Mahapurush analembanso kuti Mohan sadzakhala ndi mphamvu yekha, ndipo akamwalira, padzakhala kuchepa kwa Dharma. Ananenetsa kuti munthu wina dzina lake "Godse" adzamupha ndi "chipolopolo" (Guli), ndipo pochita zimenezo, adzafesa mbewu za Kali m'dzikoli. Ananeneratunso kuti anthu ambiri sadzamvetsa Gandhi bwino ndipo adzalungamitsa mlandu wa wakuphayo chifukwa cha kusadziwa. Monga tikudziwira, mawu aliwonse a ulosiwu wokhudza Atate wa Mtundu anakwaniritsidwa.

3.3 Ulosi wa Mphepo Yamkuntho Yaikulu ya 1999 ndi Kusweka kwa Kalpavata

M'buku lake 'Jai Birachit Chautisha', Mahapurush Achyutananda Das ananeneratu mphepo yamkuntho ya 1999 zaka 600 pasadakhale.

Odia Sloka:

ସମୁଦ୍ର ଉଠି ଆସିବ ବତାସ ବହିବ, ଶ୍ରୀ କଳ୍ପବଟ ମୋର ଭାଙ୍ଗି ପକାଇବ।
ସତ୍ୟଭାବ ହେ, ସାଲ ଅନେଶତ କର ଠାବ ହେ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Samudra uṭhi āsiba batāsa bahiba, Shrī Kaḷpabaṭa mora bhāṅgi pakāiba.
Satyabhāba he, sāla aneshata kara ṭhāba he.

Tanthauzo:
"Nyanja idzakwera ndipo mphepo yamphamvu idzawomba. Nthambi ya Kalpavata woyera (mtengo wakale wa Banyan mkati mwa malo a Kachisi wa Jagannath) idzasweka." Mahapurush ananena kuti chochitika ichi chidzachitika m'chaka cha "Anesata" (99).

Pa Okutobala 29, 1999, Mphepo Yamkuntho Yaikulu inagunda Odisha ndi mphepo yothamanga 250 km/h. Inayambitsa chiwonongeko chachikulu, kupha anthu opitilira 10,000. Mogwirizana ndi ulosiwo, nthambi yayikulu ya mtengo wakale wa Kalpavata mkati mwa kachisi wa Puri inasweka panthawi ya mphepo yamkunthoyi.

3.4 Ulosi wa Ufulu wa India (1947)

M'buku la 'Garuda Kalpa', Woyera Achyutananda ananeneratu kuti India idzamasuka ku ulamuliro wa Britain mu 1947 pogwiritsa ntchito khodi ya manambala.

Odia Sloka:

ବ୍ରହ୍ମ ଭକ୍ତି ବେଦ ବାରକୁ ଥୋଇ, ଏ ଠାରେ ବିଦେଶୀ ଯିବେ ପଳାଇ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Brahma bhakti beda bāraku thoi, e ṭhāre bideshī jibe paḷāi.

Kutanthauzira kwa Manambala:
Mahapurush anagwiritsa ntchito mawu ophiphiritsa a manambala:
  • Brahma: Akuimiridwa ndi 1 (Monga mu "Ekam Brahma").
  • Bhakti: Akuimiridwa ndi 9 (Mitundu isanu ndi inayi ya Navadha Bhakti).
  • Veda: Akuimiridwa ndi 4 (Ma Vedas anayi).
  • Baar: Akuimiridwa ndi 7 (Masiku asanu ndi awiri a sabata).

Ikaikidwa pamodzi (1-9-4-7), imapanga 1947. Ulosi ukunena kuti m'chaka chino, alendo (Achingerezi) adzathawa m'dziko lino.

3.5 Ulosi Wokhudza Kupha Mwana Wosabadwa Wachikazi

Mu 'Jaiphula Malika', Mahapurush ananeneratu zoipa za anthu kumapeto kwa Kaliyuga:

Odia Sloka:

କନ୍ୟା ରତ୍ନକୁ ଗର୍ଭର, ନଷ୍ଟ କରିବ ଥରକୁ ଥର ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ହୃଦୟେ ନ କରି ପାପ ବିଚାର।

Kumasulira mawu:

Kanyā ratnaku garbhara, naṣṭa kariba tharaku thara lo jāiphula,
hṛudaye na kari pāpa bichāra.

Tanthauzo:
"Anthu adzawononga mwana wamkazi (Kanya Ratna) mkati mwa mimba (Garbha) mobwerezabwereza popanda kumva tchimo mumitima yawo." Izi zikuwonetsa molondola nthawi yamdima yakupha ana osabadwa achikazi yomwe yavutitsa anthu m'zaka zaposachedwapa.

3.6 Ulosi wa Mliri wa 2020 (COVID-19)

Mahapurush Hadidas, kubadwanso kwa Panchasakhas wobadwa m'zaka za zana la 18, analemba mu 'Agata Bhavishyat Chautisha':

Odia Sloka:

ଦୁଇ ବିଂଶ ଯେବେ ବର୍ଷ ବୋଲାଇବ, ବହୁ ବିତିପାତ ହେବ।
ଅଜଣା ବ୍ୟାଧିରେ ବହୁ ନାଶ ଯିବେ, ହାହାକାର ପଡ଼ିଯିବ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Dui biṃsha jebe barsha bolāiba, bahu bitipāta heba.
Ajaṇā byādhire bahu nāsha jibe, hāhākāra paḍijiba.

Tanthauzo:
"Chaka cha 'Dui-Binsha' (Makumi Awiri / 2020) chikafika, padzakhala zipolowe zazikulu. Anthu adzafa ndi matenda osadziwika (Ajana Vyadhi) ndipo padzakhala kulira kwachisoni (Haahakaar) kulikonse." Izi ndi kufotokozera kolondola modabwitsa kwa mliri wa COVID-19 womwe unagwira dziko lonse mu 2020.

3.7 Ulosi wa Dama la Hirakud

M'buku la 'Chakada Madana' ndi 'Mahagupta Padmakalpa', Oyera Achyutananda ndi Arkhit Das ananeneratu kumanga dama lalikulu ku Odisha.

Odia Sloka:

ଛବିଶ ଠାବରେ ହୀରାକୁଦ ଉଛୁଳିବ, ଏ ଓଡ଼ିଶା ଅର୍ଦ୍ଧଭାଗ ଜଳମଗ୍ନ ହେବ।
ଧନଜନ ନଷ୍ଟ ହେବ କହିଲି ବୁଝାଇ, ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ କହଇ ବେଶୀ ବେଳ ନାହିଁ।
ହୀରାକୁଦ ବୋଲିଣଟି ଗ୍ରାମ ନାମ ଥିବ, ବନ୍ଧବାଡ଼ ଭାଙ୍ଗି ସବୁ ଏକାକାର ହେବ।
ସେହି ବେଳେ ଗଙ୍ଗା ପୁଣି ଆସିବେଟି ମାଡ଼ି, ବୈତରଣୀ, ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣୀ ଯେ ଦେବେ ଘୋର ରଡ଼ି।
ଓଡ଼ିଶାରେ ବଡ଼ ବନ୍ଧ ତିଆରି ଯେ ଥିବ, ସେଇବେଳେ ଶତ୍ରୁଦ୍ୱାରା ଭାଙ୍ଗିଣ ଯେ ଯିବ।
ଓଡ଼ିଶାରେ ଛଅ ଜିଲ୍ଲା ଜଳାର୍ଣ୍ଣବ ହୋଇ, ଯିବାପାଇଁ ବାଟ ନ ପାଇବେ ପୁଣି କେହି।
ହୀରାକୁଦ ବୋଲି ଗ୍ରାମର ଯେ ନାମ ଥିବ, ବନ୍ଧବାଡ଼ ଭାଙ୍ଗି ସବୁ ଏକ ଯେ ହୋଇବ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Chabiṣha ṭhābare Hīrākuda uchuḷiba, e Oḍiśhā arddhabhāga jaḷamagna heba.
Dhanajana naṣhṭa heba kahili bujhāi, dīna Achyuta kahai beśhī beḷa nāhiñ.
Hīrākuda boliṇaṭi grāma nāma thiba, bandhabāḍa bhāṅgi sabu ekākāra heba.
Sehi beḷe Gaṅgā puṇi āsibeṭi māḍi, Baitaraṇī, Brāhmaṇī je debe ghora raḍi.
Oḍiśhāre baḍa bandha tiāri je thiba, seibeḷe śhatrudwārā bhāṅgiṇa je jiba.
Oḍiśhāre chha-a jillā jaḷārṇnaba hoi, jibāpāiñ bāṭa na pāibe puṇi kehi.
Hīrākuda boli grāmara je nāma thiba, bandhabāḍa bhāṅgi sabu eka je hoiba.

Tanthauzo:
Achyutananda Das ji m'buku lake la Malika 'Chakada Madana' ndi woyera Brahma Avadhut Arakhita Das, yemwe anabadwa theka lachiwiri la zaka za zana la khumi ndi zisanu ndi zitatu ndipo anachita sadhana yangwiro mu phanga la Olashuni, m'buku lake la Malika 'Mahagupta Padmakalpa' analemba kuti dama lalikulu lidzamangidwa mu boma la Odisha lomwe dzina lake lidzakhala "Hirakud". Ananenanso kuti kumapeto kwa nthawi ya Kaliyuga, chifukwa cha kuukira kwa adani, damalo lidzasweka, zomwe zigawo zisanu ndi chimodzi za boma la Odisha zidzamira, mayendedwe adzayima, ndipo padzakhala kutayika kwakukulu kwa chuma ndi anthu. Ma Mahapurushas ananena kuti panthawi yomweyo Ganga adzaonetsa mawonekedwe ake owopsa ndipo mitsinje ya Baitarani, Brahmani, ndi zina zotero idzabweretsanso chiwonongeko choyipa.

Monga tikudziwira, m'chaka cha 1948 mu boma la Odisha ku India, ntchito yomanga dama lotchedwa Hirakud inayamba, yomwe inamalizidwa m'chaka cha 1953. M'mwezi wa Januwale wa chaka cha 1957, ndi Nduna Yaikulu ya nthawiyo Pandit Jawaharlal Nehru, kutsegulira mwalamulo kwa damali kunachitika. Ndi kutalika konse kwa 26 km, lomangidwa pamwamba pa mtsinje wa Mahanadi mu chigawo cha Sambalpur ku Odisha, damali lero ndi dama lalitali kwambiri lopangidwa ndi anthu padziko lonse. Mwanjira imeneyi, kulondola kwa gawo loyamba la ulosiwu kunatsimikiziridwa.

Posachedwa dziko lonse lidzawona gawo lachiwiri la ulosiwu likukwaniritsidwanso pamene, panthawi ya nkhondo ya India ndi China, chifukwa cha kuukira kwa mizinga kwa China, damali lidzasweka ndipo boma la Odisha lidzakumana ndi chigumula choyipa chamadzi.

3.8 Ulosi wa Kubadwa kwa Pakistan Zaka Mazana Ambiri Pasadakhale

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das ndi ena anatchula kupanga ndi dzina la Pakistan zaka 600 zapitazo m'mabuku monga 'Gupta Kheda Malika' ndi 'Bhavishyat Malika'. Momwemonso, woyera wa zaka za zana la 18 Arkhit Das anatchula izi mu 'Mahagupta Padmakalpa'.

Odia Sloka:

ପାକିସ୍ତାନର ଦଶା ଯାହା ହେବ ସହସା, ମନ କର୍ଣ୍ଣ ଦଇ ଥରେ ଶୁଣ ସେ ଭାଷା।
ଏହି ଭାରତ ସଙ୍ଗେ ପାକିସ୍ତାନଟି ରଙ୍ଗେ, ମହାସମର କରିବ ଜାଣିଥା ବେଗେ।
ପ୍ରାଚ୍ୟ ପାଶ୍ଚାତ୍ୟ ଗଣ ଏହିପରି ମିଳିଣ, ପାକିସ୍ତାନ ଲାଗି ସର୍ବେ କରିବେ ରଣ।
ପାକିସ୍ତାନ ରାଜ୍ୟଗୋଟି ଛାରଖାର ହେବ, ଚାରିଆଡ଼େ ହାହାକାର ଶୁଭୁଣ ଯେ ଥିବ।
ଚୀନ ପାକିସ୍ତାନ ପୁଣି ଆମେରିକା ଜାଣ, ଋଷିଆ ଭାରତ ପୁଣି ଜର୍ମାନ ଜାପାନ।
ପାକିସ୍ତାନ ରାଜ୍ୟ ଗୋଟା ଛାରଖାର ହେବ, ପଛ ବେଳକୁ ତ ପୁଣି ବୁଦ୍ଧି ଯେ ସ୍ଫୁରିବ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Pākistānara dashā jāhā heba sahasā, mana karṇṇa dei thare shuṇa se bhāṣā.
Ehi Bhārata saṅge Pākistānaṭi raṅge, mahāsamara kariba jāṇithā bege.
Prāchya pāshchātya gaṇa ehipari miḷiṇa, Pākistāna lāgi sarbe karibe raṇa.
Pākistāna rājyagoṭi chhārakhāra heba, chāriāḍe hāhākāra shubhuṇa je thiba.
Chīna Pākistāna puṇi Āmerikā jāṇa, Ruṣiā Bhārata puṇi Jarmana Jāpāna.
Pākistāna rājya goṭā chhārakhāra heba, pachha beḷaku ta puṇi buddhi je sphuriba.

Tanthauzo:
"Dziko lotchedwa Pakistan lidzaonekera mwadzidzidzi. Kumapeto kwa nthawi, India idzakumana ndi nkhondo yoopsa ndi Pakistan. Maiko a ku Middle East ndi Kumadzulo adzagwirizana; maiko achisilamu, maiko akumadzulo, China, ndi America adzathandiza Pakistan, pamene Russia, Germany, ndi Japan adzathandiza India. Pakistan idzawonongedwa kwathunthu (Chara-khara) mu nkhondoyi, ndipo pakati pa chisoni chadziko lonse, idzazindikira zolakwa zake pamapeto pake."

Ndikoyenera kudziwa kuti mawu akuti 'Pakistan' anapangidwa mu 1933, komabe oyera amenewa anatchula dzina lake zaka mazana ambiri m'mbuyomu.

3.9 Maulosi Okudza Mzinda wa Bhubaneswar

Maziko a Bhubaneswar wamakono anaikidwa mu 1948 ndi Nduna Yaikulu Nehru. Komabe, kutchulidwa kwake ngati likulu kukuonekera m'mabuku a Malika monga 'Chakada Madana' ndi 'Kali Agata Bhabishyant'.

Odia Sloka:

ଭୈରବୀ ଡାକିବ ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱର, ଚୈତ୍ର ମାସ ଯେ ମଙ୍ଗଳବାର।
ପୃଥିବୀରେ ଗୋଳ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ଆରମ୍ଭିବ ଯେବେ, ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱରକୁ ସୈନ୍ୟ ମାଡ଼ି ଯେ ଆସିବେ।
ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱରରେ ଲାଗିବ ଗୋଳ, ଏକାମ୍ର ବନ ଯେ ଅଛି ସେଠାର।
ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱର ତ ପୁଣି ରାଜଧାନୀ ଜାଣ, ବିଦେଶୀ ଶତ୍ରୁଙ୍କ ଦୃଷ୍ଟି ପଡ଼ିବଟି ପୁଣ।
ଭୈରବ ଡାକ ହେବ ଜାଣ, ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱରଟି ପ୍ରମାଣ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Bhairabī ḍākiba Bhubaneshwar, Chaitra māsa je Maṅgaḷabāra.
Pṛuthibīre goḷa yuddha ārambhiba jebe, Bhubaneshwaraku sainya māḍi je āsibe.
Bhubaneshwarare lāgiba goḷa, Ekāmra bana je achhi seṭhāra.
Bhubaneshwar ta puṇi rājadhānī jāṇa, bideshī shatruṅka dṛuṣṭi paḍibaṭi puṇa.
Bhairaba ḍāka heba jāṇa, Bhubaneshwarati pramāṇa.

Tanthauzo:
Oyera aneneratu kuti Lachiwiri m'mwezi wa Chaitra, phokoso loopsa (kubangula kwa Bhairavi) lidzamveka ku Bhubaneswar. Panthawi ya Nkhondo Yadziko Lonse Yachitatu, magulu ankhondo adani adzafika ku Bhubaneswar (Ekamra Bana). Mabukuwo anatchula momveka bwino kuti Bhubaneswar idzakhala likulu komanso chandamale cha adani akunja.

3.10 Ulosi Wokhudza Guru Wodziwika Wauzimu Shri Shri Thakur Anukulchandra

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, m'mabuku ake a Malika olembedwa zaka pafupifupi 600 zapitazo, ananeneratu kubadwa kwa "Yugapurusha" (Munthu wa Nthawi) yemwe adzachita masewera ambiri aumulungu ndipo mbiri yake idzafalikira padziko lonse.

Odia Sloka:

ଲୀଳାମାନ ହେବ କୀର୍ତ୍ତି ରହି ଯିବ, ଅନୁକୂଳ ନାମ ଧରି।
ଦେଶ ବିଦେଶରୁ ଭକ୍ତ ଆସିବେ, ସତସଙ୍ଗ ଆଶ୍ରା କରି।
ସତନାମ ଗୋଟି ଉଚ୍ଚାରି ବୋଲିବେ, ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ ହେବ ମେଳ।
ଶୂନ୍ୟ ମାର୍ଗେ ରହି ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଦେଖିବ, ଯୁଗପୁରୁଷଙ୍କ ଖେଳ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Līḷāmāna heba kīrtti rahi jiba, Anukūḷa nāma dhari.
Desha bidesharu bhakata āsibe, sat-saṅga āshrā kari.
Satanāma goṭi uchchāri bolibe, bhaktaṅka heba meḷa.
Shūnya mārge rahi Achyuta dekhiba, Yugapuruṣaṅka kheḷa.

Tanthauzo:
Mahapurush analemba kuti woyera wotchedwa "Anukul" adzaonekera, ndipo okhulupirira ochokera ku India ndi kunja adzafika kudzathawira mu "Satsanga" yake. Dzina loyera (Satnaam) lidzaimbidwa, ndipo padzakhala msonkhano waukulu wa okhulupirira. Achyutananda Das ananenanso kuti iye mwini, kukhala m'dziko lachinsinsi/lopanda kanthu (Shunya Maarga), adzawona masewera aumulungu a Yugapurusha uyu.

Kukwaniritsa ulosi umenewu, Anukulchandra Chakravarty anabadwa pa Seputembala 14, 1888, m'chigawo cha Pabna ku Bengal yosagawika (tsopano Bangladesh). Wodziwika padziko lonse ngati Shri Shri Thakur Anukulchandra, adayambitsa 'Satsang Ashram' ku Deoghar, Jharkhand, mu 1946. Mamiliyoni a otsatira padziko lonse adalowa nawo ntchito yake, nthawi zambiri akumutcha 'Yuga Purushottam.'

3.11 Kutchulidwa kwa Bomba la Atomu m'Mabuku a Malika

Ngakhale bomba la atomu linapangidwa pakati pa zaka za m'ma 1900, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das (m'mabuku a 'Gupta Kheda Malika' ndi 'Bhavishyat Malika') ndi Balarama Das (m'buku la 'Kali Tandaba Gita') analitchula zaka mazana ambiri zapitazo.

Odia Sloka:

ପରମାଣୁ ଯେ ବୋମା କରିଦେବେଟି ଜମା, ପୋଡ଼ିଜାଳି ଦେବା ପାଇଁ ଭାରତ ସୀମା।
ପରମାଣୁ ଯେ ବୋମା ଯାହା ଲାଗି ଗାରିମା, ଦେଖାଇ ହୁଅନ୍ତି ଆଜି ପାଶ୍ଚାତ୍ୟ ସେନା।
ତାହା ଫୁଟିବ ନାହିଁ କେଣେ ଯିବ ମିଳାଇ, ଏହା ଦେଖି ବିଦେଶୀଏ ଯିବେ ପଳାଇ।
ଅଗ୍ନିର ଦାହିକା ଶକ୍ତି ଲୋ ବଉଳ ପ୍ରଭୁ କରିବେ ହରଣ, ପରମାଣୁ ବୋମା ଯାହାଲାଗି ଗାରିମା ଶୀତଳ ହୋଇବ ଜାଣ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Paramāṇu je bomā karidebeṭi jamā, poḍijāḷi debā pāiñ Bhārata sīmā.
Paramāṇu je bomā jāhā lāgi gārimā, dekhāi huanti āji pāshchātya senā.
Tāhā phuṭiba nāhiñ keṇe jiba miḷāi, ehā dekhi bideshīe jibe paḷāi.
Agnira dāhikā shakti lo bauḷa Prabhu karibe haraṇa, paramāṇu bomā jāhā lāgi gārimā shītaḷa hoiba jāṇa.

Tanthauzo:
Oyerawo analemba kuti pa Nkhondo Yadziko Lonse Yachitatu, adani a India adzakonza zoti awononge malire a India pogwiritsa ntchito Mabomba a Atomu (Paramanu Bomba). Komabe, dongosolo lawo lidzalephera chifukwa Ambuye Kalki adzatenga mphamvu yoyaka/yowononga (Dahika Shakti) ya mabomba amenewa. Zida zomwe asilikali akumadzulo ndi adani amanyadira zidzakhala "zazizira" (zopanda mphamvu) ndipo sizingakhudze nthaka ya India. Poona ntchito yosatheka imeneyi, adani akunja adzathawa ndi mantha. Mabukuwo akuchenjeza kuti nkhondo yowopsa ya nyukiliya (Anu-Paramanu Yuddha) idzachitika pamapeto pake, kupangitsa Dziko lapansi kukhala losakhalamo, zomwe opembedza adzaziwona.

3.12 Kutchulidwa kwa Madan Mohan Malaviya, Mahatma Gandhi, ndi Motilal Nehru

Wobadwa mu 1850, woyera wamkulu Bhima Bhoi (wotsatira miyambo ya Panchasakhas) adapereka tsatanetsatane wa atsogoleri a gulu lodziyimira pawokha la India m'buku lake la 'Padmakalpa'.

Odia Sloka:

ମଦନମାଲବ୍ୟ ନାମ ବୋଲିକରି ଏକ ଭକ୍ତ ଥିବ ଜନ୍ମି,
ଗାନ୍ଧୀ ସହିତରେ ଅହିଂସା ଧର୍ମରେ ନିରନ୍ତରେ ଥିବେ ପୁଣି।
ଚୁଡ଼ଙ୍ଗ ବଂଶରେ ଉତପତ୍ତି ହୋଇ ମୋତିଲାଲ ଯେ ନେହେରୁ,
ମହାପ୍ରତାପୀ ଅଟଇ ସେହୁ ଭକ୍ତ ଆସିଛନ୍ତି ଶୂନ୍ୟପୁରୁ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Madanamālabya nāma bolikari eka bhakta thiba janmi,
Gāndhī sahitare ahinsā dharmare nirantare thibe puṇi.
Chuḍaṅga baṃshare utpatti hoi Motilāl je Neheru,
mahāpratāpī aṭai sehu bhakta āsichanti Shūnyapuru.

Tanthauzo:
Buku lidalosera za kubadwa kwa wopembedza wotchedwa "Madan Mohan Malaviya" amene, pamodzi ndi "Mahatma Gandhi," adzatsogolera gulu lozikidwa pa chipembedzo cha kusachita chiwawa (Ahimsa). Komanso, lidalitchula za kubadwa kwa wopembedza wamphamvu komanso wodziwika kwambiri "Motilal Nehru." Onse atatu adakhala atsogoleri ofunikira a Indian National Congress ndi nkhondo yomenyera ufulu.

3.13 Ulosi wa Kuphedwa kwa Mahatma Gandhi ndi Nathuram Godse

M'buku la 'Jaiphula Malika', Mahapurush Achyutananda Das adalosera za imfa ya Mahatma.

Odia Sloka:

ମୋହନ ଗାନ୍ଧୀ ମରଣ, ନାଥୁରାମ ହସ୍ତେ ହୋଇବ ଜାଣ ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ଏ କଥା ସିଦ୍ଧ ଫଳ ରେ ନିପୁଣ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Mohana Gāndhī maraṇa, Nāthurāma haste hoiba jāṇa lo jāiphula,
e kathā siddha phaḷa re nipuṇa.

Tanthauzo:
"Imfa ya Mohan Gandhi idzachitika m'manja mwa Nathuram." Monga mbiri imasonyeza, pa Januwale 30, 1948, Nathuram Vinayak Godse adapha Mahatma Gandhi.

3.14 Ufulu wa India Pansi pa Utsogoleri wa Mahatma Gandhi

M'buku la 'Kali Bhagabata', Mahapurush Abhiram Paramahansa (woyera wa m'zaka za m'ma 1900 m'miyambo ya Malika) adafotokoza za kusintha kwa ndale.

Odia Sloka:

ସତ୍ୟାଗ୍ରହୀ ହୋଇଲେ ମେଳି ।
ଅନ୍ତର ହୋଇଯିବ କଳି ।।
ଇଉରୋପିଆନ ଯିବେ ଛାଡ଼ି ।
ମୋହନ ସେନା ଦେବେ କାଢ଼ି ।।
ପ୍ରଥମ ଯୁଦ୍ଧେ ସେନାପତି ।
ହେବେ ମୋହନ ସୈନ୍ୟ ଘେତି ।।
ମୋହନ ବୋଲି ଯାଙ୍କୁ କହି ।
ମହତ ଆତ୍ମା ଅଟେ ସେହି ।।
ସେହି ନାରଦ ମହାମୁନ୍ୟ ।
ତାଙ୍କୁ କରନ୍ତି ସର୍ବେ ମାନ୍ୟ ।।

Kumasulira mawu:

Satyāgrahī hoile meḷi,
antara hoijiba kaḷi.
Iuropiāna jibe chhāḍi,
Mohana senā debe kāḍhi.
Prathama yuddhe senāpati,
hebe Mohana sainya gheti.
Mohana boli jāṅku kahi,
mahata ātmā aṭe sehi.
Sehi Nārada mahāmunya,
tāṅku karanti sarbe mānya.

Tanthauzo:
Woyerawo adalosera kuti kudzera mu kusonkhana kwa Satyagrahis, Azungu adzakakamizika kuchoka. Adazindikira "Mohan" (Gandhi) ngati mkulu/mtsogoleri wa nkhondo yoyamba yayikulu yomenyera ufulu. Adafotokozanso Mahatma Gandhi ngati mzimu waukulu (Mahatma) ndi kubadwanso kwa Narada, amene mawu ake adzalemekezedwa ndi onse. Izi zidachitika pamene utsogoleri wa Gandhi udakhala mphamvu yofunikira yomwe idapangitsa Achingerezi kuchoka ku nthaka ya India.

4 Nthawi ya Kaliyuga

Malinga ndi malemba a Sanatan, zaka zonse za Kaliyuga ndi 432,000. Bhavishya Malika imatsimikiziranso nthawi yonseyi, koma imawonjezera ulosi wofunikira: chifukwa cha machimo ochuluka a anthu, Kaliyuga sidzamaliza nthawi yake yonse.

4.1 Lamulo la Malemba la Zaka 432,000

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akulemba mu 'Adi Samhita':

Odia Sloka:

କଳିରେ ଚାରି ଲକ୍ଷ ଯାଏ, ବତିଶ ସହସ୍ର ନିର୍ଣ୍ଣୟ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Kaḷire chāri lakṣa jāe, batiśa sahasra nirṇaya.

Tanthauzo:
"Mu Kaliyuga, zaka zikwi mazana anayi ndi makumi atatu ndi ziwiri (432,000) zakhazikitsidwa ngati nthawi yake yokhalapo."

Kenako Mahapurush Achyutananda Das adalemba m'buku lake, 'Dashapatala Malika':

Odia Sloka:

କଳିଯୁଗ ଭୋଗ ପ୍ରମାଣ ସାର,
ଚାରିଲକ୍ଷ ଯେ ବତିଶ ହଜାର।

Kumasulira mawu:

Kaliyuga bhoga pramāṇa sāra,
chārilakṣha je batisha hajāra.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, nthawi yokhalapo ya Kali Yuga yoyikidwiratu ndi zaka zikwi mazana anayi, makumi atatu ndi ziwiri.

Akulemba m'buku lake la Malika, 'Parardha Jataka':

Odia Sloka:

ଚାରିଲକ୍ଷ ବତିଶି ସହସ୍ର କଳି,
କରିଲି ତିନି ଯୁଗଠାରୁ ବଳି।

Kumasulira mawu:

Chārilakṣha batishi sahasra kali,
karili tini yugaṭhāru bali.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, nthawi yokhalapo ya Kali Yuga ndi zaka zikwi mazana anayi, makumi atatu ndi ziwiri.

Komabe, a Mahapurushas sanangosiya ponena izi; adalembanso m'mabuku awo a Malika kuti chifukwa cha machimo osiyanasiyana ochitidwa ndi anthu, Kali Yuga sidzatha nthawi yake yonse.

4.2 Chifukwa chiyani Kaliyuga idzatha msanga

A Panchasakhas akufotokoza kuti ngakhale zaka zoyerekezedwa ndi 432,000, koma "Bhoga" (nthawi yeniyeni yokhalapo) idzakhala yochepa kwambiri chifukwa cha kulemera kwa machimo. M'buku la 'Dashapatala Malika', kwalembedwa kuti:

Odia Sloka:

ଚାରି ଲକ୍ଷ ଯେ ବତିଶ ସହସ୍ର, ଭୋଗ ନୋହିବ କହ ପୀତବାସ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Chāri lakṣa je batiśa sahasra, bhoga nohiba kaha Pītabāsa.

Tanthauzo:
"Eya Ambuye Pitabasa (Jagannath)! Tiuzeni momwe Kaliyuga sidzatha kukwaniritsa nthawi yake yonse ya zaka 432,000."

Mahapurush akulemba m'buku lake la Malika, 'Kalikalpa Gita':

Odia Sloka:

ଚାରି ଲକ୍ଷ ବତିଶ ସସ୍ର ବର୍ଷ ଆଉ,
ଭୋଗ ନହେବ ପାପଭାରା ଯହୁଁ।
ଆପଣେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ହୋଇବେ ଉଦିତ,
ପାପୀମାନଙ୍କୁ କରିବେ ମୁକତ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Chāri lakṣha batisha sasra barṣha āu,
bhoga naheba pāpabhārā jahuṃ.
Āpaṇe prabhu hoibe udita,
pāpīmānaṃku karibe mukata.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, chifukwa cha kulemera kwambiri kwa machimo mu Kali Yuga, Kali Yuga sidzatha kukwaniritsa nthawi yake yokhalapo ya zaka 4,32,000. Ngakhale nthawi isanakwane, Ambuye adzabwera, ochimwa adzawonongedwa, ndipo Dharma idzakhazikitsidwa.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akulembanso m'modzi mwa mabuku ake a Malika:

Odia Sloka:

କଳି ଚାରି ଲକ୍ଷ ବତିଶ ସସ୍ର ନୋହିବ ଭୋଗ,
ପାପ ଯୋଗୁଁ କ୍ଷୟ ହୋଇବ ଅଳ୍ପ ପଡ଼ିବ ଭାଗ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Kali chāri lakṣha batisha sasra nohiba bhoga,
pāpa joguṃ kṣhaya hoiba alpa paḍiba bhāga.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, Kali Yuga sidzatha kukwaniritsa nthawi yake yoyikidwiratu ya zaka 4,32,000. Chifukwa cha machimo, nthawi yake yokhalapo idzawola, kutanthauza kuti, sidzatha kukwaniritsidwa kwathunthu.

4.3 Ngati si zaka 4,32,000, ndiye kuti nthawi yeniyeni yokhalapo ya Kali Yuga ndi chiyani?

Masiku ano, anthu ena akufalitsa, pogwira mawu Malika, kuti nthawi yeniyeni yokhalapo ya Kali Yuga ndi zaka 10,000. Dziwani kuti izi ndi zolakwika kwathunthu.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das adalemba m'buku lake la Malika, 'Shibakalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta':

Odia Sloka:

ଦଶ ସହସ୍ରରେ କଳିନାଶ ହେବ ଏ ଅଟେ ବିଷମ ବାଣୀ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Dasha sahasrare kalināsha heba e aṭe biṣhama bāṇī.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, Kali Yuga idzatha m'zaka 10,000 – izi ndi mawu osagwirizana kapena onama.

A Panchasakha Mahapurushas adalemba m'malo ambiri m'mabuku awo a Malika kuti nthawi yeniyeni yokhalapo ya Kali Yuga idzakhala zaka 5,000, pambuyo pake Ambuye Kalki adzabwera ndipo Satya-Kali kapena Ananta Yuga kapena Adya Satya Yuga idzafika.

Bwerani, tiyeni tipeze zambiri pa nkhaniyi imodzi ndi imodzi kudzera m'ma sloka a Malika.

Choyamba, tiyeni tidziwe zomwe Ambuye Shri Krishna Iye Mwini adanena za nthawi yokhalapo ya Kali Yuga.

4.4 Nthawi Yeniyeni: Zaka 5,000

Mosiyana ndi zabodza zomwe ena amafalitsa kuti Kaliyuga imakhala zaka 10,000, mabuku a Malika amabwerezabwereza kunena kuti ulamuliro weniweni wa Kaliyuga umatha pambuyo pa zaka 5,000, ndikutsatiridwa ndi nthawi yosinthira.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akugwira mawu a Ambuye Krishna kwa Garuda mu 'Agata Bhavishya Prabandha Malika':

Odia Sloka:

କଳି ଚାରି ଲକ୍ଷ ବତିଶ ସହସ୍ର ନୋହିବ ଭୋଗ, ପାପ ଯୋଗୁ କ୍ଷୟ ହୋଇବ ଅଳ୍ପ ପଡ଼ିବ ଭାଗ।
ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର ଯେ ପଚାଶ ଭୋଗ ହେବ ନିକର, ଏଥୁ କିଛି ଉଣା ହୋଇବ ଶୁଣ ଗରୁଡ଼ ବୀର।

Kumasulira mawu:

Kaḷi chāri lakṣa batiśa sahasra nohiba bhoga, pāpa jogu kṣaya hoiba alpa paḍiba bhāga.
Pāñcha sahasra je pachāsha bhoga heba nikara, ethu kichi uṇā hoiba shuṇa Garuḍa bīra.

Tanthauzo:
"O Garuda wamphamvu! Mvera, Kaliyuga sidzakumana ndi zaka 432,000. Chifukwa cha tchimo, nthawi yake idzachepa. Idzatha pafupi ndi zaka 5,050.

4.5 Fanizo la Ravana

Odia Sloka:

ରାମଦାସ ବୋଲଇ ହୋ ଶୁଣ ଗୁରୁଦେବ, କଳିଯୁଗ କେତେ ପୁଣ ଭୋଗ ଯେ ହୋଇବ।
ଚାରିଲକ୍ଷ ବତିଶ ଯେ ସହସ୍ର ବରଷ, କେମନ୍ତେ ଯେ ଯୁଗ ପୁଣ ହୋଇବଟି ଶେଷ।
ଏଡ଼େ ବଡ଼ ଯୁଗ ପୁଣ ବିନାଶ ହୋଇବ, ସଂଶୟ ଫେଡ଼ିଣ ମୋତେ କହ ଗୁରୁଦେବ।
ରାମଦାସ ବାଣୀ ଶୁଣି ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ କହନ୍ତି, ତୋତେ କହୁଅଛି ରାମ ଶୁଣ ଦେଇ ମତି।
ରାବଣ ଯେ ତ୍ରେତୟା ଯୁଗରେ ତପ କଲା, ଛପନ ଗଣ୍ଡା ଯୁଗକୁ ବର ସେ ପାଇଲା।
ପାପେ ଗଲା ତାର ତିନି ଭାଗ ଯେ ବିନାଶ, ଏକଭାଗ ଭୋଗ କଲା ବିଶ୍ରବାର ଶିଷ୍ୟ।
ସ୍ୱର୍ଗେ ପଶି ଅପସରୀ ମାନଙ୍କୁ ହରିଲା, ପର ଦ୍ରବ୍ୟ ହରି ଯେବେ ପାଦେ ଖଟାଇଲା।
ଆପେ ଜଗତମାତା ଯେ ଜନକ ନନ୍ଦିନୀ, ତାଙ୍କୁ ହରି ନେଲା ଗର୍ବେ ମୂଢ଼ ବିଂଶପାଣି।
ସୁରାପାନ କଲା ସେ କ୍ଷତ୍ରିଙ୍କୁ ବଧିଲା, ବ୍ରହ୍ମହତ୍ୟା ଆଦିକରି କେତେ ପାପ କଲା।
ପାପରୁ ଯେ ତିନି ଭାଗ ହୋଇଲା ବିନାଶ, ଏକ ଭାଗ ଭୋଗ କଲା ବିଶ୍ରବାର ଶିଷ୍ୟ।
ସେହିପରି କଳିଯୁଗ ବିନାଶ ହୋଇବ, ପାଞ୍ଚସସ୍ର ଭୋଗ ହେବ ଅନ୍ୟ ପାପେ ଯିବ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Rāmadāsa bolai ho shuṇa gurudeba, kaliyuga kete puṇa bhoga je hoiba.
Chārilakṣha batisha je sahasra baraṣha, kemaṃte je yuga puṇa hoibaṭi sheṣha.
Eḍe baḍa yuga puṇa bināsha hoiba, saṃshaya pheriṇa mote kaha gurudeba.
Rāmadāsa bāṇī shuṇi Achyuta kahaṃti, tote kahuachi Rāma shuṇa dei mati.
Rābaṇa je tretayā yugare tapa kalā, chapana gaṇḍā yugaku bara se pāilā.
Pāpe galā tāra tini bhāga je vināsha, ekabhāga bhoga kalā Bishrabāra shiṣhya.
Swarge pashi apasarī mānaṃku harilā, para drabya hari jebe pāde khaṭāilā.
Āpe jagatamātā je Janaka naṃdinī, tāṃku hari nelā garve mūḍha biṃshapāṇi.
Surāpāna kalā se kṣhatriṃṃku badhilā, brahmahatyā ādi kari kete pāpa kalā.
Pāparu je tini bhāga hoilā bināsha, eka bhāga bhoga kalā bishrabāra shiṣhya.
Sehipari kaliyuga bināsha hoiba, pāṃchasasra bhoga heba anya pāpe jiba.

Mu 'Kalpatika', Achyutananda Das akufotokoza chodabwitsachi pogwiritsa ntchito chitsanzo cha Ravana. Ravana adachita kulapa kwakukulu ndipo adapeza moyo wa "56 Ganda" (nthawi yayikulu yakumwamba). Komabe, chifukwa adaba Amayi Sita, adanyoza anamwali akumwamba, ndipo adachita machimo oyipa ngati Brahman-hatya, adangosangalala ndi gawo limodzi la madalitso ake, pomwe magawo ena atatu adawonongedwa ndi machimo ake.

Momwemonso, nthawi ya Kaliyuga ya zaka 432,000 iku "dyedwa" ndi kuchuluka kwachangu kwa tchimo padziko lonse, zomwe zikupangitsa kuti ithe msanga pambuyo pa zaka 5,000 za nthawi yomwe yakumana nayo, kutsegula njira ya Ananta Yuga kapena Satya Yuga.

4.6 Zaka za Kaliyuga: Kusanthula Kwatsatanetsatane

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das adalemba mu imodzi mwa mabuku ake a Malika:

Odia Sloka:

ଚାରି ଲକ୍ଷ ଯେ ବତିଶ ସହସ୍ର,
କଳିଯୁଗର ଅଟଇ ଆୟୁଷ।
ପାପଭାରରେ ଆୟୁ କଟିଯିବ,
ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର କଳି ଭୋଗ ହୋଇବ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Chāri lakṣa je batiśa sahasra,
Kaḷiyugara aṭai āyuṣa.
Pāpabhārare āyu kaṭijiba,
Pāñcha sahasra Kaḷi bhoga hoiba.

Tanthauzo:
Kutanthauza kuti, zaka za Kaliyuga ndi zikwi mazana anayi makumi atatu ndi ziwiri (432,000), koma chifukwa cha mphamvu ya machimo, zaka izi zidzachepetsedwa ndipo zidzatha mu zaka zikwi zisanu (5,000) zokha.

Chithunzi cha Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, wobadwa m'zaka za zana la 18 m'chigawo cha Jajpur ku Odisha, Woyera Mahapurush Hadi Das adakambirana za zaka za Kaliyuga mu buku lake la Malika lotchedwa ‘Kali Chautisha’. Iye adalemba:

Odia Sloka:

ସଂବତ୍ସର ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର କଳି ହୋଇବ ଶେଷ,
ସତ୍ୟ ଯୁଗ ଆଦ୍ୟ ହୋଇବ ଶୁଭଯୋଗେ ପ୍ରବେଶ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Saṃbatsara pāñcha sahasra Kaḷi hoiba sheṣa,
Satya yuga ādya hoiba shubhajoge prabesha.

Tanthauzo:
Kutanthauza kuti, mu "Sambatsara Paancha Sahasra" (zaka 5,000), Kaliyuga idzatha, ndipo pambuyo pake, Adya Satya Yuga idzayamba ndi nthawi yabwino.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das adalemba mu buku lina la Malika:

Odia Sloka:

ପାଞ୍ଚ ଯେ ହଜାର ଥାରୁ ମହାଭୟ ହେବ,
ଦିଲ୍ଲୀ ସମ୍ରାଟକୁ ଆସି ବିପଦ ପଡ଼ିବ।
ଗାନ୍ଧାର ସେନାଟି ଗୋଳ କରିବ ଅବଶ୍ୟ,
ପାଞ୍ଚ ହଜାର ପଚାଶ କଳିର ଆୟୁଷ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Pāñcha je hajāra thāru mahābhaya heba,
Dillī samrāṭaku āsi bipada paḍiba.
Gāndhāra senāṭi goḷa kariba abashya,
Pāñcha hajāra pachāsha Kaḷira āyuṣa.

Tanthauzo:
Mahapurush akulembanso pano kuti pambuyo pa zaka 5,000 za Kaliyuga, padzakhala mpweya wamantha aakulu; masoka adzagwera Mfumu ya Delhi kapena Nduna Yaikulu ya dzikolo. Asilikali a Gandhara (Taliban) adzamenya nkhondo ndi India. Mahapurush akulembanso kuti zonsezi zidzachitika pambuyo pa kutha kwa Kaliyuga, ndiko kuti, pambuyo pa zaka 5,050 za Kaliyuga zitadutsa.

Achyutananda Das ji adalembanso vesi mu buku lake la Malika momwe sananene zaka za Kaliyuga mwachindunji ngati 5,000 kapena 5,050, koma adazitchula mosadziwika kudzera m'mawu osonyeza:

Odia Sloka:

ବିଂଶେ ମସିହା ହୋଇବ ଶେଷ,
ଅନନ୍ତ କରିବେ କଳି ବିନାଶ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Biṃshe masiha hoiba sheṣa,
Ananta karibe Kaḷi bināsha.

Tanthauzo:
Kutanthauza kuti, m'zaka za '20s'—kuyambira chaka cha zikwi ziwiri (2000) mpaka zikwi ziwiri makumi asanu ndi anayi mphambu zisanu ndi zinayi (2099)—nthawi ina mkati mwa nthawiyi, Kaliyuga idzatha. Ambuye Ananta adzawononga Kaliyuga Padziko Lapansi.

Mmodzi mwa Panchasakhas, Mahapurush Sishu Ananta Das, adafotokoza zaka za Kaliyuga mwatsatanetsatane mu buku lake la Malika ‘Aagata Bhavishya Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

ବାରଙ୍ଗ ବୋଲଇ ଶୁଣିମା ଗୋସାଇଁ କୁହ ଭବିଷ୍ୟ ବିଚାର,
କେତେବେଳେ କଳ୍କୀ ସ୍ୱରୂପ ହୋଇବେ ଶୁଣଇଁ ମୁଖୁ ତୁମ୍ଭର।
ଶିଶୁ ବୋଲନ୍ତି ହେ ଶୁଣିମା ବାରଙ୍ଗ କଳଙ୍କି ସ୍ୱରୂପ ତୁହି,
ଯୁଗଶନ୍ଧି ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର ବରଷ ଯେବେ ଯିବ ଭୋଗ ହୋଇ।
ଜେସେନେକେ ନିଶି ପାହିଲେ ପ୍ରଭାତ ଯୁଗଶନ୍ଧି ଏହା ଜାଣ,
ଯେମନ୍ତ ସମୟେ କଳଙ୍କି ସ୍ୱରୂପ ହୋଇବେ ସେ ନାରାୟଣ।
ସମକ୍ଷର ବତା ଶୁଣି ଆଣିକରି ପ୍ରମାଣ ଏହାକୁ କର,
ସବୁ ଏକଠାବେ ମିଶାଇ କରିଣ କରିବୁ ପାଞ୍ଚ ହଜାର।
ଏହି ସମୟକୁ ତଳେ କରିଥିବୁ କହଇ ହୋ ବାବୁ ତୋତେ,
ଠିକ ରେ ଏ କଥା ଦେଖାଇ କହିଲୁ ରଖିଥିବୁ ହୃଦଗତେ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Bāraṅga bolai shuṇimā gosāiṃ kuha bhabiṣya bichāra,
ketebeḷe Kalkī swarūpa hoibe shuṇaiṃ mukhu tumbhara.
Shishu bolanti he shuṇimā Bāraṅga Kaḷaṅki swarūpa tuhi,
yugashandhi pāñcha sahasra baraṣa jebe jiba bhoga hoi.
Jeseneke nishi pāhile prabhāta yugashandhi ehā jāṇa,
jemanta samaye Kaḷaṅki swarūpa hoibe se Nārāyaṇa.
Samakṣara batā shuṇi āṇikari pramāṇa ehāku kara,
sabu ekaṭhābe mishāi kariṇa karibu pāñcha hajāra.
Ehi samayaku taḷe karithibu kahai ho bābu tote,
Ṭhika re e kathā dekhāi kahilu rakhithibu hṛudagate.

Tanthauzo:
Wophunzira Baranga Das akunena kwa Mahapurush Sishu Ananta Das—"O Guru Dev, ndiuzeni za tsogolo. Ndikufuna kumva kuchokera m'kamwa mwanu nthawi yomwe Ambuye adzawonekere mu mawonekedwe Ake a Kalki."

Atamva izi, Mahapurush Sishu Ananta Das akuuza wophunzira wake wamkulu Baranga kuti pamene 'Yuga-Sandhi' (kusintha pakati pa nthawi) ikachitika, pokhapo Ambuye adzatenga chithunzi Chake cha Kalki. Kufotokoza zambiri, akunena kuti Yuga-Sandhi iyi idzachitika pamene Kaliyuga yakumana ndi zaka 5,000. Monga momwe m'mawa umabwera pambuyo pa usiku, nthawi ina imabwera pambuyo pa nthawi ina. Gawo lapakati pakati pa nthawi ziwiri limatchedwa Yuga-Sandhi. Ndipo mu Yuga-Sandhi iyi, Narayana adzatenga mawonekedwe Ake a Kalki. Yuga-Sandhi iyi idzabwera pambuyo pa zaka 5,000 za Kaliyuga zitadutsa.

Achyutananda ji, pofotokoza zaka za Kaliyuga mu buku lake la Malika ‘Kaliyuga Malika’, akunena:

Odia Sloka:

ଚହଟିବ ଲୀଳା ତୁ ଚାରିରେ ମିଶା ଏକ,
ଚଢ଼ା ତିନି ଶୂନ ତହିଁ ଯେତେ ହେଲା ଠିକ।
ଚଳିଯିବ ଘୋର କଳି ଦଳି ଦେବେ ମିଳି,
ଚେତାଇ ଗୀତ ଛନ୍ଦଇ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ସେ ଭାଳି।

Kumasulira mawu:

chahaṭiba līḷā tu chārire mishā eka,
chaḍhā tini shūna tahiñ jete helā ṭhika.
Chalijiba ghora Kaḷi daḷi debe miḷi,
chetāi gīta chhandai Achyuta se bhāḷi.

Tanthauzo:
Kutanthauza kuti, onjezani chimodzi pa nambala zinayi (4+1=5) ndikuyika ziro zitatu pambali pa chiwerengerocho; nambala yopezeka ikuyimira nthawi yomwe nthawi ya Kaliyuga yoopsa idzatha. Mu vesi lomwe lili pamwambapa, Mahapurush akufotokoza zaka za Kaliyuga mwachizindikiro. Akunena kuti 4+1=5, ndiyeno kuyika ziro zitatu pambali pa 5 kumatanthauza kuti pambuyo pa zaka 5,000 za Kaliyuga zitadutsa, nthawi imeneyo idzatha.

Mu buku lina la Malika ‘Bhavishya Chautisha’, akunena kwa wophunzira wake Ramachandra:

Odia Sloka:

ଠିକଣା ଅମରପୁର,
ଠାକୁର ତହିଁରୁ ହେବେ ବାହାର,
ରାମଚନ୍ଦ୍ର ହେ, ଠାରି ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର ଧର।

Kumasulira mawu:

Ṭhikaṇā Amarapura,
Ṭhākura tahiñru hebe bāhāra, Rāmachandra he,
ṭhāri pāñcha sahasra dhara.

Tanthauzo:
Kutanthauza kuti, O wophunzira Ramachandra, chithunzi cha Ambuye Kalki chidzachitika ku Amarapur (dera lomwe silifa—lomwe linalipo ngakhale Satya Yuga isanachitike, ndiko kuti, Jajpur), ndipo izi zidzachitika pambuyo pa zaka 5,000 za Kaliyuga zitadutsa.

Kenako, mu buku lake la Malika ‘Kali Bayalisha’, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akufotokoza za zaka za Kaliyuga m'zizindikiro. Mahapurush akunena:

Odia Sloka:

ଦଶରଥ ସୁତ ମୁଣ୍ଡରେ ତିନି ଅଙ୍କ ଚଢ଼ିବ,
ବଜ୍ର ଅଙ୍କ ଦେଇ ଏଗାର ଅଙ୍କ ତହୁଁ ଫେରିବ।
ଶେଷ ଡାହାଣ ଭାଗ ଯାହା ହେଲା ଗାଣ୍ଡିବ ଗୁଣ,
କଳି ଶେଷ ଭୋଗ ଏହିଟି କହିଲି ଅରଜୁନ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Dasharatha suta muṇḍare tini aṅka chaḍhiba,
bajra aṅka dei egāra aṅka tahuṃ pheriba.
Sheṣa ḍāhāṇa bhāga jāhā helā gāṇḍiba guṇa,
kaḷi sheṣa bhoga ehiṭi kahili Arajuna.

Tanthauzo:
Kutanthauza kuti, lembani nambala 3 pamwamba pa chiwerengero cha ana a Mfumu Dasharatha (ndiko kuti, 4^3 = 64). Mahapurush akunena kuti musinthe ndikuchotsa 11 pa nambala imeneyo (Kusintha 64 kumapereka 46, ndipo 46 - 11 = 35). Mahapurush akunena kuti muchulukitse nambala yomwe ili kumanja kwa nambala yopezeka ndi 'Gandiva' (ndiko kuti, 1000) ndipo akulemba kuti Kaliyuga idzatha mu nambala yopezeka kapena zaka 5,000.
Chidziwitso: Apa, ndi 'Gandiva', Mahapurush akutchula za mphamvu yake yomenyera, yomwe inali 1,000 Yojanas.

Achyutananda Das ji akulemba mu buku lake la Malika ‘Adi Sanhita’:

Odia Sloka:

ଏ ଯେ ସୁବାହୁ ଯୁଗ କଳି,
କ୍ଷୀଣ ଆୟୁଷ ମହାବଳି।
ପାପେ ସକଳ କ୍ଷୟ ଯିବ,
ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର ଭୋଗ ହେବ।

Kumasulira mawu:

E je subāhu yuga kaḷi,
kṣīṇa āyuṣa mahābaḷi.
pāpe sakaḷa kṣaya jiba,
pāñcha sahasra bhoga heba.

Tanthauzo:
Kutanthauza kuti, zaka 432,000 za Kaliyuga zidzachepa mpaka zaka 5,000 zokha chifukwa cha machimo.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akulemba mu buku lake la Malika ‘Kali Kalpa Gita’:

Odia Sloka:

କଳି ପାଞ୍ଚସହସ୍ର ବରଷ ଭୋଗ ହେବ,
ସେହି ଦିନୁ ସତ୍ୟକଳି ପ୍ରକାଶ ପାଇବ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Kaḷi pāñchasahasra baraṣa bhoga heba,
sehi dinu Satyakali prakāsha pāiba.

Tanthauzo:
Kutanthauza kuti, kukumana kwa Kaliyuga kudzakhala kwa zaka 5,000. Pambuyo pake, 'Satyakali'—kuwala kwa Adya Satya Yuga mkati mwa Kaliyuga—kudzawonekera.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akulemba mu buku lake la Malika ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

କଳି ପାଞ୍ଚ ହଜାରରେ ଗଙ୍ଗାଦେବୀ ମେଳ କଟକ ଠାରେ,
ଭକ୍ତ ଦେଖିବେ ନେତ୍ରରେ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Kaḷi pāñchahajārare Gaṅgādebī meḷa Kaṭaka ṭhāre,
bhakta dekhibe netrare.

Tanthauzo:
Kutanthauza kuti, pambuyo pa zaka 5,000 za Kaliyuga zitadutsa, Mulungu Ganga adzadutsa ku Cuttack; kutanthauza kuti, chilichonse kuyambira Kolkata mpaka Cuttack chidzamira; padzakhala chigumula choopsa.

Mahapurush adalemba mu buku lake la Malika ‘Kaliyuga Bhavishya Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର କଳି ହଟେ ସତ୍ୟ ଉପୁଜିବ,
ମୋର ତଥ୍ୟମାନ ସେତେବେଳେଟି ହୋଇବ।
ପୂର୍ବ ପଶ୍ଚିମ ଉତ୍ତର ଆବର ଦକ୍ଷିଣ,
ପ୍ରକାଶ ହେବେ କଳଙ୍କି ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବଚନ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Pāñcha sahasra Kaḷi hāte satya upujiba,
mora tathyamāna setebeḷeṭi hoiba.
pūrba pashchima uttara ābara dakṣiṇa,
prakāsha hebe Kaḷaṅki Achyuta bachana.

Tanthauzo:
Kutanthauza kuti, pambuyo pa zaka 5,000 za Kaliyuga zitadutsa, Kaliyuga idzatha ndipo Chowonadi (Satya) chidzafika. Kenako, kuwala kwa Ambuye Kalki kudzawonekera m'mbali zonse—Kum'mawa, Kumadzulo, Kumpoto, ndi Kumwera.

Woyera Achyutananda Das akulembanso mu buku lake la Malika ‘Kaliyuga Bhavishya Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

ଚାରି ଲକ୍ଷ ସପ୍ତ ବିଂଶ ସହସ୍ର,
କଳିଯୁଗ ଆୟୁଷ ହେବ ବିନାଶ।
ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ରଟି ଭୋଗ ହୋଇବ,
ସତ୍ୟଯୁଗ ପୁଣି ଉଦୟା ହେବ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Chāri lakṣa sapta biṃsha sahasra,
Kaḷiyuga āyuṣa heba bināsha.
pāñcha sahasraṭi bhoga hoiba,
Satyayuga puṇi udayā heba.

Tanthauzo:
Kutanthauza kuti, zaka 427,000 zidzawonongedwa kuchokera ku zaka 432,000 za Kaliyuga; zaka 5,000 zokha zidzakumana nazo, pambuyo pake Satya Yuga idzayamba.

Osati Mahapurush Achyutananda Das yekha, komanso wina wa Panchasakhas, Mahapurush Sishu Ananta Das, adakambirananso za zaka za Kaliyuga m'mabuku ake osiyanasiyana a Malika. Pofotokozera wophunzira wake Baranga mu buku lake la Malika ‘Chumbaka Malika’, adalemba:

Odia Sloka:

ଏବେ ପାଞ୍ଚ ଠିକ କହିବା ଶୁଣ,
ବାରଙ୍ଗ ବିଚାର ଚିତ୍ତରେ ଘେନ।
ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର ଯେତେବେଳେ ହେବ,
ସମ୍ପୂର୍ଣ୍ଣ ଲୀଳା ପ୍ରକାଶ ହୋଇବ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Ebe pāñcha ṭhika kahibā shuṇa,
Bāraṅga bichāra chittare ghena.
pāñcha sahasra jetebeḷe heba,
sampūrṇa līḷā prakāsha hoiba.

Tanthauzo:
Kutanthauza kuti, mvera mosamala wophunzira Baranga, gwira nambala 5. Pamene zaka 5,000 zadutsa, pokhapo Leela zathunthu za Mulungu adzawonekera.

Mahapurush Sishu Ananta Das adalembanso mu buku lake la Malika ‘Chumbaka Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

କଳିଯୁଗ ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର ଗଲେ,
ବିଷ୍ଣୁ ଜନମ ଯେ ହୋଇବେ ଭଲେ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Kaḷiyuga pāñcha sahasra gale,
Viṣṇu janama je hoibe bhale.

Tanthauzo:
Kutanthauza kuti, pambuyo pa zaka 5,000 za Kaliyuga zitadutsa, chithunzi cha Ambuye Vishnu chidzachitika.

Kufotokoza kuti Kaliyuga idzatha pambuyo pa zaka zikwi zisanu zitadutsa ndipo masewera a Ambuye adzachitika, Mahapurush Balarama Das adalemba mu buku lake la Malika ‘Bhavishyata Parardha’:

Odia Sloka:

ହସ୍ତିନା କଟକ ଯେବଣ ଭୂଇଁ,
ବର୍ତ୍ତମାନ ଦିଲ୍ଲୀ ସହର ସେହି।
ପୂର୍ବର ଚରିତ କହିବା ଶୁଣ,
ଦେବାପି ଅଛନ୍ତି ତପସ୍ୟାରେଣ।
ଉତ୍ତର ଖଣ୍ଡେ ତପସ୍ୟା କରନ୍ତି,
ତପସ୍ୟା ପୂର୍ଣ୍ଣ ହେ ହୋଇ ନାହାନ୍ତି।
ପଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ରେ ବରଷ ପୁଣ,
ତପସ୍ୟା ତାଙ୍କର ହୋଇବ ପୂର୍ଣ୍ଣ।
ବାରୁଣା କୁଳେ ପ୍ରଭୁଙ୍କୁ ଭେଟିବେ,
ପ୍ରଭୁ ଶ୍ରୀହସ୍ତେ ସିରୀପା ବାନ୍ଧିବେ।
ଶ୍ରୀପଦ୍ମ ହସ୍ତ ତା ମୁଣ୍ଡେ ଲଦିବେ,
ହସ୍ତିନା ରାଜ୍ୟ ନଗ୍ରରେ ସ୍ଥାପିବେ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Hastinā kaṭaka jebaṇa bhūiṃ,
bartamāna Dillī sahara sehi.
Pūrbara charita kahibā shuṇa,
Debāpi achhanti tapasyāreṇa.
Uttara khaṇḍe tapasyā karanti,
tapasyā pūrṇa he hoi nāhānti.
Pañcha sahasre baraṣa puṇa,
tapasyā tāṅkara hoiba pūrṇa.
Bāruṇā kuḷe prabhuṅku bheṭibe,
Prabhu shrīhaste sirīpā bāndhibe.
Shrīpadma hasta tā muṇḍe ladibe,
Hastinā rājya nagrare sthāpibe.

Tanthauzo:
Kutanthauza kuti, dziko lomwe linali Hastinapur Cuttack ndi mzinda wa Delhi wamasiku ano. Mverani nkhani yakale—Devaapi akuchita kulapa. Akuchita kulapa m'mudzi wa Kalapa m'dera la Uttarakhand ku India, komwe kudzatha pambuyo pa zaka 5,000 za Kaliyuga zitadutsa. Kulapa kukatha, adzakumana ndi Ambuye. Ambuye adzamuveka korona ndi manja Ake a lotus ndikumupanga kukhala Mfumu ya Hastinapur kapena Delhi.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das amalemba m'buku lake la Malika lotchedwa ‘Kali Kalpa Gita’:

Odia Sloka:

ଦାରୁ ଚଢ଼ି ଦାରୁରୂପ ତେଜିକରି କଳ୍କୀ ରୂପ ହୋଇବ,
ନିଷ୍ଠା ବଚନ ମୋହର ରାମଚନ୍ଦ୍ର କଳିରେ ନିଶ୍ଚେ ଘଟିବ।
ଆହୁରି କଥାଏ ଶୁଣ ରାମଦାସ କଳି ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ରରେ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Dāru chaḍhi dārurūpa tejikari Kalkī rūpa hoiba,
niṣṭhā bachana mohara Rāmachandra Kaḷire nishche ghaṭiba.
āhuri kathāe shuṇa Rāmadāsa Kaḷi pāñcha sahasrare.

Tanthauzo:
Kutanthauza kuti, pambuyo pa zaka 5,000 za Kaliyuga zitadutsa, Ambuye Jagannatha adzasiya mawonekedwe Ake a 'Daaru-rupa' kapena Jagannatha ndipo adzatenga mawonekedwe a Kalki.

Kuchokera m'mavesi onse omwe ali pamwambawa, zikuwonekera bwino kuti zaka zomwe Kaliyuga yakumana nazo ndi zaka 5,000 zokha. Sizaka 432,000 kapena zaka 10,000.

Koma funso lofunika likubwera pano— Monga tikudziwira, zaka 5,000 za Kaliyuga zadutsa kale ndipo pakali pano chaka cha 5,127 chikuyenda. Malinga ndi kuwerengera kumeneku, Kaliyuga iyenera kuti inatha kale ndipo 'Adya Satya Yuga' iyenera kuti ikuyenda! Koma chikoka cha Kaliyuga chikuonekabe ponseponse. Machimo ndi kusayeruzika zikuchuluka kulikonse. Chifukwa chiyani kusamvana kumeneku?

Yankho ndi ili motere— Choyamba, zaka 5,000 ndi mawu wamba, osati olondola kwenikweni. Zaka 5,000 zikutanthauza pafupifupi zaka 5,000. Ichi ndicho chifukwa chake m'malo ambiri, zaka 5,050 zimalembedwanso.

Chachiwiri, malinga ndi Malika, nthawi ya Kaliyuga yatha kale ndipo pakali pano, 'Sandhikala' (nthawi yosinthira) ya Kaliyuga ndi Satya Yuga ikuyenda. Malinga ndi Malika, ndi pa Sandhikala iyi pamene zochitika za kubadwa kwa Ambuye Kalki ndi kukhazikitsidwa kwa Dharma ndi Iye—kutanthauza, kuwonongedwa kwa oipa ndi chipulumutso cha odzipereka—zidzachitika. Ichi ndicho chifukwa chake machimo ndi kusayeruzika zili pamwamba kwambiri ngakhale kuti nthawi ya Kaliyuga yatha. Ziyenera kudziwika kuti kukhazikitsidwa kwa Dharma kukamalizidwa, chikoka cha Kaliyuga chidzatha kwathunthu ndipo Choonadi, Dharma, ndi Chilungamo zidzakhazikitsidwanso.

Achyutananda Das walembanso m'mabuku ake a Malika za nthawi yomwe kukhazikitsidwa kwa Dharma kumeneku kudzachitika motsogozedwa ndi Ambuye Kalki.

4.7 Kugwirizana kwa Meena-Shani

Mahapurush analemba m'buku lake lotchedwa ‘Aagata Bhavishyat Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

ଠିକଣା ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବୋଲେ,
ଠ’ ତିନି ବାମେ ପାଞ୍ଚ ରଖିଲେ ରାମଚନ୍ଦ୍ର ହେ,
ଠକିଯିବ ମୀନ ଶନି ଭଲେ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Ṭhikaṇā Achyuta bole,
ṭha’ tini bāme pāñcha rakhile Rāmachandra he,
ṭhakijiba mīna shani bhale.

Tanthauzo:
Kutanthauza kuti, ngati muyika ziro zitatu ndi nambala 5 patsogolo pake (5,000), zikutanthauza kuti pambuyo pa zaka 5,000 za Kaliyuga zitadutsa, kugwirizana kwa Meena-Shani (Saturn mu Pisces) kudzachitika, komwe kudzakhala kofunika kwambiri.

5 Meena-Shani Yoga; Malinga ndi Malika

5.1 Kodi Meena-Shani ndi chiyani?

Mu sayansi ya nyenyezi, Saturn (Shani) amaonedwa kuti ndi pulaneti yoyenda pang'onopang'ono kwambiri, kukhala mu chizindikiro chimodzi cha zodiac kwa pafupifupi zaka 2.5. Pali zizindikiro zonse 12 za zodiac mu kayendedwe ka zodiac, ndipo Saturn amakhala mu chilichonse kwa pafupifupi zaka 2.5. Izi zikutanthauza kuti Saturn imatenga pafupifupi zaka 12 × 2.5 = 30 kuti idutse mu kayendedwe konse ka zodiac.

Tiyerekeze kuti Saturn ikulowa mu chizindikiro cha zodiac lero; imabwezera ku chizindikiro chomwecho pambuyo pa pafupifupi zaka 30 itamaliza kayendedwe konse. Meena-Shani amatanthauza kayendedwe ka Saturn mu chizindikiro cha zodiac cha Pisces (Meena).

Nthawi yomaliza yomwe Saturn inadutsa mu Pisces inali mu 1995. Popeza zimatenga pafupifupi zaka 30 kuti Saturn ibwerere ku chizindikiro chomwecho, 1995 + 30 = 2025.
