Choncho, mu chaka cha 2025, Saturn idzadutsa mu Pisces kachiwiri. Pambuyo pa 2025, kayendedwe kotsatira kudzakhala 2025 + 30, komwe ndi 2054–2055.

A Panchasakha Mahapurushas analongosola momveka bwino m'mabuku awo a Malika kuti kuwonongedwa kwa oipa, chitetezo cha odzipereka, ndi kukhazikitsidwa kwa Dharma ndi Ambuye Kalki kudzachitika makamaka pa nthawi ya Meena-Shani.

Kuti timvetse bwino izi, tiyeni tiwone mavesi ena omwe atchulidwa mu Malika ndi mafotokozedwe ake.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das amalemba m'buku lake la Malika lotchedwa ‘Prachi Kalpa’:

Odia Sloka:

ମହୀମଣ୍ଡଳରେ ଚମକ ପଡ଼ିବ,
ନ ଜାଣିବେ ହୀନ ଜନ।
ମ୍ଳେଚ୍ଛ ମାରୁଣୀରେ ସଂହାର ହୋଇବ,
ମୀନ ଶନି ର ପ୍ରମାଣ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Mahīmaṇḍaḷare chamaka paḍiba,
na jāṇibe hīna jana.
Mlechcha māruṇīre saṃhāra hoiba,
mīna shani ra pramāṇa.

Tanthauzo:
Kutanthauza kuti, padzakhala kulira kwakukulu padziko lapansi; padzakhala chisokonezo kulikonse. Palibe amene adzamvetse izi kupatula odzipereka. Mu zochitika izi, anthu oipa ndi ochimwa osawerengeka adzawonongedwa. Mahapurush adati zochitika izi zidzachitika pa nthawi ya zaka 2.5 za Meena-Shani Yoga.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das amalemba m'buku lake la Malika lotchedwa ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ଯୋବରା କାଳିଆବୋଦା ପ୍ରମାଣ, ଗୁପ୍ତରେ ଲାଗିବ ସେ ମାୟା ରଣ।
ଥୋକେ ଯିବେ କାମଚଣ୍ଡୀ ମୁଖରେ, ମ୍ଳେଚ୍ଛ ମାରୁଣୀ ଯେ ହେବ ଗୁପ୍ତରେ।
ତରକ ତେରୁଆ ଯେତେ ଭକତ, ଯୋବରା କାଳିଆବୋଦାରେ ନାଶ।
ଛିନ୍ନମସ୍ତା ଦେବୀ ଖର୍ପର ପାତି, କାଠମାଳିଆଙ୍କୁ ଖାଇବ ବାଛି।
ଘୋର ଅନ୍ଧକାର ସେବେଳେ ହେବ, ସବୁ ବୁଦ୍ଧି ଜ୍ଞାନ ହଜିଣ ଯିବ।
ଅନ୍ନ ନ ମିଳିବ ଶସ୍ୟ ନିଅଣ୍ଟି, ମୀନ ଶନିଆସି ଦେବଟି ଘାଣ୍ଟି।
ଲକ୍ଷେ ଚୁଲି ସେହିଦିନ ଫିଟିବ, ହାଡ଼ିଆ କମାର ପରଣୁ ଥିବ।
ସେଦିନ ଯେ ଗୁରୁବାରଟି ଜାଣ, କାଳିଆ ପରୀକ୍ଷା ଅଟେ ପ୍ରମାଣ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Jobarā Kāḷiābodā pramāṇa,
guptare lāgiba se māyā raṇa.
Thoke jibe Kāmachaṇḍī mukhare, Mlechcha māruṇī je heba guptare.
Taraka teruā jete bhakata, Jobarā Kāḷiābodāre nāsha.
Chhinnamastā debī kharpara pāti, kāṭhamāḷiāṅku khāiba bāchi.
Ghora andhakāra sebeḷe heba, sabu buddhi jñāna hajiṇa jiba.
Anna na miḷiba shasya niaṇṭi, mīna shaniāsi debaṭi ghāṇṭi.
Lakṣe chuli sehidina phiṭiba, hāḍiā kamāra paraṇu thiba.
Sedina je gurubāraṭi jāṇa, Kāḷiā parīkṣā aṭe pramāṇa.

Tanthauzo:
Mahapurush Achyutananda Das amalemba kuti pamene Saturn ili mu Pisces, pa nthawi imeneyo, mayesero a onse achinyengo ndi osadzipereka adzachitika ku Jobra ndi Kaliaboda ku Cuttack. Amayi Aumulungu (Milungu Yachikazi) adzawononga onse osadzipereka otere. Pa nthawi imeneyo, padzakhalanso mdima kwa masiku asanu ndi awiri ndi mausiku asanu ndi awiri. Nzeru za aliyense zidzasiya kugwira ntchito. Pa nthawi imeneyo, ochimwa sadzapezanso chakudya.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das amalemba m'buku lake la Malika lotchedwa ‘Kalikalpa Gita’:

Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନି ହେବ ମୁକୁନ୍ଦ ଅଙ୍କ,
ଭାରତଶ୍ୱରୀ ହେବ ଉତପାତ।
ହେତୁ ରଖିଥାଅ ବ୍ରଜକୁମର,
ସେକାଳେ ତେଜିବି ମୁଁ କଳେବର।

Kumasulira mawu:

Mīna shani heba Mukunda aṅka,
Bhāratashwarī heba utapāta.
Hetu rakhithāa Brajakumara,
sekāḷe tejibi muñ kaḷebara.

Tanthauzo:
Kutanthauza kuti, pamene Saturn ili mu Pisces, padzakhala chisokonezo ku India. Pa nthawi imeneyo, Ambuye Wamkulu adzasiya mawonekedwe ake wamba aumunthu ndipo adzatenga mawonekedwe amphamvu kwambiri a Kalki.

M'buku lina la Malika, Mahapurush analemba:

Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନିର ସଂଯୋଗ ହେବାର ସମୟ,
ପୃଥବୀରେ ହେବ ପୁଣି ଘୋର ଯେ ପ୍ରଳୟ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Mīna shanira saṃyoga hebāra samaya,
pṛthabīre heba puṇi ghora je pralaya.

Tanthauzo:
Kutanthauza kuti, pa nthawi ya kugwirizana kwa Meena-Shani, padzakhalanso chiwonongeko choyipa (Pralaya) padziko lapansi.

Achyutananda Das ji amalemba m'buku lake la Malika lotchedwa ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନି ଆସି ଦେଉଥିବ ଘାଣ୍ଟି ଭାରତ ଲଢ଼ୋଇ ହେବ,
ଦଶ ଅଣା ଯିବ ଛଅ ଅଣା ରହିବ ମୋ ବଚନ ଅଟେ ଧୃବ।
ଅଢ଼ୋଇ ବରଷ ଘୋର ଉତ୍ପାତ ଛବିଶ ଅଙ୍କ କୁଅନା,
ଶ୍ମଶାନ ପରାୟ ଏ ସାରା ସଂସାର ଉଚ୍ଛନ୍ନ ହୋଇବ ସିନା।

Kumasulira mawu:

Mīna shani āsi deuthiba ghāṇṭi Bhārata laḍhōi heba,
dasha aṇā jiba chha' aṇā rahiba mo bachana aṭe dhruba.
aḍhāi baraṣa ghora utpāta chhabbisha aṅka kuanā,
shmashāna parāya e sārā saṃsāra uchhanna hoiba sinā.

Tanthauzo:
Kutanthauza kuti, pa nthawi yomwe Saturn ikudutsa mu Pisces, Nkhondo Yachitatu Yapadziko Lonse idzayamba Padziko Lapansi, kuphatikizapo India. Kwa zaka ziwiri ndi theka, chiwonongeko choyipa chidzaonekera Padziko Lapansi. Dziko lonse lapansi lidzawonongedwa ndipo lidzatenga mawonekedwe a malo opatsira mitembo. Pa nthawi imeneyo, anthu osawerengeka adzafa.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das analemba m'buku lina la Malika lotchedwa ‘Bhabishyata Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

ସାବଧାନ ହୁଅ ତୁହି,
ଠା ଠିକଣା କିଛି ରହିବ ନାହିଁ।
ହେ ଭକ୍ତଗଣ! ମୀନ ଶନି ଆସିଗଲେ ଧାଇଁ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Sābadhāna hua tuhi,
ṭhā ṭhikaṇā kichhi rahiba nāhiñ.
He bhaktagaṇa! Mīna shani āsigale dhāiñ.

Tanthauzo:
Kutanthauza kuti, O anthu, samalani. Meena-Shani Yoga ikubwera. Pa nthawi imeneyo, masoka ambiri adzabwera, ndipo chiwonongeko chidzagwira pafupifupi aliyense; palibe amene adzapulumuke. Nzeru za anthu zidzasiya kugwira ntchito. Chisokonezo chidzachuluka kulikonse.

Wobadwa m'zaka za zana la 19, ndakatulo woyera Bhima Bhoi analemba za nkhaniyi m'buku lake la Malika lotchedwa ‘Padmakalpa’:

Odia Sloka:

ଶୁଖିବ ଜଳ ଅବିଳମ୍ବେ,
ମୀନ ଶନିର ମଧ୍ୟଭାଗେ।
ମୋଗଲ ପଠାଣର ଧାଡ଼ି,
ମଧ୍ୟ ଓଡ଼ିଶା ଥିବ ମାଡ଼ି।
ସିଂହ ମଙ୍ଗଳ ଗ୍ରହ ଦୃଷ୍ଟି,
କିଛି ମେଦିନୀ ଯିବ ଫାଟି।
ଗିରି କନ୍ଦର ଗୁମ୍ଫାମାନ,
ମୋ ଭକ୍ତେ ରହିବେଟି ପୁଣ।
ଆସିବେ ଚଣ୍ଡୀକାଇଗଣ,
ଭକ୍ଷିବେ ନଗ୍ରରେ ପଶିଣ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Shukhiba jaḷa abiḷambe,
mīna shanira madhyabhāge.
Mogala paṭhāṇara dhāḍi,
madhya Oḍishā thiba māḍi.
Siṃha maṅgaḷa graha dṛṣṭi,
kichhi medinī jiba phāṭi.
Giri kandara gumphāmāna,
mo bhakte rahibeṭi puṇa.
Āsibe chaṇḍīkāigaṇa,
bhakṣibe nagrare pashiṇa.

Tanthauzo:
Kutanthauza kuti, pambuyo pa pakati pa Meena-Shani Yoga, mitsinje ndi madambo zidzayamba kuuma nthawi yomweyo. Magulu ankhondo a mayiko adani adzalowa ku Odisha ndikumenyana. Pakati pa Meena-Shani Yoga yomweyo, pamene Mars ili mu Leo, zivomezi zidzachitikanso. Ambuye adzasunga odzipereka Ake otetezeka mosamala m'mapanga a mapiri. Chandi ndi Yoginis adzawononga oipa ndi ochimwa.

Ndakatulo wodzipereka Narada Das atchulanso za chisokonezo chomwe chimachitika pa nthawi ya Meena-Shani Yoga m'buku lake la Malika. Iye analemba:

Odia Sloka:

ମନ ତର୍କିଥିବୁ ତେତିଶ ଅଙ୍କରେ,
ପ୍ରାଣୀଏ ନାଶ ଯିବ ରଙ୍କରେ।
ବତିଶ ତେତିଶ ପୁଣି ଚଉତିରିଶ,
ମୀନ ଶନି ଭୋଗ ହେବ ଅଢ଼ାଇ ବରଷ।
ପୃଥବୀ ହେବ ଉଲ୍ଲୋଳ,
ଯୋଗିନୀ ହେବେ ପ୍ରବଳ।
ପଡ଼ି ହାଡ଼ କଙ୍କାଳ,
ଗଡ଼ିବ ମୁଣ୍ଡ ମାଳ।
କମ୍ପିବ ଭୈରବ ଡାକରେ,
ମନ ତର୍କିଥିବୁ ତେତିଶ ଅଙ୍କରେ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Mana tarkithibu tetisha aṅkare,
prāṇīe nāsha jiba raṅkare.
Batisha tetisha puṇi chautirisha,
mīna shani bhoga heba aḍhāi baraṣa.
Pṛthabī heba ulloḷa,
yoginī hebe prabaḷa.
Paḍi hāḍa kaṅkāḷa,
gaḍiba muṇḍa māḷa.
Kampiba bhairaba ḍākare,
mana tarkithibu tetisha aṅkare.

Tanthauzo:
Kutanthauza kuti, Meena-Shani Yoga idzatenga zaka ziwiri ndi theka ndipo pa nthawi imeneyi, anthu osawerengeka adzafa. Padzakhala kulira kwakukulu Padziko Lapansi. Yoginis adzachita leela ya chiwonongeko. Pa nthawi imeneyi, mitembo idzabalalika padziko lonse. Dziko Lapansi lidzanjenjemera ndi kubangula kwa Bhairavi.

Pofotokoza zokambirana pakati pa Ambuye Krishna ndi Amayi Rukmini pa nthawi ya Dvapara Yuga, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das amalemba m'buku lake la Malika lotchedwa ‘Rukmini Gita’:

Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନି ମେଳ ଗୋ ହୋଇବ ଯେତେବେଳେ,
ସେବେଳେ ସମର ହେବ ଭାରତ ମଣ୍ଡଳେ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Mīna shani meḷa go hoiba jetebeḷe,
sebeḷe samara heba Bhārata maṇḍaḷe.

Tanthauzo:
Kutanthauza kuti, pa nthawi yomwe Saturn ikudutsa mu Pisces, nkhondo yoopsa idzachitika ku India.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das analembanso:

Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନି ପଡ଼ିଲେଟି ଘୋର ଉତ୍ପାତ,
ମନେ ରଖିଥିବୁ ରାମ ତୁହି ବ୍ରଜସୁତ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Mīna shani paḍileṭi ghora utpāta,
mane rakhithibu Rāma tuhi Brajasuta.

Tanthauzo:
Kutanthauza kuti, pamene Meena-Shani Yoga ikachitika, chisokonezo choyipa chidzaonekera Padziko Lapansi.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das analemba m'buku la Malika lotchedwa ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ତେରଦିନ ପକ୍ଷ ହୋଇବ,
ମୀନ ଶନି ଯୋଗ ପଡ଼ିବ।
ଅଢ଼ାଇ ବରଷ ରାଜନ,
ଦିବସରେ ତାରା ଗଗନ।
ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କୁ କଷଣ ପଡ଼ିବ,
ଚଉଦ୍ୱାରେ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ହୋଇବ।
ବୈଜ୍ଞାନିକ ଯନ୍ତ୍ର ଅଚଳ,
ନିକଟରେ ଗୋଳ ଚହଳ।
ତପ ସଙ୍ଗେ ତୋପ ପରୀକ୍ଷା,
ସତ୍ୟ ଅନନ୍ତଟି ଦେଖା।
ଅନନ୍ତ ଯୁଗଟି ହୋଇବ,
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ କାମନା ପୂରିବ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Teradina pakṣa hoiba,
mīna shani yoga paḍiba.
Aḍhāi baraṣa rājana,
dibasare tārā gagana.
Bhaktaṅku kaṣaṇa paḍiba,
Chaudwāre yuddha hoiba.
Baijñānika yantra achaḷa,
nikaṭare goḷa chahaḷa.
Tapa saṅge topa parīkṣā,
satya Anantaṭi dekhā.
Ananta yugaṭi hoiba,
Achyutī kāmanā pūriba.

Tanthauzo:
Kutanthauza kuti, kumapeto kwa Kaliyuga, pa nthawi yokhazikitsa Dharma, masiku khumi ndi atatu (Paksha) adzachitika. Padzakhala Meena-Shani Yoga yomwe idzatenga zaka ziwiri ndi theka. Pa nthawi imeneyo, nyenyezi zidzaonekera masana. Pa nthawi imeneyo, odzipereka adzakumana ndi mavuto ambiri. Nkhondo idzachitika ku Choudwar ku Cuttack; zipangizo za sayansi ndi zamagetsi zidzasiya kugwira ntchito. Nkhondo yoopsa idzachitika. Mizinga yamakono ndi mfuti za mayiko adani zidzatsutsidwa ndi mphamvu ya kulapa (Tapa) kwa oyera mtima ndi anzeru a ku India. Ambuye Kalki adzaonekera; odzipereka adzalandira masomphenya (Darshan) a Ambuye. Nthawi Yamuyaya (Ananta Yuga) idzafika. Chikhumbo cha Achyutananda chidzakwaniritsidwa.

Analembanso m'buku lake la Malika lotchedwa ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ଦିବସରେ ତାରା ଆକାଶୁ ଖସିବ ମୀନ ଶନିର ରାଜତ୍ୱ,
ବାଡ଼ି ବେମାରିରେ ଅନେକ ମରିବେ କହେ ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Dibasare tārā ākāśu khasiba mīna shanira rājatva,
bāḍi bemārire aneka maribe kahe dīna Achyuta.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, nyenyezi zidzagwa kuchokera kumwamba masana; anthu osawerengeka adzafa chifukwa cha matenda ndi miliri. Panthawiyo, Meena-Shani Yoga idzakhala ikuchitika.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das analemba m'buku lake la Malika lotchedwa ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନି ଆସି କରିବ ରାଜତ୍ୱ ମାନବ ମାରୁଣୀ ହେବ,
ପରାଧୀନ ହେବ ଏ ଭାରତବର୍ଷ ଶାସନ କଳ ଲାଗିବ।
ଅରାଜକ ହେବ ଏହି ଜମ୍ବୁଦ୍ବୀପ ଭକ୍ତ ହୋଇବେ ଛାନିଆଁ,
ଅନହୁତି ଅଗ୍ନି ପ୍ରଜ୍ୱଳିତ ହେବ ଉଚ୍ଛନ୍ନ ସାରା ଦୁନିଆ।
ଛିନିଛତ୍ର ହେବ ଓଡିଶା ମଣ୍ଡଳ ଥୟ ନ ପଡ଼ିବେ କେହି,
ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ପ୍ରକାଶ କରିଣ ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ କୁହଇ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Mīna shani āsi kariba rājatwa mānaba māruṇī heba,
parādhīna heba e Bhāratabarṣa shāsana kaḷa lāgiba.
Arājaka heba ehi Jambudwīpa bhakta hoibe chhāniāñ,
anahuti agni prajwaḷita heba uchhanna sārā duniā.
Chhinichhatra heba Oḍishā maṇḍaḷa thaya na paḍibe kehi,
Chakaḍā maḍāṇa prakāsha kariṇa dīna Achyuta kuhai.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, pamene Saturn alowa mu Pisces, nthawi ya chiwonongeko cha anthu idzayamba. Dongosolo la demokalase lidzagwa, ndipo India idzataya ufulu wake—ikhoza ngakhale kulamuliridwa ndi wina. Chisokonezo chidzagwira dongosolo la boma, ndipo dzikoli lidzamira mu chisokonezo. Okhulupirira adzachita mantha poona zochitika izi. Moto wa chiwonongeko sudzagwira Odisha ndi India kokha, koma dziko lonse lapansi.

Achyutananda ji akulemba mu ‘Kalikalpa Gita’ yake:

Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ରାଶିକୁ ଶନିଶ୍ଚର ଗମନ ହେଲେ ଏମନ୍ତ ଭିଆଣ,
ଭାରା ନିବାରଣେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ଦେବରାୟ ମର୍ତ୍ତ୍ୟେ ଉହ୍ଲେଇବେ ଜାଣ।
କଳ୍କୀ ରାମ ରୂପ ଧରି ହରି ଦେଶ ଭ୍ରମଣ କରିବେ,
କ୍ଷତ୍ରୀମାନଙ୍କୁ ନିକ୍ଷତ୍ରୀ କରିଣ ବସୁଧାକୁ ଉଦ୍ଧାରିବେ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Mīna rāshiku Shanishchara gamana hele emanta bhiāṇa,
bhārā nibāraṇe prabhu debarāya martye uhleibe jāṇa.
Kalkī Rāma rūpa dhari Hari desha bhramaṇa karibe,
kṣatrīmānaṅku nikṣatrī kariṇa basudhāku uddhāribe.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, kuti adzimasule Dziko Lapansi ku tchimo, Ambuye Mahavishnu adzatenga kubadwa kwatsopano pa Dziko Lapansi. Panthawi ya Meena-Shani Yoga, atatenga mawonekedwe a Kalki, Mbuye Wamkulu adzayenda m'dziko ndipo powononga oipa ndi ochimwa, adzapulumutsa Amayi Dziko Lapansi.

Mahapurush akulemba m'buku lake la Malika lotchedwa ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ଭାରତ ଲଢ଼ାଇ ହେବ,
ଭାବ ବିନୋଦିଆ ମୂର୍ତ୍ତିଙ୍କୁ ଭାବ,
ଭାବରେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ ଦାସ ଚୁମ୍ବିବ।
ବାଇଆ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ ଗୀର,
ବାରହାତ ଖଣ୍ଡା ହେବ ବାହାର,
ବିରାଟ ଗାଦୀ ସଂହାର।
ଭକତ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ ଦାସ,
ଭୂମିକମ୍ପ ହୋଇ ପୃଥବୀ ଉଶ୍ୱାସ,
ଭକ୍ତ ରହିବେ ବିଶେଷ।
ମାୟାରେ ବୁଲେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ,
ମନ୍ତ୍ର ଅଣାକାର ହୃଦରେ ଘୋଷ,
ମୀନ ଶନି ହେବ ଦୃଶ୍ୟ।
ଯୋବରା ଘାଟର ଲୀଳା,
ଜଗିଛି ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ନ କରି ହେଳା,
କଟିବ ପାପୀଙ୍କ ଗଳା।

Kumasulira mawu:

Bhārata laḍhāi heba,
bhāba binodiā mūrttiṅku bhāba,
bhābare Achyutī dāsa chumbiba.
Bāiā Achyutī gīra,
bārahāta khaṇḍā heba bāhara,
birāṭa gādī saṃhāra.
Bhakata Achyutī dāsa,
bhūmikampa hoi pṛthabī ushwāsa,
bhakta rahibe bisheṣa.
Māyāre bule Achyuta,
mantra aṇākāra hṛdare ghoṣa,
mīna shani heba dṛshya.
Jobarā ghāṭara līḷā,
jagichhi Achyuta na kari heḷā,
kaṭiba pāpīṅka gaḷā.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, nkhondo yotsalira ya Mahabharata idzachitika. Nkhondo yoopsa idzachitika ku Virat Gadi kapena Choudwar ku Cuttack, ndipo lupanga la manja khumi ndi awiri lidzapha ochimwa. Zivomezi zazikulu zidzachitika zomwe zidzawononga oipa. Mayesero a onse achinyengo adzachitika ku Jobra Ghat ku Cuttack, ndipo Amayi Aumulungu adzamwa magazi awo. Panthawiyi, okhulupirira adzatetezedwa. Mahapurush analemba kuti zochitika izi zidzachitika mu Meena-Shani Yoga.

Achyutananda Das ji akulemba m'buku lake la Malika lotchedwa ‘Tattvabodhini’:

Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ରାଶିକି ଶନିଶ୍ଚର ଯିବ,
ଓଡ଼ିଶା ନଷ୍ଟ ଭ୍ରଷ୍ଟ କରିଦେବ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Mīna rāshiki Shanishchara jiba,
Oḍishā naṣṭa bhraṣṭa karideba.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, Saturn atalowa mu Pisces, dziko la Odisha lidzawonongeka.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akulembanso m'buku lake la Malika lotchedwa ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନି ଯୋଗ ଦେଶକୁ ପଡ଼ିବ ତେର ଦିନ ପକ୍ଷ ହେବ,
ଗୁପତ ମାରୁଣୀ ସେଠାବରେ ହେବ ରାମଚନ୍ଦ୍ର ମନେ ଭାବ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Mīna shani yoga deshaku paḍiba tera dina pakṣa heba,
gupata māruṇī seṭhābare heba Rāmachandra mane bhāba.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, Meena-Shani Yoga idzachitika, ndipo padzakhala masiku khumi ndi atatu. Panthawi yomweyo, anthu adzafa mobisa, ndipo Dharma idzakhazikitsidwa.

Komanso mu ‘Kalikalpa Gita’, akulemba:

Odia Sloka:

ଚଳିଯିବ କଳି ଗୋଳ ଉପୁଜିବ ବାବୁ,
ମୀନ ଶନି ହୋଇଥିବ ଗୁପତ ରଖିବୁ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Chaḷi jiba kaḷi goḷa upujiba bābu,
mīna shani hoithiba gupata rakhibu.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, panthawi ya Meena-Shani Yoga, Kaliyuga idzatha ndipo Dharma idzakhazikitsidwa.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akulembanso m'buku lake la Malika lotchedwa ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ଛାରଖାର ହେବ ଏ ସୁନା ସଂସାର ଭଇରବୀ ଦେବ ଡାକ,
ଅକାଳ ମରଣ ଗୁପତ ମାରୁଣୀ ମୀନ ଶନିଯୋଗ ହେବ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Chārakhāra heba e sunā saṃsāra bhairabī deba ḍāka,
akāḷa maraṇa gupata māruṇī mīna shaniyoga heba.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, dziko lino lidzakhala "lowonongeka" kapena kuwonongedwa kotheratu. Amayi Bhairavi adzafuula kwambiri; ochimwa ambiri adzakumana ndi imfa yosayembekezereka, ndipo ambiri adzafa mobisa. Zochitika zonsezi za chiwonongeko zidzachitika panthawi ya Meena-Shani Yoga, kutanthauza pamene Saturn ali mu chizindikiro cha Pisces.

Kuchokera m'mavesi a Malika omwe ali pamwambapa, zikuwonekeratu kuti panthawi ya Meena-Shani Yoga, zichitika za Ambuye Kalki zowononga zidzakhala zogwira mtima kwambiri. Panthawiyi, Dziko Lapansi lonse lidzawona zochitika zazikulu komanso zosayembekezereka za tsoka. Kungofuula, nkhondo, chisokonezo, zipolowe, ndi masoka achilengedwe zidzawoneka kulikonse. Panthawiyi, oipa ndi osalungama adzawonongedwa kuchokera pa Dziko Lapansi lonse.

5.2 Mu Meena-Shani iti Leela za Ambuye Kalki adzachitika?

Titadziwa kuchokera m'mavesi omwe ali pamwambapa kuti Leela zowononga za Kalki adzachitika panthawi ya Meena-Shani, funso lalikulu limabuka: ndi panthawi iti yeniyeni ya Meena-Shani pomwe zochitika izi zidzachitika?

Meena-Shani Yoga imachitika kamodzi pa zaka 30 zilizonse. Choncho, ndikofunikira kudziwa kuti ndi Meena-Shani iti, malinga ndi Malika, yomwe idzawona zochitika za kukhazikitsidwa kwa Dharma.

Tikayang'anitsitsa, tikupeza kuti Saturn anali mu Pisces mu 1995; pakali pano, mu 2025, Saturn walowanso mu Pisces; kenako mu 2054, ndipo pafupifupi zaka 30 pambuyo pake, mu 2084, kuyenda kwa Saturn mu Pisces kudzachitikanso.

Meena-Shani iti?

Zalembedwa momveka bwino mu Bhavishya Malika kuti pamene zochitika izi zidzachitika mkati mwa zaka ziwiri ndi theka za Meena-Shani, dzina la Mfumu ya Puri, Odisha, panthawiyo lidzakhala Divyasingha Deva.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das analemba m'buku lake la Malika lotchedwa ‘Chandra Kalpa’:

Odia Sloka:

ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଥିବ ସେହି ବେଳର,
ପୃଥବୀ ହୋଇଯିବ ଘନ ଅନ୍ଧାର।
ପଞ୍ଚଭୂତର ଏ ପ୍ରଳୟ ରାଶି,
ଭୂଭାରାକୁ ରାମ ଯିବ ରେ ଗ୍ରାସୀ।
ସେକାଳେ ମୀନେ ଶନି ଥିବ ଚଲି,
ନାଶିବ କାଳ ହେବ ଜଳ ଖେଳି।
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ କହଇ ଗୁରୁ ସ୍ମରିଣ,
ଚନ୍ଦ୍ର କଳ୍ପରେ ମୁଁ କଲି ବଖାଣ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Dibyasiṃha thiba sehi beḷara,
pṛthabī hoijiba ghana andhāra.
pañchabhūtara e pralaya rāśi,
bhūbhārāku rāma jiba re grāsī.
sekāḷe mīne śani thiba chali,
nāśiba kāḷa heba jaḷa kheḷi.
Achyuta kahai guru smariṇa,
chandra kalpare muñ kali bakhāṇa.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, pamene Saturn ali mu Pisces, panthawiyo, mdima waukulu udzaphimba Dziko Lapansi kwa masiku 7 ndi mausiku 7. Kuwonongeka kobwera chifukwa cha zinthu zisanu (Dziko, Madzi, Moto, Mpweya, ndi Ether) kudzadya Dziko Lapansi lonse. Mahapurush analemba kuti panthawiyo, dzina la Mfumu ya Puri lidzakhala ‘Divyasingha’.

Tsopano pali mfundo zina zofunika kuzindikira pano. Mfumu yapano ya Puri, Shri Divyasingha Deva (IV), ali ndi zaka pafupifupi 72. Mu Meena-Shani yotsatira—ndiko kuti, zaka 30 kuchokera pano—adzakhala ndi zaka zoposa 100 ndipo pali mwayi wochepa kwambiri kuti adzakhalabe pampando wachifumu wa Puri panthawiyo!

Tsopano anthu ena, omwe amati nthawi ya zochitika ndi Meena-Shani yotsatira (ndiko kuti, chaka cha 2054–2057), akuganiza kuti zochitika izi zikhoza kuchitika pa nthawi ya ulamuliro wa mfumu ina yotchedwa Divyasingha! Koma ndikuuzeni kuti izi sizingatheke. Mfumu yapano ya Puri ndi Mfumu ya 19, ndipo malinga ndi Malika, Mfumu ya 19 idzakhala Mfumu yomaliza ya Kaliyuga. Ndiko kuti, malinga ndi Malika, pambuyo pa Mfumu yapano Shri Divyasingha Deva, sipadzakhalanso Mafumu a Puri.

Kukambirana komwe kuli pamwambapa kumatsimikizira kuti zochitika zonse zatsoka zidzachitika panthawi ya Meena-Shani Yoga pakati pa 2025–2028, pa nthawi ya ulamuliro wa Shri Divyasingha Deva IV.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akulembanso m'buku lake la Malika lotchedwa ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ବହୁତ ଅନ୍ୟାୟ ପାପର ଭାରା,
ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ହୋଇବେ ଧରା।
ବଡ଼ ଦେଉଳରେ ପାଣି ପଶିବ,
ସିଂହ ଦ୍ୱାରେ ରକ୍ତଧାର ବହିବ।
ଟିକରା ଗୋହିରୀ ହୋଇବ ରଡ଼ି,
ଧାନ ଥିବ ଯିବ କଣ୍ଠା ଅଗାଡ଼ି।
ସେବେଳକୁ ଏକା ଅକ୍ଷର ସାର,
ସପତ ଦିନ ଯେ ହେବ ଅନ୍ଧାର।
ଗାଲୁଆ ମାଲୁଆ ଭକତ ଛନ୍ତି,
ବଗୁଲିଆ କର୍ମେ ପେଟ ପୋଷନ୍ତି।
ଏମାନେ ଯେ ଦେବୀ ଖପରରେ ଯିବେ,
ଭକତମାନେ ଆନନ୍ଦରେ ରହିବେ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Bahuta anyāya pāpara bhārā,
Dibyasiṃha deba hoibe dharā.
Baḍa deuḷare pāṇi pashiba,
siṃhadvāre raktadhāra bahiba.
ṭikarā gohirī hoiba raḍi,
dhāna thiba jiba kaṇṭhā agāḍi.
sebeḷaku ekā akṣara sāra,
sapata dina je heba andhāra.
gāluā māluā bhakata chanti,
baguliā karme peṭa poṣanti.
emāne je debī khaparare jibe,
bhaktamāne ānandare rahibe.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, kumapeto kwa Kaliyuga, padzakhala kupanda chilungamo kwambiri ndi katundu wochuluka wa tchimo. Panthawiyo, madzi a m'nyanja adzalowa mu Kachisi wa Jagannath. Nkhondo idzayamba mkati mwa kachisi. Mtsinje wa magazi udzayenda kudutsa pa Chipata cha Mkango (Singhadwara). Panthawiyo, mdima udzaphimba Dziko Lapansi lonse kwa masiku asanu ndi awiri ndi mausiku asanu ndi awiri. Onse omwe ali okhulupirira achinyengo, omwe amadzichirikiza ndi machitidwe olakwika, adzawonongedwa ndi Amayi Aumulungu. Okhulupirira adzakhala ndi nthawi yosangalala. Panthawiyo, Mfumu ya Puri idzakhala Shri Divyasingha Deva, yemwe adzagwidwa kapena kutengedwa ndi opondereza.

Zikuwonekeratu kuchokera m'vesi lomwe lili pamwambapa kuti pamene zochitika za kukhazikitsidwa kwa Dharma zidzachitika, ndipo pamene padzakhala mdima kwa masiku asanu ndi awiri ndi mausiku asanu ndi awiri pa Dziko Lapansi, Mfumu ya Puri panthawiyo idzakhala Shri Divyasingha Deva.

Mahapurush wina wochokera ku Odisha wobadwa m'zaka za zana la 20 analemba m'buku lake limodzi kuti pambuyo pa chaka cha 2033, kuwala kwa nthawi yatsopano (Satyayuga) kudzawonekera; kutanthauza kuti, malinga ndi Mahapurush, Leela zonse za kusintha kwa nthawi adzamalizidwa chaka cha 2033 chisanafike. Tidzakupatsani mavesi okhudzana ndi izi mu Mutu 12. Mfundo ndi yakuti zochitika zonse zatsoka zidzachitika chaka cha 2033 chisanafike.

Mahapurush Bhima Bhoi (wolemba ndakatulo wakhungu wokhulupirira wobadwira ku Sambalpur, Odisha m'zaka za zana la 19) analemba pa nkhaniyi m'buku lake la Malika lotchedwa ‘Padma Kalpa’:

Odia Sloka:

ନିଶ୍ଚୟ କଳଙ୍କି ହୋଇବ, ଯେ ଦିନ ମୀନ ଶନି ଭୋଗ।
ପଞ୍ଚଗ୍ରହ ଯେ ଯୁକ୍ତ ହେବେ, ଆଗାମୀ ଅଢ଼ାଇର ଯୋଗେ।
ତଦନ୍ତେ ଷଡ଼ ଗ୍ରହ ଆସି, ଶନି କୁମ୍ଭରୁ ଯିବେ ଖସି।
ପ୍ରବେଶ ମୀନ ସଙ୍ଗତରେ, ଟାକିଥାଅ ହେ ସୁଜ୍ଞ ନରେ।
ହୋଇବ ଅସଂଖ୍ୟ ସମର, ଦମନ ହୋଇବ ବାହାର।
ବିଷ୍ଣୁ ନାଶିବ ଅବହେଳେ, ଜଗତେ ରଖି ବାହୁବଳେ।
ମ୍ଳେଚ୍ଛଙ୍କୁ ଦମନ ନାଶିବ, ଦୁର୍ଗା ଖପର ପକାଇବ।
ଅସଂଖ୍ୟ କଳି ସେ ଦମନ, ଶୁଣ ହେ ସାଧୁ ସୁଜ୍ଞଜନ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Nishchaya Kaḷaṅki hoiba, je dina mīna shani bhoga.
Pañchagraha je yukta hebe, āgāmī aḍhāira joge.
Tadante ṣaḍa graha āsi, shani kumbharu jibe khasi.
Prabesha mīna saṅgatare, ṭākithāa he sujña nare.
Hoiba asaṃkhya samara, damana hoiba bāhāra.
Biṣṇu nāshiba abaheḷe, jagate rakhi bāhubaḷe.
Mlechchaṅku damana nāshiba, Durgā khapara pakāiba.
Asaṃkhya kaḷi se damana, shuṇa he sādhu sujñajana.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, pamene Saturn alowa mu Pisces, Leela zowononga za Ambuye Kalki adzachitika. Nthawi imeneyi idzatenga zaka ziwiri ndi theka. Panthawiyi, nkhondo zosawerengeka zidzamenyedwa pakati pa mayiko osiyanasiyana. Ambuye Kalki adzateteza okhulupirira, ndipo Amayi Durga adzawononga oipa ndi ochimwa.

M'vesi lomwe lili pamwambapa, Mahapurush adalongosola momveka bwino kuti ndi mu Meena-Shani iti pomwe Leela zowononga za Kalki adzachitika. Pofotokoza za Meena-Shani imeneyo, adati tsiku lomwe Saturn alowa mu Pisces, mapulaneti 5 adzakhala atapezeka kale mu Pisces. Ndiko kuti, tsiku limenelo, mapulaneti okwana 6 adzapezeka mu Pisces—mapulaneti 5 omwe analipo kale ndi Saturn mmodzi, okwana mapulaneti 6.

Tikayang'ana Meena-Shani ya 2025, tikupeza kuti mawu a Mahapurush akukhala olondola kwambiri. Pa 29 Marichi 2025, tsiku lomwe Saturn adzalowa mu Pisces, mapulaneti asanu (Rahu, Venus, Mercury, Dzuwa, ndi Mwezi) anali atapezeka kale mu Pisces.

Tsopano, tikayang'ana Meena-Shani ya 2054, tikupeza kuti pa 14 Meyi 2054, tsiku lomwe Saturn adzalowa mu Pisces, mapulaneti 2 okha (Venus ndi Mars) adzapezeka mu Pisces pasadakhale, ndipo ndi Saturn, amapanga mapulaneti 3. Pomwe malinga ndi Saint Bhima Bhoi, payenera kukhala mapulaneti okwana 6 mu Pisces, osati 3.

Tikayang'ana Meena-Shani Yoga ya chaka cha 2084, tikupeza kuti pa 20 Marichi 2084, tsiku lomwe Saturn adzalowa mu Pisces, mapulaneti 2 okha (Mercury ndi Dzuwa) adzapezeka mu Pisces pasadakhale. Ngakhale kuphatikiza Saturn, ndi mapulaneti 3. Izi zikutanthauza kuti kuyenda kwa mapulaneti 6 sikuchitika nayonso pano.

Zikuwonekeratu kuti Mahapurush sakunena za Meena-Shani Yoga ya 2054 kapena 2084. Meena-Shani yomwe akunena m'buku lake la Malika lotchedwa ‘Padma Kalpa’ ndi Meena-Shani ya 2025.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das adanenanso m'buku lake la Malika kuti ndani adzakhala Mahant (Mtsogoleri) wa Padmaban Peeth yake yomwe ili ku Nemalo, Cuttack, pamene Leela zowononga izi adzachitika. Iye analemba:

Odia Sloka:

ଗଗନାନନ୍ଦ ଯେ ମହନ୍ତ କଳି ଶେଷ ବେଳରେ,
ନବ୍ୟକ୍ତ କଥା କହିଲି ରାମ ହେତୁ ଚିତ୍ତରେ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Gaganānaṃda je mahaṃta kaḷi śeṣa beḷare,
Nabyakta kathā kahili rāma hetu chittare.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, O wophunzira Ramadas, pamene Kaliyuga idzatha, panthawiyo munthu wotchedwa Gaganānanda adzakhala Mtsogoleri wa Peeth yanga.

Mahapurush analemba m'buku lina la Malika kuti:

Odia Sloka:

ଗଗନାନନ୍ଦ ଯେ ମହନ୍ତ ହୋଇବ ଗଗନେ ଉଡ଼ିବ ଧୂଳି,
ଓଡ଼ିଶା ଦେଶ ଯେ ଚମକି ପଡ଼ିବ ପଡ଼ିଯିବ ହାଲହୁଳି।

Kumasulira mawu:

Gaganānaṃda je mahaṃta hoiba gagane uḍiba dhūḷi,
Oḍishā desha je chamaki paḍiba paḍijiba hālahuḷi.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, Leela za Mulungu adzachitika pa nthawi ya Mahant Gaganānanda. Pa nthawiyo, padzakhala kulira kwakukulu m'boma la Odisha.

5.3 Mahant Gaganānanda Goswami

Ndikofunika kudziwa kuti m'badwo wa 14 wa Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, mbadwa yake komanso Shri Gaganānanda Goswamiji wazaka 85–90 panopa, ndiye Mutu wa mpando wauzimu wa Mahapurush, Padmaban, Nemalo. Izi zikuwonetsanso kuti Mahapurush akulankhula za nthawi ino.

Kuchokera pa mfundo zinayi zomwe zili pamwambapa, zikuwonekeratu kuti malinga ndi Malika, Meena-Shani momwe Leela zowononga za Kalki adzachitikira adzabwera chaka cha 2033 chisanafike, ndipo pa nthawi ya Meena-Shani imeneyo, Shri Divyasingha Deva adzakhala Mfumu ya Puri ndipo Shri Gaganānanda adzakhala Mahant wa Padmaban Peeth, Nemalo. Osati zokhazi, koma pa tsiku limene Saturn adzalowa mu Pisces, Shad-graha (mapulaneti 6) Yoga idzapangidwa mu Pisces.

Zikuwonekeratu kuti Mahapurush akuloza ku Meena-Shani yomwe idzafika pakati pa 2025–2028 chifukwa mfundo zonse zinayi zomwe zili pamwambapa zikugwirizana ndi Meena-Shani Yoga iyi yokha osati ina.

Nkosafunikira kunena kuti malinga ndi Malika, zochitika zazikulu zowononga zomwe Ambuye Kalki adzachita zidzachitika pakati pa 2025–2028.

Dziwani: Masiku ano anthu ena akunena kuti Meena-Shani yomwe yatchulidwa mu Malika si Meena-Shani yamakono, koma yotsatira yomwe idzafika mu 2054! Zingakhale koyenera kuti okhulupirira onse omwe ali ndi chikhulupiriro mu Malika asadabwe ndi izi, chifukwa mawu otere ndi olakwika kwathunthu.

6 Mfumu ya Puri kumapeto kwa Kaliyuga

M'mutu wapitawu, tidawona kuti zochitika zonse zazikulu za kukhazikitsidwa kwa Dharma zidzachitika pa nthawi ya ulamuliro wa Mfumu ya Puri, Shri Divyasingha Deva. M'mutu uno, tidzamvetsetsa izi kudzera m'mavesi ena a Malika.

Mmodzi mwa Panchasakhas, Mahapurush Jagannath Das, analemba m'buku lake la Malika lotchedwa ‘Kali Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

ପୁରୁଷୋତ୍ତମ ଦେବ ରାଜା ଠାରୁ,
ଉନ୍ନବିଂଶ ରାଜା ହେବ ସେଠାରୁ।
ସେଠାରୁ ପୁଣ ରାଜା ନାହିଁ ଆଉ,
ଅକୁଳି ହୋଇବେ କୁଳକୁ ବହୁ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Puruṣottama Deba rājā ṭhāru,
unnabiṃsha rājā heba seṭhāru.
Seṭhāru puṇa rājā nāhiṃ āu,
akuḷi hoibe kuḷaku bahu.

Tanthauzo:
Kutanthauza, kuyambira Mfumu Purushottama Deva pambuyo pa Mfumu Indradyumna, mafumu okwana 19 adzakhala Mafumu a Jagannath Puri pa nthawi zosiyanasiyana. Mahapurush analemba kuti Mfumu ya 19 idzakhala yopanda mwana wamwamuna ndipo adzakhala mfumu yomaliza; kutanthauza kuti, palibe wina amene adzakhala mfumu pambuyo pake.

Ngati tiyang'ana momwe zinthu zilili panopa, tikupeza kuti Mfumu yamakono ya Puri ndi Mfumu ya 19 ndipo ilibe mwana wamwamuna. Chizindikiro chomveka cha Mahapurush ndi chakuti masewera onse a Kalki okhazikitsa Dharma adzachitika pa nthawi ya ulamuliro wa mfumu yamakono.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das analemba m'buku lake la Malika lotchedwa ‘Adi Samhita’:

Odia Sloka:

ଶ୍ରୀ ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ନାମେ ରାୟେ,
ଏ ଯୁଗ ପ୍ରାନ୍ତେ ସେ ଉଦୟେ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Shri Dibyasiṃha nāme rāye,
e juga prānte se udaye.

Tanthauzo:
Kutanthauza, pa kutha kwa Kaliyuga, Shri Divyasingha adzakhala Mfumu ya Puri.

M'buku lina la Malika, analemba kuti:

Odia Sloka:

ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଅଙ୍କେ ବାବୁ ସରବ ଦେଖିବୁ,
ଛାଡ଼ି ଚକା ଗଲୁ ବୋଲି ନିଶ୍ଚୟ ଜାଣିବୁ,
ନର ବାଳୁତ ରୂପରେ ଆମ୍ଭେ ଜନମିବୁ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Dibyasiṃha aṅke bābu saraba dekhibu,
chhāḍi chakā galu boli nishchaya jānibu,
nara bāḷuta rūpare āmbhe janamibu.

Tanthauzo:
Kutanthauza, pamene ulamuliro wa Mfumu yotchedwa Shri Divyasingha Deva ukuchitika ku Puri, pa nthawiyo masewera onse a Mulungu a Ambuye Kalki adzachitika. Pa nthawiyo, Ambuye adzachoka ku Kachisi Wake wa Jagannath ndipo adzabadwa m'thupi la munthu ngati Kalki Avatar.

Kenako m'buku la Malika ‘Kalikalpa Gita’, Achyutanandaji akulemba kuti:

Odia Sloka:

ବୃଦ୍ଧ ବାଳକ ରାଜା ହୋଇଥିବ,
ଗୁପତରେ ସଣ ସାଲ ଚାଲିବ।
ବାଳବୃଦ୍ଧ ଅଙ୍କେ ବତାସ ହେବ,
ଜଳ ପବନ ବତାସ ବହିବ।
ଜଳ ପବନ ବତାସ ବହିବ,
ପୃଥିବୀ ହୀନମାନ ବହୁ ହୋଇବ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Bruddha bāḷaka rājā hoithiba,
gupatare saṇa sāla chāliba.
bāḷabruddha aṅke batāsha heba,
jaḷa pabana batāsha bahiba.
jaḷa pabana batāsha bahiba,
pṛthibī hīnamāna bahu hoiba.

Tanthauzo:
Kutanthauza, “Baalak-Briddha” adzakhala Mfumu ya Puri; ndiko kuti, munthu adzakhala mfumu amene adzakwera pampando wachifumu ali mwana ndipo adzakhalabe pa udindowo mpaka ukalamba. Pa nthawi ya ulamuliro wake, zochitika monga masoka a madzi ndi masoka a mphepo zidzachitika.

Zikudziwika kuti mwa mafumu onse omwe adakhalapo mpaka pano, palibe amene adakhala mfumu kuyambira ubwana mpaka ukalamba kupatula Shri Divyasingha Deva. Mfumu yamakono Shri Divyasingha Deva adakwera pampando wachifumu pa July 7, 1970, ali ndi zaka 17 zokha. Ali pa udindowu, walowa mu ukalamba ndipo panopa ali ndi zaka pafupifupi 72. Kudzera mu vesi ili, m'chilankhulo choyerekeza, Mahapurush adawulula kuti Divyasingha Deva adzakhala mfumu pa kutha kwa Kaliyuga.

Achyutananda Dasji analembanso m'buku lake la ‘Kalikalpa Gita’ kuti:

Odia Sloka:

ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ଅଙ୍କରେ ଜାଣ,
ଧୂମକେତୁ ହେବ ପୂର୍ବଭାଗେଣ।
ଉଲ୍‌କାପାତ ହେବ ହାଲହୁଳି,
ଶ୍ୱାନ ଶୃଗାଳ ଛାଡ଼ିବେ ବୋବାଳି।
ମାତିବେ କାଳୀ ଯେ କରାଳୀଗଣ,
ଲୋକେ ମରିବେ ହୋଇ ରଣଭଣ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Dibyasiṃha Deba aṅkare jāṇa,
dhūmaketu heba pūrbabhāgeṇa.
ulkāpāta heba hālahuḷi,
shwāna shṛgāḷa chhāḍibe bobāḷi.
mātibe kāḷī je karāḷīgaṇa,
loke maribe hoi raṇabhaṇa.

Tanthauzo:
Kutanthauza, pamene Shri Divyasingha Deva ali Mfumu ya Puri, pa nthawiyo nyenyezi yochita mchira idzawoneka kum'mawa. Padzakhala mvula ya nyenyezi padziko lonse lapansi. Nyama zodya nyama monga agalu ndi ankhandwe zidzachoka m'nkhalango ndikupita kumadera okhala anthu. Amayi Kali ndi anzawo a Yoginis adzawononga ochimwa padziko lapansi, zomwe zidzachititsa kuti anthu osawerengeka afe.

Mahapurush adadziwitsa mwachidule kudzera mu vesi lomwe lili pamwambapa kuti zochitika za kukhazikitsidwa kwa Dharma zidzachitika pa nthawi ya Mfumu ya Puri yotchedwa Shri Divyasingha Deva pa kutha kwa Kaliyuga.

Mahapurush adauza wophunzira wake Ramadas m'buku lina la Malika kuti:

Odia Sloka:

କେଉଁ କାଳ ବୋଲି ବୋଇଲୁ ଶୁଣ ରାମ ରତନ,
ଥିବ ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ରାଜନ କୋଳେ କନ୍ୟା ରତନ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Keuñ kāḷa boli boilu śuṇa Rāma ratana,
thiba Dibyasiṃha rājana koḷe kanyā ratana.

Tanthauzo:
Kutanthauza, Ee wophunzira Ramadas, mvetsera za nthawi yomwe ndikulankhula—pa nthawi yomwe zochitika izi zidzachitika, ulamuliro wa Mfumu yotchedwa Divyasingha udzakhala ukuchitika, amene sadzakhala ndi mwana wamwamuna; koma ana aakazi okha.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das analemba m'buku lina la Malika kuti:

Odia Sloka:

ବାରଣ ବେଳକୁ କରିବ ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ନୃପତି,
ତାଙ୍କ କୁଳେ ପୁତ୍ର ନୋହିବ ଭଣେ ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Bāra bāra beḷaku kariba Dibyasiṃha nṛpati,
tāṅka kuḷe putra nohiba bhaṇe dīna Achyutī.

Tanthauzo:
Kutanthauza, pa kutha kwa Kaliyuga, padzakhala ulamuliro wa Mfumu yotchedwa Divyasingha amene adzakhala wopanda mwana wamwamuna.

Ulosi uwu wa Mahapurush ukugwirizananso bwino ndi Mfumu yamakono ya Puri, Shri Divyasingha Deva. Mfumu yamakono ilibe mwana wamwamuna; ili ndi ana aakazi anayi okha.

M'buku lina la Malika, ‘Kaliyuga Gita’, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das analemba mawu olankhulidwa ndi Ambuye Krishna kwa Arjuna mu Dwaparayuga, momwe Ambuye Krishna adati:

Odia Sloka:

ଶ୍ରୀକ୍ଷେତ୍ର ରାଜନ ମୋର ନ ସେବି ପୟର।

Kumasulira mawu:

Shrikshetra rājana mora na sebi payara.

Tanthauzo:
Kutanthauza, pa kutha kwa Kaliyuga, Mfumu ya Puri sidzachita ntchito yanga ya tsiku ndi tsiku (mwa mawonekedwe a Jagannath).

Achyutananda Dasji kenako analemba kuti:

Odia Sloka:

ନ ଥିବ ରାଜନ ଶ୍ରୀକ୍ଷେତ୍ରେ କ୍ଷେତ୍ର ମଉଡ଼ ମଣି,
ରାଜ୍ୟ ରାଜକୁଳ କନ୍ୟାକୁ ନ କରିବ ବର୍ଣ୍ଣି।

Kumasulira mawu:

Na thiba rājana Shrīkṣetre kṣetra mauḍa maṇi,
rājya rājakuḷa kanyāku na kariba barṇṇi.

Tanthauzo:
Kutanthauza, Mfumu ya Puri sidzakhala ku Shrikshetra kapena Puri, koma idzakhala kwina. Sidzakwatira mwana wamkazi wa mfumu ya Odisha koma idzakwatira mwana wamkazi wa mfumu yochokera ku boma lina.

Woyera mtima wamkulu wa Odisha wobadwa m'zaka za zana la 18, Mahapurush Arakshita Das, analemba m'buku lake la Malika ‘Dwadasa Chandrika’ kuti:

Odia Sloka:

ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ନୃପତି ନିଶ୍ଚୟ ଆସିବେ ଫେରି,
କ୍ଷେତ୍ରକୁ ନ ଯାଇ ରହିବେ ଜାଣ ସେ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି।
ଭେଦ ମୁଁ କାହୁଁ ଜାଣିଥାନ୍ତି ଆଦିମାତା କହିଲେ,
ଭଣେ ଅରକ୍ଷିତ ଅଧମ ତହୁଁ ଶୁଣିଲି ଭଲେ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Dibyasiṃha Deba nṛpati nishchaya āsibe pheri,
kṣhetraku na jāi rahibe jāṇa se Khaṇḍagiri.
bheda muñ kāhuñ jāṇithānti Ādimātā kahile,
bhaṇe Arakṣhita adhama tahuñ śuṇili bhale.

Tanthauzo:
Mahapurush akulemba kuti pa kutha kwa Kaliyuga, Divyasingha Deva adzakhala Mfumu ya Puri ndipo sadzakhala ku Shrikshetra kapena Puri, koma adzakhala kudera la Khandagiri kapena Bhubaneswar. Mahapurush akulembanso momwe iye mwini adadziwira chinsinsi cha nkhani izi; Amayi Oyambirira (Adi Mata) adamuuza izi.

Ndidziwitse owerenga kuti mafumu onse asanakhale Mfumu yamakono ankakhala ku Puri, Odisha, ndipo ankachita ntchito ya tsiku ndi tsiku ya Ambuye Jagannath, koma Mfumu yamakono Shri Divyasingha Deva amachita ntchito ya Ambuye Jagannath pa nthawi zina zapadera zokha. Sakukhala ku Puri koma amakhala ku Shahid Nagar, Bhubaneswar. Kuwonjezera apo, Shri Divyasingha Deva sanakwatire mwana wamkazi wa mfumu yochokera ku Odisha koma adakwatira mwana wamkazi wa Lieutenant Colonel Anant Singh Charak waku Jammu.

Maulosi atatu omwe ali pamwambapa akugwirizana bwino ndi Mfumu yamakono ya Puri, Shri Divyasingha Deva. Izi ndi umboninso kuti Kaliyuga idzatha pa nthawi ya ulamuliro wa Mfumu Shri Divyasingha Deva.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das analemba m'buku lina la Malika ‘Tera Janma Sharana’ kuti:

Odia Sloka:

ମାଘ ସପ୍ତମୀରେ ବିକାଶ ହୋଇବ ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ନୃପବର,
ଅନନ୍ତ କେଶରୀ ରାଜତ୍ୱ କରିବେ ଏହି ଏକାମ୍ର ନଗର।

Kumasulira mawu:

Māgha Saptamīre bikāsha hoiba Dibyasiṃha nṛpabara,
Ananta Keshari rājattwa karibe ehi Ekāmra Nagara.

Tanthauzo:
Kutanthauza, pamene Divyasingha Deva ali Mfumu ya Puri, ndiye kuti ulamuliro wa Ananta Keshari, ndiko kuti, Ambuye Kalki, udzachitika. Idzakhala tsiku la Magha Saptami; Bhubaneswar (Ekambra Nagar) adzakhala malo.

Vesi ili likuwonetsanso kuti Mfumu ya Puri pa nthawi ya masewera a Mulungu a Kalki adzakhala Shri Divyasingha Deva.

Mu buku lake la Malika ‘Mohakalpa’, Achyutanandaji anaperekanso chizindikiro china chosonyeza kuti Shri Divyasingha Deva adzakhala mfumu yomaliza ya Puri. Mahapurush akulemba kuti:

Odia Sloka:

ଲଲାଟ କେଶରୀ ଧନୁଶର ଧରି ଆଗେ ସେ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ କରିବେ,
ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ନୃପତି ଯେ ଜାଣ ବୈଷ୍ଣବ ଧର୍ମ ଛାଡିବେ।
କ୍ଷତ୍ରି ବୃତ୍ତି ଧରି ପଦାତିଙ୍କ ସହ କରିବେ ବହୁ ସମର,
ପ୍ରଭୁଙ୍କ ଦେଖି ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ପଡିବେ ପଦ୍ମପାଦର।
ନବଲକ୍ଷ ଧର୍ମ-ହୀନ ବ୍ୟକ୍ତି ତହିଁ ତୁରିତେ ହେବେ ଛେଦନ,
ଲଲାଟ କେଶରୀ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ଘାତେ ତହିଁ ମେଦିନୀ ଯେ କମ୍ପମାନ।
କ୍ଷେତ୍ର ଠାରୁ ଆଦି ଓଡିଶା ରାଜ୍ୟରେ ଯେତେକ ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣ ଥିବେ,
ଗାୟତ୍ରୀ ମନ୍ତ୍ରକୁ ପରୀକ୍ଷା ନ ଦେଇ ସମସ୍ତେ ତହିଁ ମରିବେ।
ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣମାନଙ୍କୁ ଗାୟତ୍ରୀ ମନ୍ତ୍ର ପୁଛିବେ,
ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣ ହତ୍ୟା ବୋଲି ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣମାନଙ୍କୁ କହି ବଧ୍ୟ ନ କରିବେ।
ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ପରୀକ୍ଷା କରିବେ ଗାୟତ୍ରୀ ମନ୍ତ୍ରକୁ ଜାଣ,
ଯେଉଁ ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣଟି ପରୀକ୍ଷା ନ ଦେବ ନାଶିବ ଯେ ସୁଦର୍ଶନ।
ଅନନ୍ତ କେଶରୀ ସୁଦର୍ଶନ ଚକ୍ର ଶୂନ୍ୟ ଗଗନେ ରଖିବେ,
ଚଉଷଠି ଅକ୍ଷର ପ୍ରମାଣ ନ ପାଇ ଶୂନ୍ୟରେ ଥାଇ ଛେଦିବେ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Lalāṭa Kesharī dhanushara dhari āge se juddha karibe,
Dibyasiṃha Deba nṛpati je jāṇa baiṣṇaba dharma chhāḍibe.
Kṣatri bṛutti dhari padātiṅka saha karibe bahu samara,
Prabhuṅka dekhi Dibyasiṃha Deba paḍibe padmapādara.
Nabalakṣa dharma-hīna byakti tahiṃ turite hebe chhedana,
Lalāṭa Kesharī juddha ghāte tahiṃ medinī je kampamāna.
Kṣetra ṭhāru ādi Oḍishā rājyare jeteka brāhmaṇa thibe,
Gāyatrī mantraku parīkṣā na dei samaste tahiṃ maribe.
Dibyasiṃha Deba brāhmaṇamānaṅku Gāyatrī mantra puchhibe,
Brāhmaṇa hatyā boli brāhmaṇamānaṅku kahi badhya na karibe.
Dibyasiṃha Deba parīkṣā karibe Gāyatrī mantraku jāṇa,
Jeuṃ brāhmaṇaṭi parīkṣā na deba nāshiba je Sudarshana.
Ananta Kesharī Sudarshana chakra shūnya gagane rakhibe,
Chauṣaṭhi akṣara pramāṇa na pāi shūnyare thāi chhedibe.

Tanthauzo:
Tanthauzo, Mfumu ya Puri, Shri Divyasingha Deva, adzasiya Vaishnava Dharma yake ndikulandira ntchito ya Kshatriya ndikumenya nkhondo zambiri. Ataona Ambuye Kalki, adzagwada pa mapazi a Ambuye, kulandira madalitso a Ambuye, ndikuwononga anthu osakhulupirira lakh asanu ndi anayi. M'dera la Jagannath, Puri, A-Brahmin onse omwe alipo adzayesedwa ndi Gayatri Mantra. Poopa tchimo lakupha A-Brahmin, Divyasingha Deva sadzapha A-Brahmin omwe adzalephera mayesowo. Mahapurush akulemba kuti onse adzawonongedwa ndi Sudarshana Chakra ya Ambuye. Ambuye adzaika Sudarshana Chakra Yake kumwamba kopanda kanthu ndikuwadula mitu A-Brahmin omwe sadzawerenga Gayatri Mantra ndi chakra chomwecho.

Apa, Mahapurush akupereka umboni kuti Mfumu ya Puri, Shri Divyasingha Deva, adzakumana ndi Ambuye Kalki ndikuthandiza kukhazikitsa Dharma. Zikuonekeratu kuti machitidwe owononga a Ambuye Kalki adzachitika pa nthawi ya ulamuliro wa Shri Divyasingha Deva.

Kenako Mahapurush akulemba mu buku lake la Malika ‘Kalpa Tika’ kuti:

Odia Sloka:

ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ସେ ଯେ କ୍ଷତ୍ରିୟ ରାଜନ,
ତାହାଙ୍କୁ ଫିରିଙ୍ଗୀ କରିଥିବେ ସେ ବନ୍ଧନ।
କ୍ଷେତ୍ରର ନୃପତି ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଯେ ରାଜନ,
ତାହାଙ୍କୁ ଯେ ରଖିଥିବେ ଶ୍ରୀ ହରି ଗୋପନ।
ବନ୍ଧନର ଦୁଇ ଦିନରେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ଯେ ଶ୍ରୀ ହରି,
ରାତ୍ରିକାଳେ ରାଜା ପାଶେ ବିଜେ ଯେ ଶ୍ରୀ ହରି।
ରାଜାର ବନ୍ଧନ ଫେଇ ପ୍ରଭୁ ବାସୁ ଦେବେ,
ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ତଳ ପୁରେ ଗୋପନେ ରଖିବେ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Dibyasiṃha Deba se je Kṣhatriya rājana,
tāhānku Phiringī karithibe se bandhana.
Kṣhetrara nṛupati Dibyasiṃha je rājana,
tāhānku je rakhithibe Shrī Hari gopana.
Bandhanara dui dinare Prabhu je Shrī Hari,
rātrikāḷe rājā pāśe bije je Shrī Hari.
rājāra bandhana phei Prabhu bāśu debe,
Khaṇḍagiri taḷa pure gopane rakhibe.

Tanthauzo:
Tanthauzo, pamene Mfumu ya Puri, Shri Divyasingha Deva, akagwidwa ukapolo ndi magulu ankhondo akunja, ndiye patatha masiku awiri, Ambuye Kalki adzamufikira mfumuyo usiku, kumumasula ku ukapolo, ndikumubisa kumapazi a phiri la Khandagiri.

Komanso, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akulemba mu buku lake la Malika ‘Padmakalpa Tika’ kuti:

Odia Sloka:

ଅନନ୍ତ ଗୁମ୍ଫା ମଧ୍ୟରେ ମୋ ଭକ୍ତ ରହିବେ,
ସେହିବେଳେ ସରକାର ଖାଦ୍ୟ ପକାଇବେ।
ଅନନ୍ତ କେଶରୀ ସ୍ୱାମୀ ଥିବେ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି,
ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଲଲାଟ କେଶରୀ ଆଦିକରି।

Kumasulira mawu:

Ananta gumphā madhyare mo bhakta rahibe,
sehibeḷe sarakāra khādya pakāibe.
Ananta Keshari swāmī thibe Khaṇḍagiri,
Dibyasiṃha Lalāṭa Keshari ādikari.

Tanthauzo:
Tanthauzo, pamene tsoka lalikulu la madzi lidzachitika, bhakta odalira a Ambuye Kalki adzakhala otetezeka mu phanga la Ananta lomwe lili pa phiri la Khandagiri. Pa nthawiyo, boma lidzapereka chakudya ndi zothandizira kwa ozunzidwa. Mahapurush analemba kuti pa nthawiyo, Ananta Madhava, kutanthauza, Ambuye Kalki, adzakhala ku Khandagiri, Odisha. Mfumu Divyasingha Deva adzakhalanso Naye.

Mahapurush analembanso mu buku lake la Malika ‘Kalpa Tika’ kuti:

Odia Sloka:

ଅନନ୍ତ କେଶରୀ ସ୍ୱାମୀ ଥିବେ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି,
ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ସେ ଯେ ଲଲାଟକେଶରୀ।
ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ଠାବରେ ସେ ପ୍ରଭୁଙ୍କୁ ଦେଖିବେ,
ଲଲାଟକେଶରୀ ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହକୁ ଚିହ୍ନିବେ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Anaṃta kesharī swāmī thibe Khaṇḍagiri,
Dibyasiṃha deba se je lalāṭakesharī.
Khaṇḍagiri ṭhābare se prabhuṃku dekhibe,
Lalāṭakesharī Dibyasiṃhaku chihnibe.

Tanthauzo:
Tanthauzo, pamene Ambuye Ananta Keshari, kutanthauza, Ambuye Kalki, ali ku Khandagiri, ndiye pa nthawiyo Divyasingha Deva adzakhalanso ku Khandagiri. Ku Khandagiri, Divyasingha Deva adzakhala ndi masomphenya (Darshan) a Ambuye.

Kuchokera m'mavesi atatu onse omwe ali pamwambawa, zikudziwika kuti pa nthawi ya machitidwe aumulungu a Ambuye Kalki, Mfumu ya Puri adzakhala Divyasingha Deva.

6.1 Kukomana kwa Ambuye Kalki ndi Baladeva ndi Divyasingha Deva ndi Mafumu Ambiri

Bhavishya Malika ili ndi nkhani zachinsinsi zambiri ndi maulosi. Pakati pa maulosiwa pali ulosi wa mafumu ambiri okumana ndi Ambuye.

Mahapurush akulemba mu buku lake la Malika kuti m'tsogolomu, nthawi idzafika pamene asilikali akunja adzagonjetsa kuchokera ku Chhatia, Jajpur mpaka ku Nilagiri kapena Nilachal, Puri. Ngakhale gulu lalikulu la asilikali a adani lidzalowa mkati mwa Kachisi wa Jagannath. Ataona zonsezi mu chisoni chachikulu, Ambuye, atamizidwa mu kulapa, adzakumbukira Sahadeva, Nakula, Ashwatthama, ndi Shalya. Ambuye akangowakumbukira, kusinkhasinkha ndi kulapa kwa ankhondo anayi olimba mtima, akatswiri a kuwombera mivi, kudzatha, ndipo onse anayi adzafika ku Chhatia ndikugwada pa mapazi a Ambuye, kufunsa lamulo Lake. Ambuye adzalamula anayiwo kuti awononge gulu lalikulu la asilikali a Mughal ndikuteteza bhakta. Malinga ndi lamulo la Ambuye, anayiwo adzawononga asilikali a Mughal ndiyeno adzabwerera kwa Ambuye. Pa nthawiyo, bhakta osawerengeka adzakhala ndi Ambuye ku Chhatia, ndipo kwa masiku 27, Chhatia adzakhala malo okumanira a bhakta. Pa nthawiyi, mafumu a Russia, Nepal, Manipur, Ali, Kujang, Kanika, ndi zina zotero, adzakumana ndi Ambuye, kulandira Darshan Yake, ndikudzipereka pa mapazi Ake. Pa nthawiyo, Ambuye adzatenga onsewo ndikufika ku Sealdah kukapempha Ambuye Baladeva kuti awononge gulu lankhondo lakunja lotsala. Mwanjira imeneyi, mafumu onse omwe ali ndi Ambuye adzapita ku Sealdah ndikukumana ndi Baladeva, kumene Arjuna ndi Bhima adzakhalapo kale.

Apa Mahapurush akulemba kuti pa nthawiyi, pakati pa mafumu omwe adzakumana ndi Ambuye Ananta Keshari ndi Ambuye Baladeva, Mfumu ya Puri, Shrikshetra, adzakhalanso. Pofotokoza kuti adzakhala ndani, Mahapurush akulemba mu buku lake la Malika kuti:

Odia Sloka:

କ୍ଷେତ୍ରରେ ଯେ ରାଜା ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ତାହାଙ୍କ ସଙ୍ଗତେ ସ୍ଥିତି।

Kumasulira mawu:

Kṣhetre je rājā Dibyasiṃha Deba tāhāṅka saṅgate sthiti.

Tanthauzo:
Tanthauzo, pakati pa mafumu omwe adzalandira Darshan ya Ambuye adzakhala Mfumu ya Shrikshetra Puri, Divyasingha Deva.

Kuchokera ku zokambirana zomwe zili pamwambazi, zikuonekeranso kuti Mfumu ya Puri pa nthawi ya machitidwe aumulungu a Kalki adzakhala Shri Divyasingha Deva.

Kuchokera m'mavesi 17 omwe ali pamwambawa, zatsimikiziridwa bwino kuti kumapeto kwa Kaliyuga, pamene Ambuye Kalki atabadwa ndipo machitidwe Ake owononga akachitika padziko lonse lapansi, pamene kuwonongedwa kwa oipa, chipulumutso cha bhakta, ndi ntchito yokhazikitsa Dharma zikachitika, pa nthawiyo mfumu yotchedwa Divyasingha Deva adzakhala pa mpando wachifumu wa Jagannath Puri, ndipo adzakhalanso mfumu yomaliza kukhala pa udindowu.

Shri Divyasingha Deva adzakhala mfumu yomaliza—izi zinalembedwa ndi Mahapurush Jagannath Das, mmodzi wa Panchasakhas, mu buku lake la Malika, lomwe tatchula mu vvesi loyamba la chaputala chino.

Koma chinthu chofunika kudziwa ndi chakuti osati Jagannath Das yekha, komanso Mahapurush wina pakati pa Panchasakhas, Shishu Ananta Das, analembanso za nkhaniyi mu buku lake la Malika ‘Patta Madana’. Mahapurush akulemba kuti:

Odia Sloka:

ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ନୃପ ରାଜତ୍ୱ କରିବେ ଆଉ ଶାସନ ନ ଥିବ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Dibyasiṃha nṛupa rājattwa karibe āu śāsana na thiba.

Tanthauzo:
Tanthauzo, Mfumu Divyasingha Deva adzalamulira ndipo pambuyo pake, palibe mfumu ina yomwe idzalamulira. Divyasingha Deva adzakhala mfumu yomaliza. Mwa kuyankhula kwina, Kaliyuga idzatha pa nthawi yake, ndipo machitidwe owononga a Ambuye Kalki adzachitika.

6.2 Shri Divyasingha Deva

Mfundo yofunika kudziwa apa ndi yakuti Mahapurush sanangonena kuti dzina la Mfumu ya Puri kumapeto kwa Kaliyuga lidzakhala Divyasingha Deva; m'malo mwake, ananena zinthu zambiri za izi, monga kuti adzakhala mfumu ya 19, adzakhala Divyasingha Deva wachinayi, adzakhala wopanda mwana wamwamuna, adzakhala ndi ana aakazi okha, sadzakhala ku Puri koma ku Bhubaneswar, sadzatha kuchita ntchito za tsiku ndi tsiku za Ambuye Jagannath, adzakhala mfumu ali mwana ndikukhalabe mfumu mpaka ukalamba, ndi zina zotero.

Zonsezi zikugwirizana ndi Mfumu Shri Divyasingha Deva wamakono bwino kwambiri.

7 Zizindikiro za Kutha kwa Kaliyuga ku Kachisi wa Jagannath

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das ndi Panchasakhas ena alemba m'malo ambiri m'mabuku awo a Malika kuti bhakta adzalandira zizindikiro zambiri kuchokera ku Kachisi wa Jagannath zomwe adzadziwira kuti Kali Yuga yatha ndipo Ambuye Kalki abwera. Zizindikirozi zidzawoneka osati kuchokera ku Kachisi wa Jagannath kokha komanso kuchokera ku nyumba za amonke ndi akachisi ena ku India, koma makamaka adzawoneka kuchokera ku Kachisi wa Jagannath womwe uli ku Puri.

Odia Sloka:

ବାରମ୍ବାର ଶ୍ରୀ ମନ୍ଦିରରେ ପୁଣି,
ନାନା ଅଘଟଣ ସତ୍ୟ ମୋ ବାଣୀ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Bārambāra shrīmandirare puṇi,
nānā aghaṭana satya mo bāṇī.

Tanthauzo:
Mahapurush Achyutananda Das analemba mu buku lake la Malika kuti kumapeto kwa Kali Yuga, mitundu yosiyanasiyana ya zochitika zoipa (zizindikiro zoipa) idzawoneka mobwerezabwereza mu Shrimandir (Kachisi wa Jagannath) womwe uli ku Puri. Ananenanso kuti mawu anga awa adzatsimikizira kukhala owona kwathunthu.

7.1 Mbiri ya zizindikiro zoipa ku Kachisi wa Jagannath

Lero tikupeza kuti pazaka 15-20 zapitazi, zizindikiro zoipa zambiri zawoneka ku Kachisi wa Jagannath, monga mwala waukulu kugwa kuchokera ku "Amla Bedha" ya kachisi mu chaka cha 1990; miyala kugwa pansi kuchokera ku zigawo zapamwamba za kachisi, kuphatikizapo malo opatulika kwambiri, kangapo pazaka zaposachedwa; mbalame zoipa (zodya mitembo) zikukhala pa ndodo ya mbendera ya kachisi ndi Aruna Stambha kutsogolo kwa Singhadwara (Chipata cha Mkango); miluwati yosawerengeka ikuzungulira pamwamba pa kachisi; denga lokongoletsedwa ndi miyala yamtengo wapatali (Ratnachandua) lomwe lili pamwamba pa milungu likuyaka moto; 'Patitapabana Bana' (mbendera) yomwe imakwezedwa pamwamba pa kachisi ikuyaka moto; Neelachakra ya matani 2.2, yopangidwa ndi Ashtadhatu (zitsulo zisanu ndi zitatu), ikupindika chifukwa cha mphepo zamphamvu kwambiri; 'Patitapabana Bana' ikuwuluka ndikugwera m'nyanja pa nthawi ya mphepo yamkuntho yoopsa; nthambi ya mtengo waukulu wa Kalpavata (mtengo wa mkuyu) womwe uli m'malo a kachisi ikuthyoka; Patitapabana Bana ikuyaka chifukwa cha mphezi; masitovu a dothi pafupifupi 40 akuwonongedwa ndi zigawenga mu khitchini yopatulika ya kachisi; ming'alu ikupezeka mu Aruna Stambha ya kachisi, mtengo mkati mwa Natamandapa, holo yomwe ili kutsogolo kwa malo opatulika kwambiri (Jagamohana), ndi pafupi ndi Singhadwara; 'Padma Pakhuda' (mwala wa duwa la lotus) kusowa kuchokera ku Ratnasinghasana (mpando wachifumu wokongoletsedwa ndi miyala yamtengo wapatali); mphezi kugunda khitchini ya kachisi; zakuba zikuchitika mu kachisi usiku; kuwonjezeka kwa makoswe mu kachisi chifukwa cha kuchepa kwa bhakta pa nthawi ya mliri wa Corona, kubweretsa chisokonezo ndikusokoneza miyambo ya kachisi; madontho a magazi akupezeka m'malo ambiri kuphatikizapo Ratnasinghasana ya kachisi, kutsatiridwa ndi kusamba kwakukulu kwachikhalidwe (Mahasnana) kwa Chaturdha Murti (milungu inayi); mlengalenga kutembenuka kukhala wofiira kuzungulira kachisi; kusowa kwadzidzidzi kwa mileme yomwe inkawoneka yambiri mu kachisi kale; kugwiritsa ntchito mafoni ngakhale kuti aletsedwa mu kachisi; kujambula mavidiyo achinyengo m'malo a kachisi pogwiritsa ntchito makamera achinsinsi obisika m'magalasi; kulowa kwa anthu osakhala A-Hindu ndi osakhala A-Sanatani mu kachisi ngakhale kuti aletsedwa; kudzipha kapena imfa chifukwa cha zifukwa zina zikuchitika mkati mwa kachisi; imfa pa Baisi Pahacha, kutanthauza, masitepe 22 a kachisi; ming'alu ikupezeka mu Gumuta (denga) la Hasti Dwara (Chipata Chakumpoto) cha kachisi, ndi zina zotero.

Maulosi osiyanasiyana okhudza zina mwa zochitika izi amapezeka m'mabuku osiyanasiyana a Malika, momwe zikunenedwa bwino kuti zochitika izi zidzachitika kumapeto kwa Kali Yuga ndipo zidzapatsa bhakta chizindikiro cha kubwera kwa Ambuye Kalki padziko lapansi. Choncho, omwe akusonyeza kukayikira kwawo ndi kusakhulupirira kwawo lero ponena za kutha kwa Kali Yuga ndi kubwera kwa Ambuye Kalki ayenera kuganizirenso maganizo awo.




Mahapurush Achyutananda Das analemba mu 'Jaiphula Malika' yake kuti:

Odia Sloka:

ବଡ଼ ଦେଉଳୁଁ ପଥର,
ଖସୁଥିବ ପୁଣି ଥରକୁ ଥର ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ତୁହି ଦେଖିବୁ ବେନି ନେତ୍ରର।

Kumasulira mawu:

Baḍa deuluṃ pathara,
khasuthiba puṇi tharaku thara lo jāiphula,
tuhi dekhibu beni netrara.

Tanthauzo:
Mahapurush analemba kuti pa nthawi yotchulidwayo, zochitika za miyala kugwa mobwerezabwereza mu Kachisi wa Jagannath womwe uli ku Puri zidzawonekera, zomwe bhakta adzawona bwino ndi maso awo.

Ngati tigwirizana ndi chowonadi cha ulosi uwu, tidzapeza kuti pambuyo pa mwala wolemera matani 5-6 kugwa kuchokera ku "Amla Bedha" ya kachisi pa June 14, 1990, zochitika zoterezi zachitika kangapo. Pa August 13, 1992, miyala iwiri ikuluikulu inagwa molunjika mu malo opatulika kwambiri a kachisi. Mofananamo, pa October 26, 1998, mwala wolemera 15 kg unagubuduka kuchokera ku kachisi waukulu ndikugwera pafupi ndi kachisi wa Bimala mkati mwa malire a malo a kachisi waukulu. Pa November 1, 2011, mwala wolemera pafupifupi tani 1 unagwa kuchokera pafupi ndi Dadhinauti ya chipata chakumadzulo kwa Kachisi wa Jagannath.

Komanso mu chaka cha 2015, pa February 2, mwala wolemera 30-40 kg unagwa pansi kuchokera ku Jalakrida Mandapa yomangidwa pafupi ndi Kachisi wa Bimala kumadzulo kwa Kachisi wa Jagannath. Ponena za masiku aposachedwa, pa August 3, 2022, chidutswa cha pulasitala cholemera pafupifupi 1.5 kg chinagwa kuchokera padenga la malo opatulika kwambiri a kachisi womangidwa m'zaka za zana la khumi ndi ziwiri uno. Pa October 23, 2022, mwala unagwa kuchokera kum'mwera chakum'mawa kwa kachisi pafupi ndi Majana Mandapa ndi Charcha Ghara, ndipo patangotha sabata imodzi, pa October 28, 2022, mwala wolemera 2-3 kg unagwa kuchokera pamwamba pa chithunzi cha Sakhi pafupi ndi malo olumikizana a Natamandapa ndi Jagamohana. Ngakhale mu chaka cha 2024, pa March 9, mwala wolemera pafupifupi makilogalamu anayi unagwa kuchokera padenga la Kachisi wa Baraha womwe uli patsogolo pa Kachisi wa Bimala m'dera la Kachisi wa Jagannath.

Mwanjira imeneyi, ulosi woperekedwa zaka mazana ambiri zapitazo ndi Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wokhudza miyala yomwe ikugwa "tharaku thara" (mobwerezabwereza) kuchokera ku Kachisi wa Jagannath watsimikiziridwanso kukhala woona m'zaka zingapo zapitazi.

Odia Sloka:

ବଡ଼ ଦେଉଳରୁ ମୋର ପଥର ଖସିବ,
ସେହିଦିନଠାରୁ ସଖୀ ବିପଦ ପଡ଼ିବ।
ଅଅଁଳା ବେଢ଼ାରୁ ପଥର ଖସିବ ଯୁଗ ଶେଷ ହେବାପାଇଁ,
ଜାଣି ମୁଁ ଗରୁଡ଼ ଏ ଶକୁନମାନ ଗୁପତେ ଦେଲି ଚେତେଇ।
ଅଅଁଳା ବେଢ଼ାରୁ ଏକ ପଥର ଖସିବ,
ତେଇଶି ବରଷେ କଳି ଦେଶ ଛାଡ଼ି ଯିବ।
ଚୁଳରୁ ପଥର ଯେବେ ଖସିବ ସୁତ,
ଖସିଲେ ଅଅଁଳା ବେଢ଼ାରୁ ହେବ ଏ କଳି ହତ।
ମୋର ବଡ଼ ଦେଉଳରୁ ଖସିବ ପଥର,
ଶ୍ରୀକ୍ଷେତ୍ର ରାଜନ ମୋର ନ ସେବି ପୟର।
ବସିବ ଯେ ଗୃଧ୍ର ପକ୍ଷୀ ତୋ ଦେଉଳ ଚୁଳେ,
ଖସିବ ପଥର ଖଣ୍ଡ ପଡ଼ିବ ଯେ ତଳେ।
ବତାସରେ ହେ। ନୀଳଚକ୍ର ବକ୍ର ହେବ ବାରେ ହେ।
ବଡ଼ ଦେଉଳରୁ ମୋର ଖସିବ ପଥର,
ବସିବ ଯେ ଗୃଧ୍ର ପକ୍ଷୀ ଅରୁଣ ଖମ୍ବର।
ବତାସର ହେ। ବକ୍ର ହେବ ନୀଳଚକ୍ର ମୋର ହେ।
ତରକିବେ ତେରଜଣ ଜାଣିଥା ନିକର,
ବଡ଼ ଦେଉଳୁ ପଥର ଖସି ବାରମ୍ବାର।
ଗୃଧ୍ରପକ୍ଷୀ ମଥାପରେ ବସିଣ ଯେ ଯିବ,
ସେହିଦିନୁ ହତଶିରୀ ପୃଥ୍ବୀ ହୋଇବ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Baḍa deularu mora pathara khasiba,
sehidinaṭhāru sakhī bipada paḍiba.
Aaṃlā beḍhāru pathara khasiba yuga sheṣha hebāpāiṃ,
jāṇi mu Garuḍa e shakunamāna gupate deli chetei.
Aaṃlā beḍhāru eka pathara khasiba,
teishi baraṣhe kali desha chāḍi jiba.
Chularu pathara jebe khasiba suta,
khasile aaṃlā beḍhāru heba e kali hata.
Mora baḍa deularu khasiba pathara,
Shrīkṣhetra rājana mora na sebi payara.
Basiba je giddha pakṣhī to deula chule,
khasiba pathara khaṃḍa paḍiba je tale.
Batāsare he. Nīlachakra bakra heba bāre he.
Baḍa deularu mora khasiba pathara,
basiba je giddha pakṣhī Aruṇa khambara.
Batāsara he. Bakra heba Nīlachakra mora he.
Tarakibe terajaṇa jāṇithā nikara,
baḍa deulu pathara khasi bārambāra.
Giddhapakṣhī mathāpare basiṇa je jiba,
sehidinu hatashirī pṛithwī hoiba.

Tanthauzo:
Mahapurush Achyutananda Das wakambirana mwatsatanetsatane zizindikiro zambiri zoipa za kutha kwa Kali Yuga zomwe zapezeka kuchokera ku Kachisi wa Jagannath ku Puri m'mabuku ambiri a Malika kuphatikizapo 'Rukmini Gita', 'Garuda Samvada', 'Hari Arjuna Chautisha', 'Kaliyuga Gita', 'Jai Birachita Chautisha', ndi 'Chakada Madana'. M'nkhaniyi, iye akulemba kuti pamene mwala ugwa kuchokera ku "Amla Bedha" ya Kachisi wa Jagannath, okhulupirira odzipereka ayenera kumvetsa kuti kutha kwa Kali Yuga kwayandikira kwambiri ndipo masoka ndi mavuto ambiri adzagwera dziko lapansi posachedwa. Iye akunena kuti kugwa kwa mwala uwu kudzakhala chizindikiro chofunikira komanso chinsinsi cha kutha kwa Kali Yuga, ndipo patatha zaka 23 kuchokera pakugwa kwake, Kali Yuga idzatha. Iye akunenanso kuti panthawi yomwe yatchulidwayo, mbalame yoipa idzakhala pamwamba pa Kachisi wa Jagannath ndi Aruna Stambha yomwe ili patsogolo pa Singhadwara ya kachisi. Osati zokhazo, Neelachakra yolemera matani 2.2 yopangidwa ndi Ashtadhatu pamwamba pa kachisi idzapindika chifukwa cha mphepo yamphamvu.

Owerenga adzadabwa kudziwa kuti maulosi onse omwe atchulidwa pamwambapa olembedwa zaka mazana ambiri zapitazo akwaniritsidwa tsopano mu Kachisi wa Jagannath. Pakati pa mvula yamphamvu, choyamba, pa June 14, 1990, mwala wolemera matani 5-6 unagwa kuchokera ku "Amla Bedha" ya kachisi. Kenako mbalame yoipa inapezekanso itakhala pa Aruna Stambha.

N'zodabwitsa kuti palibe mbalame yomwe inawonekera ikuuluka pamwamba pa kachisi wa Jagannath Puri, ndipo asayansi ndi ofufuza analephera kufufuza chifukwa chake izi zinachitika. Koma m'nthawi yomaliza ya Kali Yuga ino, kutsimikizira maulosi a Malika kukhala oona, mbalame zoipa monga miluwati/zigololo zawoneka zikukhala pa Patitapabana Bana (ndodo ya mbendera) pamwamba pa kachisi nthawi zambiri. M'zaka zaposachedwapa, zomwezo zinachitika pa August 11, 2018, December 26, 2018, ndi July 23, 2020, pamene mbalame zoipa zinawoneka zikukhala pa ndodo ya mbendera ya kachisi. Pa December 7, 2021, mbalame zambiri zotere zinawoneka zikuzungulira m'gulu pamwamba pa Kachisi wa Jagannath. Chochitika chofananacho chinawoneka kachiwiri pa August 17, 2022. Mndandanda uwu sunayime apa; m'miyezi ya May, June, ndi September ya chaka cha 2023, anthu okhala ku Puri adawonanso chochitika chosasangalatsa ichi pamene mbalame zofananazo zinawoneka zikukhala pa ndodo ya mbendera yoyikidwa pamwamba pa kachisi.

Posachedwapa mu chaka cha 2025, mbalame inawoneka pamwamba pa Aruna Stambha pa September 4, pamene miluwati inawoneka ikukhala pamwamba pa kachisi pa September 2, September 16, ndi October 14. Pa December 9, mbalame zambiri zinapezeka zikuzungulira Shrimandir.

Kuphatikiza pa zonsezi, mphepo yamkuntho yoopsa kwambiri, 'Fani', yomwe inagunda gombe la Puri pa May 3, 2019, inapindika Neelachakra pamwamba pa kachisi, motero kukwaniritsa ulosi wina wolembedwa zaka mazana ambiri zapitazo m'mndandanda uwu.

N'zofunika kudziwa kuti maulosi awa olembedwa m'zaka za zana la 16 si zizindikiro chabe za kutha kwa Kali Yuga, komanso ndi zizindikiro zoperekedwa ndi Panchasakhas za kubwera kwa Kalki Avatar, chifukwa Kalki Avatar adzafika kokha kumapeto kwa Kali Yuga, monga zafotokozedwera m'malemba onse kuphatikizapo Bhavishya Malika.





Odia Sloka:

ନୀଳଚକ୍ର ଧ୍ବଜା ଖସିବ ପ୍ରଭୁ ରଙ୍ଗା ଅଧର,
ଯାଜନଗ୍ରେ ଜନମ ହୋଇବେ ବିଷ୍ଣୁ ଶର୍ମା ଗୃହର।
ହର ଉଦ୍ୟାନରେ ମାୟା ପ୍ରକାଶିବ ମ୍ଳେଚ୍ଛକୁ ଗ୍ରାସୁଥିବେ,
ନୀଳଚକ୍ର ବାନା ଝଡ଼ିବ ନିଶ୍ଚୟ ଭକତଙ୍କୁ ଲୋଡ଼ିଯିବେ।
ନୀଳଚକ୍ର ପରୁ ପତାକା ଛିଡ଼ିଣ ପଡ଼ିବ ସମୁଦ୍ର କୂଳେ,
କଳକୀ ରୂପକୁ ଚିନ୍ତା ପୁଣ ହେବ ଭାବିବେ କ୍ଷୀରାଧି କୂଳେ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Nīlachakra dhwajā khasiba prabhu raṃgā adhara,
Jājanagre janma hoibe Biṣhṇu Sharmā gṛihara.
Hara udyānare māyā prakāshiba mlechchaku grāsuthibe,
Nīlachakra bānā jhaḍiba nishchaya bhakataṃku loḍijibe.
Nīlachakra paru patākā chiḍiṇa paḍiba samudra kūle,
Kalki rūpaku chiṃtā puṇa heba bhābibe kṣhīrādhi kūle.

Tanthauzo:
Mahapurush Achyutananda Das adatchula chizindikiro china cha kutha kwa Kali Yuga ndi kubadwa kwa Ambuye Kalki m'mabuku ake a Malika 'Shibakalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta' ndi 'Kalikalpa Gita'. Iye analemba kuti nthawi idzafika pamene Patitapabana Bana yomwe ili pamwamba pa Neelachakra ya Shrimandir idzauluka kuchoka pamalo pake. Mahapurush analembanso kuti mbendera idzauluka ndikugwera m'nyanja.

Ulosi uwu woperekedwa ndi Mahapurush Achyutananda Das unatsimikiziridwanso kukhala woona. Tsiku limodzi mphepo yamkuntho yoopsa kwambiri 'Fani' isanagunde gombe la Puri, pa May 2, 2019, chifukwa cha mphepo yamphamvu, Patitapabana Bana inawuluka ndikugwera m'nyanja. Ndendende chochitika chofananacho chinachitikanso pa May 18, 2020, masiku awiri mphepo yamkuntho 'Amphan' isanagunde gombe la Odisha.



Odia Sloka:

ନୀଳାଚଳ ଛାଡ଼ି ଆମ୍ଭେ ଯିବୁ ଯେତେବେଳେ,
ଲାଗିବ ରତ୍ନଚାନ୍ଦୁଆ ଅଗ୍ନି ସେତେବେଳେ।
ନିଶାକାଳେ ହେ। ନୀଳାଚଳୁ ଚୋରିଯିବ ହେଲେ ହେ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Nīlāchala chāḍi āmbhe jibu jetebele,
lāgiba ratnachāṃduā agni setebele.
Nishākāle he. Nīlāchalu chorijiba hele he.

Tanthauzo:
Pogwira mawu a Ambuye, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das analemba m'buku lake la Malika 'Jai Birachita Chautisha' kuti pamene Ambuye Jagannath adzachoka ku Nilachala, ndiko kuti, Shrimandir, kuti adzabadwe m'thupi la munthu, panthawiyo denga lokongoletsedwa ndi miyala yamtengo wapatali lomwe lili pamwamba pa Mulungu atatu oyera mu Kachisi wa Jagannath lidzagwira moto.

N'zodabwitsa kuti chochitika cha Ratnachandua kugwira moto chinachitika mu chaka cha 2004-05, ndipo motero ulosi uwu wa Mahapurush watsimikiziridwanso kukhala woona.

Odia Sloka:

କଳ୍ପବଟ ଡ଼ାଳ ଛେଦନ ଭୋଇବ ଦେଖିବୁ ତୁ ନୟନରେ,
ଶିବକଳ୍ପ ବାଣୀ ଅନ୍ୟଥା ନୋହିବ ଶୁଣ ରାମଦାସ ତୁରେ।
ଏକ ଦକ୍ଷିଣରେ ନବ ଯେ ଥିବ,
କଳ୍ପବଟ ମୋର ଛେଦନ ହୋଇବ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Kalpabaṭa ḍāla chedana hoiba dekhibu tu nayanare,
Shibakalpa bāṇī anyathā nohiba shuṇa Rāmadāsa ture.
Eka dakṣhiṇare naba je thiba,
kalpabaṭa mora chedana hoiba.

Tanthauzo:
Mahapurush Achyutananda Das analemba m'buku lake la Malika 'Shibakalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta' zaka 600 zapitazo kuti nthawi idzafika pamene nthambi ya Kalpavata yomwe ili m'dera la Kachisi wa Jagannath idzathyoka. Iye analembanso m'buku lina lake kuti pamene pali 'naini' pafupi ndi 'imodzi', ndiko kuti, nambala 19, pokhapo chochitika ichi chidzachitika.

Ndendende molingana ndi mawu a Mahapurush, ulosi uwu unakwaniritsidwa mu chaka cha 2019 pamene mphepo yamkuntho yoopsa kwambiri 'Fani', yomwe inagunda gombe la Puri pa May 3, inawononga Kalpavata wamkulu, wakale kwambiri womwe uli m'dera la kachisi.

Odia Sloka:

ରକ୍ତଛିଟା ପଡ଼ିବ ଯେ ରତ୍ନସିଂହାସନେ,
ଏହି ଘଟଣା ଘଟିବ ମନେ ରଖ ଦିନେ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Raktachīṃṭā paḍiba je ratnasiṃhāsane,
ehi ghaṭaṇā ghaṭiba mane rakha dine.

Tanthauzo:
Mwa zizindikiro zambiri zomwe Mahapurush adatchula kuti zidzapezeka kuchokera ku Kachisi wa Jagannath ku Puri kumapeto kwa Kali Yuga, chimodzi chinali kuwona madontho a magazi pa Ratnasinghasana ya mafano aumulungu.

Ulosi uwu wolembedwa ndi Mahapurush unatsimikiziridwanso kukhala woona. M'zaka zingapo zapitazi, madontho a magazi agwera m'malo ambiri mu kachisi, kuphatikizapo Ratnasinghasana, pambuyo pake, malinga ndi miyambo ya kachisi, Chaturdha Murti anayeretsedwa kudzera mu mwambo wa Mahasnana (kusamba kwakukulu). Mwachitsanzo, pa March 6, 2021, magazi anawoneka pafupi ndi 'Bhitar Katha' yomwe ili patali pang'ono ndi malo opatulika kwambiri a kachisi. Pa April 18, 2021, chifukwa cha mkangano pakati pa apolisi awiri omwe anatumizidwa kuti ateteze kachisi, madontho a magazi anagwa m'dera la kachisi. Pa March 7, 2022, panthawi ya 'Abakasha Niti' (mwambo), madontho a magazi anapezeka pafupi ndi 'Bhitar Katha' mkati mwa Jagamohana ya kachisi (holo yomwe ili patsogolo pa malo opatulika kwambiri). Kenako pa April 11, 2022, madontho a magazi anawoneka pafupi ndi Dhukudi Dwara ya kachisi. Pa November 3, 2022, pambuyo pa miyambo ya 'Mangala Alati', 'Mailama', ndi 'Tadapa Lagi', madontho a magazi anawoneka pafupi ndi 'Bhitar Katha'. Chochitika chotere chinawoneka kachiwiri mu sabata yachitatu ya January mu chaka cha 2023. Pambuyo pa kutha kwa miyambo ya 'Abakasha' ndi 'Besa Lagi' ya Ambuye Jagannath, madontho a magazi anawoneka mu Shrimandir, chifukwa chake chopereka (Bhoga) kwa Gopalaballabha sichinatheke. Kenako pa June 4, 2023, pambuyo pa kusamba kwaumulungu (Devasnana) kutatha, madontho a magazi anawoneka pa Snana Mandapa. Ngakhale posachedwapa, pa October 13, 2023, chifukwa cha mkangano waukali pakati pa atumiki awiri wokhudza kugawa Prasad nthawi yomweyo pambuyo pa 'Madhyahna Dhupa' mu Shrimandir, madontho a magazi anagwa mu malo opatulika kwambiri a kachisi. Mu chaka cha 2024, m'mawa kwambiri wa April 23, pambuyo potsegulidwa kwa Singhadwara, mayi wochokera ku banja la mtumiki anayika dzanja lake pa chitseko cha kachisi pamene chala chake chinaphwanyidwa mmenemo. Zotsatira zake, magazi anatuluka kuchokera ku chala chake pafupi ndi Dhukudi Dwara pafupi ndi Bedha yamkati. M'mikhalidwe yotere, Mahasnana ya Ambuye Wamkulu idachitika ngakhale Mangala Alati isanachitike. Mofananamo, zochitika za kutuluka magazi m'dera la kachisi zinapitirira mu chaka cha 2025.

7.2 Kugwa kwa fano la Ambuye Balabhadra panthawi ya Rath Yatra 2024.

Panthawi ya Rath Yatra ya masiku 11 ku Shri Jagannath Puri, miyambo ya 'Adapa Mandapa Bije' inayamba nthawi ya 7 PM madzulo a July 9, 2024. Panthawiyi, pamene ankatenga fano la Ambuye Balabhadra kuchokera pa ngolo kupita ku Adapa Mandapa ya Kachisi wa Gundicha, Iye anagwa chafufumimba pa Charamala, chifukwa chake atumiki 12 anavulalanso. Chochitika chosasangalatsa chotere chinachitika koyamba m'mbiri. Kugwa kwa Ambuye Balabhadra mwanjira imeneyi ndi chizindikiro chenicheni cha masoka omwe akubwera, omwe akutchulidwa motere m'buku la 'Niladri' lokhudzana ndi malamulo ndi ndondomeko za Shrikhetra, monga momwe adalamulira Ambuye Brahma ndi Ambuye Vishnu.

Odia Sloka:

ମୁରବୈରିଣ ଏତସ୍ୟ ଜଗଦୀଶସ୍ୟ ଭୂପତେହଃ।
ଭଦ୍ରାୟାଶ୍ଚ ବଳସ୍ୟାପି ପ୍ରମାଦାତ୍ ପତନଂ ଯଦି।।
ମହାଭୟଂ ନୃପସ୍ୟାପି ରାଜ୍ୟସ୍ୟାପି ଭବେଦ୍ଧ୍ରୁବମ୍।
ଦୁର୍ଭିକ୍ଷଂ ମରଣଂ ଚୈବ ଜାୟତେ ଚ ପୁନଃ ପୁନଃ।।

Kumasulira mawu:

Murabairiṇa etasya jagadīshasya bhūpatehaḥ.
Bhadrāyāshcha balasyāpi pramādāt patanaṃ yadi.
Mahābhayaṃ nṛipasyāpi rājyasyāpi bhabeddhrubam.
Durbhikṣhaṃ maraṇaṃ chaiba jāyate cha punaḥ punaḥ.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, "O Mfumu, ngati mosadziwa Jagannath, Balabhadra, kapena Subhadra agwa pansi, ndiye kuti tsoka lalikulu lidzagwera mfumu ndi dziko. Njala idzachitika, ndipo miliri idzagunda mobwerezabwereza."

Moto mu malo opatulika kwambiri a Kachisi wa Mahakal, Ujjain mu chaka cha 2024

M'mawa wa Lolemba, March 25, 2024, panthawi ya chikondwerero cha Holi, moto unayaka mu malo opatulika kwambiri a Kachisi wa Mahakaleshwar ku Ujjain chifukwa chotaya 'gulal' (ufa wamitundu) panthawi ya Bhasma Aarti. Anthu pafupifupi 14, kuphatikizapo wansembe wamkulu ndi atumiki ena, anavulala ndi moto pa chochitika ichi.

7.3 Zizindikiro zazikulu zoipa zopezeka kuchokera ku Kachisi wa Jagannath mu chaka cha 2025

Zizindikiro zoipa zopezeka kuchokera ku Kachisi wa Jagannath zinapitirira mu chaka cha 2025. Kudzera mu zizindikiro zambiri, Ambuye Jagannath anapatsa okhulupirira ake chizindikiro cha masoka omwe akubwera, zina mwa zizindikiro zazikulu zomwe tikukupatsani.

(a) Kuthyoka kwa Akshaya Danda pa February 14

Monga tsiku lililonse, masana a February 14, 2025 (Lachisanu), pamene mtumiki wa Chunara anafika ku Neelachakra pamwamba pa kachisi kuti asinthe Patitapabana Bana kapena mbendera yayikulu, anapeza Akshaya Danda (ndodo ya nsungwi yomwe imagwira Patitapabana Bana) itathyoka. Pambuyo pake, atumiki anangomanga mbendera ku Neelachakra yokha.

Kuthyoka kwa Akshaya Danda mwanjira imeneyi kunapweteka maganizo a okhulupirira odzipereka ambiri, ndipo anasonyeza chisoni chachikulu pa izi. Pamodzi ndi izi, okhulupirira odzipereka ambiri anachitcha chizindikiro cha masoka omwe akubwera posachedwa.

(b) Madontho a magazi anapezeka mu kachisi pa February 16; Mahasnana ya milungu idachitika

Pa February 16 (Lamlungu), pambuyo pa kumaliza kwa miyambo ya m'mawa monga 'Dwara Phita Niti' ndi 'Mangala Alati', pomwe Darshan wa Ambuye Jagannath anayamba, patapita nthawi pang'ono, atumiki anapeza madontho a magazi pafupi ndi dera la 'Bhitar Katha'. Nthawi yomweyo pambuyo pa chochitikacho, makonzedwe anapangidwa a 'Bada Mahasnana Niti' wa milungu, ndipo Darshan anaimitsidwanso kwa kanthawi.

(c) Kalulu akuuluka ndi Patitapabana Bana pa April 13

Pa April 13, 2025, okhulupirira a Ambuye Jagannath omwe ali ku Puri Shrimandir anawona chizindikiro chosayembekezereka. Cha m'ma 5 PM, kalulu anawoneka akuzungulira kachisi atanyamula Patitapabana Bana m'kamwa mwake. Malinga ndi malipoti, kaluluyo anazungulira kachisi pafupifupi ka 4-5 kenako anawulukira kunyanja kudutsa pa chipata chakumadzulo.

Kanema wa chochitikachi anafalikira kwambiri pa malo ochezera a pa intaneti. Okhulupirira ambiri anasonyeza chisoni ndi nkhawa za izi. Okhulupirira ambiri anachilumikiza ndi masoka omwe akubwera, ndipo ena anachitcha chizindikiro choyipa chosayembekezereka choperekedwa ndi Ambuye Jagannath.

(d) Madontho a magazi anapezeka mu kachisi pa April 21 ndipo Mahasnana wa milungu anachitika

Pa April 21 (Lolemba) nayenso, madontho a magazi anawoneka pafupi ndi Bhitar Katha mu Shrimandir, pambuyo pake Mahasnana wa mafano a Ambuye anachitika moyenera malinga ndi miyambo. Pakadali pano, Darshan wa Ambuye Wamkulu kwa okhulupirira anasokonezedwa kwa pafupifupi ola limodzi. Malinga ndi malipoti, madontho a magazi anapezeka mkati mwa kachisi chifukwa cha zidutswa za galasi zolasa phazi la wapolisi.

(e) Kalulu atakhala pamwamba pa Patitapabana Bana pa September 2

Kalulu atakhala pa Patitapabana Bana pamwamba pa Shrimandir wawoneka kangapo m'zaka zingapo zapitazi. Nthawi iliyonse chochitika chotere chikawoneka, chimawonetsedwa pafupifupi pa ma TV onse akumaloko ku Odisha. Pa September 2, zomwezo zinachitikanso pamene kalulu anawoneka akuyuluka pafupi ndi Neelachakra ndipo patapita nthawi anapezeka atakhala pa Patitapabana Bana omwe ali pamwamba pa Neelachakra.

(f) Kalulu atakhala pa Aruna Stambha kachiwiri pa September 4

Osati Kachisi wa Jagannath wokha, komanso Bhavishya Malika akufotokoza za kukhala kwa kalulu kapena mbalame ina iliyonse yoyipa pa Aruna Stambha yomwe ili kutsogolo kwa Singhadwara ya kachisi ngati chizindikiro choyipa. Pa September 4, ndiko kuti, patangopita masiku awiri kuchokera pamene kalulu anawoneka pamwamba pa Shrimandir pa September 2, kalulu anawonekeranso atakhala pa Aruna Stambha. Anthu anawonanso izi ngati chizindikiro choyipa. Kudzera mu chochitikachi, ambiri anafotokoza kuti ulosi wa Malika wolembedwa zaka 600 zapitazo ukukwaniritsidwa.

(g) Kalulu atakhala pamwamba pa kachisi kachiwiri pa September 16

Patangopita masiku 15 kuchokera pa September 2, m'mawa kwambiri pa September 16, kalulu anawonekeranso atakhala pamwamba pa Patitapabana Bana wa kachisi.

(h) Kalulu anapezeka atakhala pa Patitapabana Bana wa Shrimandir kachiwiri pa October 14

Kalulu anapezeka atakhala pamwamba pa kachisi kawiri mu mwezi wa September. Chochitikachi sichinathe apa; anthu okhala ku Puri anawonanso kubwereza kwa chochitika ichi pa October 14. Kukhala mobwerezabwereza kwa kalulu pamwamba pa kachisi kunafotokozedwa ndi okhulupirira ambiri ngati chizindikiro cha masoka omwe akubwera.

(i) Akalulu osawerengeka akuzungulira pamwamba pa Shrimandir pa December 9

Monga pa December 7, 2021, ndi August 17, 2022, chizindikiro choyipa chinawonekeranso pa December 9, 2025, pamene akalulu osawerengeka anapezeka akuzungulira pamwamba pa Kachisi wa Jagannath. Chochitikachi chinakopa chidwi osati cha okhulupirira ku Puri kapena Odisha okha, komanso a omwe amakhala kunja kwa Odisha. Okhulupirira ambiri anatcha chochitika ichi chizindikiro cha chochitika chachikulu choyipa chomwe chikubwera posachedwa.

7.4 Zizindikiro zoyipa zolandiridwa kuchokera ku nyumba za amonke ndi akachisi ena ofunika mu chaka cha 2025

Moto wayaka m'dera la Kachisi wa Mahakaleshwar ku Ujjain

Pa May 5, 2025, nthawi ya 12 PM, moto unayaka mu chipinda chowongolera cha bungwe lowongolera kuipitsa chilengedwe chomwe chili pafupi ndi Shankh Dwara m'dera la Kachisi wotchuka padziko lonse wa Baba Mahakaleshwar ku Ujjain. Pambuyo pa chochitikachi, mantha anayamba m'dera la kachisi. Moto utayaka, kulowa kwa okhulupirira mu kachisi kunaimitsidwa kwa kanthawi.

Moto wayaka m'dera la Peetha la Mahapurush Achyutananda Das

Pa tsiku la Devasnana Purnima, June 11, 2025, moto umene unayaka m'dera la Nemal Padmabana Peetha la Mahapurush Achyutananda Das lomwe lili ku Nischintakoili, Cuttack unawonekeranso ngati chizindikiro choyipa kwambiri ndi okhulupirira a Malika. Mpweya wa mantha unakula pakati pa gulu la zikwi za okhulupirira pamene choyikapo nyali chomwe chinali m'deralo chinayaka moto mwadzidzidzi, ndipo patangopita mphindi zochepa, malawi a moto anayamba kutuluka.

Mtengo wa Nemal banyan wadulidwa ndi boma

Kumayambiriro kwa August, matumba ambiri a mtengo wakale wa banyan womwe uli kutsogolo kwa kachisi womwe uli ku Sadhana Peetha ya wolemba wamkulu wa Malika, woyera mtima wamkulu Shri Achyutananda Das, anadulidwa. Akuti, boma linatenga sitepe yotereyi chifukwa cha ntchito yachitukuko ya Achyutananda Das Peetha. Panali kutsutsidwa kwakukulu kwa izi ndi anthu akumaloko ndi ansembe, ndipo pa zionetsero zawo, nthambi zochepa zokha za mtengo wa banyan zinasiyidwa osadulidwa. Ndikoyenera kudziwa kuti Mahapurush Achyutananda Das analemba Malika ali pansi pa 'Nemal Banyan Tree' ali mu chikhalidwe cha kusinkhasinkha. Kudulidwa kwa mtengo wa Nemal banyan kunawoneka ngati chizindikiro choyipa kwambiri ndi okhulupirira a Malika.

Mbendera yagolide yoyikidwa pamwamba pa Kachisi wa Mahakaleshwar yagwa

Pa June 1, 2025, chizindikiro choyipa chinawonekeranso kuchokera ku Kachisi wotchuka padziko lonse wa Mahakaleshwar womwe uli ku Ujjain. Kwenikweni, chifukwa cha mphepo yamphamvu, mbendera yagolide yoyikidwa pamwamba pa Kachisi wa Mahakaleshwar inagwa mwadzidzidzi. Wansembe wa kachisi anafotokoza chochitikachi ngati nkhani yodetsa nkhawa kuchokera ku malingaliro achipembedzo.

Chigoba cha Ambuye Shiva chopangidwa pa Shivalinga mu Kachisi wa Mahakaleshwar chathyoka ndikugwa

Chochitika chapadera chinachitika pa August 18 ku Kachisi wa Mahakal womwe uli ku Ujjain. Apa, monga tsiku lililonse nthawi ya 8:00 PM pa nthawi yokongoletsa (Shringara) ya Shivalinga, chigoba cha Ambuye Shiva chinapangidwa ndi Bhang (phala la chamba). Bhang wambiri anagwiritsidwa ntchito pa izi. Nthawi yomweyo pambuyo pa izi, Aarti anali pafupi kuyamba pamene Bhang Shringara anathyoka ndikugwa. Mphuno, milomo, ndi diso limodzi zinathyoka pa chigoba cha Ambuye Shiva ndikugwa.

Chithunzi chapadera chawoneka mu Kachisi wa Maa Samaleswari

Kuchokera ku kachisi wotchuka wa Maa Samaleswari womwe uli ku Lakhanpur, Jharsuguda, Odisha, usiku wa Lachinayi, August 21, 2025, okhulupirira analandira chizindikiro cha Mulungu wamkazi kusiya fano Lake ndikutuluka. Wansembe atatsegula zitseko za kachisi m'mawa kwambiri kuti achite 'Mangala Alati', mapazi opita kunja, opaka ndi Alta (utoto wofiira), a Mulungu wamkazi anawoneka, ndipo pamodzi ndi izi, lupanga Lake (Khanda) ndi mphanda (Trishul), zomwe zinali zolumikizidwa ku fano la Mulungu wamkazi, zinapezeka zitagwa pambali.

Malinga ndi 'Bhavishya Malika', mu gawo lomaliza la Kali Yuga, isanakhazikitsidwe Dharma, milungu yonse idzasiya malo awo ndikutuluka kuti ichite ntchito yowononga oipa ndi ochimwa. Chochitikachi ndi umboni wakuti nthawi imeneyo yafika ndipo ulosi umenewo ukukwaniritsidwa.

Moto wayaka m'mafano opatulika mu Kachisi wa Brahmagiri Jagannath

Pa Ogasiti 28, 2025, mafano a Ambuye Jagannath, m'bale wamkulu Balabhadra, ndi amayi Subhadra anayaka moto mu Kachisi wa Brahmagiri Jagannath womwe uli m'mudzi wa Kandagoda m'chigawo cha Puri ku Odisha. Kwenikweni, atamaliza kupembedza kwamadzulo, ansembe anapita kunyumba kwawo. Nthawi ya 8:30 PM, chifukwa cha utsi umene unkatuluka mu kachisi, pamene chitseko cha kachisi chinathyoka, zinaoneka kuti mafano mkati anali kuyaka moto. Atakambirana ndi Muktimandapa, Shaligrama anapembedzedwa kuyambira tsiku limenelo. Potsatira kusonkhanitsa Daru (nkhuni zopatulika) mu Nabakalebara yomwe ikubwera, mafano atsopopano anaikidwa.

Chizindikiro cha chiwonongeko chomwe chikubwera chilandiridwa kuchokera ku Kachisi wa Lingaraj, mtengo woyimira mbendera ya kachisi wathyoka

Lolemba, Seputembala 15, 2025, chifukwa cha ukali wa mvula yamphamvu ndi mphepo, mtengo woyimira mbendera wa Kachisi wa Lingaraj womangidwa m'zaka za zana la 11 womwe uli ku Bhubaneswar unathyoka. Lachiwiri m'mawa, atawona ndi atumiki, mbendera inakwezedwa pa mtengo watsopano.

Mbendera yomwe ili pamwamba pa Kachisi wa Lingaraj ku Bhubaneswar imang'ambika chifukwa cha mvula yamphamvu ndi mphepo yamkuntho. Malo opatulika kwambiri agwidwa ndi mphezi; mdima wagubuduzira kachisi.

Pakati pa mvula yamphamvu ndi mphepo yamkuntho yoopsa Lachitatu, Okutobala 8, 2025, Kachisi wa Lingaraj womwe uli ku likulu la Odisha, Bhubaneswar, adakhudzidwa ndi mphezi. Ichi chinalinso chizindikiro cha chochitika chosayenera. Mbendera ya kachisi inang'ambika chifukwa cha mphepo yamkuntho, pamene makamera a CCTV a kachisi ndi dongosolo la kuyatsa zidawonongeka ndi mphezi. Zinanenedwa kuti okhulupirira ena ndi atumiki omwe anali mu kachisi adagwidwanso ndi magetsi.

(j) Kwa nthawi yoyamba m'mbiri yake yazaka 500, Bal Bhog sanaperekedwe mu Kachisi wa Banke Bihari ku Vrindavan

Thakur Banke Bihari, yemwe adawonekera zaka mazana asanu zapitazo, amapatsidwa Bhog (chakudya choperekedwa) m'magawo anayi a tsiku. Asanakhazikitse mpando wa Darshan wam'mawa, Bal Bhog amaperekedwa. Kachisi asanatseke masana, Raj Bhog amaperekedwa, ndipo zitseko zikatsegulidwa madzulo, Utthapan Bhog amaperekedwa. Zitseko zikatseka, Shayan Bhog amaperekedwa. Mgwirizano wokonza Bhog umaperekedwa kwa wopanga maswiti (Halwai). Wopanga maswiti amakonza Bhog ndikupereka kwa atumiki, ndipo iwo amapereka Bhog.

Koma pa Disembala 15 nthawi ya 8:45 AM, zitseko za kachisi zinatsegulidwa, koma wopanga maswiti sanafike ndi Bal Bhog. Atafunsidwa, zinapezeka kuti wopanga maswiti sanafike chifukwa chosalipidwa kwa ndalama za mwezi uliwonse. Kwa nthawi yoyamba m'mbiri yake yazaka 500, chinthu chotere chinachitika pamene Bal Bhog sakanaperekedwa kwa Banke Bihari Ji tsiku limenelo.

7.5 Kukonza kwa Rath Yatra pa nthawi yosayenera

Monga tonse tikudziwa, Rath Yatra yakhala ikukonzedwa chaka chilichonse pa Dwitiya Tithi ya Shukla Paksha ya mwezi wa Ashadha. Mwambo umenewu wakhala ukutsatiridwa nthawi zonse m'makhachisi ena a Jagannath ku India ndi kunja, kuphatikizapo Kachisi wa Jagannath ku Puri. Koma m'zaka zingapo zapitazi, pakhala pali nthawi zambiri pamene ISKCON yapezeka ikuswa mwambo umenewu.

Pankhaniyi, pambuyo poti zotsutsa zidatulutsidwa ndi Mkulu wa Oyang'anira a Shree Jagannath Temple Administration ndi Wapampando wa Shree Jagannath Temple Managing Committee, Gajapati Maharaj Divyasingha Deva, ndipo madandaulo olembedwa adaperekedwa ku likulu la ISKCON lomwe lili ku Mayapur, kusintha kwina kunachitikadi, koma ISKCON ikadali ikuoneka ikukonza ma Rath Yatra osayenera nthawi yake ku India komanso makamaka kunja.

Motsatira izi, ISKCON posachedwapa idakonza Rath Yatra ku Taiwan pa Novembala 23, 2025, yomwe idatsutsidwa ndi anthu a Odia padziko lonse lapansi komanso okhulupirira ambiri a Jagannath ku Odisha. Okhulupirira adawona kuti sikungoswa malamulo achikhalidwe komanso adawona ngati kunyalanyaza lamulo la Ambuye Jagannath Mwiniwake.


7.6 Kulakwitsa mu kalendala ya Chingerezi ya 2026 yosindikizidwa ndi Shrimandir Administration

Posachedwapa, kulakwitsa kwakukulu kwawonekera mu kalendala ya Chingerezi ya 2026 yoperekedwa ndi Jagannath Temple Administration. Kwenikweni, chithunzi cha Ratnabedi chinasindikizidwa mu kalendala momwe dongosolo la Utatu silili lolondola. Kuchokera pa malingaliro a wowonera, mu chithunzi cha kalendala, Ambuye Jagannath waikidwa kumanzere kwambiri, kenako Mulungu Subhadra, ndiyeno m'bale wamkulu Balabhadra, pamene mu dongosolo lolondola, Ambuye Balabhadra ayenera kukhala kumanzere kwambiri, kenako Mulungu Subhadra, ndiyeno Shri Jagannath. Ndiko kuti, kulakwitsa ndikuti mu kalendala, chithunzi cha m'bale wamkulu Balabhadra chinasindikizidwa m'malo mwa Ambuye Jagannath, ndipo chithunzi cha Ambuye Jagannath chili m'malo mwa Ambuye Balabhadra. Kuwonjezera pa izi, Chakraraj Sudarshan sanapatsidwe malo mu chithunzi cha Ratnasinghasana mu kalendala. Osati izi zokha, chithunzi cha Rath Yatra chinasindikizidwanso mu kalendala, chomwe chikuwonetsa ngolo ya Amayi Subhadra choyamba, kutsatiridwa ndi ngolo ya Ambuye Wamkulu Jagannath, ndipo potsiriza ngolo ya Ambuye Balabhadra.

Chifukwa cha zolakwika izi, pali kusakhutira ndi mkwiyo m'maganizo mwa okhulupirira. Palinso mkwiyo pakati pa atumiki a Kachisi wa Jagannath pankhaniyi. Malinga ndi okhulupirira ena, ichi ndi chizindikiro choperekedwa ndi Ambuye Jagannath Mwiniwake cha masoka omwe akubwera komanso kusintha kwa dongosolo la dziko m'chaka chatsopano.

8 Kubadwa kwa Ambuye Kalki – Liti ndi Kuti?

Malinga ndi Bhavishya Malika, Ambuye Kalki anabadwa kale. Osati Ambuye Kalki okha, koma ankhondo onse amphamvu a nthawi ya Mahabharata monga Yudhishthira, Bhima, Arjuna, Nakula, Sahadeva, Bhishma, Drona, Ashwatthama, Karna, Shalya, Jayadratha, Barbarika, Abhimanyu, Babruvahana, Ekalavya, Ghatotkacha, Duryodhana, Shakuni, ndi ena, onse anabadwanso padziko lapansi lino. Malinga ndi Malika, nthawi ino ankhondo onsewa adzamenyana pamodzi kumbali ya Dharma motsutsana ndi Adharma.

Komabe, sitidzakambirana za ankhondo ena pano ndipo tidzangochepetsa mawu athu kwa Parahbrahma Narayana Mahavishnu Ambuye Kalki Ram.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das analemba mu buku lake la Malika ‘Shibakalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta’ –

Odia Sloka:

ନୀଳଚକ୍ର ଧ୍ବଜା ଖସିବ ପ୍ରଭୁ ରଙ୍ଗା ଅଧର,
ଯୋଜନଗ୍ରେ ଜନମ ହୋଇବେ ବିଷ୍ଣୁଶର୍ମା ଗୃହର।

Kumasulira mawu:

Nīḷachakra dhwajā khasiba prabhu raṅgā adhara,
yojanagre janama hoibe Biṣṇusharmā gṛuhara.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, pamene Patitapabana Bana (mbendera) ya Kachisi wa Puri Jagannath iuluka kuchokera pamalo pake n'kupita kwina, pa nthawi yomweyo, Mahaprabhu Kalki adzabadwa ku Jajanagar kapena Jajpur ku Odisha m'nyumba ya Brahmin yemwe amayimba matamando a Ambuye Vishnu.

Ndikuyenera kufotokozera owerenga kuti chochitika ichi chawonedwa nthawi zambiri kuchokera ku Kachisi wa Jagannath, mwachitsanzo m'chaka cha 1990-91, m'chaka cha 2005, kenako pa Cyclone Fani ndi Cyclone Amphan m'zaka za 2019 ndi 2020 motsatana, ndi zina zotero.

Mahapurush analemba mu buku lake la Malika kuti kubadwa kwa Mahaprabhu Kalki kudzachitika kokha pambuyo pa chochitika ichi. Popeza chochitika ichi chachitika, zikhoza kunenedwa motsimikiza kuti Ambuye Kalki anabadwa. Koma popeza chochitika ichi chachitika m'zaka zosiyanasiyana, sizikuwoneka kuti n'kotheka kufika pa chaka chenicheni cha kubadwa kwa Mahaprabhu kuchokera pa chochitika ichi chokha.

Kuti tidziwe chaka chomwe Mahaprabhu anabadwira, tidzayenera kuthandizidwa ndi mavesi ena a Malika.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akulemba mu buku lake la Malika ‘Tera Janma Sharana’ –

Odia Sloka:

ମାଘ ସପ୍ତମୀରେ ବିକାଶ ହୋଇବ ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ନୃପବର,
ଅନନ୍ତ କେଶରୀ ରାଜତ୍ୱ କରିବେ ଏହି ଏକାମ୍ର ନଗର।
କେଉଁ ଦିନ ତୁମ୍ଭେ ପ୍ରକାଶ ହୋଇବ କହେ ରାମଦାସ ଯହିଁ,
ବିଶାଖାପାଟଣା ନୃପ ନାଶ ହେଲେ ମୁହିଁ ବିକାଶ ହୁଅଇ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Māgha saptamīre bikāsha hoiba Dibyasiṃha nṛpabara,
Ananta Keshari rājatwa karibe ehi Ekāmra nagara.
Keuñ dina tumbhe prakāsha hoiba kahe Rāmadāsa yahiñ,
Viśākhāpāṭaṇā nṛpa nāśa hele muhiñ bikāsha huai.

Tanthauzo:
Kudzera m'mavesi omwe ali pamwambawa, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akuuza wophunzira wake Ramadas kuti pamene mfumu yotchedwa Shri Dibyasingha Deb ikulamulira ku Puri, pa nthawi imeneyo Ananta Keshari, ndiko kuti, Mahaprabhu Kalki, adzakhala ndi ulamuliro wake ku Ekamra Nagar kapena Bhubaneswar. Idzakhala tsiku lachisanu ndi chiwiri (Saptami Tithi) la mwezi wa Magha. Mahapurush akunenanso kuti pa nthawi imeneyo, inenso ndidzakhala ndi Ambuye ndikukula. Izi zidzachitika pamene Mfumu ya Visakhapatnam (ndiko kuti, Nduna Yaikulu ya Andhra Pradesh) ifa.

Ngati tiganizira mosamala, tidzapeza kuti kudzera mu chizindikirocho, Mahapurush akukamba za imfa ya Nduna Yaikulu ya Andhra Pradesh, Y.S. Rajasekhara Reddy, yomwe inachitika pa ngozi ya helikopita m'chaka cha 2009. Kuchokera pa vesi lomwe lili pamwambali, Mahapurush sakunena chaka chobadwira cha Ambuye, koma akunenadi kuti cha m'chaka cha 2009, Leela za Kalki adzapitirira.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das analemba mu ‘Jaiphula Malika’ yake –

Odia Sloka:

ସେହି ଶ୍ରୀ କୃଷ୍ଣ ଚୈତନ୍ୟ,
ଜନ୍ମିଛନ୍ତି ଆସି ଏହି ମର୍ତ୍ତ୍ୟେଣ ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ସତଚାଳିଶକୁ ତୁହି ଘେନ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Sehi Shrī Kṛuṣṇa Chaitanya,
janmichhanti āsi ehi martyeṇa lo jāiphula,
satachāḷishaku tuhi ghena.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, Ambuye yemwe adabwera kale ngati Shri Krishna ndi Shri Chaitanya, adzabwera kudziko la anthu monga Kalki pa nambala 47.

Zikudziwika bwino kuti nambala 47 iyi ndi ya Mfumu ya Puri, Shri Dibyasingha Deb, yomwe imagwera pakati pa zaka za 2005 ndi 2008.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akulemba mu buku lake la Malika ‘Kalpatika’ –

Odia Sloka:

ନୀଳାଦ୍ରି ଛାଡି ପ୍ରଭୁ ପୀତବାସ,
ମର୍ତ୍ତ୍ୟରେ ଜନ୍ମ ଲଭିବେ ଅବଶ୍ୟ।
ଗୁଣରେ ଭେଦି ଭେଦ ଅଙ୍କେ ଜାଣ,
ଦେଲେ ଯେତେ ସାଲ ହେଲା ପ୍ରମାଣ।
ଏହି ସାଲରେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ଜନମିବେ,
ଅଙ୍କ ପ୍ରମାଣ ଯେ କହିଲୁ ଏବେ।
ବିଂଶ ରେବ ଅଙ୍କ ଯେବଣ ଦିନ,
ଇନ୍ଦୁ ପରେ ବିନ୍ଦୁ ଉଦେ ଗଗନ।
ଏମନ୍ତ ଲକ୍ଷଣ ଦେଖିବୁ ଯେବେ,
ପ୍ରଭୁ ଜନମ ହୋଇଥିବେ ତେବେ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Nīḷādri chhāḍi prabhu pītabāsa,
martyare janma labhibe abashya.
guṇare bhedi bheda aṅke jāṇa,
dele jete sāla helā pramāṇa.
ehi sālare prabhu janamibe,
aṅka pramāṇa je kahilu ebe.
biṃsha reba aṅka jebaṇa dina,
indu pare bindu ude gagana.
emanta lakṣaṇa dekhibu jebe,
prabhu janma hoithibe tebe.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, Mahaprabhu adzatengadi kubadwa padziko lapansi atasiya malo ake a Jagannath. Pamene akunena chaka chobadwira cha Mahaprabhu, Mahapurush akuti pamene muwonjezera makumi awiri (20) ku nambala ya Revati Nakshatra (27), ndiye nambala yomwe ibwera iyenera kumvedwa ngati chaka chobadwira cha Ambuye. Ndiko kuti, 20+27 = 47.
