Zikudziwika bwino kuti nambala iyi ndi chaka cha ulamuliro (Anka) cha Mfumu ya Puri, Dibyasingha Deb, chomwe chikuonetsa chimodzi mwa zaka zapakati pa 2005 ndi 2008.

Mahapurush analembanso mu chimodzi mwa mabuku ake a Malika –

Odia Sloka:

ଏହି କଥାମାନ ନିଶ୍ଚୟ ଘଟିବ ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ପଞ୍ଚଚାଳିଶ,
ଶୂନ୍ୟ ବାନା ଧରି ଆସିବେ ଶ୍ରୀ ହରି ଧରିଣ କଳକୀ ବେଶ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Ehi kathāmāna nishchaya ghaṭiba Dibyasiṃha pañchachāḷisha,
shūnya bānā dhari āsibe Shrī Hari dhariṇa Kaḷakī besha.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, pamene padzakhala chaka cha 45 cha Maharaj Shri Dibyasingha Deb wa Puri, ndiye Ambuye Shri Hari adzatenga kubadwa kwa Kalki ndikutsika padziko lapansi.

Ndikoyenera kudziwa kuti chaka cha 45 cha Maharaj Shri Dibyasingha Deb wa Puri chikusonyeza chaka chapakati pa 2005 ndi 2008.

Achyutananda Das Ji analembanso m'buku lake la Malika –

Odia Sloka:

ଅତି ଅନ୍ୟାୟ ହୋଇବ ସଂସାର,
କାହାରି ନ ଥିବ କୁଳ ବିଚାର।
ବହୁତ ଉତ୍ପାତ ସଂସାରେ ହେବ,
ନିର୍ଭୟ ହୋଇ ଜଣେ କେ ନଥିବ।
ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ପଞ୍ଚଚାଳିଶେ,
ଆସିବେ ଶ୍ରୀ ହରି କଳକୀ ବେଶେ।
ନାଶିବେ ଯାବତ୍ ଅବନୀଭାର,
ଏଇଠୁ ହୋଇବ ପୃଥବୀ ସଂହାର।

Kumasulira mawu:

Ati anyāya hoiba saṃsāra,
kāhāri na thiba kuḷa bichāra.
bahuta utpāta saṃsāre heba,
nirbhaya hoi jaṇe ke nathiba.
Dibyasiṃha deba pañchachāliśe,
āsibe Śrī Hari Kaḷakī beśe.
nāśibe jābat abanībhāra,
eithu hoiba pṛuthabī saṃhāra.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, kumapeto kwa Kaliyuga, padzakhala kupanda chilungamo kwakukulu padziko lapansi. Palibe amene adzakhala ndi nkhawa za banja kapena kutsatira Dharma. Padzakhala chisokonezo kulikonse; palibe amene adzakhala ndi masiku amtendere ndi chisangalalo. Panthawiyo, m'chaka cha 45 cha mfumu yotchedwa Dibyasingha Deb (pakati pa 2005-2008), Ambuye Shri Hari adzabwera padziko lapansi lino monga Kalki. Adzachotsa katundu wa tchimo padziko lapansi, kuwononga oipa, ndi kupha 'Mlechhas'.

A Mahapurushas ananena za kubadwa kwa Ambuye Kalki m'malo angapo m'mabuku ambiri.

M'buku lina la Malika, ‘kalikalpa Gita’, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das analemba –

Odia Sloka:

ବିରଜା ପୀଠ ବୈତରଣୀ ନଦୀ,
ଦଶାଶନୀ ଘାଟ ଉଦୟ ଭେଦି।
ସପତ ମାତୃକା ଟେକିଅଛି ଶିର,
ତାଙ୍କ ଅଧରୁ ବହିବ ରୁଧିର।
ସେହି ସଂକେତକୁ ଜାଣିଥାଅ ରାମ,
କଳଙ୍କି ଉଦୟ ନିଶ୍ଚୟ ଜାଣ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Birajā pīṭha Baitaraṇī nadī,
Dashāshanī ghāṭa udaya bhedi.
Sapata mātṛkā ṭekiachhi shira,
tāṅka adharu bahiba rudhira.
Sehi saṅketaku jāṇithāa Rāma,
Kaḷaṅki udaya nishchaya jāṇa.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, Mtsinje wa Baitarani umayenda pafupi ndi Kachisi wa Biraja womwe uli m'chigawo cha Jajpur ku Odisha. Pafupi ndi Dashashwamedha Ghat wa Mtsinje womwewo wa Baitarani kuli kachisi wa Saptamatruka, kumene Amayi Mulungu Asanu ndi Awiri—omwe ndi Chamunda, Varahi, Indrani, Vaishnavi, Brahmani, Kaumari, Maheshwari, ndi Narasimhi—alipo.

Mahapurush akulemba kuti pamene magazi adzatuluka m'milomo, ndiko kuti, m'kamwa, mwa Amayi a Saptamatruka, chidzakhala chizindikiro chimene okhulupirira ayenera kumvetsetsa kuti Ambuye Kalki wabadwa kapena kubadwa kwake kwayandikira kwambiri.

Nkodziwika bwino kuti chochitika ichi chachitika kale mu Kachisi wa Saptamatruka, kutsimikizira ulosi wa Mahapurush Achyutananda Das kukhala woona.

Malinga ndi ansembe a Kachisi wa Biraja, Jajpur, ndi Kachisi wa Saptamatruka, komanso anthu akumaloko, kutuluka kwa magazi m'kamwa mwa Amayi a Saptamatruka kwachitika kale mu chaka cha 2005.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das analemba m'buku lake la Malika ‘Shibakalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଯେ ଦିନ ବିରଜା ଆସ୍ଥାନ ଛୋଡ଼ିବ ଭୀମ ସିଂହନାଦ ହେବ,
ଗର୍ଭସ୍ଥିତ ଶିଶୁଭୂମିରେ ପଡ଼ିବେ ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱରକୁ ଯିବ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Je dina Birajā āsthāna choḍiba bhīma siṃhanāda heba,
garbhasthita śiśubhūmire paḍibe Bhubaneśwaraku jiba.

Tanthauzo:
Apa, Mahapurush akufotokoza nthawi ya kubadwa kwa Mahaprabhu m'chilankhulo chizindikiro. Akulemba kuti tsiku limene Maa Biraja adzachoka pampando wake, panthawiyo mwana (Ambuye Kalki) adzalowa m'mimba mwa amayi ake, amene pambuyo pake adzapita ku Bhubaneswar.

Nkodziwika bwino kuti Maa Biraja adachoka pampando wake mu chaka cha 2004. M'mwezi wa September 2004, anthu ena andale adakonza chiwembu ndikuba chifaniziro choyera cha Maa Biraja (chomwe chili mu Kachisi wa Biraja, Jajpur). Pambuyo pa miyezi pafupifupi 6, mu March 2005, chifaniziro choyera cha Amayi chidabwerera ku kachisi.

Kuchokera m'mavesi asanu ndi limodzi omwe ali pamwambawa, zatsimikiziridwa bwino kuti Mahaprabhu Kalki wabadwa, ndipo nthawi yake ndi pafupifupi zaka za 2005-2008. Choncho, owerenga onse adziwe bwino kuti avatar ya Kalki ilipo padziko lapansi panopa.

8.1 Kodi Mahaprabhu wabadwira kuti?

Malinga ndi Puranas, malo obadwirira Ambuye Kalki ndi mudzi wa Sambhal. Bhavishya Malika ikunenanso kuti Ambuye Kalki adzabadwira m'mudzi wa Sambhal. Aliyense akugwirizana pa mfundo imeneyi.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das analemba m'buku lake la Malika ‘Chakada Madana’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ କଷଣ ଦେଖି ପ୍ରଭୁ ଭଗବାନ,
କଳିରେ ସମ୍ବଳ ନଗ୍ରେ ଜନମ ହୋଇଣ।
ବିଷ୍ଣୁଶର୍ମା ପିତାଙ୍କର ନାମଟି ଅଟଇ,
ମାତା ନାମ ସୁମତି ଯେ କହିଲୁ ବୁଝାଇ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Bhaktaṅka kaṣaṇa dekhi prabhu bhagabāna,
Kaḷire Sambaḷa nagre janama hoiṇa.
Biṣṇusharmā pitāṅkara nāmaṭi aṭai,
mātā nāma Sumati je kahilu bujhāi.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, poona mavuto a okhulupirira pa nthawi ya Kali, Ambuye Kalki adzabadwira ku Sambal Nagar. Dzina la abambo a Mahaprabhu lidzakhala Vishnusharma ndipo dzina la amayi ake lidzakhala Sumati.

Mahapurush analembanso mu ‘Chakada Madana’ –

Odia Sloka:

ସମ୍ବଳ ନଗରେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ସେ ଗୁପ୍ତେ ହେବେ ଜନମ,
ଛତିଆ ବଟରେ ଲାଗିବ ଦିବାରାତ୍ରି ସଂଗ୍ରାମ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Sambaḷa nagare prabhu se gupte hebe janama,
Chhatiā baṭare lāgiba dibārātri saṅgrāma.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, Mahaprabhu Kalki adzabadwira mobisa ku Sambal Nagar. Nkhondo ya usana ndi usiku idzayambika ku Chhatia Bata.

Achyutananda Das Ji analembanso m'buku lake la Malika ‘Chakada Madana’ –

Odia Sloka:

ମାରୁ ମହରଗ ରାଜକର ଆସି ଘରେ ଘରେ ପଶିଯିବ,
ବାରଣ ବେଳରେ ସମ୍ବଳନଗ୍ରେ କଳ୍କୀ ଉଦୟ ହୋଇବ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Māru maharaga rājakara āsi ghare ghare paśijiba,
bāraṇa beḷare Sambaḷanagre Kaḷkī udaya hoiba.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, pamene imfa, njala, ndi misonkho zidzalowa m'nyumba iliyonse ndipo chisokonezo chidzafalikira kulikonse, Ambuye Kalki adzaonekera ku Sambal Nagar pa nthawi yomaliza ya Kaliyuga.

Mahapurush analembanso mu ‘Chakada Madana’ –

Odia Sloka:

କହନ୍ତି ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଶୁଣ ରାମଦାସ ଭକତ ମୋ ଗଳା ମାଳ,
ଭକତ ନିମନ୍ତେ ସମ୍ବଳନଗ୍ରେ ହେବି କଳ୍କୀ ଅବତାର।

Kumasulira mawu:

Kahanti Achyuta śuṇa Rāmadāsa bhakata mo gaḷā māḷa,
bhakata nimante Sambaḷanagre hebi Kaḷkī abatāra.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akulemba mu ‘Tera Janma Sharana’ –

Odia Sloka:

କଳକୀର ସୀମା କାଳ ପୂରିଗଲେ କଳ୍କୀ ବିଷ୍ଣୁଯଶା ପୁରେ,
ସମ୍ବଳ ଗ୍ରାମରେ ଜାତ ହୋଇଥିବେ ମ୍ଲେଚ୍ଛ ସଂହାର କାଳରେ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Kaḷakīra sīmā kāḷa pūrigale Kaḷkī Biṣṇujashā pure,
Sambaḷa grāmare jāta hoithibe mlechha saṃhāra kāḷare.

Tanthauzo:
"Pamene nthawi ya Kaliyuga idzakwaniritsidwa, Kalki adzabadwira m'nyumba ya Vishnuyasha m'mudzi wa Sambal kuti awononge Mlechhas."

Komabe, kumene 'Sambal' kuli ndi nkhani yotsutsana m'maganizo mwa anthu ambiri ngakhale lero. M'nkhaniyi, tidzathetsa izi.

8.2 Kodi Bhavishya Malika ikunena chiyani pa nkhaniyi?

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akulemba m'buku lake la Malika ‘Jaiphula Malika’ –

Odia Sloka:

ସେହି ବେଳ କାଳ ଜାଣି,
ଓଡିଶାରେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ଜନ୍ମିବେ ପୁଣି ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
କେହି ତାଙ୍କ ମାୟାକୁ ନ ଚିହ୍ନି।

Kumasulira mawu:

Sehi beḷa kāḷa jāṇi,
Oḍishāre prabhu janmibe puṇi lo jāiphula,
kehi tāṅka māyāku na chinhi.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, panthawiyo pamene tchimo lili pachimake, Ambuye Kalki adzabadwira m'boma la Odisha. Chinyengo cha Ambuye chidzakhala champhamvu kwambiri moti palibe amene adzamuzindikire.

Mahapurush analembanso m'buku lina la Malika –

Odia Sloka:

ଉତ୍କଳ ଭୂଇଁରେ କଳ୍କୀର ଜନମ,
ଗ୍ରାମ ନାମ ରାଜହଂସ।
କଳ୍କୀ ଜନମି ଲୀଳା ପ୍ରକାଶିବ,
ଏହା କହିଲି ପ୍ରକାଶ୍ୟ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Utkala bhuiñre Kaḷkīra janama,
grāma nāma Rājahansa.
Kaḷkī janami līḷā prakāśiba,
ehā kahili prakāshya.

Tanthauzo:
"Kubadwa kwa Kalki kudzakhala pa dziko la Utkala (Odisha), m'mudzi wotchedwa Rajahansa. Atabadwa, Kalki adzaonetsa masewera ake aumulungu."

Tsopano, ponena za kumene ku Odisha Ambuye Kalki adzabadwira, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das iyemwini analemba m'buku lina la Malika, ‘Bhabishya Jatipataka Gita’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଓଡିଶା ଦେଶରେ ଗୁପ୍ତ ଗଙ୍ଗା ନଦୀ ତୀର,
ସମ୍ବଳ ନଗ୍ରେ ବିଷ୍ଣୁବିପ୍ର ଦ୍ୱିଜବର।

Kumasulira mawu:

Oḍiśā deśare gupta Gaṅgā nadī tīra,
Sambaḷa nagre Biṣṇubipra dwijabara.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, malo obadwirira Mahaprabhu adzakhala pafupi ndi mabanki a Gupta Ganga (Mtsinje wa Baitarani) ku Sambal Nagar, Odisha. Pamalo pomwepo, m'nyumba ya Brahmin wabwino amene amayimba matamando a Ambuye Vishnu, Mahaprabhu Kalki adzabadwa.

Kuchokera m'mavesi onse awiri omwe ali pamwambawa, zikuwonekera bwino kuti malinga ndi Bhavishya Malika, Ambuye Kalki adzabadwira ku Sambal, komwe kuli m'boma la India la Odisha.

8.3 Kodi Sambal kuli kuti ku Odisha?

Mmodzi mwa Panchasakhas, Mahapurush Jagannath Das, analemba m'buku la Malika ‘Bhabishyat Parardha’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଯାଜପୁର ସମ୍ବଳ ନଗ୍ରରେ,
ଦୁଇ ଜଣ ଅଛନ୍ତି ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣ ଘରେ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Jājapura Sambaḷa nagrare,
dui jaṇa achhanti brāhmaṇa ghare.

Tanthauzo:
M'vesi lomwe lili pamwambali, Mahapurush akulemba – “Jajapura Sambala nagrare”. Ndiko kuti, “Mu Sambal Nagar wa Jajpur”. Zikuwonekera bwino kuti Sambal Nagar ili ku Jajpur.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das analemba m'buku lake la Malika ‘Kalikalpa Gita’ –

Odia Sloka:

କ୍ଷୀର ନଦୀ ଦକ୍ଷିଣ ଯେ ବିରଜା ମଣ୍ଡଳ,
ଖ୍ୟାତ ଯେ କମଳପୁର ଗୁପ୍ତ ଯେ ସମ୍ବଳ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Kṣhīra nadī dakṣhiṇa je Birajā maṇḍala,
khyāta je Kamalapura gupta je Sambaḷa.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, kumwera kwa mtsinje wa Khira ndi kumpoto kwa Mahanadi kuli dera la kachisi wa Biraja. Pafupi ndi dera la Biraja kuli mudzi wotchedwa Kamalapur. Uwu ndi umene umatchedwa mobisa mudzi wa Sambal.

Kudzera m'vesi ili, Mahapurush wavumbulutsa kuti Sambal ili m'dera la Biraja kapena chigawo cha Jajpur ku Odisha.

Tiyeni timvetsetse kumene Sambal kuli ku Odisha kudzera m'mavesi ena.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das analemba m'buku lake la Malika ‘Gurubhakti Gita’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଗୟାରେ ସମ୍ବଳ ନଗ୍ର ବିଷ୍ଣୁଶର୍ମା ଘର,
ଜନମିଣ ତପସ୍ୟା କରିବେ ଚକ୍ରଧର।

Kumasulira mawu:

Gayāre Sambaḷa nagra Biṣṇusharmā ghara,
janamiṇa tapasyā karibe Chakradhara.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, Chakradhara (Ambuye Vishnu) adzabadwira m'nyumba ya wokhulupirira wokhala ku Sambal Nagar, Gaya, ndipo pambuyo pake adzachita kulapa.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das analembanso m'buku lake la Malika ‘Kalikalpa Gita’ –

Odia Sloka:

ବିଷ୍ଣୁଶର୍ମା ଗୃହେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ହୋଇବେ ଜାତ,
ଗୟାରେ ସମ୍ବଳ ନଗ୍ର ହରିହର କ୍ଷେତ୍ର।

Kumasulira mawu:

Biṣṇusharmā gruhe prabhu hoibe jāta,
Gayāre Sambaḷa nagra Harihara kṣhetra.

Tanthauzo:
"Ambuye adzabadwira m'nyumba ya Vishnusharma, m'dera la Harihara ku Sambal Nagar, Gaya."

Malinga ndi mavesi onse awiriwa, kubadwa kwa Ambuye kudzachitika ku Sambal Nagar ku Gaya. Tsopano, Gaya imeneyo idzakhala iti ndipo ili kuti ku Odisha adauzidwa ndi Ambuye Krishna mwiniwake kwa Agopa ake khumi ndi awiri, zomwe Mahapurush Achyutananda Das adazitchula m'buku lake la Malika ‘Biraja Mahatmya’. Mahapurush analemba –

Odia Sloka:

ଶୁଣ ବାର ସୁତ ନିହାର ବଚନ ଏ ଅଟେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଠାର,
ନାଭିଗୟା ତୀର୍ଥେ ହରିହର କ୍ଷେତ୍ର ଗ୍ରାମଟି ସମ୍ବଳ ପୁର।

Kumasulira mawu:

Śuṇa bāra suta nihāra bachana e aṭe Achyuta ṭhāra,
Nābhigayā tīrthe Harihara kṣhetra grāmaṭi Sambaḷa pura.

Tanthauzo:
"Mverani inu ana khumi ndi awiri, ili ndi mawu otsimikizika ochokera kwa Achyuta: mudzi wa Sambalpur uli m'dera la Harihara pamalo opatulika a Nabhigaya."

Ndiko kuti, dera la Harihara lili pamalo opatulika a Nabhigaya, omwe amatchedwa Sambal.

M'makambirano apamwambawa, tawona m'malo ambiri kuti Mahapurush adalongosola malo obadwirira a Mahaprabhu ngati dera la Biraja kapena malo opatulika a Gaya (Nabhigaya). Tsopano tidzadziwa ndendende kumene madera amenewa ali, zomwe zikhulupiriro za nthano zokhudzana nawo zili, ndi kufunika kwake.

8.3.1 Dera la Biraja

Kachisi wa 'Biraja' ndi kachisi wamkulu yemwe ali m'mphepete mwa Mtsinje wa Baitarani ku Jajpur, Odisha, kumene Adi Shakti amapembedzedwa ngati 'Biraja'. Kwenikweni, kalekale, Brahma Ji mwiniwake adachita Yajna (nsembe) pano ku Brahmakunda, pambuyo pake Amayi Parvati adatuluka pamoto wa nsembe ndikulangiza Brahma Ji kuti amukhazikitse ndi dzina lakuti 'Biraja'. Derali limatchedwanso dera la Biraja pambuyo pa dzina la Mulungu Biraja.

Panopa ndi pamene mchombo (Nabhi) wa Mulungu Sati unagwa, zomwe zidapangitsa kuti ikhale imodzi mwa Shakti Peethas 51. Uwu ndi kachisi yekhayo kumene Mulungu amapembedzedwa ngati Mahishasura Mardini wokhala ndi manja awiri.

Malowa amadziwikanso ngati Nabhigaya chifukwa cha Pinda Daan (nsembe za makolo). Kwenikweni, ndi Shakti Peetha yekhayo kumene Pinda Daan amachitika. Pafupi ndi Brahmakunda, pali chitsime chakuya chotchedwa Nabhikunda, chomwe madzi ake anthu amachitira Pinda Daan kwa makolo awo. Malowa amatchulidwanso mu Skanda Purana.

8.3.2 Nabhigaya

Zatchulidwa m'mapurana kuti mu Satya Yuga, panali chiwanda chotchedwa Gayasura. Ngakhale adabadwira m'banja la ziwanda, mtima wake udalimbikitsidwa ndi Dharma ndi kulapa. Anali wokhulupirira kwambiri, wamphamvu kwambiri, ndipo anali ndi thupi lalikulu. Nthawi zonse ankagwira ntchito yopindulitsa dziko lapansi ndi ena. Zatchulidwa m'mapurana kuti chiwandacho chidachita kulapa kwakukulu, komwe Ambuye Vishnu adakondwera nako, ndipo Ambuye adamupempha kuti apemphe madalitso. Gayasura adapempha madalitso apadera. Adati, “Zolengedwa zikafike kumwamba pongondikhudza.” Ambuye Vishnu adapereka madalitsowa powona kulapa kwake kwakukulu. Koma chifukwa chake, Yamaloka (dziko la akufa) lidayamba kukhala lopanda anthu, chifukwa aliyense amene Gayasura adakhudza adapita kumwamba. Izi zidakhala vuto lalikulu kwa milungu ndi Yamraj. Milungu yonse idapita kwa Utatu (Brahma, Vishnu, Mahesh) ndikupempha kuti apeze yankho la vutoli. Milungu idapempha Utatu kuti aletse Gayasura. Utatu udaganiza zoletsa Gayasura, yemwe anali kuyambitsa zopinga m'malamulo a chilengedwe ngakhale anali wolungama. Atatuwo adadzibisa ngati Abrahmin atatu, adapita kwa Gayasura, ndipo adati akufuna kuchita Yajna mosalekeza kwa masiku asanu ndi awiri ndipo chifukwa cha izi, amafunikira malo akulu ndi opatulika. Adapempha Gayasura kuti apereke thupi lake pa Yajna, koma sanayenera kusuntha kwa masiku asanu ndi awiriwa. Gayasura adagwirizana ndi izi. Gayasura adafalitsa mutu wake ku Gaya (Bihar) kumene Ambuye Vishnu adakhala, adayika mchombo wake ku Nabhigaya (Jajpur, Odisha) kumene Brahma Ji adakhala, ndipo adayika mapazi ake ku Pithapuram (Andhra Pradesh) kumene Ambuye Shiva adakhala, ndipo motero Yajna idayamba. Utatu udachita Yajna mosalekeza kwa masiku asanu ndi awiri.

Gayasura ankapitiriza kuwerengera masiku akumva tambala akulira m'mawa. Podziwa izi, tsiku lomaliza, Ambuye Shiva adadzibisa ngati tambala ndikulira usiku usanathe. Atamva kulira kwa tambala, Gayasura adaganiza kuti kwacha ndipo adasuntha thupi lake poganiza kuti masiku asanu ndi awiri atha. Utatu nthawi yomweyo udawonekera pamaso pake ndipo udati Gayasura adaswa lonjezo lake losasuntha thupi lake kwa masiku asanu ndi awiri. Adati chilango choswa lonjezo chinali imfa. Gayasura wolungama adavomereza izi popanda kudandaula. Utatu udakondwera ndi chipembedzo chake ndipo adamupempha kuti apemphe madalitso. Kenako Gayasura adawapempha kuti dzina lake likumbukiridwe pamodzi ndi Utatu. Adawapempha kuti azikongoletsa thupi lake nthawi zonse ndi zovala za Abrahmin. Adapemphanso madalitso oti ngati makolo a wina aliyense apatsidwa nsembe (Pinda Daan ndi Tarpan) m'malo atatuwa, asabadwenso.

Potsatira chikhumbo cha Gayasura, Ambuye Vishnu adadzibisa ngati Gadadhar ku Gaya, Brahma Ji amapembedzedwa ngati Girija Devi ku Nabhigaya, ndipo Ambuye Shiva amapembedzedwa ku Pithapuram ngati Kukkuteshwar (Ambuye amene adadzibisa ngati tambala). Motere, malo atatuwa adadziwika chifukwa cha Pinda Daan ndi Tarpan:

  • Shirogaya (Gaya, Bihar)
  • Nabhigaya (Jajpur, Odisha)
  • Padagaya (Pithapuram, Andhra Pradesh)

Umboni woti malo opatulika a Nabhigaya ali ku Jajpur umapezekanso m'mabuku a Malika a Panchasakha Mahapurushas.

M'buku la Malika ‘Chakada Madana’, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das analemba –

Odia Sloka:

ଯାଜପୁରଠାରେ ନାଭିଗୟା ତୀର୍ଥ ଏକଥା ଜାଣ ସତ୍ୱର।

Kumasulira mawu:

Jājapurathāre Nābhigayā tīrtha ekathā jāṇa satwara.

Tanthauzo:
"Dziwani izi motsimikiza, malo opatulika a Nabhigaya ali ku Jajpur."

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, akulosera za kubadwa kwake kwachikhumi zaka pafupifupi 600 zapitazo, analemba m'buku la Malika ‘Kalikalpa Gita’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଦଶମ ଜନମ ମୋହର ହୋଇବ ପ୍ରକାଶ,
ଯାଜପୁର ନାଭିଗୟା ହରିହର ଖ୍ୟାତ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Daśama janama mohara hoiba prakāśa,
Jājapura Nābhigayā Harihara khyāta.

Tanthauzo:
"Kubadwa kwanga kwachikhumi kudzawonekera m'dera lodziwika bwino la Harihara ku Nabhigaya, Jajpur."

Kudzera mu vesi limeneli, zikuwonekeranso kuti malo opatulika a Nabhigaya ali ku Jajpur.

Mahapurush analemba za nkhani yomweyi m'buku lake la Malika ‘Kalikalpa Gita’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଯାଜପୁର ପଞ୍ଚକୋଶ ବିରଜା ମଣ୍ଡଳ,
ନାଭିଗୟା ବିଷ୍ଣୁଶର୍ମା ଘରେ ହେବ ଖେଳ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Jājapura pañchakośa Birajā maṇḍala,
Nābhigayā Biṣṇusharmā ghare heba kheḷa.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, dera la Biraja lili mkati mwa mtunda wa makosha asanu kuchokera ku Jajpur. Ambuye Kalki adzabadwira m'nyumba ya wokhulupirira Vishnu wokhala m'dera lomwelo la Biraja, Nabhigaya.

Choncho, kuchokera pa zokambirana zapamwambazi, zikuwonekeratu kuti Sambal Nagar, malo opatulika a Gaya, ndi dera la Biraja kumene Panchasakhas adalosera kubadwa kwa Ambuye Kalki, kwenikweni kuli mkati mwa chigawo cha Jajpur ku Odisha.

Kuti timvetsetse bwino za malo obadwirira a Ambuye Kalki, tidzatenga mavesi ena omwe atchulidwa mu Malika.

Mahapurush Shishu Ananta Das analemba m'buku lake la Malika ‘Chumbaka Malika’ –

Odia Sloka:

କଳ୍କୀ ଅବତାରେ ଓଡିଶା ମଣ୍ଡଳେ ତୁମ୍ଭେ ଜନମ ହୋଇବ,
ଦୁଷ୍ଟ ଜନ ନାଶି ଭକତଙ୍କୁ ଉଶ୍ୱାସୀ ଧର୍ମକୁ ତୁମ୍ଭେ ପାଳିବ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Kaḷkī abatāre Oḍiśā maṇḍaḷe tumbhe janama hoiba,
duṣhṭa jana nāśi bhaktaṅku uśwāsī dharmaku tumbhe pāḷiba.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, Kalki Avatar adzabadwira m'boma la Odisha. Mahaprabhu Kalki adzawononga oipa, adzapulumutsa okhulupirira, ndipo adzakhazikitsanso Dharma Padziko Lapansi.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, akutchula Ambuye, analemba m'buku lake la Malika –

Odia Sloka:

ଇନ୍ଦୁ ପରେ ବିନ୍ଦୁ ଦେଖିବ ଜାଣ,
ଆମ୍ଭ ଆବିର୍ଭାବ ଯାଜପୁରେଣ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Indu pare bindu dekhiba jāṇa,
āmbha ābirbhāba Jājapureṇa.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, pamene kadontho kapena nyenyezi ikawoneka pamwamba pa mwezi kumwamba, dziwani kuti kuwonekera kwa Ambuye Wamkulu kudzachitika ku Jajpur.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, m'buku lake la Malika ‘Jai Birachita Chautisha’, analemba mobwerezabwereza mawu a Ambuye eniake –

Odia Sloka:

ଯେତେବେଳେ ନୀଳାଚଳ ଛାଡିକରି ଯିବୁ,
ଯାଜପୁର ବିପ୍ର ଘରେ ଯାଇଁ ଜନମିବୁ।
ଜଗିଥିବୁ ହେ। ଯେ ଯେତେ କହିଲେ ନ ଶୁଣିବୁ ହେ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Jetebeḷe Nīḷāchaḷa chhāḍikari jibu,
Jājapura bipra ghare jāiñ janamibu.
Jagithibu he. Je jete kahile na śuṇibu he.

Tanthauzo:
"Mukachoka ku Nilachala (Puri), mudzapita kukabadwira m'nyumba ya Brahmin ku Jajpur. Khalani maso; musamvere chilichonse chimene wina aliyense anena."

M'buku la Malika ‘Tatwabodhini’, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das analemba –

Odia Sloka:

ଯାଜନଗ୍ରେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ଜନମ ହେବେ,
ଜନମ ହୋଇବେ ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣ ଭାବେ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Jājanagre prabhu janama hebe,
janama hoibe brāhmaṇa bhābe.

Tanthauzo:
"Ambuye adzabadwira ku Jajanagar; Adzabadwa ngati Brahmin."

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das analemba m'buku lake la Malika lotchedwa 'Bigyanakalpa' –

Odia Sloka:

ଯାଜନଗ୍ରେ ମହାପ୍ରଭୁ ଜନମ ହୋଇବେ,
ଗରୁଡକୁ ସଙ୍ଗେ ଘେନି ଲୀଳା ଯେ କରିବେ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Jājanagre Mahāprabhu janama hoibe,
Garuḍaku saṅge gheni līḷā je karibe.

Tanthauzo:
"Mahaprabhu adzabadwira ku Jajanagar; atatenga Garuda, adzachita masewera ake aumulungu."

Kuchokera m'mavesi omwe ali pamwambawa, palibenso kukayika kuti kubadwa kwa Mahaprabhu Kalki kunali kotsimikizika kuti kudzachitika ku Sambal Nagar, malo opatulika a Nabhigaya, dera la Biraja, chigawo cha Jajpur, Odisha.

Ngati tiyang'ana pa Bhagavata Purana ndi Mahabharata zolembedwa kale kuposa Bhavishya Malika, zikuwonetsanso ku Jajpur, Odisha ponena za malo obadwirira Ambuye Kalki.

M'nthawi zakale, Maharishi Vedavyas adanena kuti malo obadwirira Ambuye Kalki ndi 'mudzi wa Sambhal' mu Shrimad Bhagavata Mahapurana. Iye analemba –

Sloka ya Sanskrit:

संभलग्राममुख्यस्य ब्राह्मणस्य महात्मनः ।
भवने विष्णुयशसः कल्किः प्रादुर्भविष्यति ।।

Kumasulira mawu:

Śambhalagrāmamukhyasya brāhmaṇasya mahātmanaḥ |
bhavane viṣṇuyaśasaḥ kalkiḥ prādurbhaviṣyati ||

Tanthauzo:
"M'nyumba ya Brahmin wamkulu, mkulu wa mudzi wa Sambhal, dzina lake Vishnuyasha, Ambuye Kalki adzawonekera."

Pambuyo pake, mu 'Vanaparva' ya Mahabharata, Maharishi Vyas mwiniwake adasintha pang'ono izi ndipo adatcha malo obadwirira Ambuye Kalki kuti 'Sambhoota Sambhal Grama'. Iye analemba –

Sloka ya Sanskrit:

कल्कि विष्णुयशा नाम द्विजः कालप्रचोदितः ।
उत्पत्स्यते महावीर्यो महाबुद्धिपराक्रमः ।।
सम्भूतः सम्भलग्रामे ब्राह्मणावसथे शुभे ।
मनसा तस्य सर्वाणि वाहनान्यायुधानि च ।।
उपस्थास्यन्ति योधाश्च शस्त्राणि कवचानि च ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Kalki Vishnuyashā nāma dwijaḥ kālaprachoditaḥ |
utpatsyate mahāvīryo mahābuddhiparākramaḥ ||
sambhūtaḥ sambhalagrāme brāhmaṇāvasathe shubhe |
manasā tasya sarvāṇi vāhanānyāyudhāni cha ||
upasthāsyanti yodhāshcha shastrāṇi kavachāni cha |

Tanthauzo:
Kudzera m'mavesi omwe ali pamwambapa, Maharishi Vyas adanena kuti Ambuye Kalki adzabadwira mu 'Sambhoota Sambhal Grama', kutanthauza mudzi wa Sambhal 'wokhazikitsidwa' kapena 'wopangidwa', mudzi wa Sambhal womwe ukanakhazikitsidwa ngati malo okhala a Brahmins kuti achite Yajnas ndi kulambira. Iye analemba kuti Ambuye Kalki adzabadwira m'nyumba ya Brahmin wabwino amene amakhala m'mudzi wa Sambhalwu ndipo amayimba matamando a Ambuye Vishnu.

Tsopano, ngati tifufuza m'masamba a mbiri yakale, tidzapeza kuti malo okhala a Brahmins okhazikitsidwa kuti achite Yajna ndi kulambira ali m'chigawo cha Jajpur ku Odisha.

Kwenikweni, dzina la Jajpur linaperekedwa m'zaka za zana la 10 pambuyo pa dzina la Mfumu ya Somavamsi Jajati (Yayati). Mfumu Jajati inakhazikitsa mzindawu ndipo inachita Abhinav Jajatinagar kukhala likulu lake. Pambuyo pake, kusintha dzina lake, poyamba unatchedwa Jajanagar ndiyeno Jajpur. Mfumu Jajati Keshari inabweretsa pafupifupi 10,000 Brahmins kuchokera ku Kannauj ku Uttar Pradesh ndipo inawakhazika ku Jajpur, Odisha, kuti achite Ashwamedha Yajnas khumi (Dashashwamedha). Ngakhale lero, Dashashwamedha Ghat m'mphepete mwa Mtsinje wa Baitarani imatikumbutsa za chochitika ichi. Komabe, Maharishi Vyas adaloza ku malo okhala a Brahmins omwe adakhazikika kum'mawa kwa kachisi wa Biraja ku Jajpur powatcha 'Sambhoota Sambhal Grama' mu 'Vanaparva' ya Mahabharata.

9 Khandagiri – Ku Bhubaneswar kapena kwina?

Munthu aliyense amene ali ndi chidwi ndi Bhavishya Malika alibe kukayika za kumene Mahaprabhu Kalki adzabadwire. Amadziwa bwino kuti avatar ya Kalki idzabadwira m'mudzi wa Sambal womwe uli ku Jajpur, Odisha.

Iwo alibenso kukayika kuti atabadwira ku Jajpur, Ambuye adzakhala pamalo otchedwa Khandagiri.

Mmodzi mwa Panchasakhas, Mahapurush Shishu Ananta Das, analemba m'buku lake la Malika lotchedwa 'Chumbaka Malika' –

Odia Sloka:

ଏହି କଳିଯୁଗେ କଳ୍କୀ ଅବତାରେ ହରି,
ଶ୍ରୀ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରିରେ ଥିବେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ବିଜେ କରି।

Kumasulira mawu:

Ehi kaliyuge Kalki abatare Hari,
Shri khaṇḍagirire thibe prabhu bije kari.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, mu Kaliyuga, Ambuye Hari adzakhala ku Khandagiri mu mawonekedwe Ake a Kalki.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akulemba mu 'Agata Bhabishyata Malika' yake –

Odia Sloka:

ଗୋପନେ ମୁକୁନ୍ଦ ଯେ ଆସି,
ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରିରେ ଥିବେ ବସି।

Kumasulira mawu:

Gopane Mukunda je asi,
Khandagirire thibe basi.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, Ambuye adzakhala mobisa ku Khandagiri.

Mahapurush Sishu Ananta Das analembanso mu imodzi mwa mabuku ake a Malika, 'Patta Madana' –

Odia Sloka:

ଭକତ ସୁମେଳ ହୋଇ ଏକ ଠୁଳ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ପାଶେ ଯିବେ,
ଅନନ୍ତ ମୂରତି ଦର୍ଶନ ସାରିଣ ସର୍ବେ ସର୍ବଙ୍କୁ ଚିହ୍ନିବେ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Bhakata sumeḷa hoi eka ṭhuḷa Khaṇḍagiri pāshe jibe,
ananta mūrati darshana sāriṇa sarbe sarbaṅku chinhibe.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, pamalo otchedwa Khandagiri, padzakhala msonkhano wa opembedza. Kumeneko, opembedza adzapeza darshan (masomphenya) a Ambuye Kalki ndipo adzadziwana.

Mpaka pano, aliyense akugwirizana, koma kukayika kwa anthu kumabwera ponena za kumene Khandagiri iyi ili. Masiku ano, kudzera m'mavidiyo ndi ma PDF ena, zonenetsa zosiyanasiyana zikupangidwa za Khandagiri. Malinga ndi anthu ena, malowa otchedwa Khandagiri, kumene Ambuye adzakhala, ali ku Jajpur, Odisha, pamene ena akunena kuti Khandagiri ili m'chigawo cha Dhenkanal ku Odisha.

9.1 Khandagiri ili kuti?

Kudzera m'nkhaniyi, ndiloleni ndinene kuti mayina onse omwe adatchulidwa pamwambapa okhudza Khandagiri (Jajpur, Dhenkanal), onse ndi olakwika kwathunthu.

Kufotokoza kumene Khandagiri ili, mmodzi mwa Panchasakhas, Mahapurush Yasovanta Das, analemba m'buku lake la Malika lotchedwa 'Basanta Manjari' –

Odia Sloka:

ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ମହିମା କହିତ ନୁହଁଇ,
ଲକ୍ଷ ଲକ୍ଷ ଭକ୍ତ ତହିଁ ସେଠାରେ ଅଛଇ।
ଗୁପତ ଲୀଳା ହୋଇବ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ଠାରେ,
ତପସ୍ୟାରେ ରହିଥିବେ ଋଷିଙ୍କ ସଙ୍ଗରେ।
ଗୁପ୍ତ ଗଙ୍ଗା ସେକାଳରେ ବାହାର ହୋଇବ,
ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱରକୁ ସେହି ଭସାଇଣ ଦେବ।
ତ୍ରିଭୁବନ ସ୍ୱର କଥା କହିତ ନୁହଁଇ,
ବାରଭୁଜା ଦୁର୍ଗାମାତା ଜଗି ରହିଛଇଁ।
ଆହୁରି କଥାଏ ବାବୁ ଶୁଣ ମନଦେଇ,
ଭୂଆସୁଣୀ ଜଗିଅଛି ସେଠାବକୁ ତହିଁ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Khaṇḍagiri mahimā kahita nuhañi,
lakṣha lakṣha bhakta tahin seṭhāre achhai.
Gupata līḷā hoiba Khaṇḍagiri ṭhāre,
tapasyāre rahithibe ruṣhiṅka saṅgare.
Gupta Gaṅgā sekāḷare bāhara hoiba,
Bhubaneśwaraku sehi bhasāiṇa deba.
Tribhubana swara kathā kahita nuhañi,
bārabhuja Durgāmātā jagi rahichhaiṃ.
Āhuri kathāe bābu śuṇa manadei,
Bhūāsuṇī jagiachhi seṭhābaku tahiñ.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, ulemerero wa Khandagiri ndi wosafotokozeka. Kumeneko kuli mamiliyoni a opembedza. Leela zaumulungu zobisika adzachitika ku Khandagiri. Anzeru ambiri ndi amonke akuchita mapemphero kumeneko. Mtsinje wobisika (Gupta Ganga) ukuyenda kumeneko, womwe udzabweretsa chiwonongeko ku Bhubaneswar panthawi ya Leela za chiwonongeko cha Kalki. Ulemerero wa Bhubaneswar umenewo ndi waukulunso. Kumeneko, Amayi Barabhuji ndi Amayi Bhuasuni alipo, omwe adzachita ntchito yowononga kumapeto kwa Kali Yuga.

Mu shloka yomwe ili pamwambapa, Mahapurush adalongosola kuti Khandagiri ili ku Bhubaneswar.

Kufotokoza malo a Khandagiri, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das analembanso m'buku lake la Malika lotchedwa 'Chakada Madana' –

Odia Sloka:

ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱରରେ ଲାଗିବ ଗୋଳ,
ଏକାମ୍ର ବନ ଯେ ଅଛି ସେଠାର।
ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ସିଦ୍ଧ ସାଧୁ ଅଛନ୍ତି,
କଳ୍କୀ ରୂପକୁ ଧ୍ୟାନ କରନ୍ତି।
କ୍ଷୀର ଧାର ବହିଯାଏ ଗୁପ୍ତର,
ଗୁମ୍ଫାରେ ଭଜନ ହୁଏ ସତ୍ୱର।
ଘଟଣା ସ୍ଥାନ ଯେ ଅଟଇ ସେହି,
କଳିର ଭାରତ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ସେଠାହିଁ।
ଉଦୟଗିରି ଯେ ଅପୋଡା ସ୍ଥାନ,
ସେଠାବରେ ବହୁ ସାଧୁ ଆଶ୍ରମ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Bhubaneśwarare lāgiba goḷa, ekāmra bana je achhi seṭhāra.
Khaṇḍagiri siddha sādhu achhanti, Kaḷkī rūpaku dhyāna karanti.
Kṣhīra dhāra bahijāe guptara, gumphāre bhajana hue satwara.
Ghaṭaṇā sthāna je aṭai sehi, Kaḷira bhārata yuddha seṭhāhiñ.
Udayagiri je apoḍā sthāna, seṭhābare bahu sādhu āśrama.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, padzakhala nkhondo ku Bhubaneswar, ndipo nkhalango ya Ekamra ili kumeneko. Kenako Mahapurush adanena kuti Khandagiri ilinso kumeneko, kumene anzeru ambiri opatulika adzakhala ndipo adzayimba mosalekeza dzina la Kalki ndi kusinkhasinkha mawonekedwe Ake. M'mapanga amenewo a Khandagiri, masana ndi usiku bhajans ndi kirtans zidzachitika. Kumeneko, mtsinje wobisika (Kshira Nadi) udzayendanso. Khandagiri imeneyo idzakhala "Malo Ochitikira" (Ghatana Sthana), kumene nkhondo yotsala ya Mahabharata idzachitika. Pafupi pali Udayagiri, kumene malo opatulika a anzeru ambiri opatulika ali.

Kudzera mu shloka yomwe ili pamwambapa, Mahapurush adalongosola momveka bwino kuti Khandagiri kumene oyera mtima osawerengeka adzakhala, kumene kuyimba mobisa kwa dzina la Kalki kudzapitirira m'mapanga, ndipo kumene nkhondo yotsala ya Mahabharata idzachitika, ili ku Bhubaneswar, kumene nkhalango ya Ekamra ilinso.

Apa ndikufuna kukopa chidwi cha owerenga ku mfundo yofunika. M'mabuku a Malika, Bhubaneswar nthawi zambiri amatchedwa Ekamra Kshetra, Ekamra Van, kapena Ekamra Kanan. Kwenikweni, malinga ndi Ekamra Purana ya zaka za zana la 13, panali mtengo umodzi wokha wa mango pafupi ndi malo a mulungu wamkulu Lingaraj. Choncho, dera la Lingaraj limatchedwanso Ekamra Kshetra (dera lomwe lili ndi mtengo umodzi wa mango).

Ichi ndicho chifukwa chake Mahapurushas nthawi zambiri amatcha Bhubaneswar Lingaraj Kshetra (dera la kachisi wa Lingaraj) kapena Ekamra Van Kshetra (dera la mtengo umodzi wa mango).

Osati izi zokha, chifukwa cha malo a Bindu Sarovar ku Bhubaneswar, Panchasakhas m'mabuku awo a Malika adatchulanso Bhubaneswar ngati dera la Bindu Sarovar.

Mahapurush analemba kufotokoza mu 'Garuda Gita' –

Odia Sloka:

ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱର ଯେ ଏକାମ୍ର କାନନ ଏ ସବୁ ମୋ ଗୁପ୍ତ ସ୍ଥାନ,
ଆଉ ପୃଥ୍ବୀରେ ଯେତେ ସିଦ୍ଧ ସ୍ଥାନ ପୁରାଣେ ଅଛି ବର୍ଣ୍ଣନ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Bhubaneśwara je ekāmra kānana e sabu mo gupta sthāna,
āu pruthwīre jete siddha sthāna purāṇe achhi barṇana.

Tanthauzo:
Ambuye Krishna akuuza wopembedza Wake, Garuda, kuti Ekamra Kanan yomwe ili ku Bhubaneswar ndi malo obisika komanso malo a Siddha (malo angwiro).

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das analemba m'buku lake la Malika 'Chhayalisha Patala' –

Odia Sloka:

ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱର ଏକାମ୍ରରେ,
ରହିବେ ଭକ୍ତ ତିନିଘରେ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Bhubaneśwara Ekāmrare, rahibe bhakta tini ghare.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, opembedza a Ambuye adzakhala m'dera la Bhubaneswar Ekamra.

Mahapurush Sishu Ananta Das analemba m'buku lake la Malika 'Chumbaka Malika' –

Odia Sloka:

ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱର ସୀମା ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରିରେ,
ପଞ୍ଚବିଂଶ ଭକ୍ତ ଥିବେ ତଟରେ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Bhubaneśwara sīmā Khaṇḍagirire, pañchabiṃśha bhakta thibe taṭare.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, Khandagiri ili m'malire a Bhubaneswar kumene opembedza 25 adzatenga nawo mbali pa masewera aumulungu.

Mahapurush akulembanso mu 'Chakada Madana' –

Odia Sloka:

ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱରରେ ଗୋଳ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ଠାର,
ଭାବି କହଇ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ ନିଜ ନାମ ସ୍ମର।

Kumasulira mawu:

Bhubaneśwarare goḷa Khaṇḍagiri ṭhāra,
bhābi kahai Achyutī nija nāma smara.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, padzakhala nkhondo m'dera la Khandagiri ku Bhubaneswar.

Mmodzi mwa a Panchasakhas, Mahapurush Yasovanta Das, akulemba m'buku lake la Malika –

Odia Sloka:

ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱର ଠାରେ ଜାଣ,
ବିନ୍ଦୁ ସେ ସରୋବର ପୁଣ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Bhubaneśwara ṭhāre jāṇa,
Bindu se sarobara puṇa.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, dziwani kuti Bindu Sarovar ili ku Bhubaneswar.

Mahapurush Yasovanta Das akulemba m'buku lake la Malika ‘Bhavishyata Parardha’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱର ଲିଙ୍ଗରାଜ ସ୍ଥଳୀ,
ଦୁଇ ପୁରୁଷ ଏକଜଣ ସ୍ତିରୀ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Bhubaneśwara Liṅgarāja sthaḷī,
dui puruṣha ekajaṇa stirī.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, Bhubaneswar ndi mpando wa Lingaraj, kutanthauza kuti kachisi wa Lingaraj ali ku Bhubaneswar.

Ngati titagwiritsa ntchito Google, ngakhale pamenepo titha kudziwa mosavuta kuti kaya ndi Khandagiri kapena Ekamra Kanan kapena Bindu Sarovar kapena kachisi wa Lingaraj, zonse zili ku Bhubaneswar komweko.

Khandagiri, yomwe idzakhala malo okhala a Ambuye, kumene oyera mtima ambiri adzakhala akuyimba dzina la Kalki—kufotokoza kuti Khandagiri iyi ili ku Bhubaneswar (Ekamra Kshetra), Mahapurush Achyutananda Das analembanso mu ‘Chakada Madana’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ପାଦଦେଶେ ଭରତ ପାଦୁକା,
ଛଦ୍ମବେଶୀ ସାଧୁ ପୂଜା କରୁଅଛି ଏକା।
ଗୁପ୍ତଗଙ୍ଗା ବହିଅଛି ଗୁପ୍ତ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି,
ସିଦ୍ଧ ସାଧୁ ଗୁପ୍ତ ବେଶେ ରହିଛନ୍ତି ପୁରି।
ଏକାମ୍ର ବନ ଅଟଇ ଶିବ ଯୋଗ ସ୍ଥାନ,
ଶୁକପକ୍ଷୀ ପାଇଲା ଯେଉଁଠି ବ୍ରହ୍ମଜ୍ଞାନ।
ଭୂଆସୁଣୀ ଦେବୀ ଅଛି ଜାଣିଥା ସତ୍ୱର,
ସେ ଦେବୀ ଘାତରେ ପୃଥବୀ ହେବ କମ୍ପମାନ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Khaṇḍagiri pādadeśe Bharata pādukā, chhadmabeśī sādhu pūjā karuachhi ekā.
Guptagaṅgā bahiachhi gupta Khaṇḍagiri, siddha sādhu gupta beśe rahichhanti puri.
Ekāmra bana aṭai Śiba yoga sthāna, śukapakṣhī pāilā jeuṇṭhi brahmajñāna.
Bhūāsuṇୀ debī achhi jāṇithā satwara, se debī ghātare pruthibī heba kampamāna.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, kumapazi a phiri la Khandagiri kuli Bharata Paduka, yomwe woyera mtima wobisala akupembedza. Mu Khandagiri yomweyo, mtsinje wa Gupta Ganga ukuyenda mobisa. Kumeneko (ku Khandagiri), oyera mtima ambiri opambana akukhala mobisala. Nkhalango ya Ekamra ili kumeneko, yomwe ndi malo a Yoga a Ambuye Shiva. Kumeneko, mbalame ya Shuka inapeza chidziwitso cha Brahma. Pafupi pali Mulungu Bhuasuni, yemwe adzachita ntchito yowononga Padziko Lapansi kumapeto kwa Kali Yuga.

Ndikufuna kuuza owerenga pano kuti m'ndime imodzi yomwe ili pamwambapa, Mahapurush adanena kuti Khandagiri ili m'dera la nkhalango ya Ekamra ndipo oyera mtima ambiri akuchita mapemphero ku Khandagiri. Mtsinje ukuyendanso mobisa kumeneko. M'ndime ino, Mahapurush akubwereza zomwezo.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das analembanso mu ‘Tera Janma Sharana’ yake –

Odia Sloka:

ଲୀଳା ପ୍ରକାଶିବ ଲୀଳାମୟଙ୍କର ସତ୍ୟ ଯେ ଏକାମ୍ର ବନ,
ଲୀଳା କରୁଥିବେ ଅନନ୍ତ ମାଧବ ସର୍ବେ ଆନନ୍ଦ ହୋଇଣ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Līḷā prakāśiba līḷāmayankara satya je Ekāmra bana,
līḷā karuthibe Ananta Mādhaba sarbe ānanda hoiṇa.

Tanthauzo:
Apa Mahapurush adanenanso kuti malo okhala a Ambuye ndi Bhubaneswar. Koma sanatchule dzina la Bhubaneswar kapena Khandagiri. Mahapurush adatchula malowo kuti ‘Ekamra Van’. Mahapurush adalemba kuti Leela zaumulungu za Ambuye adzawonekera kuchokera kudera la Ekamra Van. M'dera lomwelo la Ekamra Van, Ambuye Ananta Madhava, ndiye kuti, Kalki, adzachita Leela zaumulungu, kuwona ndi kumva zomwe chisangalalo cha opembedza sichidzakhala ndi malire.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das analembanso mu ‘Aagata Bhavishyata Malika’ yake –

Odia Sloka:

କଳା ଧଳା ଅଶ୍ୱ ଉପରେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ହେବେ ବାହାର,
ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱରରେ ରହିବେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ରଙ୍ଗା ଅଧର।
ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ସିଦ୍ଧ ସ୍ଥାନରେ ଯେତେ ଅଛନ୍ତି ଋଷି,
ସକଳେ ବାହାର ହୋଇବେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ନାମକୁ ଘୋଷି।
ସକଳେ ସେବେଳେ ଠୁଳ ଯେ ହେବେ ବିନ୍ଦୁ ସାଗର,
କପିଳେଶ୍ୱରରେ ଭେଟିଣ ହୋଇବେ ସୂପକାର।

Kumasulira mawu:

Kaḷā dhaḷā ashwa upare prabhu hebe bāhāra,
Bhubaneshwarare rahibe prabhu raṅgā adhara.
Khaṇḍagiri siddha sthānare jete achhanti ruṣi,
sakaḷe bāhāra hoibe prabhu nāmaku ghoṣi.
Sakaḷe sebeḷe ṭhuḷa je hebe Bindu Sāgara,
Kapileshwarare bheṭiṇa hoibe sūpakāra.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, Ambuye adzapita kukawononga pa kavalo wa “Kala-Dhala” kapena wakuda ndi woyera. Asanatero, Ambuye adzakhala ku Bhubaneswar. Anzeru ndi amonke onse ochita mapemphero pamalo opambana a Khandagiri adzatuluka m'mapanga panthawiyo, akulengeza dzina la Ambuye, ndipo adzasonkhana ku Bindu Sarovar yomwe ili ku Bhubaneswar. Kenako, ophika adzakumana nawo onse ku kachisi wa Kapileshwar wapafupi.

M'ndime yomwe ili pamwambapa, Khandagiri imatchedwanso malo opambana omwe adzakhala malo okhala a anzeru ndi amonke ambiri. Ndipo akunenanso kuti kaya ndi Khandagiri kapena Bindu Sarovar kapena Kachisi wa Kapileshwar, zonsezi zili ku Bhubaneswar ndipo zili pafupi wina ndi mnzake.

Mmodzi mwa a Panchasakhas, Mahapurush Sishu Ananta Das, atafunsidwa ndi wophunzira wake Baranga Das kuti Ambuye Kalki adzabadwira kuti ndipo adzakhala kuti, akufotokoza m'buku lake la Malika ‘Chumbaka Malika’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଶୁଣ ହେ ବାରଙ୍ଗ କହିବା ସେ ରଙ୍ଗ ପ୍ରଭୁ ଅବତାର ସ୍ଥାନ,
ଶ୍ରୀ ବିରଜା କ୍ଷେତ୍ରେ ଜନମ ଲଭିବେ ଅନନ୍ତ ମିଶ୍ର ଗୃହେଣ।
ଜନମ ହୋଇବେ ଗୃହକୁ ଛାଡିବେ ତପସ୍ୟା କରିବେ ଯାଇଁ,
ସିଦ୍ଧଗିରି ସ୍ଥାନ ସିଦ୍ଧଙ୍କ ସଦନ ରହିବେ ସେ ଭାବଗ୍ରାହୀ।
ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ସ୍ଥାନ ଏକାମ୍ର କାନନ ସୁବର୍ଣ୍ଣଚ୍ୟୁତ ଗିରିରେ,
ସେହି ସ୍ଥାନେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ବିଜେ କରିଥିବେ ଭକ୍ତ ଜନ ସଙ୍ଗତରେ।
ଶ୍ରୀ ଅନନ୍ତ ବାସୁଦେବ ବିପ୍ରଲିଙ୍ଗ ଭେଟ ସେ ଯହିଁ ଆଗରେ,
ବିନ୍ଦୁସାଗରେ ସ୍ନାନ ବିଧି ସାରିଣ ସିଦ୍ଧ କେଦାର ମଧ୍ୟରେ।
ଶ୍ରୀ ବିନ୍ଦୁ ସାଗରେ ସ୍ନାନ ସାରିବେ ସମସ୍ତ ଭକତ ମିଳି,
ନିଜ କଳା ଭକ୍ତି କଳା ପ୍ରକାଶିବେ ଛାଡିବେ କଳିର ମଳି।

Kumasulira mawu:

Shuṇa he bāraṅga kahibā se raṅga prabhu abatāra sthāna,
Shrī Birajā kṣetre janama labhibe Ananta Mishra gruheṇa.
Janama hoibe gruhaku chhāḍibe tapasyā karibe jāiñ,
Siddhagiri sthāna siddhaṅka sadana rahibe se bhābagrāhī.
Khaṇḍagiri sthāna Ekāmra kānana Subarṇachyuta girire,
sehi sthāne prabhu bije karithibe bhakta jana saṅgatare.
Shrī Ananta Bāsudeba bipraliṅga bheṭa se jahiñ āgare,
Bindusāgare snāna bidhi sāriṇa siddha Kedāra madhyare.
Shrī Bindu Sāgare snāna sāribe samasta bhakata miḷi,
nija kaḷā bhakti kaḷā prakāshibe chhāḍibe kaḷira maḷi.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, Ambuye adzabadwira m'nyumba ya wopembedza wotchedwa Ananta Mishra ku Shri Viraja Kshetra (Jajpur). Atabadwa, Ambuye adzachoka panyumba pake ndikupita kumalo a oyera mtima opambana kukachita mapemphero. Mahapurush sanangoyimira ponena kuti "malo opambana," adanenanso kuti dzina la malowo lidzakhala Khandagiri, yomwe idzakhala ku Ekamra Kanan. M'dera lomwelo la Khandagiri la Ekamra Kanan, Ambuye adzakhala ndi opembedza Ake. Mahapurush adanena kuti kachisi wa Shri Ananta Vasudev adzakhala kumeneko, ndipo Linga, ndiye kuti, kachisi wa Lingaraj, adzakhalanso kumeneko. Ambuye adzasamba mu Bindu Sagar ndikupita kukachezera kachisi wa Siddha Kedara (Kedareshwar).

Mahapurush adanenanso kuti opembedza onse adzasamba mu Bindu Sarovar yomweyo, pambuyo pake kuwala kwaumulungu kudzawonekera mwa opembedza, ndipo onse adzachotsedwa dothi la Kali Yuga.

Kudzera m'ndime yomwe ili pamwambapa, Sishu Ananta Dasji adalongosola kuti kumene kuli kachisi wa Shri Ananta Vasudev, kumene kuli kachisi wa Kedareshwar, kumene kuli Lingaraj, mu Bhubaneswar yomweyo (dera la Ekamra Van), Khandagiri ili. Khandagiri iyi ndi dera la oyera mtima opambana, ndipo Ambuye adzakhala pano.

Kubadwanso kwa Achyutananda Dasji, Saint Hadi Das, adalemba m'buku lake la Malika ‘Kali Chautisha’ –

Odia Sloka:

କର୍ଣ୍ଣ ଦେଇ ଶୁଣ ସୁଜନେ କଳିଯୁଗ ବିଚାର,
କଳ୍କୀ ରୂପରେ ଜନମ ହେବେ ଶ୍ରୀ ଚକ୍ରଧର।
କ୍ଷୀର ଧାର ବହି ଯାଉଛି ସ୍ଥାନ ଅମରାବତୀ,
ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ସ୍ଥାନ ଗୁପତେ ସିଦ୍ଧ ସାଧୁ ଅଛନ୍ତି।
ଗୁମ୍ଫାରେ ଭଜନ ଲାଗିଛି ରାତ୍ର ଦିନ ମଧ୍ୟରେ,
ଗୁପତେ ଖେଳ ସେ ଖେଳିବେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ସେହି ଠାବରେ।
ଝାଡଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ଗୁମ୍ଫା ମଧ୍ୟରେ ପ୍ରଭୁଙ୍କର ସେ ଠାବ,
ଝଲମଲ ଜ୍ୟୋତି ବାହାରେ ଲୀଳା ଚହଟ ହେବ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Karṇa dei shuṇa sujane kaḷiyuga bichāra,
Kaḷkī rūpare janama hebe Shrī Chakradhara.
Kṣīra dhāra bahi jāuchhi sthāna Amarābatī,
Khaṇḍagiri sthāna gupate siddha sādhu achhanti.
Gumphāre bhajana lāgichhi rātra dina madhyare,
gupate kheḷa se kheḷibe prabhu sehi ṭhābare.
Jhāḍakhaṇḍagiri gumphā madhyare prabhuṅkara se ṭhāba,
jhalamala jyoti bāhāre līḷā chahaṭa heba.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, kumapeto kwa Kali Yuga, Ambuye Maha Vishnu adzabadwira Padziko Lapansi monga Kalki. Masewera Ake aumulungu adzachitika koyamba m'dera la mtsinje wa Kshira (Vaitarani), Amaravati, ndiye kuti, Jajpur. Kenako masewera Ake adzachitika ku Khandagiri kumene oyera mtima opambana adzakhala otanganidwa ndi bhajan-kirtan usana ndi usiku m'mapanga mobisa. Mahapurush adanena kuti Ambuye adzakhala ndikuchita masewera Ake m'dera lomwelo la Khandagiri kapena Jhadakhandagiri.

Saint Hadi Das adalemba m'buku lake la Malika ‘Kali Chautisha’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଘଟଣା ପ୍ରତିମା ପୁରୁଷ ଲିଙ୍ଗରାଜଙ୍କ ପୁର,
ଘୋର କଳି ମହାଭାରତ ହେବ ସେହି ଠାବର।

Kumasulira mawu:

Ghaṭaṇā pratimā puruṣha Liṅgarājaṅka pura,
ghora kaḷi mahābhārata heba sehi ṭhābara.

Tanthauzo:
Mahapurush akulemba kuti "Malo Ochitikira" adzakhala dera la Lingaraj kapena Bhubaneswar, ndipo nkhondo yotsala ya Mahabharata idzachitika kumeneko. Zikudziwika kuti tidawonapo kale ndime yomwe Mahapurush Achyutananda Das adalemba – “Ghatana sthana je atai sehi, kalira bharata yuddha sethahin”. Achyutananda Dasji adalemba ndime iyi yokhudza Khandagiri m'buku lake la Malika ‘Chakada Madana’ ndipo adanena kuti Khandagiri ndi “Ghatana Sthana,” kumene nkhondo yotsala ya Mahabharata idzachitika.

Pambuyo pake, Hadi Das adalemba – Bhubaneswar kapena dera la Lingaraj ndi “Ghatana Sthana” ndipo nkhondo ya Kali Mahabharata idzachitika kumeneko.

Izi zikutanthauza kuti zomwe Achyutananda Das akunena za Khandagiri mu ‘Chakada Madana’ ndi zomwe kubadwanso kwake, Hadi Das, akunena za dera la Lingaraj (Bhubaneswar) mu ‘Kali Chautisha’. Izi zikutsimikizira kuti malo onse awiri, ndiye kuti, Khandagiri ndi Bhubaneswar, akuloza kumalo amodzi. Mwachidule, Khandagiri ili ku Bhubaneswar.

Zindikirani – Mpaka pano m'ndime zambiri zomwe zili pamwambapa, taphunzira kuti Khandagiri ku Bhubaneswar ndi dera la oyera mtima opambana ndi oyera. Oyera mtimawo ali otanganidwa ndi bhajan-kirtan ya Ambuye Kalki usana ndi usiku m'mapanga a Khandagiri. Tsopano tidzaphunzira kuti ndi anzeru ndi amonke ati omwe adzachita mapemphero kwa Ambuye Kalki ku Khandagiri kumeneko komanso kuti ndi anzeru angati omwe adzachita bhajan-kirtan m'mapanga.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akulemba mu ‘Aagata Bhavishya Prabandha Malika’ yake –

Odia Sloka:

ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରିକି ଯେ ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱର କେତେକ ଦୂର,
ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ମଧ୍ୟେ ଅଛନ୍ତି ସାଧୁସନ୍ଥ ନିକର।
ନାରଦ ମାର୍କଣ୍ଡ ଶମ୍ଭୁ ଯେ ଅଗସ୍ତି ଉପଲସ୍ତି,
ଦୁର୍ବାସା କଶ୍ୟପ ଚ୍ୟବନ କୁମ୍ଭଋଷି ସନ୍ତତି।
ପରାଶର ବଶିଷ୍ଠାଦି ହୋ ବିଶ୍ୱାମିତ୍ରଙ୍କୁ ଘେନି,
ଷାଠିଏ ସହସ୍ର ଋଷି ହୋ ଗିରି ମଧ୍ୟରେ ପୁଣି।
ଦେବଋଷି ବ୍ରହ୍ମଋଷି ଯେ ସର୍ବେ ମେଳି ହୋଇଣ,
ଗୁମ୍ଫାରେ ଭଜନ କରନ୍ତି କଳ୍କୀ ନାମ ପୁଣ।
ଦେବତାମାନେ ଯେ ସେଠାରେ ରାତ୍ରେ ଗୋଷ୍ଠୀ କରନ୍ତି,
କଳ୍କୀର ଆଦ୍ୟ ମନ୍ତ୍ରକୁ ନିତ୍ୟେ ଜପୁଛନ୍ତି।

Kumasulira mawu:

Khaṇḍagiriki je Bhubaneshwara keteka dūra,
Khaṇḍagiri madhye achhanti sādhusantha nikara.
Nārada Mārkaṇḍa Shambhu je Agasti Upalasti,
Durbāsā Kashyapa Chyabana Kumbharuṣi santati.
Parāshara Bashiṣṭhādi ho Bishwāmitraṅku gheni,
ṣāṭhie sahasra ruṣi ho giri madhyare puṇi.
Debaruṣi Brahmaruṣi je sarbe meḷi hoiṇa,
gumphāre bhajana karanti Kaḷkī nāma puṇa.
Debatāmāne je seṭhāre rātre goṣṭhī karanti,
Kaḷkīra ādya mantraku nitye japuchhanti.

Tanthauzo:
Mahapurush adagwira mawu zokambirana za Krishna-Garuda m'buku lake la Malika. Garuda akuti dera la Khandagiri sili kutali ndi Bhubaneswar (lili pafupi kwambiri). Kuli oyera mtima ambiri ku Khandagiri. Kumeneko, Narada, Markandeya, Shambhu, Agastya, Upalasti, Durvasa, Kashyapa, Chyavana, “Kumbharushi Santati” kutanthauza Dridhasyu, Parashara, Va\-shish\-tha, Vishwamitra, ndi ena, okwana anzeru khumi ndi awiri ndipo kuphatikizapo iwo okwana anzeru 60,000, Devarishis, ndi Brahmarishis ali otanganidwa ndi dzina-bhajan la Ambuye Kalki m'mapanga. Milungu imachita misonkhano m'mapanga amenewo usiku ndipo tsiku lililonse imayimba mantra ya Ambuye Kalki.

M'ndime ino, Garudaji sanangofotokoza za anzeru ochita mapemphero m'mapanga a Khandagiri komanso adanena kuti phanga la Khandagiri silidzakhala kutali ndi Bhubaneswar, kapena kuti, lidzakhala pafupi kwambiri.

Kuchokera m'ndime 15 zomwe zili pamwambapa, ziyenera kuti zakhala zomveka kwa owerenga kuti malinga ndi Bhavishya Malika, Khandagiri—kumene Kalki adzakhala, kumene oyera mtima opambana osawerengeka adzakhala akukhala, kumene bhajan-kirtan ya dzina la Kalki idzakhala ikuchitika mosalekeza m'mapanga, kumene mtsinje udzayenda mobisa, kumene nkhondo yotsala ya Mahabharata idzachitika—ili ku Bhubaneswar kokha kapena dera la Lingaraj kapena dera la Ekamra Van, ndipo palibe kwina.

10 Cholinga cha Kubadwa kwa Kalki

Monga tonse tikudziwa, Ambuye amatenga kubadwa khumi Padziko Lapansi kuti akhazikitse chilungamo (Dharma). Kaya ndi Satya Yuga, Treta Yuga, kapena Dvapara Yuga, cholinga cha kubadwa kwa Ambuye Padziko Lapansi chimakhalabe chimodzimodzi—kukhazikitsa Dharma.

Mu Shrimad Bhagavad Gita, Ambuye Krishna adauza Arjuna –

Sloka ya Sanskrit:

परित्राणाय साधूनां विनाशाय च दुष्कृताम् ।
धर्मसंस्थापनार्थाय संभवामि युगे युगे ।।

Tanthauzo:
Kuti nditeteze oyera mtima (opembedza), kuti ndiwononge oipa, ndiponso kuti ndikhazikitse chilungamo cholimba, ndimadzionetsera ndekha nthawi ndi nthawi.

M'mabuku a Bhavishya Malika nawonso, zikunenedwa kuti pofuna kuwononga oipa, kuteteza opembedza ndi oyera mtima, ndiponso kukhazikitsanso Dharma, Ambuye Maha Vishnu adzatsika Padziko Lapansi monga kubadwa Kwake kwachikhumi ndi komaliza, Kalki.

Zokambirana pakati pa Ambuye Krishna ndi bwenzi lake Arjuna mu Dvapara Yuga zidagwiridwa mawu zaka pafupifupi 600 zapitazo mu Kali Yuga ndi Mahapurush Achyutananda Das m'buku lake la Malika 'Chausathi Patala'. Mahapurush adalemba –

Odia Sloka:

ପାପ ଭାରା ପୃଥ୍ବୀ ସହି ନପାରି,
ମୋ ଆଗରେ କଲା ବେଗେ ଗୁହାରି।
କଳ୍କୀ ଅବତାର ମୁହିଁ ହୋଇବି,
ଦୁଷ୍ଟ ପ୍ରାଣୀମାନଙ୍କୁ ବିନାଶିବି।
ବସି ନରପତି ହୃଦ ଦେଶରେ
ପ୍ରଜା ପାଳିବି ଉତ୍ତମ ଭାବରେ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Pāpa bhārā pṛthwī sahi napāri,
mo āgare kalā bege guhāri.
Kaḷkī abatāra muhiñ hoibi,
duṣṭa prāṇīmānaṅku bināshibi.
Basi narapati hṛda deshare,
prajā pāḷibi uttama bhābare.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, Dziko Lapansi, popeza silingathe kunyamula katundu wa machimo kumapeto kwa Kali Yuga, lidzapempha pamaso panga. Kenako ndidzatenga kubadwa kwa Kalki ndipo ndidzawononga oipa ndikupulumutsa opembedza. Ndikukhala ku India, ndidzasamalira ulamuliro wa dziko lonse ndipo ndidzalamulira anthu mwachilungamo komanso moyenera.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akulemba m'buku lina la Malika –

Odia Sloka:

ମହାଭୟଙ୍କର ଉତ୍ପାତ ହେବ ଧରିତ୍ରୀ ବକ୍ଷରେ ଜାଣ,
କଳ୍କୀ ରୂପରେ ମ୍ଲେଚ୍ଛ ସଂହାରି ଭାରା ଉଶ୍ୱାସିବେ ପୁଣ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Mahābhayaṅkara utpāta heba dharitrī bakṣhare jāṇa,
Kaḷkī rūpare mlechha saṃhāri bhārā uśwāsibe puṇa.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, padzakhala chisokonezo chachikulu Padziko Lapansi. Kalki mwiniwake adzawononga oipa ndikuchotsa katundu wa machimo a Dziko Lapansi.

Pogwira mawu zokambirana za Krishna-Arjuna, Mahapurush akulembanso m'buku lake la Malika –

Odia Sloka:

କଳି ଶେଷ ବେଳେ ମୁହିଁ କଳ୍କୀ ରୂପ ହେବି,
ସନ୍ଥଙ୍କୁ ରଖିଣ ଦୁଷ୍ଟଜନମାନଙ୍କୁ ନାଶିବି।

Kumasulira mawu:

Kaḷi śeṣha beḷe muñhi Kaḷkī rūpa hebi,
santhaṅku rakhiṇa duṣhṭajanamānanku nāśhibi.

Tanthauzo:
Shri Krishna akuti kumapeto kwa Kali Yuga, ndidzatenga kubadwa kwa Kalki. Ndikuteteza oyera mtima, anthu oyera, ndi anthu okhala ndi mtima woyera, ndidzawononga oipa ndi ochimwa onse.

Pogwira mawu mawu a Ambuye kachiwiri, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akulemba m'buku lake la Malika 'Chausathi Patala' –

Odia Sloka:

ବେଶୀ ପାପ ଯେତେବେଳେ ହେବ କଳ୍କୀ ରୂପେ ମୁଁ ଉଦୟ ହେବି,
ଓଡିଶା ମଧ୍ୟରେ ସ୍ଥାନେ ସ୍ଥାନେ ମନ ପରୀକ୍ଷା ଯାଇ କରିବି।

Kumasulira mawu:

Beśī pāpa jetebeḷe heba Kaḷkī rūpe mu udaya hebi,
Oḍiśā madhyare sthāne sthāne mana parīkṣhā jāi karibi.

Tanthauzo:
Ambuye akuti mu Kali Yuga, pamene tchimo lifika pachimake, ndidzauka mu mawonekedwe anga a Kalki. Ndidzayesa opembedza m'malo osiyanasiyana ku Odisha, kusankha pakati pa zabwino ndi zoipa, ndikuchita motero.

Pogwira mawu a Ambuye, mmodzi wa Panchasakhas, Mahapurush Balaram Das, analemba m'buku lake la Malika 'Kali Aagata Bhavishyanta' –

Odia Sloka:

ଚଣ୍ଡୀ ଯେ ଚାମୁଣ୍ଡା ମାତିବେ,
ଖପର କରେ ଘେନିଥିବେ।
ରୋଧିର ବୃଷ୍ଟି ଯେ ହୋଇବ,
କେ ଅବା କାହିଁ ନାଶ ଯିବ।
ଯାହାକୁ ରଖିବିଟି ଟିକିଏ ମୁହିଁ,
ସେ ପ୍ରାଣୀମାନେ ଥିବେ ରହି।
ଦୁଷ୍ଟ ଲୋକ ଯେ ନାଶ ହେବେ,
ସନ୍ଥ ଯେ ଅବଶ୍ୟ ରହିବେ।
ଅନେକ ଉତ୍ପାତ ଲାଗିବ,
ଶୂନ୍ୟରେ ଚହଳ ପଡିବ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Chaṇḍī je Chāmuṇḍā mātibe,
khapara kare ghenithibe.
Rodhira bṛṣṭi je hoiba,
ke abā kāhiñ nāsha jiba.
Jāhāku rakhibiṭi ṭikie muhiñ,
se prāṇīmāne thibe rahi.
Duṣṭa loka je nāsha hebe,
santha je abashya rahibe.
Aneka utpāta lāgiba,
shūnyare chahaḷa paḍiba.

Tanthauzo:
Ambuye akuti pa nthawi yokhazikitsa Dharma kumapeto kwa Kali Yuga, Amayi Ashta Chandis ndi Yoginis ena adzayamba kuwononga kwambiri. Padzagwa mvula yamagazi kuchokera kumwamba. Padzakhala zosokoneza zambiri ndipo masoka adzachokera ku chabe, mwachitsanzo, kuchokera kumlengalenga. Okhawo amene ndidzawateteza adzakhala otetezeka. Oipa adzawonongedwa, ndipo oyera mtima ndi anthu opatulika adzapulumutsidwa.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akulemba m'buku lake la Malika 'Kali Kalpa Gita' –

Odia Sloka:

କଳ୍କୀ ରାମ ରୂପ ଧରି ହରି ଦେଶ ଭ୍ରମଣ କରିବେ,
କ୍ଷତ୍ରୀମାନଙ୍କୁ ନିକ୍ଷତ୍ରୀ କରିଣ ବସୁଧାକୁ ଉଦ୍ଧାରିବେ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Kaḷkī Rāma rūpa dhari Hari desha bhramaṇa karibe,
kṣhatrīmānanku nikṣhatrī kariṇa basudhāku uddhāribe.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, kumapeto kwa Kali Yuga, Ambuye adzatenga mawonekedwe Ake a Kalki ndikuyenda dziko lonse lapansi ndipo adzapulumutsa Amayi Dziko Lapansi powononga oipa.

Mu 'Kali Kalpa Gita' momwemo, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akulembanso –

Odia Sloka:

ମ୍ଲେଚ୍ଛ ନିଧନ ଦୁଷ୍ଟ ସଂହାରଣ,
କରିବେ ନିଶ୍ଚେ ଦେବ କଳ୍କୀ ରାମ।
ଏମନ୍ତ ମହୀ ଯେ ହେବ ଉଶ୍ୱାସ,
କଳିକଳ୍ପ କହେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଦାସ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Mlechha nidhana duṣhṭa saṃharaṇa,
karibe niśhche deba Kaḷkī Rāma.
Emanta mahī je heba uśwāsa,
Kaḷikalpa kahe Achyuta dāsa.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, Ambuye Kalki adzapulumutsadi Dziko Lapansi powononga Mlechhas (anthu osaphunzira/oipa) ndi kuwononga oipa.

10.1 Kubwera kwa Ambuye Kalki

Mmodzi wa Panchasakhas, Mzimu Wamkulu Sishu Ananta Das, analemba pa nkhaniyi m'buku lake la Malika 'Chumbaka Malika':

Odia Sloka:

କଳ୍କୀ ଅବତାର ଓଡ଼ିଶା ମଣ୍ଡଳେ ତୁମ୍ଭେ ଜନମ ହୋଇବ,
ଦୁଷ୍ଟ ଜନ ନାଶି ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କୁ ଆଶ୍ୱାସି ଧର୍ମକୁ ତୁମ୍ଭେ ପାଳିବ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Kaḷkī abatāra Oḍiśā maṇḍaḷe tumbhe janama hoiba,
duṣhṭa jana nāśhi bhaktaṅku āśwāsi dharmaku tumbhe pāliba.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, Ambuye Wamkulu Kalki adzabadwira ku Odisha. Pambuyo pake, Ambuye adzawononga oipa ndikuteteza opembedza kuti akhazikitsenso Dharma.

11 Zichitika za Chiwonongeko: Chiyambi ndi Mapeto Okhazikika?

Monga tikudziwira kuchokera ku zolemba za Bhabishya Malika, mu nthawi yosintha pakati pa Kali Yuga ndi Satya Yuga, zochitika zowononga zosayembekezereka zidzachitika padziko lonse lapansi motsogozedwa ndi Ambuye Kalki, kudzera mwa izo Mlecchas (anthu osaphunzira), ochimwa, oipa, ndi osalungama onse adzawonongedwa. Bhabishya Malika imaperekanso chitsimikizo kuti opembedza onse adzakhala otetezeka pa nthawi ya zochitika izi ndipo sayenera kuopa.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, m'buku lake la Malika 'Chhayalisha Patala', akugwira mawu a Ambuye Wamkulu momwe Ambuye akupereka tsatanetsatane wa chiwonongeko chochepa chomwe chikubwera (Khanda Pralaya).

Mahapurush akulemba:

Odia Sloka:

କାଳଭାବେ ମହାଶକ୍ତି ଖଣ୍ଡ ପ୍ରଳୟରେ,
ସୃଷ୍ଟି ନାଶ କରି ଲୀଳା କରେ ପାପ ଭାରେ।
ମାତ୍ର ମହାପ୍ରଳୟକୁ ରହିଥିବୁ ଟାକି,
ସେ କାଳରେ ଚରାଚର ନ ରହିବେ ବାକି।
ଚଣ୍ଡୀ ଚାମୁଣ୍ଡା ସହିତ ସଂହାରିଣୀ ଶକ୍ତି,
ଖଣ୍ଡା ଖପର ହାତରେ ବୁଲି ଦିବାରାତ୍ରି।
ରୋଗ ଶୋକ ଦୁଃଖ ଜାଲ ସଂସାରେ ପାତିବେ,
ପର ଆପଣାର ଭେଦ କିଛି ନ ରଖିବେ।
ଅଣଚାଶ ପବନ ଯେ ରୁଦ୍ର ମୂର୍ତ୍ତି ଧରି,
ଉପାଡ଼ିବ ସୁମେରୁ ସହିତ ଯେତେ ଗିରି।
ଚାରିମେଘ ଚାରିଦିଗେ ଆଚ୍ଛନ୍ନ କରିବେ,
ମହାଘୋର ବିପତ୍ତିକୁ ଆଣି ଘଟାଇବେ।
ଅନ୍ଧାର ହୋଇବ ପୃଥ୍ବୀ ବଢ଼ିବ ଉତ୍ପାତ,
ନକ୍ଷତ୍ର ଉଲକା ଛିଣ୍ଡିବ ନାହିଁ ଆଦି ଅନ୍ତ।
ଭୈରବୀ ରଡ଼ିବେ ପୃଥ୍ବୀ କାନ୍ଦିବ ସକଳ,
ପାପମାତ୍ରା ପ୍ରବଳରେ ହେବ ଏହି ଫଳ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Kālabhābe mahāshakti khaṇḍa praḷayare,
Sṛṣṭi nāsha kari līḷā kare pāpa bhāre.
Mātra mahāpraḷayaku rahithibu ṭāki,
Se kāḷare charāchara na rahibe bāki.
Chaṇḍī Chāmuṇḍā sahita saṃhāriṇī shakti,
Khaṇḍā khapara hātare buli dibārātri.
Roga shoka duḥkha jāla saṃsāre pātibe,
Para āpaṇāra bheda kichhi na rakhibe.
Aṇachāsha pabana je rudra mūrtti dhari,
Upāḍiba Sumeru sahita jete giri.
Chārimegha chāridige āchhanna karibe,
Mahāghora bipattiku āṇi ghaṭāibe.
Andhāra hoiba pṛthwī baḍhiba utpāta,
Nakṣatra ulakā chhiṇḍiba nāhiñ ādi anta.
Bhairabī raḍibe pṛthwī kāndiba sakaḷa,
Pāpamātrā prabaḷare heba ehi phaḷa.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, Mphamvu Yaikulu mu mawonekedwe a Nthawi (Kala) idzabweretsa chiwonongeko chochepa ndikuwononga dziko lapansi. Pa nthawi ya chiwonongeko, zamoyo zonse zidzakhudzidwa. Chandi-Chamunda ndi mphamvu zowononga zidzayenda usana ndi usiku zitanyamula malupanga (Khanda) ndi zigaza (Khapar). Ululu, kuzunzika, matenda, ndi chisoni zidzafalikira padziko lonse lapansi. Kusiyana konse pakati pa 'wako' ndi 'mlendo' kudzatha. Mphepo makumi anayi ndi zisanu ndi zinayi, zitenga mawonekedwe owopsa a Rudra, zidzazulira mapiri onse kuphatikizapo Sumeru wamkulu. Mitambo inayi, yomwe ndi Avarta, Samvarta, Pushkara, ndi Drona, idzazungulira mbali zinayi ndikupanga masoka owopsa. Mdima waukulu udzaphimba Dziko Lapansi; dzuwa ndi mwezi sizidzawala. Pa nthawi imeneyo, nyenyezi zowala zidzagwa kuchokera kumwamba ndikukhala chifukwa cha chiwonongeko. Mu mdima umenewo, Amayi Bhairavi adzabangula mwaukali ndikuwononga ochimwa osawerengeka.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, pogwira mawu a Ambuye, akulemba m'buku lake la Malika 'Agata Bhabishyata Malika':

Odia Sloka:

କାହିଁ ଜଳ ରୂପେ ମୁହିଁ ଜଗତ ନାଶିବି,
କାହିଁ ପବନ ରୂପରେ ସକଳ ଗ୍ରାସିବି।
କାହିଁ ଯୋଗମାୟା ନେଇ ସଂହାର କରିବି,
କାହିଁ ନିଜେ ବିଶ୍ଵରୂପ ଧାରଣ କରିବି।
କାହିଁ ମଉନ ବ୍ରତ ମୁଁ ଧାରଣ କରିବି,
କାହିଁ କାଳରୂପେ ମୁହିଁ ପାପୀଙ୍କୁ ନାଶିବି।

Kumasulira mawu:

Kāhiñ jala rūpe muhiñ jagata nāshibi,
kāhiñ pabana rūpare sakaḷa grāsibi.
kāhiñ yogamāyā nei saṃhāra karibi,
kāhiñ nije bishwarūpa dhāraṇa karibi.
kāhiñ mauna brata muñ dhāraṇa karibi,
kāhiñ kāḷarūpe muhiñ pāpīṅku nāshibi.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, Ambuye Kalki adzabweretsa chiwonongeko chachikulu padziko lapansi m'mawonekedwe Ake osiyanasiyana. M'malo ena, Adzatenga mawonekedwe a madzi ndikumiza chilichonse; m'malo ena, Adzaomba chilichonse kutali mu chiwonongeko cha mphepo. Nthawi zina, Adzabweretsa chiwonongeko pamene akusunga lonjezo la chete, ndipo m'malo ena, Adzapha oipa mu mawonekedwe a Nthawi. M'malo ena, Adzatenga mawonekedwe Ake Onse, ndipo m'malo ena, Adzabweretsa chiwonongeko kudzera mu mphamvu Yake ya Yogamaya.

Malinga ndi Bhabishya Malika, Ambuye Kalki adzabweretsa chiwonongeko ichi kudzera mu madzi osefukira owopsa, zivomezi zazikulu, mphepo zamkuntho zowononga, madzi osefukira amoto, masoka a mlengalenga, miliri yoopsa, ndi nkhondo yapadziko lonse ya nyukiliya.

11.1 Zivomezi

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das analemba m'buku lake la Malika 'Gupta Kheda Malika':

Odia Sloka:

ହିମାଳୟ ଏଭରେଷ୍ଟ ଟଳି ଯେ ପଡିବ,
ଯେବେ ଏହି ଧରା ପରେ ଭୂମିକମ୍ପ ହେବ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Himālaya Everest ṭaḷi je paḍiba,
Jebe ehi dharā pare bhūmikampa heba.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, pa nthawi ya machitidwe owononga a Kalki kumapeto kwa Kali Yuga, zivomezi zamphamvu zotere zidzagwedeza Dziko Lapansi kotero kuti ngakhale 'Himalayas' ndi nsonga zake monga 'Everest' zidzasweka. Opembedza adzakhala otetezeka, pamene ochimwa adzawonongedwa.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akulembanso m'buku lake la Malika 'Gupta Kheda Malika':

Odia Sloka:

ଭୂମିକମ୍ପେ ପ୍ରକମ୍ପିତ ହୋଇବ ଧରଣୀ,
ମାଟି ତଳେ ଲୁଚିଯିବ କୋଠାବାଡି ପୁଣି।

Kumasulira mawu:

Bhūmikampe prakampita hoiba dharaṇī,
māṭi taḷe luchijiba koṭhābāḍi puṇi.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, Dziko Lapansi lonse lidzagwedezeka ndi zivomezi, ndipo chifukwa cha zivomezi zimenezo, nyumba zazikulu zidzakwiriridwa pansi pa nthaka.

Mahapurush analemba m'buku lina la Malika:

Odia Sloka:

ପାତାଳେ ବାସୁକୀ ଟେକିବ ମୁଣ୍ଡ,
ତିନିଥର ଯେ କମ୍ପିବ ବ୍ରହ୍ମାଣ୍ଡ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Pātāḷe Bāsukī ṭekiba muṇḍa,
tini thara je kampiba brahmāṇḍa.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, chifukwa cha kulemera kwambiri kwa machimo kumapeto kwa Kali Yuga, njoka Vasuki, yomwe imagwira Dziko Lapansi pamutu pake, idzagwedeza mutu wake mobwerezabwereza. Chifukwa cha izi, zivomezi zazikulu zitatu zidzachitika pa Dziko Lapansi.

Woyera mtima wamkulu wobadwira ku Odisha m'zaka za zana la 20, Thakur Shri Abhiram Paramahansa, analemba m'buku lake la Malika 'Baishnaba Gita':

Odia Sloka:

ଭୂମିକମ୍ପ ହେବ ଅପାର,
ନାଶ ହୋଇବେ ନାରୀ ନର।

Kumasulira mawu:

Bhūmikampa heba apāra,
nāśha hoibe nārī nara.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, mu gawo lomaliza la Kali Yuga, padzakhala zivomezi zosawerengeka zomwe amuna ndi akazi osawerengeka adzafera.

11.2 Madzi Osefukira (Jala Pralaya)

Pamene akukambirana za zoopsa za madzi osefukira, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akulemba m'buku lake la Malika 'Gupta Kheda Malika':

Odia Sloka:

ଭାରତକୁ ଛାଡି ଦେଲେ ସବୁ ରାଷ୍ଟ୍ର ଯିବ, ମହାସାଗର ଜଳରେ ଡୁବି ମରୁଥିବ।
ଧରଣୀ ଦେହରେ ଜଳ ମାରିବ ଲହରୀ, ଜଳାର୍ଣ୍ଣବ ହୋଇଯିବ ଚଉଦିଗ ଭାରି।
ଅଛଇ ବରଫଗଡ଼ ଉତ୍ତର ମେରୁରେ, ତରଳିବ ଧୀରେ ଧୀରେ ସୂର୍ଯ୍ୟଙ୍କ ରଶ୍ମିରେ।
ରୌଦ୍ରତାପେ ଯେତେବେଳେ ମେଦିନୀ କମ୍ପିବ, ସେତିକିବେଳେ ବରଫଗଡ଼ ତରଳିବ।
ବହିବ ପାଣିର ସୁଅ ସମୁଦ୍ରକୁ ପୁଣ, ଆଟଲାଣ୍ଟିକ ବଢ଼ିବ କାହିଁ କେତେ ଜାଣ।
ସମୁଦ୍ରର ପାଣି ଧୀରେ ବଢ଼ି ବଢ଼ି ଯିବ, ସାଗର ମାତିବ ଶେଷେ ଆଉ ଯାହା ଥିବ।
ଅନାଉ ଅନାଉ କାହିଁ ଜଳ ମାଡିଯିବ, ମାଟିର ଲକ୍ଷେ ଘନ ଫୁଟ ଉପରେ ରହିବ।
ଭୂଗୋଳର ସ୍ଥିତି ବଡ଼ ବିଚିତ୍ର ତୁ ଜାଣ, ଭାରତ ରହିଛି ସର୍ବ ଉର୍ଦ୍ଧ୍ଵରେ ତ ପୁଣ।
ସୃଷ୍ଟିର କର୍ତ୍ତା ପ୍ରଭୁ ଜଗତପତି ଜାଣ, ଭାରତ ଭୂମିରେ କରିବେ ବୈକୁଣ୍ଠ ସ୍ଥାପନ।
ପାଣିର ଘେରରେ ଆଉ ସବୁ ମହାଦେଶ, ଧୀରେ ଜାଣ ବୁଡିଯିବ ସେ ମହାବିନାଶ।
ଦେଖିବ ଏ ଦୁଇ ଆଖି ସମୟ ଆସିଛି, ସାମନା କରି ପାରିଲେ ସମୟକୁ ଇଛି।

Kumasulira mawu:

Bhāratku chāḍidele sabu rāṣṭra jiba, Mahāsāgara jalare ḍubi maruthiba.
Dharaṇī dehare jala māriba laharī, Jalārṇaba hoijiba chaudiga bhāri.
Achai baraphagaḍa uttara merure, taraḷiba dhīre dhīre sūryāṅka raśmire.
Raudratāpe jetebeḷe medinī kampiba, setikibeḷe baraphagaḍa taraḷiba.
Bahiba pāṇira sua samudruku puṇa, Āṭlāṇṭika baḍhiba kāhiñ kete jāṇa.
Samudrara pāṇi dhīre baḍhi baḍhi jiba, sāgara mātiba śeṣe āu jāhā thiba.
Anāu anāu kāhiñ jala māḍijiba, māṭira lakṣhe ghana phuṭa upare rahiba.
Bhūgoḷara sthiti baḍa bichitra tu jāṇa, Bhārata rahichi sarba urdhware ta puṇa.
Sruṣṭira kartā prabhu jagatpati jāṇa, Bhārata bhūmire karibe Baikuṇṭha sthāpana.
Pāṇira gherare āu sabu mahādeśa, dhīre jāṇa buḍijiba se mahābināśa.
Dekhiba e dui ākhi samaya āsichi, sāmnā karipārile samayaku ichhi.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, kupatula India, pafupifupi mayiko onse adzamira m'madzi a nyanja. Padzakhala madzi osefukira pa Dziko Lapansi, ndipo mbali zonse zinayi zidzamira. Chifukwa cha kutentha kwa dzuwa komwe kukuchulukirachulukira, madzi oundana omwe ali ku North Pole adzayamba kusungunuka, zomwe zidzakhudza kwambiri Nyanja ya Atlantic. Madzi m'nyanja adzapitirira kukwera, zomwe zidzachititsa kuti mizinda ya m'mphepete mwa nyanja imire. M'malo ena, mafunde a tsunami okwera ngati mapiri adzauka. Dziko lonse lidzasintha. Koma Ambuye Wamkulu adzateteza India, ndipo zotsatira za madzi osefukira awa zidzakhala zochepa ku India. Kupatula India, makontinenti ena onse adzagwa chifukwa cha madzi osefukira.

11.3 Mphepo Yowononga (Pavan Pralaya)

Mmodzi wa Panchasakhas, Mahapurush Sishu Ananta Das, akulemba m'buku lake la Malika 'Patta Madana':

Odia Sloka:

ଧର୍ମହୀନ ହେବେ ସକଳ ଏ ଜନ ଧର୍ମାଧର୍ମ ନ ରହିବ,
ଅଣଚାଶ ବାୟୁ ବହିବ ଏମନ୍ତ ପୃଥିବୀ ଟଳଟଳ ଧ୍ରୁବ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Dharmahīna hebe sakaḷa e jana dharmādharma na rahiba,
aṇachāśha bāyu bahiba emanta pruthibī ṭaḷaṭaḷa dhruba.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, kumapeto kwa Kali Yuga, aliyense adzakhala wosalungama; palibe amene adzakhala ndi chipembedzo/ntchito yotsalira. Ichi chidzakhala chifukwa chake Ambuye Wamkulu Kalki adzakhazikitsa Dharma. Mphepo makumi anayi ndi zisanu ndi zinayi zidzaomba motere kuti Dziko Lapansi lidzagwedezeka.

Mahapurush akulemba m'buku lina la Malika:

Odia Sloka:

ପବନ ବହି ଅଣଚାଶ,
କ୍ଷିତିକି କରିଦେବ ଧ୍ଵଂସ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Pabana bahi aṇachāśha,
kṣhitiki karideba dhwaṃsa.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, pa nthawi ya machitidwe owononga a Ambuye Kalki, mphepo makumi anayi ndi zisanu ndi zinayi zidzaomba ndikubweretsa chiwonongeko ku Dziko Lapansi.

Woyera mtima wakhungu wobadwira ku Odisha m'zaka za zana la 19, Bhima Bhoi, analembanso pa nkhaniyi m'buku lake la Malika. Mahapurush akulemba:

Odia Sloka:

ଅଣଚାଶ ଖଣ୍ଡ ପବନ ବହିବ ପର୍ବତ ଦୋହଲିଯିବ,
ଚାରିମେଘ ମିଳି ବର୍ଷିବେ ଚାରିଦିଗ ଅନ୍ଧାର ଘୋଟିବ।
ମୋ ଭାଇ, ଚନ୍ଦ୍ର ସୂର୍ଯ୍ୟ ଦେଖାଯିବ ନାହିଁ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Aṇachāśha khaṇḍa pabana bahiba parbata dohalijiba,
chārimegha miḷi barṣhibe chāridiga andhāra ghoṭiba.
Mo bhāi, chandra sūrya dekhājiba nāhiñ.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, kumapeto kwa Kali Yuga, nthawi idzafika pamene mphepo makumi anayi ndi zisanu ndi zinayi zidzaomba ndipo ngakhale mapiri ndi zitunda zidzagwedezeka. Mitambo inayi yotchedwa Avarta, Samvarta, Pushkara, ndi Drona idzagwa mvula pamodzi. Dzuwa ndi mwezi sizidzawoneka kumwamba, ndipo mdima waukulu udzaphimba mbali zonse zinayi.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das analembanso:

Odia Sloka:

ଟଳଟଳ ହେଉଥିବ ସପ୍ତଦ୍ଵୀପ ମହୀ,
ସେ ବେଳେ ଥଳକୂଳ ରହିବଟି ନାହିଁ।
ଅଣଚାଶ ଖଣ୍ଡ ହୋଇ ପବନ ବହିବ,
ସରକାର ବୁଦ୍ଧିବିଦ୍ୟା ଉଭେଇ ଯିବ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Ṭaḷaṭaḷa heuthibā saptadwīpa mahī,
se beḷe thaḷakūḷa rahibāṭi nāhiñ.
aṇachāśha khaṇଡa hoi pabana bahiba,
sarakāra buddhibidyā ubhei jiba.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, pa nthawi yokhazikitsa Dharma kumapeto kwa Kali Yuga, padzakhala chisokonezo padziko lonse lapansi kuphatikizapo makontinenti asanu ndi awiri. Kuomba ndi ukali waukulu kwambiri, mphepo makumi anayi ndi zisanu ndi zinayi zidzawononga nyumba zonse. Pa nthawi imeneyo, palibe amene adzakhala otetezeka. Mphamvu ya boma ndi nzeru zake sizidzakhala zopanda ntchito. Okhawo amene amatsatira njira ya Dharma ndipo adzipereka kwathunthu kwa Ambuye Wamkulu adzalandira chitetezo.

Odia Sloka:

କର ପତ୍ର ଯୋଡି କହଇ ବାରଙ୍ଗ ଗୁରୁପାଦେ ଶିର ଦେଇ,
ପ୍ରଳୟ କାଳର ବାରଣ କହିବା କଳି ଶେଷ ହେବ ଯହିଁ।
ଶୁଣରେ ବାରଙ୍ଗ ଏମନ୍ତ କାଳ ଯେ ଦିନେ ଆସି ପହଞ୍ଚିବ,
ଦଶ ଗ୍ରାମ ମଧ୍ୟେ ଜଣେକ ରହିବେ ଦେବତାଙ୍କ ଆବିର୍ଭାବ।
ସେ କାଳେ ବାରଙ୍ଗ ଅଣଚାଶ ବାୟୁ ଭାଦ୍ରବ ପୂର୍ଣ୍ଣିମା ଦିନ,
ଏମନ୍ତ ପବନ ବହିବ ବାରଙ୍ଗ ସକଳ ହୋଇବ ଲୀନ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Kara patra joḍi kahai Bāraṅga gurupāde śira dei,
praḷaya kāḷara bāraṇa kahibā kaḷi śeṣha heba jahiñ.
Śuṇare Bāraṅga emanta kāḷa je dine āsi pahañchiba,
daśha grāma madhye jaṇeka rahibe debatāṅka ābirbhāba.
Se kāḷe Bāraṅga aṇachāśha bāyu Bhādraba pūrṇimā dina,
emanta pabana bahiba Bāraṅga sakaḷa hoiba līna.

Tanthauzo:
Atafunsidwa ndi wophunzira Baranga za machitidwe owononga a Kalki omwe akuchitika kumapeto kwa Kali Yuga, Mahapurush Sishu Ananta Das analemba m'buku lake la Malika kuti anthu adzaona tsiku limene munthu mmodzi yekha, ngati mulungu, adzapulumuka pakati pa midzi 10. Pa nthawi imeneyo, mphepo makumi anayi ndi zisanu ndi zinayi zidzaomba ndi liwiro lalikulu ndikuwononga nyumba zonse. Limenelo lidzakhala tsiku la mwezi wathunthu wa mwezi wa Bhadra.

11.4 Chigumula cha Moto (Agni Pralaya)

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das analemba m'buku lake la Malika lotchedwa 'Gupta Kheda Malika':

Odia Sloka:

ଧନ, ସମ୍ପତ୍ତି ଓ ଟଙ୍କା ଯାହା ଅଛି ଜମା,
ସବୁଯିବ ପୋଡି ଭାଇ ନିଆଁର ଗଞ୍ଜମା।
ଚଉଦିଗେ ନିଆଁ କାହୁଁ ଦଗ୍ଧି ଏ ଧରଣୀ,
ଓଦା କଞ୍ଚା ନ ମାନିବ ସବୁ ଖାଏ ଅଗ୍ନି।
ଅଗ୍ନିର କରାଳ ରୂପ ଜୀବାତ୍ମା ଦେଖିବ,
ହାଁ ହାଁ ହୋଇ ତାର ପ୍ରାଣ ଛାଡିଯିବ।
ସମଗ୍ର ବିଶ୍ଵରେ ଧ୍ଵଂସ ଲୀଳୋ ଯେ ଚାଲିବ,
କେଉଁଠି କମ୍ ବେଶୀ ଜାଣେ ତା ଦଇବ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Dhana, sampatti o ṭaṅkā jāhā achi jamā,
sabujiba poḍi bhāi niāñra gañjamā.
Chaudige niāñ kāhuñ dagdhi e dharaṇī,
odā kañchā na māniba sabu khāe agni.
Agnira karāḷarūpa jībātmā dekhiba,
hāñ hāñ hoi tāra prāṇa chāḍijiba.
Samagra biśware dhwaṃsa līḷā je chāliba,
keuṇṭhi kam beśī jāne tā daiba.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, kuvina kwa moto kudzawoneka mbali zonse zinayi. Dziko lapansi lidzayaka. Anthu ambiri adzafa. Chuma, katundu, ndi ndalama zidzasanduka phulusa. Zochitika zowononga izi zidzakhala padziko lonse lapansi, zikuwonekera pang'ono m'malo ena ndipo zambiri m'malo ena.

11.5 Tsoka la Mlenga/Mlengalenga

Odia Sloka:

ନାନା ଉତ୍ପାତ ଯାକ ଅନ୍ତରୀକ୍ଷେ ହେବ,
ଭକ୍ତ ବିନା ଅନ୍ୟ କେହି ଜାଣି ନ ପାରିବ।

Kumasulira mawu:

nānā utpāta jāka antarikhye heba,
bhakta binā anya kehi jāni na pāriba.

Tanthauzo:
Mahapurush Achyutananda Das analemba m'buku lake la Malika lotchedwa 'Chakada Madana' kuti kumapeto kwa nthawi ya Kali Yuga, pamene kukhazikitsidwa kwa Dharma kukupitirira, padzakhala zosokoneza zambiri mumlengalenga. Padzakhala kusintha kwambiri kwa mapulaneti ndi nyenyezi, komanso malo awo ndi kuthamanga kwawo. Mahapurush analemba kuti kupatula opembedza, anthu wamba sadzadziwa za zinthu izi.

Mahapurush analembanso za nkhaniyi m'buku lake la Malika lotchedwa ‘Amara Jumara Samhita’:

Odia Sloka:

କଳିଯୁଗ ଶେଷବେଳେ ପୃଥ୍ବୀ ଉତ୍ପାତ,
ରଣ ଗୋଳ ସେ କାଳେ ହୋଇବ ବହୁତ।
ଚାରିଦିଗମାନଙ୍କରେ ଲାଞ୍ଜାମାନ ପଡି,
ସେ କାଳେ ବହୁତ ଶବ୍ଦ ହେବ ଘଡ଼ଘଡ଼ି।

Kumasulira mawu:

Kaḷiyuga śheṣhabeḷe pruthbī utpāta,
raṇa goḷa se kāḷe hoiba bahuta.
chāridigamānaṅkare lāñjāmāna paḍi,
se kāḷe bahuta śhabda heba ghaḍaghaḍi.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, kumapeto kwa Kali Yuga, padzakhala zosokoneza zambiri ndi nkhondo zankhanza padziko lapansi. Padzakhala miyala yochokera kumwamba/asteroids ikugwa mbali zonse zinayi ndipo mawu adzachokera kumlengalenga.

11.6 Matenda ndi Miliri

A Mahapurush analemba m'mabuku angapo a Malika kuti kumapeto kwa Kali Yuga, matenda ambiri ndi miliri idzakakamiza anthu kuti agwade. Malika ananena momveka bwino kuti madokotala adzalephera kuzindikira ndi kuchiza matenda ndi miliri imeneyo, ndipo mankhwala omwe adzapereke adzakhala opanda ntchito.

A Mahapurush ananenanso kuti kwenikweni, matenda ndi miliri imeneyi idzabwera chifukwa cha mphamvu za amayi monga Yogamaya, Ashta Chandis, 64 Yoginis, Dakinis, Pisachinis, ndi zina zotero. Mphamvu za amayi izi zidzayendayenda padziko lonse lapansi m'njira zobisika ngati matenda amenewa ndikulowa m'matupi a anthu.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akulemba m'buku lake la Malika lotchedwa ‘Bigyanakalpa’:

Odia Sloka:

ଦୁର୍ଭିକ୍ଷ ପଡିବ ଲୋକେ ଯିବେ ବହୁ ମରି,
ଚଉଷଠି ରୋଗ ଭ୍ରମୁଥିବେ ଫେରି ଫେରି।

Kumasulira mawu:

Durbhikṣha paḍiba loke jibe bahu mari,
chauṣhaṭhi roga bhramuthibe pheri pheri.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, pa nthawi yokhazikitsa Dharma kumapeto kwa Kali Yuga, padzakhala njala ndi chilala, zomwe zidzachititsa kuti anthu ambiri afe. Mitundu makumi asanu ndi anayi ndi inayi ya matenda idzafalikira padziko lonse lapansi.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akulembanso m'buku la Malika lotchedwa ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ବାଡି ବେମାରୀରେ ଅନେକ ମରିବେ କହେ ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Bāḍi bemārīre aneka maribe kahe dīna Achyuta.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, kumapeto kwa Kali Yuga, matenda ambiri adzakhala chifukwa cha imfa ya anthu osawerengeka.

Mahapurush analembanso m'mabuku ake a ‘Jaiphula Malika’ ndi ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ଅଚିହ୍ନା ରୋଗ ଘୋଟିବ,
ବଇଦର ବୁଦ୍ଧି ବଣା ହୋଇବ ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ମହୌଷଧି କାମ ନ କରିବ।
ଅଚିହ୍ନା ବ୍ୟାଧି ଯେ ଜଗତ୍ରେ ଘୋଟିବ ବଇଦ ବଣା ହୋଇବେ,
ରୋଗ ନ ଚିହ୍ନିଣ ବଣା ହେଉଥିବେ ଅନେକ ପ୍ରାଣୀ ମରିବେ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Achinhā roga ghoṭiba,
baidara buddhi baṇā hoiba lo jāiphula,
mahauṣadhi kāma na kariba.
Achinhā byādhi je jagatre ghoṭiba baida baṇā hoibe,
roga na chinhiṇa baṇā heuthibe aneka prāṇī maribe.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, kumapeto kwa Kali Yuga, padzabwera matenda osadziwika omwe nzeru za madokotala zidzasiya kugwira ntchito. Matenda amenewa sadzadziwika, ndipo mankhwala adzakhala opanda ntchito powachiza, chifukwa chake anthu osawerengeka adzafa.

Mahapurush akulemba m'buku lina la Malika:

Odia Sloka:

ରୋଗ ରୂପେ ଯୋଗମାୟା ଶରୀରେ ଖେଳିବ,
କାହାର ଆୟତ ପୁଣି ସେଠାରେ ନ ଥିବ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Roga rūpe Yogamāyā sharīre kheḷiba,
kāhāra āyata puṇi seṭhāre na thiba.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, kumapeto kwa Kali Yuga, Amayi Yogamaya adzalowa m'matupi a anthu ngati matenda.

Mahapurush akulembanso kuti:

Odia Sloka:

ଗଦ ମହୌଷଧି କରିବନି କାର୍ଯ୍ୟ ମାଡି ଆସିବଟି ରୋଗ,
ସେ କାଳେ ଜାଣିବୁ ଅଳ୍ପ ଦିନେ ପୃଥ୍ବୀକୁ ପ୍ରଳୟ ଯୋଗ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Gada mahauṣhadhi karibani kārjya māḍi āsibaṭi roga,
Se kāḷe jānibu alpa dine pruthbīku praḷaya yoga.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, pamene miliri yotere idzabwera yomwe mankhwala adzakhala opanda ntchito powachiza, ndiye kuti ziyenera kumvedwa kuti nthawi ya chiwonongeko padziko lapansi yayandikira.

Mahapurush akulembanso kuti:

Odia Sloka:

ବ୍ୟାଧି ହୋଇ ପିଶାଚୀ ଆସିବେ,
ପ୍ରାଣୀମାନଙ୍କୁ ଗୁପ୍ତେ ନାଶିବେ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Byādhi hoi piśhāchī āsibe,
prāṇīmānanku gupte nāśhibe.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, mphamvu za amayi zidzalowa mobisa m'matupi ngati matenda ndikuwononga anthu ambiri. Koma pamodzi ndi izi, Mahapurush ananenanso m'buku lake la Malika njira yoti anthu athawire matenda ndi miliri imeneyi.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akulemba m'buku lake la Malika lotchedwa ‘Chausathi Patala’:

Odia Sloka:

ସତ୍ୟ ଶାନ୍ତି ଦୟା କ୍ଷମାରେ ଯେଉଁ ପ୍ରାଣୀ ରହିବେ,
ରୋଗ କବଳରେ କେବେହିଁ ସେମାନେ ନ ପଡିବେ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Satya śhānti dayā kṣhamāre jeuñ prāṇī rahibe,
roga kabaḷare kebehiñ semāne na paḍibe.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, anthu amene adzakhala ndi makhalidwe abwino monga choonadi, mtendere, chifundo, ndi kukhululukira m'machitidwe awo adzakhala opanda matenda amenewa.

11.7 Nkhondo Yadziko Lonse

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das analemba m'buku lake la Malika lotchedwa ‘Jaiphula Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

ଲାଗିବ ମହାସମର,
ଦେଶ ଦେଶ ମଧ୍ୟେ ଅତି ପ୍ରବଳ ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ପୃଥିବୀ ହୋଇଯିବ ନାରଖାର।

Kumasulira mawu:

Lāgiba mahāsamara,
deśha deśha madhye ati prabala lo jāiphula,
pruthibī hoijiba nārakhāra.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, nkhondo yayikulu idzachitika padziko lapansi. Padzakhala nkhondo yaukali pakati pa mayiko, zomwe zidzachititsa chiwonongeko cha dziko lapansi.

Achyutananda Das ji analembanso mu ‘Jaiphula Malika’ momwemo:

Odia Sloka:

ଦେଶ ଦେଶ ମଧ୍ୟେ ପୁଣ,
ଭୟଙ୍କର ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ହେବ ପ୍ରମାଣ ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ଏହା କଳି ଶେଷ ବୋଲି ଜାଣ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Deśha deśha madhye puṇa,
bhayaṅkara juddha heba pramāṇa lo jāiphula,
ehā kaḷi śeṣha boli jāṇa.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, kumapeto kwa Kali Yuga, nkhondo zankhanza kwambiri zidzayamba pakati pa mayiko osiyanasiyana. Mwa mawu ena, pamene nkhondo yowopsa idzayamba pakati pa mayiko, ziyenera kumvedwa kuti ndi mapeto a Kali Yuga.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akulemba m'buku lake la Malika lotchedwa ‘Gupta Kheda Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

ଅଣୁ ପରମାଣୁ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ଭାରି ଭୟଙ୍କର,
ଗୋଟିଏ ଅଣୁ ବୋମାର କରାମତି ସାର।
ଲକ୍ଷ ଲକ୍ଷ ଅଣୁବୋମା ଗର୍ଜିବ ମହୀରେ,
କାନ୍ଦିବ ଧରଣୀ ରାଣୀ ହା ହୁତାଶରେ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Aṇu paramāṇu juddha bhāri bhayaṅkara,
goṭie aṇu bomāra karāmati sāra.
lakṣha lakṣha aṇubomā garjiba mahīre,
kāndiba dharaṇī rāṇī hā hutāśhare.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, nkhondo ya atomiki-nyukiliya idzachitika padziko lapansi yomwe idzakhala yowopsa kwambiri. Dziko lonse lapansi lidzagwedezeka ndi kugwiritsa ntchito mabomba ambiri a nyukiliya. Amayi Dziko lapansi adzalira.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akunena kuti mu nthawi ino, nkhondo yaukali idzachitika pakati pa India ndi Pakistan. Malinga ndi Mahapurush, nkhondo imeneyi pakati pa India ndi Pakistan idzasanduka nkhondo yadziko lonse chifukwa mayiko ambiri adzathandiza Pakistan pa nkhondoyi, pamene mayiko ena adzamenyera India. Pa nkhondoyi, mayiko akuluakulu padziko lapansi adzagawidwa m'magulu awiri.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akulemba mu ‘Gupta Kheda Malika’ yake:

Odia Sloka:

ଚୀନ ପାକିସ୍ଥାନ ପୁଣି ଆମେରିକା ଜାଣ,
ଋଷିଆ ଭାରତ ପୁଣି ଜର୍ମାନ ଜାପାନ।
ଭାରତ ସପକ୍ଷ ହେବେ ମହାଶକ୍ତି ଜାଣ,
ଇରାକ ଯେ ସମର୍ଥନ ଦେବ ପାକିସ୍ଥାନ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Chīna Pākisthāna puṇi Āmerikā jāṇa,
Ruṣhiā Bhārata puṇi Jarmāna Jāpāna.
Bhārata sapakṣha hebe mahāśhakti jāṇa,
Irāka je samarthana deba Pākisthāna.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, pa nkhondoyi, mayiko monga China, America, ndi Iraq adzathandiza Pakistan, pamene mphamvu monga Russia, Germany, ndi Japan adzamenyera India.

Mahapurush akulemba m'buku la Malika lotchedwa ‘Bhabishyat Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

ଏହି ଭାରତ ସଙ୍ଗେ ପାକିସ୍ଥାନଟି ରଙ୍ଗେ,
ମହାସମର କରିବ ଜାଣିଥା ବେଗେ।
ପୁଣି ଚୀନ୍ ସଇନୁ ତୁର୍କୀ ଇରାନ ଜାଣ,
ବ୍ରିଟିଶ ଯେ ସଇନ୍ୟ ସବେ ମିଳିଣ।
ପାକସ୍ଥିାନକୁ ବଳ ଅସ୍ତ୍ର ସର୍ବେ ଆବର,
ପାକିସ୍ଥାନକୁ ଦେବେ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ଲାଗିବ ଘୋର।
ଏହା ଦେଖି ରୁଷିଆ ଆସି ହୋଇବ ଠିଆ,
ଭାରତ ସମରକ୍ଷେତ୍ରେ ସେ ଏକୁଟିଆ।
ଅସ୍ତ୍ର ଶସ୍ତ୍ର ସଇନ୍ୟ ଯୋଗାଇ ଦେବ ଜାଣ,
ଭାରତ ସହିତ ମିଶି କରିବ ରଣ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Ehi Bhārata saṅge Pākisthānṭi raṅge,
mahāsamara kariba jāṇithā bege.
Puṇi Chīna sainu Turkī Irāna jāṇa,
Briṭisha je sainya sabe miḷiṇa.
Pākisthānku baḷa astra sarbe ābara,
Pākisthānku debe juddha lāgiba ghora.
Ehā dekhi Ruṣiā āsi hoiba ṭhiā,
Bhārata samarakṣetre se ekuṭiā.
Astra shastra sainya jogāi deba jāṇa,
Bhārata sahita mishi kariba raṇa.

Tanthauzo:
Mahapurush analemba kuti dziko lotchedwa Pakistan lidzachita nkhondo yaukali ndi India. Pa nkhondoyi, mayiko monga China, Turkey, Iran, England, ndi America adzathandiza Pakistan ndikupereka thandizo lankhondo. Poona zonsezi, bwenzi lapamtima la India, Russia, lidzabwera loyamba ndekha kudzathandiza India. Lidzapereka India mitundu yonse ya zida, mgwirizano wachuma, ndi thandizo lankhondo ndipo lidzachita nkhondo yaukali motsutsana ndi mayiko adani m'malo mwa India.

Mahapurush analemba kuti osati Russia kokha, komanso Japan ndi Germany adzamenyeranso India pa nkhondoyi. Mu ‘Gupta Kheda Malika’ momwemo, akulembanso kuti:

Odia Sloka:

ଜାପାନର ରାଗ ଅଛି ହିରୋଶିମା କଥା,
ପକାଇଥିଲା ବୋମା ଯୁଦ୍ଧେ ଜାଣିଥା।
ସେହି କ୍ରୋଧ ଲିଭିନାହିଁ ଜାପାନର ଜାଣ,
ଯୁଦ୍ଧେ ଭାରତ ପଟ ନେବେ ସେହି ପୁଣ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Jāpānara rāga achi Hirośhimā kathā,
pakāithilā bomā juddhe jāṇithā.
Sehi krodha libhināhiñ Jāpānara jāṇa,
juddhe Bhārata paṭa nebe sehi puṇa.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, Japan sadzaiwala kugwetsedwa kwa bomba la atomiki pa iwo ndi America. Mkwiyo wa Japan kwa America sudzakhazikika. Lawi la kubwezera lidzapitirira kuyaka mumtima mwake, chifukwa chake Japan adzamenyana ndi America kumbali ya India pa nkhondo yachitatu yapadziko lonse. Pa pempho la Japan, Germany adzamenyeranso India.

12 Nkhondo Yotsalira ya Mahabharata mu Kali Yuga

A Panchasakhas analemba m'mabuku a Bhabishya Malika kuti Nkhondo Yadziko Lonse yomwe idzachitika kumapeto kwa Kali Yuga idzakhala nkhondo yotsalira ya Mahabharata. A Mahapurush anafotokoza kuti nkhondo ya masiku 18 ya Mahabharata kumapeto kwa Dwapara Yuga sinamalizidwe kwathunthu chifukwa, panthawiyo, Ambuye Krishna anaganiza kuti asachite gawo lomaliza la nkhondoyo panthawiyo, koma kuti amalize kumapeto kwa Kali Yuga.

Osati zokhazi, a Mahapurushas adalemba mwatsatanetsatane za nkhondoyi m'mabuku awo a Malika ndipo adawulula zinthu zambiri, monga nthawi yomwe idzachitike, anthu angati omwe adzafe panthawiyo, nthawi yake idzakhala yayitali bwanji, idzatenga mawonekedwe otani, idzachitika motsogozedwa ndi ndani, ndani adzatenga nawo mbali kuchokera ku mbali ya India, komwe idzathera, ndi zomwe zidzachitike pambuyo pa nkhondoyi, ndi zina zotero.

12.1 Nkhondoyi idzachitika liti?

Odia Sloka:

ଯୁଗଶେଷ କଳି ଭାରତ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ହୋଇବ ଘୋର,
ଯମ ମାଆ ସାତ ଭଉଣୀ ଧରିଥିବେ ଖପର।

Kumasulira mawu:

Yugaśheṣha kaḷi Bhārata juddha hoiba ghora,
Yama māā sāta bhauṇī dharithibe khapara.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, nkhondo yotsalira ya Mahabharata idzamalizidwa kumapeto kwa Kali Yuga. Mu nkhondoyi, "Yama Maa Sata Bhauni" kapena Amayi Milungu Asanu ndi Awiri (Saptamatruka) omwe ali ku Biraja Khetra (Chamunda, Varahi, Indrani, Vaishnavi, Brahmani, Kaumari, Maheshwari, ndi Narasimhi) adzawononga ochimwa.

Pofotokoza nthawi yomwe nkhondoyi idzachitika kumapeto kwa Kali Yuga, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das adalemba m'buku lake la Malika ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନି ଆସି ଦେଉଥିବ ଘାଣ୍ଟି ଭାରତ ଲଢ଼ାଇ ହେବ,
ଦଶ ଅଣା ଯିବ ଛଅ ଅଣା ରହିବ ମୋ ବଚନ ଅଟେ ଧ୍ରୁବ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Mīna Shani āsi deuthibā ghāṇṭi Bhārata laḍhāi heba,
Dasha aṇā jiba chha-a aṇā rahiba mo bachana aṭe dhruba.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, nkhondo yotsalira ya India (Mahabharata) idzayamba pa nthawi ya Meena-Shani (Pisces-Saturn) mgwirizano, momwe anthu osawerengeka adzafera.

12.2 Nkhondoyi idzachitika motani ndipo idzamenyedwa kuti kumapeto kwake?

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akulemba m'buku lake la Malika:

Odia Sloka:

ମହାଭାରତ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ଓଳିଏ ବାକି ଅଛି ଜାଣ,
କଳିଯୁଗରେ ବିଶ୍ୱଯୁଦ୍ଧ ହୋଇ ପୂରଣ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Mahābhārata juddha oḷie bāki achi jāṇa,
Kaḷiyugare biśhwajuddha hoi pūraṇa.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, dziwani kuti gawo la nkhondo ya Mahabharata latsala, lomwe lidzamalizidwa ngati Nkhondo Yadziko Lonse ku Kali Yuga.

Mahapurush akulembanso m'buku lake la ‘Jaiphula Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

ଓଳିଏ ଭାରତ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ,
ଓଡ଼ିଶା ଦେଶରେ ପୁଣି ହୋଇବ ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
କହେ ଯବନ ବାହି ଆସିବ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Oḷie Bhārata juddha,
Oḍiśhā deśhare puṇi hoiba lo jāiphula,
kahe jabana bāhi āsiba.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, nkhondo yotsalira ya Mahabharata idzachitika ku Odisha, ndipo panthawiyo, asilikali ambiri a mayiko adani adzalowa mkati mwa India.

Saint Achyutananda Das akulemba m'buku lina la Malika:

Odia Sloka:

ଏ କଳିଯୁଗରେ ମହାଭାରତଟି ଉତ୍କଳ ଭୂଇଁ ପ୍ରମାଣ,
ଉତ୍କଳରୁ ସବୁ ଉତ୍ପତ୍ତି ହୋଇବ ଠିକେ ଠିକେ ଏହା ଶୁଣ।

Kumasulira mawu:

E kaḷiyugare Mahābhārataṭi Utkaḷa bhūiñ pramāṇa,
Utkaḷaru sabu utpatti hoiba ṭhike ṭhike ehā śhuṇa.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, nkhondo yotsalira ya Mahabharata idzamenyedwa ku Kali Yuga, yomwe idzachitika pa nthaka ya Utkala kapena Odisha.

12.3 Nkhondoyi idzatenga nthawi yayitali bwanji?

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das adaneneratunso m'buku lake la Malika ‘Amara Jumara Samhita’ kuti nkhondo yotsalira ya Mahabharata idzatenga miyezi 13 yonse.

Mahapurush adalemba:

Odia Sloka:

ଓଳିଏ ଯେ ରହିଥିବ ଶୁଣ ହେତୁ ଦ୍ୱାରେ,
ଭାରତ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ହୋଇବ ସେତିକି ବେଳରେ।
ସେହି ଓଳିକରେ ନାଥ କଳି ଶେଷ ହେବ,
ସେ ଓଳିକେ ତେର ମାସ ଭୋଗ ଯେ ହୋଇବ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Olie je rahithiba shuṇa hetu dwāre,
Bhārata juddha hoiba setiki xelare.
Sehi olikare nātha kali shesha hexa,
Se olike tera māsa bhoga je hoiba.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, nkhondo yotsalira ya Mahabharata idzatenga miyezi khumi ndi itatu yonse, ndipo ikadzatha nkhondoyo, mphamvu ya Kali Yuga pa Dziko Lapansi idzatha kotheratu.

12.4 Ndani adzatenga nawo mbali kuchokera ku mbali ya India kumapeto kwake?

A Mahapurushas adalemba kuti kumapeto kwa nkhondoyi, pamene mbali imodzi padzakhala magulu ankhondo akuluakulu a mayiko adani, mbali inayo, ankhondo onse amphamvu a nthawi ya Mahabharata ndi zikwi zambiri za oyera mtima, anzeru, ndi owona adzapezeka motsogozedwa ndi Ambuye Wamkulu Kalki mwiniwake.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das adalemba m'buku lake la ‘Bhabishyat Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

ଏହି ବେଳେ ଭାରତ ନିଶ୍ଚେ ହାରିଯିବ ତ,
ହାହାକାର ପଡ଼ିଯିବ ଚାରିଆଡ଼େ ତ।
ଭାରତ ନରନାରୀ ସର୍ବେ ଡାକିବେ ହରି,
ରକ୍ଷାକର ପ୍ରଭୁ ନିଶ୍ଚେ ଗଲୁଟି ସରି।
ଆହେ କୁଞ୍ଜବିହାରୀ ବାରେ ଶୁଣ ଗୁହାରି,
ବଇକୁଣ୍ଠ ତେଜି ଥରେ ଆସ ଶ୍ରୀ ହରି।
ଦେଖ ନେତ୍ର ଫେଡ଼ିତ ପୁଣ୍ୟଭୂମି ଭାରତ,
ବିଦେଶୀଙ୍କ ହସ୍ତେ ହୁଏ କି ହନ୍ତସନ୍ତ।
ଭାରତର କାଳର ଶୁଣି ସେ ଆଦିମୂଳ,
ବଇକୁଣ୍ଠ ପୁର ତେଜି ହେବେ ବାହାର।
କଳ୍କୀ ଦେବ ନାମ ବହି ସେ ନାରାୟଣ,
ଉତ୍କଳ ଦେଶ କଟକରେ ଦେବେ ଦର୍ଶନ।
ସେହି ବେଳରେ ଜାଣ ଶ୍ରୀକୃଷ୍ଣ ବଳରାମ,
ଭୀମ ସହ ବାହାରିବେ ବୀର ଅର୍ଜୁନ।
ଭୀଷ୍ମ ଦ୍ରୋଣ ଆବର ଅଶ୍ୱତ୍ଥାମା ଯେ ବୀର,
କର୍ଣ୍ଣ ଶଲ୍ୟ ଜୟଦ୍ରଥ ହେବେ ବାହାର।
ବେଲାଲସେନ ବୀର ପୁଣି ହେବ ବାହାର,
ବାଳବୀର ଅଭିମନ୍ୟୁ ଘେନି ସଙ୍ଗର।
ବୀର ବବ୍ରୁବାହନ ବାହାରିବଟି ଜାଣ,
ଏକଲବ୍ୟ ଘଟୋତ୍କଚ ସଙ୍ଗେ ଘେନିଣ।
ସର୍ବେ ହୋଇଣ ମେଳି ବିଦେଶୀୟଙ୍କୁ ଦଳି,
ଦେବେ ମୁହୂର୍ତ୍ତକ ମଧ୍ଯେ ଲଗାଇ କଳି।
ମନ୍ତ୍ର ଶକତି ବଳେ ଅପଲକ ନେତ୍ରରେ,
ଚାହିଁ ବିଜ୍ଞାନ ଶକ୍ତିକି ଜିଣିବେ ହେଳେ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Ehibeḷe Bhārata nishche hārijiba ta,
hāhākāra paḍijiba chāriāḍe ta.
Bhārata naranārī sarbe dākibe Hari,
rakhākara prabhu nishche galuṭi sari.
Āhe Kuñjabihārī bāre shuṇa guhāରି,
Baikuṇṭha teji thare āsa Shrī Hari.
Dekha netra pheḍita puṇyabhūmi Bhārata,
bideshīṅka haste hue ki hantasanta.
Bhāratara kāḷara shuṇi se ādimūḷa,
Baikuṇṭhapura teji hebe bāhāra.
Kalkī deba nāma bahi se Nārāyaṇa,
Utkaḷa desha Kaṭakare debe darshana.
Sehibeḷere jāṇa Shrī Kṛṣṇa Baḷarāma,
Bhīma saha bāhāribe bīra Arjuna.
Bhīṣma Droṇa ābara Ashwatthāmā je bīra,
Karṇa Shalya Jayadratha hebe bāhāra.
Belāḷasena bīra puṇi hebe bāhāra,
bāḷabīra Abhimanyu gheni saṅgara.
Bīra Babhrubāhana bāhāribaṭi jāṇa,
Ekalabya Ghaṭotkacha saṅge gheniṇa.
Sarbe hoiṇa meḷi bideshīyaṅku daḷi,
debe muhūrttaka madhye lagāi kaḷi.
Mantra shakti baḷe apalaka netrare,
chāhi bijñāna shaktiki jiṇibe heḷe.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, pa Nkhondo Yadziko Lonse Yachitatu, poona kuukira kwa adani akunja ku India kuchokera mbali zonse zinayi, anthu a ku India adzachita mantha ndikulira mokhetsa misozi. Popeza alibe thandizo, onse adzakumbukira Ambuye ndikupempha chitetezo. Adzanena—O Ambuye, imvani pempho lathu, tipulumutseni, chokani ku Vaikuntha ndipo bwerani ku Dziko Lapansi kudzatichengetsa.

Atakhudzidwa ndi kuitana kwachisoni kumeneku kwa odzipereka ndipo poona kuukira kwa India ndi adani akunja, Brahman Wamkulu Ambuye Narayana (Maha Vishnu) mwiniwake, m'kubadwa kwake kwa Kalki, adzaonekera ku Cuttack, Odisha, ndipo adzapereka darshan kwa odzipereka.

Mahapurush adalemba kuti pamodzi ndi Ambuye Kalki panthawi yomweyo, Bhima, Arjuna, Bhishma, Drona, Ashwatthama, Karna, Shalya, Jayadratha, Barbarika, Abhimanyu, Babhruvahana, Ekalavya, ndi Ghatotkacha, omwe adabadwa kale, adzamenya nkhondo ku mbali ya India ndipo adzagonjetsa kwambiri mayiko adani. Mphamvu ya mantra yauzimu ya ankhondo amphamvu amenewa idzakhala yapamwamba kuposa mphamvu zamakono zochokera ku sayansi za adani.

Pa nkhani yomweyi, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das adalemba m'buku lake la Malika ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ପ୍ରସ୍ତାବିତ କଳି ଭାରତ ସରିବ ହେବ ଭାରତ ସମର।
ତିନିଯୁଗ ଯୋଦ୍ଧା ଏକ ଯୁଗରେ ମହାସମର ଲାଗିବ,
କହଇ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଭକ୍ତ ହେବେ ମୁକ୍ତ ମନୋସ୍କାମନା ପୂରିବ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Prastābita kaḷi Bhārata sariba heba Bhārata samara.
Tinijuga joddhā eka jugare mahāsamara lāgiba,
kahai Achyuta bhakta hebe mukta manoskāmanā pūriba.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, nkhondo ya Mahabharata idzamenyedwa ku Kali Yuga, momwe ankhondo amphamvu a zaka zitatu zapitazo adzatenga nawo mbali.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akulembanso mu ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ଓଳିଏ ଭାରତ ସମର ହେବ ଦେଲି ଚେତାଇ,
ସ୍ୱୟଂ ପରଂବ୍ରହ୍ମ ମୂରତି ପ୍ରଭୁ ଜଗତସାଇଁ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Oḷie Bhārata samara heba deli chetāi,
swayaṃ Parambrahma mūrati prabhu Jagatasāiñ.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, nkhondo yotsalira ya Mahabharata idzachitika motsogozedwa ndi Brahman Wamkulu Ambuye Narayana (kubadwa kwa Kalki) mwiniwake.

Pa nkhaniyi, mmodzi wa a Panchasakhas, Mahapurush Yashovanta Das, adalembanso m'buku lake la Malika ‘Nidagha Nighanta’:

Odia Sloka:

ଓଳିଏ ରହି ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ହେବ,
ଆଦେଶ ଦେବେଟି ମାଧବ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Oḷie rahi juddha heba,
ādeśha debeṭi Mādhaba.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, nkhondo yotsalira ya Mahabharata idzamalizidwa, ndipo Madhava mwiniwake, Ambuye Wamkulu Kalki, adzapereka lamulo loyambitsa nkhondoyi.

Woyera mtima wamkulu wa ku Odisha wobadwa m'zaka za zana la 20, Thakur Shri Abhiram Paramahansa, adalembanso za nkhaniyi m'buku lake la Malika ‘Baishnaba Gita’:

Odia Sloka:

ତୋପର ତପର ସମର,
ହୋଇବ ଅତି ଭୟଙ୍କର।
ତପକୁ ତୋପ ନ ପାରିବ,
ତପର ବଳ ବେଶୀ ହେବ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Topara tapara samara,
hoiba ati bhayaṅkara.
tapaku topa na pāriba,
tapara baḷa beśhī heba.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, padzakhala nkhondo yoopsa pakati pa mphamvu ya mfuti za mayiko adani a India ndi mphamvu ya Tapasya ya oyera mtima ndi anzeru osawerengeka a India, momwe mphamvu ya mizinga yamakono ya adani idzakhala yopanda mphamvu pamaso pa mphamvu ya Tapasya ya India, ndipo pamapeto pake India idzapambana mu nkhondo yayikuluyi.

12.5 Nkhondoyi idzamalizidwa kuti ku India?

A Mahapurushas adalemba m'mabuku a Malika kuti asilikali akunja akafika ku Odisha, adzamenya nkhondo m'malo osiyanasiyana ku Odisha, monga makamaka ku Birat Gadi ya Cuttack, Choudwar, Jobra, Barabati, ndi Kaliaboda; ku Puri; ku Jajpur; ku Gohira Tikiri ya Bhadrak; ku Paradip; ku Balasore, ndi zina zotero.
