Ngakhale nkhondoyi idzachitika m'malo onsewa ku Odisha, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das adanena momveka bwino kuti idzamalizidwa kotheratu ku Khandagiri ku Bhubaneswar pamene Ambuye Wamkulu mwiniwake adzawononga asilikali onse a mayiko adani ndi Sudarshana Chakra yake.

Mahapurush adalemba m'buku lake la Malika ‘Kalpatika’:

Odia Sloka:

ଓଡ଼ିଶା ଦେଶରେ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ଠାରେ ବହୁତ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ହୋଇବ,
ଅନନ୍ତ କେଶରୀ ରୂପ ଧରି ପ୍ରଭୁ ଚକ୍ର ଧରି ବିନାଶିବ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Oḍiśhā deśhare Khaṇḍagiri ṭhāre bahuta juddha hoiba,
Ananta Keśharī rūpa dhari prabhu chakra dhari bināśhiba.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, padzakhala nkhondo yoopsa kwambiri ku Khandagiri ku Odisha. Pamalo omwewo, Ambuye Wamkulu Kalki adzawononga asilikali osawerengeka ndi Sudarshana Chakra yake.

12.6 Kodi nkhondoyi idzabweretsa chiyani ndipo chidzachitika chiyani pambuyo pake?

Mu ‘Amara Jumara Samhita’, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das adalemba vesi lomwe gawo lake loyamba ndi motere:

Odia Sloka:

ସେହି ଓଳିକରେ ନାଥ କଳି ଶେଷ ହେବ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Sehi oḷikare nātha kaḷi śheṣha heba.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, nkhondo ya Mahabharata ikangotha, mphamvu ya Kali Yuga idzatha.

Kenako Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akulembanso vesi m'buku lake la Malika ‘Chakada Madana’, lomwe mzere wake woyamba ukuti:

Odia Sloka:

ଓଳିଏ ଭାରତ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ଯେ ସରିବ ପୃଥିବୀ ଶାନ୍ତ ହୋଇବ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Oḷie Bhārata juddha je sariba pruthibī śhānta hoiba.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, nkhondo yotsalira ya Mahabharata ikadzatha, Dziko Lapansi lidzakhala lamtendere. Kenako chisokonezo chonse pa Dziko Lapansi chidzatha.

12.7 Kufotokozera za mkhalidwe wa Maiko Amphamvu monga America ndi Europe pa Nkhondo Yadziko Lonse

A Panchasakha Mahapurushas analemba m'mabuku awo a Malika kuti Nkhondo Yachitatu Yadziko Lonse idzakhala yowopsa kwambiri moti mitundu yonse idzakhudzidwa nayo. Maiko onse omwe amaonedwa lero ngati mphamvu zamphamvu kwambiri padziko lonse, nkhondo yomaliza ya Kali Yuga iyi idzawasiya nawonso atawonongedwa.

Pofotokoza za mkhalidwe wa America pa nkhondoyi, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das analemba m'buku lake la Malika lotchedwa ‘Gupta Kheda Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

ଆମେରିକା ରାଷ୍ଟ୍ର ଭାଇ ମୂଳୁ ଧ୍ୱଂସ ଯିବ,
ଗର୍ବରେ ଜଳୁଛି ରାଜା ବିଶ୍ୱ ମୁଖ୍ୟ ହେବ।
ଧରାକୁ ମଣୁଛି ସରା କହେ ମୁହିଁ ଶ୍ରେଷ୍ଠ,
ଉଡ଼ାଇବି ପୃଥିବୀକୁ କରେ ମୁଁ ଶପଥ।
କାହାରି ଗର୍ବ ସହି ନ ପାରିବେ ଧାତା,
ଜଳାଇ ଦେବେ ତାହାକୁ ଉଡ଼ାଇବେ ମଥା।
ନ୍ୟୁୟର୍କ ସହର ଧ୍ୱଂସ ପଦା ହୋଇଯିବ,
ହାହାକାର ପଡ଼ିଯିବ ଅଥର୍ବ ନିର୍ବେଦ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Āmerikā rāṣhṭra bhāi mūḷu dhwaṅsa jiba,
garbare jaḷuchi rājā biśhwa mukhya heba.
dharāku maṇuchi sarā kahe muiñ śhreṣhṭha,
uḍāibi pruthibīku kare muñ śhapatha.
kāhāri garba sahi na pāribe dhātā,
jaḷāi debe tāhāku uḍāibe mathā.
New York sahara dhwaṅsa padā hoijiba,
hāhākāra paḍijiba atharba nirbeda.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, dziko lotchedwa America lidzadziyesa lokha mtsogoleri wa dziko lonse. Litamira mu kunyada ndi kudzikuza, lidzadzitcha lokha labwino kwambiri ndikuopseza kuphulitsa maiko ena pa nkhondo. Mahapurush ananenanso kuti Ambuye Wamkulu salekerera kunyada kwa aliyense ndipo chifukwa chake adzaphwanyanso kunyada kwa America. Mzinda wa New York ku America udzawonongedwa, ndipo dzikolo lidzakumana ndi chiwonongeko chonse ngati mboni ya chiwonongeko chachikulu.

Mu ‘Gupta Kheda Malika’ momwemo, Saint Achyutananda Das akulembanso:

Odia Sloka:

ଆମେରିକା ଇଉରୋପ ମହାଦେଶ ଜାଣ,
ସ୍ଥିତି ହରାଇବ ତାର ବିଷାକ୍ତ ପବନ।
ସବୁରି ଧ୍ୱଂସର କାରଣ ଆମେରିକା ଜାଣ,
ନିଶ୍ଚିହ୍ନ ହୋଇବ ଯୁଦ୍ଧେ ଗର୍ବି ଅଟେ ପୁଣ।
ନିଜର ଇଜ୍ଜତ ପୃଥିବୀ ପରେ ଦେଖାଇଲା,
ଯୁଦ୍ଧକୁ ତ ଜୀବନରେ ସିଦ୍ଧହସ୍ତ କଲା।

Kumasulira mawu:

Āmerikā Iuropa mahādesha jāṇa,
Sthiti harāiba tāra biṣākta pabana.
Saburi dhwaṃsara kāraṇa Āmerikā jāṇa,
Nishchinha hoiba juddhe garbi aṭe puṇa.
Nijara ijjata pṛthwī pare dekhāilā,
Juddhaku ta jībanare siddhahasta kalā.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, chifukwa cha Nkhondo Yachitatu Yadziko Lonse chidzakhala dziko lotchedwa ‘America’. Dzikolo lidzakhazikitsa ulemu ndi kunyada kwake pa Dziko lonse lapansi ndikupeza luso lomenya nkhondo. Mahapurush analembanso kuti chifukwa cha kunyada kumeneku, dzina lake ndi chizindikiro chake zidzafafanizidwa mu Nkhondo Yachitatu Yadziko Lonse. Nkhondo ikatha, nyengo kumeneko idzakhala yoipa, ndipo chifukwa cha zimenezo, anthu osawerengeka adzafa m'makontinenti a America ndi Europe.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akulemba mu ‘Bhabishyat Malika’ yake:

Odia Sloka:

ଶେଷେ ଇଉରୋପ ଦେଶ ଯୁଦ୍ଧେ ହୋଇବ ଧ୍ୱଂସ,
ବଞ୍ଚି ରହିବେ କେବଳ ଭକ୍ତ ଅଂଶ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Śheṣhe Iuropa deśha juddhe hoiba dhwaṅsa,
bañchi rahibe kebaḷa bhakta aṅśha.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, pa nkhondoyi, kontinenti ya Europe idzawonongedwa kwathunthu. Kupatula okhulupirira odzipereka a Ambuye, palibe amene adzapulumuka m'maiko amenewo.

Mu ‘Jaiphula Malika’, Saint Achyutananda Das akulemba:

Odia Sloka:

ପଶ୍ଚିମ ଉତ୍ତର ଦେଶ,
ହାଡ଼ ମାଳ ହୋଇଯିବ ବିଶେଷ ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ମହୀ ଦୁଲୁ ଦୁଲୁ ହେବ ଧ୍ୱଂସ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Paśhchima uttara deśha,
hāḍa māḷa hoijiba biśheṣha lo jāiphula,
mahī dulu dulu heba dhwaṅsa.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, maiko omwe ali kumadzulo ndi kumpoto adzawonongedwa mu Nkhondo Yadziko Lonse.

Achyutananda Das ji akulembanso mu ‘Jaiphula Malika’ momwemo:

Odia Sloka:

ଜର୍ମାନୀ ରୁଷିଆ ଆଦି,
ଇଟାଲୀ ଜାପାନ ତୁରସ୍କ ଗାଦି ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ସେହି ସରବେ ହୋଇବେ ରଦ୍ଦି।

Kumasulira mawu:

Jarmānī Ruṣhiā ādi,
Itālī Jāpāna Turuska gādi lo jāiphula,
sehi sarabe hoibe raddi.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, maiko monga Germany, Russia, Italy, Japan, ndi Turkey, adzawonongedwa kwambiri ndi Nkhondo Yadziko Lonse.

Monga tanenera kale, zochitika zonse zomwe zatchulidwa pamwambapa zidzachitika makamaka pa nthawi ya Meena-Shani yoga, ndiko kuti, pakati pa zaka 2025 ndi 2028.

Pa Marichi 29, 2025, Saturn adalowa mu chizindikiro cha Pisces (Meena). Nthawi zina akuyenda kubwerera mmbuyo (Vakri) kapena kuyenda molunjika ndipo nthawi zina akuyenda molunjika (Margi), Saturn adzakhalabe mu chizindikiro cha Pisces mpaka Febuluwale 23, 2028. Pakadali pano, kuyambira Juni 3, 2027, mpaka Okutobala 20, 2027, padzakhala kuyenda kwakanthawi kochepa kwa Saturn mu chizindikiro cha Aries (Mesha).

Tsopano funso lofunika likubwera pano. Patha pafupifupi miyezi 10 kuchokera pamene Meena-Shani yoga inayamba, koma zochitika zowonongazi sizinayambebe. Chifukwa chiyani? Inde, masoka achilengedwe akuchitika padziko lonse, nkhondo zikuchitikanso pakati pa maiko angapo, koma sizikuchitika pa mlingo waukulu womwe maulosi awo apangidwa ndi a Panchasakhas m'mabuku a Malika! Funso limabwera mobwerezabwereza m'maganizo mwa ambiri a ife kuti mlingo wa zochitikazi udzakula liti!

Kuyambira liti ndendende Chiwonongeko Chachikulu chidzayamba? Kodi Bhabishya Malika akunena chiyani pa nkhaniyi? Malika akunena kuti masewera owononga adzachitika mu Meena-Shani, koma Malika akufotokoza chiyambi cha masewera owonongawa ngati gawo lomaliza la chaka cha 2026 m'malo mwa chaka cha 2025.

Mmodzi mwa a Panchasakhas, Mahapurush Yashovanta Das, analemba za zosokoneza zosanayembekezereka zomwe zidzachitika padziko lonse pa nthawi ya Meena-Shani m'mabuku ake a Malika ndipo anapereka chidziwitso cha nthawi yake yeniyeni. M'modzi mwa mabuku ake a Malika, analemba:

Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନି ଯୋଗରେ ଯେ ଦ୍ୱିତୀୟ ଚରଣ,
ଅକାଳେ ମଡ଼କ ପଡ଼ିବ ସେହି ସମୟରେ ଜାଣ।
ଭୂମିକମ୍ପରେ କମ୍ପିବ ସକଳ ମେଦିନୀ,
କାଳ ଭୈରବୀ ଛାଡ଼ିବେ ଶୂନ୍ୟରେ ଯେ ରଡ଼ି।
ମୀନ ଶନି ଯୋଗରେ ଯେ ତୃତୀୟ ଚରଣ,
ଜଳ ପ୍ରଳୟ ହୋଇବ ଭଲେ ତୁହି ଜାଣ।
ହୁତୁ ହୁତୁ ହୋଇ ଜଳୁଥିବେ ହିଙ୍ଗୁଳା ମାତିବେ,
ବଡ଼ ବଡ଼ କୋଠା ଘର ଜାଳି ଯେ ଦେବେ।
ଚୋର ଟାଉଟର ସଂଖ୍ୟା ପ୍ରବଳ ବଢ଼ିବ,
ସେଥିପାଇଁ ଦେବୀଙ୍କର କୋପେ ନାଶ ଯିବ।
ନ ଦେଖିଲା ନେତ୍ର ପୁଣି ସେ ସମୟେ ଦେଖିବ,
ତ୍ରାହି ତ୍ରାହି ବୋଲି ପୁଣି ଡାକ ଛାଡୁଥିବ।
ହରି ଆଶ୍ରା କର ତୁହି ନିସ୍ତାର ପାଇବୁ,
ଦାସ ଯଶୋବନ୍ତ କହେ ବିହନ ହୋଇବୁ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Mīna śhani yogare je dwitīya charaṇa,
akāḷe maḍaka paḍiba sehi samayare jāṇa.
bhūmikampare kampiba sakaḷa medinī,
kāḷa bhairabī chāḍibe śhūnyare je raḍi.
mīna śhani yogare je tṛutīya charaṇa,
jaḷa pralaya hoiba bhale tuhi jāṇa.
hutu hutu hoi jaḷuthibe hiṅguḷā mātibe,
baḍa baḍa koṭhā ghara jāḷi je debe.
chora ṭāuṭara saṅkhyā prabala baḍhiba,
sethipāiñ debīṅkara kope nāśha jiba.
na dekhilā netra puṇi se samaye dekhiba,
trāhi trāhi boli puṇi ḍāka chāḍuthiba.
Hari āśhrā kara tuhi nistāra pāibu,
dāsa Yaśhobanta kahe bihana hoibu.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, m'zaka ziwiri ndi theka za Meena-Shani yoga, pa gawo lachiwiri (zaka 2026-2027) ndi gawo lachitatu (zaka 2027-2028), imfa zosanayembekezereka zidzachitika zambiri, ndipo chisokonezo chidzalamulira kulikonse. Zivomezi zamphamvu zidzachitika, Dziko lonse lapansi lidzagwedezeka. Padzakhala kulira kwa Bhairavi, ndiko kuti, Amayi Bhairavi adzabangula mwadzidzidzi koopsa kuchokera kumwamba. Zoopsa za chigumula chamadzi zidzaoneka pa Dziko lapansi. Chifukwa cha mkwiyo wa Amayi Hingula, nyumba zazikulu, nyumba zapamwamba, ndi zina zotero, zidzayaka kwambiri. Mahapurush analemba kuti anthu oipa onse adzakhala ozunzidwa ndi mkwiyo wa Amayi Milungu. Pa nthawiyo, anthu adzaona zonse zomwe maso awo sanathe kuziona mpaka nthawiyo, ndiko kuti, zonse zidzachitika m'njira yosanayembekezereka. Anthu onse adzalira chifundo. Amene adzadzipereka ku mapazi a Ambuye Shri Hari ndikutenga chitetezo chake, Mahapurush anati iwo okha adzapeza chipulumutso ku zonsezi ndikukhala mbewu za Satya Yuga.

Mu vesi lomwe lili pamwambapa, Mahapurush Yashovanta Das adanena kuti masewera owonongawa adzayamba bwino kuyambira gawo lachiwiri la Meena-Shani yoga, ndiko kuti, zaka 2026-2027.

Pa nthawi ya Kali Yuga iyi, Mahapurushas ambiri anabadwira ku Odisha nthawi ndi nthawi. M'zaka za m'ma 1900, Mahapurush wobadwira ku Odisha adavumbulutsa maulosi omwe adanenedwa ndi Ambuye Wamkulu mwiniwake, Amayi Yogamaya, ndi milungu ndi milungukazi kuti apindulitse okhulupirira odzipereka. Mahapurush analemba:

Odia Sloka:

ବିଂଶ ଛବିଶ ସାଲ ସରିବ,
ଶ୍ରୀ ହରି ଆଦେଶ ଦେବୀଙ୍କୁ ହେବ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Binśha chabisha sāla sariba,
Śhrī Hari ādeśha debīṅku heba.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, gawo lomaliza la chaka cha 2026 likafika kapena chaka cha 2026 chikatha, Ambuye Kalki adzapereka lamulo ku mphamvu zonse za Mulungu (Amayi Ashta Chandis, 64 Yoginis, Katyayanis zisanu ndi zinayi za crore) kuti ayambe chiwonongeko.

Mahapurush kenako analembanso:

Odia Sloka:

ସମୟ ବିଂଶ ଛବିଶ ସାଲ ସରିବ ଯେବେ,
ସବୁ ଦେବା ଦେବୀ ବାହାରେ ବିଶ୍ୱଯୁଦ୍ଧ କରିବେ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Samaya binśha chabisha sāla sariba jebe,
sabu debā debī bāhāre biśhwajuddha karibe.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, pambuyo pa chaka cha 2026, milungu ndi milungukazi zonse zidzaliza lipenga la nkhondo ya Deva-Asura. Chiyambi cha nkhondo (Nkhondo Yadziko Lonse) chidzachitika padziko lonse.

Choyenera kudziwa ndi chakuti m'mavesi onse awiri omwe ali pamwambapa, Mahapurush analemba momveka bwino “Chaka cha 2026”.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akulemba m'buku lake la Malika lotchedwa ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ଛଅ ସାତ ମିଶାଇଣ ତେର ଯୋଗ କର,
ଏହିଠାରୁ ଘୋର ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ଭାରତ ବକ୍ଷର।
ସେ ବେଳେ ଖଣ୍ଡ ପ୍ରଳୟଟି ହେବ ଜାଣ,
ଛିନ୍ନଛତ୍ର ଏ ପୃଥିବୀ କହିଲି ପ୍ରମାଣ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Chha-a sāta mishaiṇa tera yoga kara,
ehi thāru ghora juddha bhārata bakṣhara.
se beḷe khaṇḍa pralayaṭi heba jāṇa,
chhinnachhatra e pruthibī kahili pramāṇa.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, phatikizani zisanu ndi chimodzi (6) ndi zisanu ndi ziwiri (7) kenako onjezani khumi ndi zitatu (13) pa chiwerengerocho, ndiko kuti, 6+7+13=26, kuchokera pamenepo nkhondo yowopsa idzayamba ku India. Pambuyo pa nthawi yomweyo, zochitika za chiwonongeko chochepa (Khanda Pralaya) zidzachitika ndipo Dziko lapansi lidzayamba kuwonongedwa.

Choyenera kudziwa ndi chakuti pano Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, mosiyana ndi mavesi awiri omwe ali pamwambapa, sanalembe “Chaka cha 2026” koma analemba 26 basi. Mwina pano, ndi 26, chizindikiro cha Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chikuloza ku chaka cha 2026 chokha.

Mahapurush wobadwira m'zaka za m'ma 1900 akulembanso:

Odia Sloka:

ବିଂଶ ଛବିଶ ପରେ ତ କରିବୁ ବିଚାର,
ମନେ ନ କରିବୁ ଯଦି ଯିବୁ ଯମପୁର।

Kumasulira mawu:

Binśha chabisha pare ta karibu bichāra,
mane na karibu jadi jibu Yamapura.

Tanthauzo:
Mu vesi lomwe lili pamwambapa, Amayi Yogamaya akunena kuti pambuyo pa chaka cha 2026, aliyense adzaweruzidwa. Onse omwe ndi osakhulupirira, oipa, ochimwa, ndi zina zotero, onse adzawonongedwa.

Choncho, ziyenera kumvedwa momveka bwino kuti masewera owononga a Kalki ofotokozedwa ndi Malika adzachitika pa nthawi ya Meena-Shani yoga yomweyo, koma izi sizidzachitika mu chaka cha 2025 koma zidzayamba kuyambira gawo lomaliza la 2026.

Kwa zaka zingati zonse masewera owonongawa adzakhalapo, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akulemba pa nkhaniyi m'buku lake la Malika lotchedwa ‘Kaliyuga Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

ଚବିଶ ଠାବରୁ ଅଣତିରିଶ ଯାଏ ପ୍ରମାଦ ଅଛି ଅପାର,
ଅଣତିରିଶ ଅଙ୍କରେଟି ହୋ ଗରୁଡ଼ ମହାକଳ୍କୀ ଅବତାର।
ଅଣତିରିଶ ଭିତରେଟି ଗରୁଡ଼ ସବୁ ଉତ୍ପାତ ସରିବ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Chabiśha ṭhābaru aṇatiriśha jāe pramāda achi apāra,
aṇatiriśha aṅkareṭi ho Garuḍa mahā Kaḷkī abatāra.
aṇatiriśha bhitareṭi Garuḍa sabu utpāta sariba.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, masewera owononga adzayamba kuyambira nambala 24 ndipo adzakhalapo mpaka nambala 29. Masewera onse owononga adzatha pa nambala 29.

Dziwani kuti mu vesi lomwe lili pamwambapa, Mahapurush wakambirana za nambala 24, osati chaka cha 2024. Koma Mahapurush wafotokoza momveka bwino kuti masewera owonongawa, kuyambira nambala 24 ndikukhala mpaka nambala 29, adzapitirira kwa zaka 6 zonse. Mahapurush sanafotokoze bwino kuti zidzayamba chaka chiti ndipo zidzatha chaka chiti, koma adanena kuti kuyambira nambala 24 mpaka nambala 29, ndiko kuti, kwa zaka 6 zonse, masewera owonongawa adzapitirira.

Popeza kuchokera m'mavesi omwe ali pamwambapa tadziwa kale kuti masewera owonongawa adzayamba kuyambira gawo lomaliza la chaka cha 2026, tsopano titha kufika mosavuta pa mfundo iyi kuti nambala 24 ya ‘Kaliyuga Malika’ ikuloza ku chaka cha 2026-2027. Mofananamo, nambala 29 ya mndandanda womwewo wa buku la Malika lomweli ikuloza ku chaka cha 2031-2032.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, kudzera mu Malika Pothi yake, anafotokozanso –

Odia Sloka:

ଏହା ତ ହେଉଛି କାଳର ଭୟ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ ତହିଁ,
ଏହା ତ କଳ୍କୀ ଖେଳଟି ଦାସ ଦେଲି ବୁଝାଇ।
କଳ୍କୀ ଖେଳ କରୁଅଛନ୍ତି ହେତୁ ପ୍ରମାଣ,
ଆଉ ବା ଏକତିରିଶ ସେ ତ ହେତୁରେ ଜାଣ।
ମହାମାରୀ ସେ ତ ଆସିବ କହେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ ଆସ,
ତେତିଶି ମସିହା ସୁଦ୍ଧାଟି କହେ ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ।
ଠା ଠିକଣା ଯେ ରହିବ ନାହିଁ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ କହି,
ଏଭଳି ସମୟ ଆସିବ ଦାସ ଦେଲି ବୁଝାଇ।
କାହାରି ପତା ରହିବ ନାହିଁ ହେତୁରେ ଜାଣ,
ବୈଜ୍ଞାନୀମାନେ ବାଟଟି ପାଇବେ ନାହିଁରେ ଜାଣ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Ehā ta heuchhi kāḷara bhaya Achyutī tahiñ,
ehā ta Kaḷkī kheḷaṭi dāsa deli bujhāi.
Kaḷkī kheḷa karuachhanti hetu pramāṇa,
āu bā ekatiriśa se ta heture jāṇa.
Mahāmārī se ta āsiba kahe Achyutī āsa,
Tetiriśhi masihā suddhāṭi kahe dīna Achyuta.
Ṭhā ṭhikaṇā je rahiba nāhiñ Achyutī kahi,
ebhaḷi samaya āsiba dāsa deli bujhāi.
Kāhāri patā rahiba nāhiñ heture jāṇa,
Baijñānīmāne bāṭaṭi pāibe nāhiñ re jāṇa.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, Ambuye Kalki adzachita masewera Ake (masewera Ake owononga). Masewera owonongawa adzapitirira mpaka chaka cha 2033. Pakadali pano, miliri yambiri idzabwera, yomwe idzabweretsa chisokonezo m'dera la anthu. Nthawi idzafika pamene padzakhala chisokonezo kulikonse. Anthu onse adzakhala mu mkhalidwe woipa. Ngakhale chidziwitso cha asayansi sichidzakhala chothandiza.

Komabe, Achyutananda Das ji adanenanso mu ‘Kaliyuga Malika’ yake kuti zochitika zazikulu zowononga zidzatha pa nambala 27.

Mahapurush analemba –

Odia Sloka:

ସତାଇଶ ଅଙ୍କେ ଦକ୍ଷିଣ ଦିଗରୁ ସମୁଦ୍ର ଆସିବ ମାଡ଼ି,
ଉତ୍ତରରୁ ଗଙ୍ଗା ଉଛୁଳି ପଡ଼ିଣ ତହିଁ ସଙ୍ଗେ ଦେବ ଧାଡ଼ି।
ପଶ୍ଚିମ ଦିଗରୁ ଜଳ ସ୍ରୋତ ଏକ ଆସି ତହିଁ ଯିବ ମିଶି,
ଖଣ୍ଡ ପ୍ରଳୟର ସୃଜନା କରିବ ପାପୀ ତହିଁ ଯିବେ ଭାସି।
ପୁଣ୍ୟାତ୍ମାମାନଙ୍କୁ ସ୍ୱୟଂ ବଳଦେବ ଉଦ୍ଧାର କରି ରଖିବେ,
ଭାରତର ଗିରି ପର୍ବତ ପାହାଡ଼େ ନେଇ ଯତନେ ପାଳିବେ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Satāisha aṅke dakhina digaru samudra āsiba māḍi,
uttararu Gaṅgā uchhuḷi paḍiṇa tahiñ saṅge deba dhāḍi.
Pashchima digaru jala srota eka āsi tahiñ jiba mishi,
khaṇḍa praḷayara sṛujanā kariba pāpī tahiñ jibe bhāsi.
Puṇyātmāmānaṅku swayaṃ Baḷadeba uddhāra kari rakhibe,
Bhāratara giri parbata pāhāḍe nei jatane pāḷibe.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiko kuti, pa nambala 27, mafunde a nyanja adzabwera kuchokera Kumwera ndipo adzakumana ndi mtsinje wa Ganges wodzaza kuchokera Kumpoto. Pa nthawi yomweyo, mafunde owopsa a tsunami adzabweranso kuchokera Kumadzulo ndikubweretsa chisokonezo. Mu chiwonongeko chochepa ichi, ochimwa osawerengeka adzafa. Amene ali oyera mtima ndi miyoyo yabwino, Baladev Mwiniwake adzawapulumutsa ndikuwasunga motetezeka mosamala pa mapiri ndi zitunda za India.

Malinga ndi Mahapurush, ili lidzakhala tsoka lomaliza pakati pa masoka akuluakulu omwe adzachitika pa nambala 27, ndiko kuti, mu chaka cha 2029-2030 malinga ndi kuwerengera komwe kwatchulidwa pamwambapa.

Tsopano tiyeni tiyesetse kumvetsa nthawi ino ndi mavesi ena. Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akulemba mu ‘Bhabishyata Malika’ –

Odia Sloka:

ପାଞ୍ଚ ବର୍ଷ କାଳ ସତୁରି ଯାଏ ଠୁଳ,
ଲାଗି ରହିବ ତୃତୀୟ ମହାସମର।
ପାଞ୍ଚ ବର୍ଷ କାଳ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ହୋଇବ ଘୋର,
ଚମକ ପଡିବ ସ୍ଵର୍ଗ ମର୍ତ୍ତ୍ୟ ପାତାଳ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Pāñcha barṣha kāḷa saturi jāe ṭhuḷa,
lāgi rahiba tṛutīya mahāsamara.
pāñcha barṣha kāḷa juddha hoiba ghora,
chamaka paḍiba swarga martya pātāḷa.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiye kuti, Nkhondo Yachitatu Yapadziko Lonse idzakhalapo zaka zisanu. Padzakhala nkhondo yoopsa kwa zaka zisanu; Kumwamba, Dziko Lapansi, ndi Kumunsi kwa Dziko lapansi zidzanjenjemera.

Achyutananda Das ji akulemba mu buku lina la Malika lake –

Odia Sloka:

ଶ୍ରୀ ହରି କହନ୍ତି ବାସବ ଶୁଣ,
ଚବିଶ ଠାରୁ ସତେଇଶ ଜାଣ।
ଏଥି ଭିତରେ ଯେ ହୋଇବ ଗୋଳ,
କାହାରି ନ ରହିବ ଥଳକୁଳ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Śhrī Hari kahanti Bāsaba śhuṇa,
chabiśha ṭhāru sataiśha jāṇa.
ethi bhitare je hoiba goḷa,
kāhāri na rahiba thaḷakuḷa.

Tanthauzo:
Mu vesi lomwe lili pamwambapa, Ambuye Shri Krishna, akukambirana ndi Indra, akunena kuti padzakhala nkhondo yoopsa kuyambira nambala 24 mpaka nambala 27.

Zikuwonekeratu kuchokera m'mavesi onse awiri omwe ali pamwambapa kuti nkhondo yayikulu kapena Nkhondo Yachitatu Yapadziko Lonse idzakhalapo pafupifupi zaka 4-5. Ndiye kuti, kuyambira mu 2026-2027 ndikutha mu 2029-2030.

Choncho, chisokonezo chachikulu chomaliza chotchulidwa ndi Mahapurush mu ‘Kaliyuga Malika’ mu nambala 27 kapena chaka cha 2029-2030 chikugwirizana bwino ndi mavesi onse awiri omwe ali pamwambapa.

Kuchokera pazokambirana zomwe zili pamwambapa, ngati chigamulo chaperekedwa mwachidule:

Chaka cha 2026 – 2027: Nthawi ya chiwonongeko idzayamba.
Chaka cha 2029 – 2030: Zochitika zoopsa kwambiri ndi zowononga zidzatha.
Chaka cha 2031 – 2032: Zochitika zonse zowononga zidzatha.

Dziwani: Zochitika zazikulu zowononga zomwe zikuyembekezeka kutha pofika chaka cha 2029 zikuphatikizapo zonse zomwe zimakambidwa nthawi zambiri, monga – zivomezi zazikulu, chisokonezo chamadzi choopsa, masoka a mphepo, masoka a moto, kugwa kwa miyala yochokera kumwamba (meteorites), matenda-miliri, nkhondo ya India ndi mayiko adani, Nkhondo Yapadziko Lonse, nkhondo ya Kali-Mahabharata, kuphulika kwa Hirakud ndi madamu ena, mdima kwa masiku asanu ndi awiri ndi mausiku asanu ndi awiri, Bhairavi Dak, ndi zina zotero.

13 Chiyambi cha Satyayuga

Kuti alowe mu Satyayuga, ndikofunikira kuti munthu ayende panjira ya Dharma. Ayenera kulankhula zoona nthawi zonse ndikutsatira zoona zokha; sayenera kutenga njira yamisala koma njira yamtendere. Ayenera kukhala ndi chikondi, kukoma mtima, ndi chifundo kwa zamoyo zonse. Sayenera kupweteka zamoyo zilizonse kudzera m'maganizo ake, mawu ake, ndi khalidwe lake. Ayenera kukhala wofunira zabwino zamoyo zonse popanda chifukwa, khalidwe lake liyenera kukhala logolowereka kwa ena, ayenera kukhala wosavuta komanso woona mtima, sayenera kukhala ndi chidani kwa aliyense, ndipo ayenera kudziwa momwe angalamulire malingaliro ake. Ayenera kukhala wokhulupirira Mulungu, nthawi zonse ayenera kukhazikitsa maganizo ake mwa Mulungu, azikhala nthawi yoyimba nyimbo zotamanda, kutchula mayina a Mulungu, kulowa m'misonkhano yauzimu (Satsang), kupemphera mapemphero atatu tsiku lililonse (Trisandhya), kutsatira malemba opatulika, ndikuwerenga Shrimad Bhagavat Mahapurana (yomwe imadziwika kuti Mahapurana pakati pa Puranas 18) tsiku lililonse. Ayenera kukhala amene amawerenga kapena kumva masewera a Mulungu. Ayenera kudya chakudya choyera komanso cha Sattvic (chamasamba). Pamodzi ndi kuchita ntchito zake zapadziko lapansi, ayeneranso kuchita Yoga, kutchula mayina, ndi zina zotero. Ayenera nthawi zonse kulemekeza makolo ake ndi akuluakulu, kusonyeza chikondi kwa achinyamata, ndikukhazikitsa ubwenzi ndi anzawo.

Aliyense wa ife akufuna kulowa mu Satyayuga pothawa zochitika zowononga zomwe zikubwera, masoka achilengedwe, ndi Nkhondo Yapadziko Lonse, ndipo mwa kupeza chisomo cha Ambuye Kalki. Koma kodi kulowa mu Satyayuga nkosavuta choncho? Ayi. Kulowa mu Satyayuga sikuli ngati kupanga yankho mu galasi ndikumwa kuti aliyense amene amamwa motere adzalowa mu Satyayuga!

Kuti alowe mu Satyayuga, munthu aliyense ayenera kubweretsa kusintha kwakukulu m'moyo wake. Ndikofunikira kuti pamodzi ndi kuchita ntchito ndi maudindo apadziko lapansi, ayambe kukhala ndi moyo wauzimu.

Kufotokoza momwe nthawi yomwe ikubwera idzakhalira kwa okhulupirira, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akulemba mu buku lake la Malika ‘Chakada Madana’ –

Odia Sloka:

କଳିଯୁଗ ହଟି ଯାଇ ସତ୍ୟର ପ୍ରକାଶ,
ଭକତମାନେ ରହିବେ ଅତ୍ୟନ୍ତ ଉଲ୍ଲାସ।
ବହୁ କଷ୍ଟ ପାଇଛନ୍ତି ଭକତ ମୋହର,
ଅଳ୍ପ ଦିନରେ ଦୁଃଖ କଷ୍ଟ ଯିବ ଦୂର।
ଏ କଳି ହଟିବ ଯୁଗ ଅନନ୍ତ ହୋଇବ,
ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ କାମନା ପୂରଣ ହୋଇବ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Kaḷiyuga haṭi jāi satyara prakāśha,
bhakatamāne rahibe atyanta ullāsa.
bahu kaṣhṭa pāichhanti bhakata mohara,
alpa dinare duḥkha kaṣhṭa jiba dūra.
e kaḷi haṭiba juga ananta hoiba,
dīna Achyutī kāmanā pūraṇa hoiba.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiye kuti, okhulupirira anavutika kwambiri mu Kaliyuga, koma patapita nthawi yochepa pamene Kaliyuga idzatha ndipo kuwala kwa nthawi yosatha (Ananta Yuga) kudzawonekera, okhulupirira adzakondwera kwambiri. Ndiye kuti, zisoni zawo zonse ndi zowawa zidzachoka.

Woyera Bhima Bhoi akulemba mu buku lina la Malika lake ‘Stuti Chintamani’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଦୁଷ୍ଟ ନିବାରିବେ ସନ୍ଥଙ୍କୁ ରଖିବେ ପାଳିବେ ଜନ ପରଜା,
କଳିଯୁଗ ଶେଷେ ସତ୍ୟ ଉପୁଜିବ ଅନାଦି ହୋଇବେ ରାଜା।
ହରଷ ହୋଇବେ ଭକତ ସକଳ ହରି ହରିବେ ଯେ ଦୁଃଖ,
ହରିବୋଲ ହୁଳହୁଳି ଶବଦରେ ପୂରିବ ଏହି ତ୍ରୈଲୋକ୍ୟ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Duṣṭa nibāribe santhaṅku rakhibe pāḷibe jana parajā,
Kaḷiyuga sheṣe satya upujiba Anādi hoibe rājā.
Haraṣa hoibe bhakata sakaḷa hari haribe je duḥkha,
Haribola huḷuhuḷi shabadare pūriba ehi traiḷokya.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiye kuti, Ambuye Wamkulu adzawononga oipa ndikuteteza oyera mtima ndi okhulupirira, ndipo adzasamalira anthu apadziko lapansi. Kaliyuga idzatha ndipo Satyayuga idzafika, ndipo Ambuye Kalki Mwiniwake adzakhala wolamulira dziko lonse lapansi. Ambuye adzachotsa zisoni za okhulupirira onse ndikuwasunga osangalala. Okhulupirira adzakondwera, ndipo maiko onse atatu adzamveka ndi kutchula dzina 'Hari'.

13.1 Kodi Satyayuga idzayamba liti?

Bhabishya Malika ikunena kuti Ananta Yuga kapena Adi Satyayuga idzayamba mu chaka cha 2033. Mahapurush analemba mu buku lake la Malika –

Odia Sloka:

ଦୁଇ ଡାହାଣରେ ଶୂନ ଏକ ତିନି ରଖ,
ସମ୍ପୂର୍ଣ୍ଣ ସତ୍ୟ ପ୍ରକାଶ ହୋଇବ ପ୍ରତ୍ୟକ୍ଷ।
ମ୍ଲେଚ୍ଛ ଯବନ ଗଣ ବିନାଶଟି ଧ୍ରୁବ,
ଦୁଇ ଶୂନ ଏକ ତିନି ଅଙ୍କରୁ ଆରମ୍ଭ।
ଅନ୍ୟାୟ ଅସତ୍ୟ ମାନ ହଟି ଯାଉଥିବ,
ଅଧର୍ମର ନାଶେ ଧର୍ମ ପ୍ରଭାବୀ ହୋଇବ।
ତେତିଶିରେ ଚାରି ପାଦ ଧର୍ମ ପ୍ରକାଶିବ,
ସତ୍ୟ ଶାନ୍ତି ଦୟା କ୍ଷମା ତହୁଁ ବିକଶିବ।
ସରଳ ସଜ୍ଜନ ସାଧୁ ସନ୍ଥ ଜନ ମେଳ,
ହେବା ପାଇଁ ଏ ଧରା ରେ ଆସିଗଲା ବେଳ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Dui ḍāhāṇare shūna eka tini rakha,
sampūrṇṇa satya prakāsha hoiba pratyakṣa.
Mlechha jabana gaṇa bināshaṭi dhruba,
dui shūna eka tini aṅkaru ārambha.
Anyāya asatya māna haṭi jāuthiba,
adharmara nāshe dharma prabhābhī hoiba.
Tetishire chāri pāda dharma prakāshiba,
satya shānti dayā kṣamā tahuñ bikaśhiba.
Saraḷa sajjana sādhu santha jana meḷa,
hebā pāiñ e dharā re āsigalā beḷa.

Tanthauzo:
Mahapurush analemba kuti kuyambira chaka cha 2013, chiwonongeko cha Kaliyuga ndi kuwala kwa Choona zidzayamba pang'onopang'ono. Kuyambira nthawi imeneyo, chiwonongeko cha oipa ndi ochimwa chidzayamba. Mphamvu ya bodza, kupanda chilungamo, ndi kusayeruzika idzachepa, ndipo chikoka cha Dharma chidzayamba kuwonjezeka. Mahapurush analembanso kuti popitiriza motere, mu chaka cha 2033, kuwala kwa mizati yonse inayi ya Dharma kudzawonekera, kutanthauza kuti kukhazikitsidwa kwathunthu kwa Dharma kudzachitika. Kuyambira nthawi imeneyo, makhalidwe abwino a Choona, Mtendere, Kukoma mtima, ndi Kukhululukira adzayamba kukula kwathunthu. Padzakhala mgwirizano wa anthu onse olemekezeka, okoma mtima ndi oyera mtima, oyera mtima, anzeru.

Mahapurush akulembanso –

Odia Sloka:

ଅତ୍ୟନ୍ତ ଗୁପତୁ ଗୁପତ, ତେତିଶି ଅଙ୍କେ ହେବ ଖ୍ୟାତ।
ଭକତମାନେ ଯେ ଜାଣିବେ, ସମସ୍ତେ ହରଷ ହୋଇବେ।
ସଂସାର ଉଶ୍ଵାସ ହୋଇବ, କାଳର ଭୟ ଯେ ଛାଡିବ।
ଅନ୍ନ ବସ୍ତ୍ରରେ ସୁଖୀ ହେବେ, ଧର୍ମ ପଥକୁ ଆଚୋରିବେ।
ଶସ୍ୟ ଯେ ବହୁତ ହୋଇବ, ଲକ୍ଷ୍ମୀ କୃପାରୁ ସବୁ ହେବ।
ସତ୍ୟରେ ସର୍ବେ ଆତଜାତ, ସତ୍ୟ କଳି ଯେ ହେବ ଖ୍ୟାତ।
ପୁରାଣ ଭାଗବତ ମାନ, କରିବେ ଗାନ ସଂକୀର୍ତ୍ତନ।
କଳି କଳ୍ମଷ ନାଶ ଯିବ, ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବାକ୍ୟ ନ ଟଳିବ।
କହନ୍ତି ବ୍ରହ୍ମାଣ୍ଡ କରତା, ଅନନ୍ତ ଯୁଗର ବ୍ୟବସ୍ଥା।
ମ୍ଲେଚ୍ଛ ନଥିବେ ଏକ ଜାଣ, ଭକ୍ତ ଭଗବାନ ମିଳନ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Atyanta gupatu gupata, Tetishi aṅke heba khyāta.
Bhakatamāne je jāṇibe, samaste haraṣa hoibe.
Saṃsāra ushwāsa hoiba, kāḷara bhaya je chhāḍiba.
Anna bastrare sukhī hebe, dharma pathaku āchoribe.
Shasya je bahuta hoiba, Laxmī kṛpāru sabu heba.
Satyare sarbe ātajāta, satya kaḷi je heba khyāta.
Purāṇa Bhāgabata māna, karibe gāna saṅkīrttana.
Kaḷi kalmaṣa nāsha jiba, Achyuta bākya na ṭaliba.
Kahanti Brahmāṇḍa karatā, ananta jugara byabasthā.
Mlechha nathibe eka jāṇa, bhakta Bhagavān milana.

Tanthauzo:
Mahapurush anati izi ndi chinthu chinsinsi kwambiri ndipo zidzachitika mu nambala 33 (chaka cha 2033). Okhulupirira okha ndiwo adzadziwa izi. Adzakondwera kwambiri podziwa izi. Dziko lapansi lidzakhala lopanda tchimo; sipadzakhala mantha kapena chisokonezo m'maganizo mwa aliyense. Aliyense adzakhala wosangalala, aliyense adzakhala ndi chakudya chokwanira ndi zovala, aliyense adzatsatira njira ya Dharma. Ndiye kuti padzakhala zokolola zabwino kwambiri, ndipo izi zidzakhala chifukwa cha chisomo cha Amayi Lakshmi. Aliyense adzayenda panjira ya Choona; nthawi yosatha idzafika. Aliyense adzawerenga Puranas ndi Bhagavat ndikuyimba nyimbo zotamanda ndi kutchula mayina. Kaliyuga idzawonongedwa kwathunthu; mawu awa sadzalephera konse. Mahapurush ananenanso kuti iyi idzakhala nthawi yosatha ndipo iyi idzakhala dongosolo la nthawi yosatha (Adya Satyayuga). Sipadzakhala wosayeruzika, woipa, kapena mlechha mmodzi padziko lapansi. Okhulupirira okha ndiwo adzatsala ndipo onse adzagwirizana ndi Mulungu.

Mahapurush wobadwa m'zaka za zana la 20 (amene tidamukambirana kale), akutchula Ambuye Wamkulu, Amayi Yogamaya, ndi milungu ndi milungu yachikazi, analemba –

Odia Sloka:

ତିନି ତିନି ଚାରି ତିନି ଧାଡିରେ ତ ଜାଣ,
ଏହି ଦିନ ଠାରୁ ହେବ ସତ୍ୟର ନିୟମ।
ଗୁରୁବାର ଦିନ ଥିବ ଶୁଣୁଥାଅ ନର,
ସେହି ଦିନଠାରୁ ସତ୍ୟ ଭୋଗ ତ ଆମର।

Kumasulira mawu:

Tini tini chāri tini dhāḍire ta jāṇa,
ehi dina thāru heba satyara niyama.
Gurubāra dina thiba śhuṇuthāa nara,
sehi dinathāru satya bhoga ta amara.

Tanthauzo:
Apa Mahapurush akupempha kulemba “3” kanayi motsatizana. Ndiye kuti 3333. Mahapurush akunena kuti kuyambira lero, Satyayuga idzayamba padziko lapansi. Apa ndi 3333, Mahapurush akutanthauza – tsiku lachitatu, mwezi wachitatu, ndi zaka makumi atatu ndi zitatu, ndiye kuti, Marichi 3, 2033. Mahapurush analembanso kuti tsikulo lidzakhala Lachinayi ndipo Satyayuga idzayamba kuyambira tsiku lomwelo.

Mahapurush analembanso kamodzi –

Odia Sloka:

ତିନି ତାରିଖ ତ ତିନି ମାସ ବିଂଶ ଶାଳ,
ତେତିଶ ଅଙ୍କ ତ ପରେ ସତ୍ୟ ସତ୍ୟ ବୋଲ।

Kumasulira mawu:

Tini tārikha ta tini māsa biṃśha śhāḷa,
tetishā aṅka ta pare satya satya bola.

Tanthauzo:
Ndiye kuti, pambuyo pa tsiku lachitatu la mwezi wachitatu wa nambala makumi atatu ndi zitatu ya chaka chakhumi ndi chiwiri, ndiye kuti, Marichi 3, 2033, Satyayuga idzabwera.

Choncho, palibe kukayika tsopano kuti nthawi yatsopano (Ananta Yuga kapena Adi Satyayuga) idzayamba mu chaka cha 2033. Idzakhala nthawi ya wokhulupirira ndi Mulungu okha. Mwa mawu ena, okhulupirira okha ndiwo adzalowa mu nthawi ino. Ambuye adzateteza Dziko Lapansi ndi Choona, Dharma, ndi Chilungamo, ndipo okhulupirira onse adzachita Choona, Mtendere, Kukoma mtima, ndi Kukhululukira m'miyoyo yawo.

14 Chidule

Bhabishya Malika ndi gulu la mabuku ambiri olembedwa ndi Panchasakhas obadwa pafupifupi zaka 600 zapitazo ndi oyera mtima ena a Odia ndi amuna akuluakulu obadwa m'zaka za zana la 17, 18, 19, ndi 20. Mabuku a Malika analembedwa makamaka ndi Panchasakhas a Ambuye Shri Chaitanya Mahaprabhu. Panchasakhas awa amabwera ngati anzawo apamtima a Ambuye mu nthawi iliyonse ndi mayina osiyanasiyana, ndipo mu Kaliyuga nayenso, pafupifupi zaka 600 zapitazo, Panchasakhas awa adapeza kuyandikira kwa kubadwa kwa Ambuye, Chaitanya Mahaprabhu. Mayina a Panchasakhas awa obadwa mu Kaliyuga ndi – Achyutananda Das, Balaram Das, Jashobanta Das, Shishu Ananta Das, ndi Jagannath Das.

Panchasakhas amaonedwa kuti ndi mbali za kubadwa (Anshavatar) kwa Ambuye, ndipo pafupifupi zaka 600 zapitazo, mabuku a Malika analembedwa ndi iwo okha pa lamulo la Ambuye. Cholinga chokha cha kulembedwa kwa mabuku a Malika ndi ubwino wa okhulupirira – kudzutsira chikumbumtima chogona cha okhulupirira kumapeto kwa Kaliyuga ndikuwapulumutsa powatsogolera. Panchasakhas analemba momveka bwino m'mabuku awo kuti mabuku awa sayenera kuwululidwa kwa aliyense kupatula okhulupirira. Panchasakhas anafotokozanso kuti okhawo ndiwo adzatha kuvomereza mawu a Malika omwe akhala okhulupirira a Ambuye kuyambira kubadwa ndi nthawi zakale.

Popeza Malika ndi mawu a Mahaprabhu Mwiniwake ndipo analembedwa ndi Panchasakhas okha pa lamulo la Ambuye, choncho mawu aliwonse a Malika ali ngati mzere wojambulidwa mu mwala womwe sungathe kulakwitsa ndipo sungathe kusinthidwa. Palibe chodabwitsa pa izi kuti maulosi osawerengeka a Malika olembedwa pa nthawi ino adatsimikizira kukhala oona 100%. Mwachitsanzo – ulosi wa ufulu wa India, ulosi wa kubadwa kwa Gandhiji, ulosi wa kukhazikitsidwa kwa dziko lotchedwa Pakistan, ulosi wa kumanga damu la Hirakud ku Odisha, ndi zina zotero.

Bhabishya Malika ikukambirana mwatsatanetsatane mitu monga kutha kwa Kaliyuga, Kalki Avatar, chiwonongeko cha oipa ndi ochimwa, chitetezo cha okhulupirira, kukhazikitsidwa kwa Dharma, kufika kwa Ananta Yuga, ndi zina zotero. Malika akunena kuti nthawi ya Kaliyuga, yomwe inali zaka 4,32,000, idzachepetsedwa mpaka zaka 5,000 zokha chifukwa cha machimo osiyanasiyana ochitidwa mu Kaliyuga. Patapita zaka 5,000, Ambuye Jagannath adzachoka ku Nilachala Dham Yake, Puri, ndikubadwa m'thupi la munthu ku Biraja Kshetra, Jajpur ku Odisha, ndipo pambuyo pake adzakhala ku Khandagiri, Bhubaneswar. Malinga ndi Malika, uku kudzakhala Kalki Avatar wa Mulungu.

Malika akunena kuti kumapeto kwa Kaliyuga, okhulupirira adzapeza zizindikiro zambiri zotere kuchokera ku kachisi wa Puri Jagannath ndi nyumba za amonke ndi akachisi ena a India, powona izi adzamvetsa kuti Kaliyuga yatha ndipo Ambuye Kalki wabadwa. Mwachitsanzo, kuwuluka kwa mbendera yayikulu kuchokera ku kachisi wa Jagannath, kugwa mobwerezabwereza kwa miyala kuchokera ku kachisi, Nilachakra kukhala yopendekeka, kukhala pafupipafupi kwa mbalame ya chiwombankhanga pamwamba pa kachisi, ndi zina zotero. Zikudziwika kuti zizindikiro zonsezi zawonedwa ndi okhulupirira m'zaka zaposachedwapa.

Malika ikuloza kubadwa kwa Ambuye Kalki mu chaka china pakati pa 2005-2008. Malinga ndi Malika, cholinga cha Kalki Avatar ndi kuwononga oipa, kuteteza okhulupirira, ndikukhazikitsanso Dharma padziko lapansi. Malika akunena kuti Yuga-parivartana iyi idzayamba mu 2026-2027 pa nthawi ya Meena-Shani yoga ndi Ambuye Kalki ndikutha kwathunthu mu 2031-2032. Pakadali pano, zochitika zonse zazikulu zowononga zikuyenera kuchitika pofika chaka cha 2029.

Malika akunenanso kuti pa nthawi yomwe Ambuye adzakhazikitsa Dharma padziko lapansi, dzina la Mfumu ya Puri lidzakhala Shri Dibyasingha Deb, kutanthauza kuti masewera onse owononga a Ambuye adzachitika pa nthawi ya ulamuliro wa Mfumu ya Puri Dibyasingha Deb. Malinga ndi Malika, masewera owononga awa adzakhala makamaka kudzera mu chisokonezo cha Zinthu Zisanu (Panchabhuta) ndi Nkhondo Yapadziko Lonse.

Malika akunena kuti pambuyo pa zochitika zonse zowononga kutha kwathunthu pofika chaka cha 2031-2032, Adi-Satyayuga kapena Anantayuga idzayamba mu chaka cha 2033.
